### [1] [Intrinsic mirror symmetry for orbifold cubic surfaces and the $A_1$ spherical DAHA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38556)
*Pierrick Bousseau,Sayan Chattopadhyay*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文确定了一个含三个节点的轨形三次曲面（orbifold cubic surface）的内蕴镜像（intrinsic mirror），并借助一次穿孔环面（once-punctured torus）的 $PGL(2,\mathbb{C})$ 与 $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$ 特征簇（character variety）之间的镜像对称解释所得几何；进而用高亏格 Gromov-Witten 理论构造该镜像的量子化，并证明其恢复 $A_1$ 球面双仿射 Hecke 代数（spherical double affine Hecke algebra）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅指出研究目标为确定特定轨形三次曲面的内蕴镜像，并通过特征簇间的镜像对称理解其几何。

Method: 采用内蕴镜像（intrinsic mirror）构造以处理含三个节点的轨形三次曲面；通过一次穿孔环面的 $PGL(2,\mathbb{C})$ 与 $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$ 特征簇之间的镜像对称进行几何解释；并利用高亏格 Gromov-Witten 理论（higher genus Gromov-Witten theory）构造镜像的量子化（quantization）。

Result: 得到了该轨形三次曲面的内蕴镜像，并给出与特征簇镜像对称相对应的几何解释；所构造的量子化可恢复 $A_1$ 球面双仿射 Hecke 代数。

Conclusion: 摘要未给出独立结论段落，其核心论断为：内蕴镜像的量子化在高亏格 Gromov-Witten 理论框架下与 $A_1$ 球面双仿射 Hecke 代数相对应。

Abstract: We determine the intrinsic mirror of an orbifold cubic surface with three nodes and interpret the resulting geometry through mirror symmetry between the $PGL(2,\mathbb{C})$ and $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$ character varieties of the once-punctured torus. We further construct a quantization of the mirror using higher genus Gromov-Witten theory and show that it recovers the $A_1$ spherical double affine Hecke algebra.

</details>


### [2] [Structure of higher-genus open-closed Gromov--Witten theory of $\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^{1}}(p-1)\oplus\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^{1}}(-p-1)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40257)
*Shuai Guo,Jingyi Xu,Qingsheng Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究环面 Calabi–Yau 三维流形 \(X_p = \operatorname{Tot}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^1}(p-1) \oplus \mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^1}(-p-1))\)（\(p\ge 2\)）的闭与开 Gromov–Witten 势（Gromov–Witten potential），在环面权（torus weights）非零条件下证明闭与开镜像对称（mirror symmetry），将其与镜像曲线上的拓扑递归（topological recursion）相联系，并给出多项式结构与双标度极限（double-scaling limit）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要指出该工作的动机在于研究这类局部环面 Calabi–Yau 三维流形的闭与开 Gromov–Witten 势的镜像对称及其可计算结构，并验证 Caporaso–Griguolo–Mariño–Pasquetti–Seminara 提出的高亏格拟设（higher-genus ansatz）与双标度猜想（double-scaling conjecture）。

Method: 方法上，作者在环面权非零条件下建立闭与开镜像对称，将相关势与镜像曲线上的拓扑递归相对应；并由此推导高亏格闭势与稳定开势的多项式结构，以及镜像曲线上拓扑递归的双标度极限。

Result: 主要结果包括：在环面权非零条件下证明闭与开镜像对称；建立高亏格闭势与稳定开势的多项式结构；建立镜像曲线上拓扑递归的双标度极限；并且对闭势的结果证明了 Caporaso–Griguolo–Mariño–Pasquetti–Seminara 的高亏格拟设与双标度猜想。

Conclusion: 结论表明，所研究的环面 Calabi–Yau 三维流形 \(X_p\) 的闭与开 Gromov–Witten 势可通过镜像曲线上的拓扑递归统一定性，并给出多项式结构与双标度行为；这同时验证了既有文献中的高亏格拟设与双标度猜想。

Abstract: We study the closed and open Gromov--Witten potentials of the toric Calabi--Yau threefold $$ X_p=\operatorname{Tot}\bigl(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^{1}}(p-1)\oplus\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^{1}}(-p-1)\bigr),\qquad p\geq 2. $$ We prove closed and open mirror symmetry under a nonvanishing condition on the torus weights, relating these potentials to topological recursion on the mirror curves. We establish polynomial structures for both the higher-genus closed potentials and the stable open potentials. We also establish double-scaling limits for topological recursion on the mirror curves. In particular, our results for the closed potentials prove the higher-genus ansatz and the double-scaling conjecture of Caporaso--Griguolo--Mariño--Pasquetti--Seminara.

</details>

### [3] [A Dynamical Theory of LoRA in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39367)
*Théo Marchetta,Filippo Alessandroni,Alessandro Breccia,Alessandro Ingrosso,Federica Gerace*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文在可解的双任务教师-学生模型（teacher-student model）中，对低秩适应（Low-Rank Adaptation, LoRA）在持续学习（continual learning）中的动力学给出渐近精确刻画，并基于此提出状态相关掩码策略以缓解灾难性遗忘（catastrophic forgetting）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 LoRA 被广泛使用，但其在持续学习中的动力学以及低秩更新影响灾难性遗忘的机制仍不清楚。论文旨在从理论与几何角度阐明这些问题。

Method: 在高维在线学习（online-learning）极限下，针对可解的双任务教师-学生模型，推导有限宏观序参量（order parameters）的闭合常微分方程组，从而得到两个任务阶段泛化误差（generalization errors）的精确表达式，并据此分析状态相关掩码策略与适配器秩的作用。

Result: 理论定量吻合有限维模拟，揭示 LoRA 的双重效应：低秩适应减少对第一任务所学特征的干扰，但其初始化减缓对第二任务的适应。冻结承载最强第一任务表征的隐藏单元并限制适应于互补子空间，可显著减少遗忘同时保持新任务的可塑性；迁移能力仅随目标任务的固有维度（intrinsic dimensionality）提升至饱和，而遗忘随秩持续增长。结果在序列式 MNIST 基准上得到定性复现。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了低秩适应如何在序列任务间组织信息的动力学与几何解释，并表明结构性分区与秩选择可有效权衡遗忘与可塑性。

Abstract: Despite the widespread use of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), little is known about its dynamics in continual learning and the mechanisms by which low-rank updates affect catastrophic forgetting. We provide an asymptotically exact dynamical characterization of LoRA in a solvable two-task teacher-student model. In the high-dimensional online-learning limit, we derive a closed system of ordinary differential equations for a finite set of macroscopic order parameters, yielding exact expressions for the generalization errors throughout both the initial Task 1 learning phase and the subsequent LoRA fine-tuning on Task 2. The theory quantitatively matches finite-dimensional simulations and exposes two characteristic effects of LoRA: low-rank adaptation reduces interference with features learned on the first task, but its initialization slows adaptation to the second task. Building on this mechanistic picture, we analyze a state-dependent masking strategy that freezes hidden units carrying the strongest first-task representations and restricts adaptation to the complementary subspace. This structural partitioning markedly reduces forgetting, while preserving plasticity on the new task. Our framework further clarifies the role of adapter rank: transfer improves only up to the intrinsic dimensionality of the target task and saturates beyond it, while forgetting continues to grow with rank. These results provide a dynamical and geometric account of how low-rank adaptation organizes information across sequential tasks and are qualitatively reproduced on a sequential MNIST benchmark.

</details>

### [4] [On Gauge Gravity's 'Hypermomentum Challenge' to the Geometric Trinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38292)
*Kartik Tiwari*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 该文辨析"几何三位一体"（Geometric Trinity）所主张的广义相对论（general relativity）与其挠率（torsion）、非度规性（non-metricity）重述之间的动力学等价性，指出该等价仅在对物质部分施加实质限制时成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期关于引力可分别由曲率、挠率或非度规性编码的等价性主张受到关注，但其与将引力视为规范理论（gauge theory）、不同几何属性耦合不同物质属性的长期传统存在直接冲突，故需澄清二者分歧。

Method: 本文属简短的理论比较研究：对照两种时空几何化框架，分析其分歧的确切来源、性质与含义，并考察等价性主张成立的先决条件。

Result: 等价性主张仅在物质部分受到实质限制时成立，即要求物质不耦合于独立的仿射联络（independent affine connection）；而该相关耦合目前仍超出经验可达范围。

Conclusion: 由于相关耦合尚无实证检验途径，两种框架之间的取舍取决于本体论、认识论与方法论三种简洁性（parsimony）之间的权衡。

Abstract: Claims concerning a dynamical equivalence between general relativity and two reformulations of it, in which gravity is encoded in torsion and non-metricity respectively, have attracted some attention. If taken to be true, this 'Geometric Trinity' suggests that it is impossible to determine, even in principle, whether one inhabits a spacetime solution possessing curvature, torsion or non-metricity (insofar as the probes of geometry are paths of test particles). This claim is in direct tension with the long tradition of studying gravity as a gauge theory, in which distinct geometric attributes couple to distinct properties of matter (such as energy-momentum to curvature and spin angular momentum to torsion). In this short paper, I compare these two conflicting frameworks for geometrizing spacetime and describe the precise source, nature and implications of their disagreement. The equivalence claim holds only under a substantive restriction on the matter sector, namely that matter does not couple to an independent affine connection. Because the relevant couplings remain beyond current empirical reach, the choice between the frameworks turns on a trade-off among ontological, epistemic, and methodological forms of parsimony.

</details>

### [5] [Fisher and DALI posterior approximations for testing gravity with gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38399)
*Felipe A. da Silva Barbosa,Davi C. Rodrigues,Josiel Mendonça Soares de Souza,Miguel Quartin*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 该文评估了基于 Fisher 矩阵（Fisher Matrix）及其高阶扩展 DALI 的引力波参数估计后验近似方法，在 TIGER 形式化下检验广义相对论时的精度与计算效率。singlet-DALI 与 doublet-DALI 相较精确后验采样可提速 20–1000 倍且系统性优于标准 Fisher 矩阵，triplet-DALI 稳定性不足。文中还发布了符号计算的 SymDALI 代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波参数估计中精确后验采样计算成本高，尤其在检验广义相对论（General Relativity）时需处理波形偏离参数，因此需要评估更高效近似方法的可行性与精度。

Method: 采用 IMRPhenomD 波形，在 TIGER 形式化下比较精确后验采样与复杂度递增的近似：传统 Fisher 矩阵、Fisher 采样（singlet-DALI）、doublet-DALI 与 triplet-DALI，并以 Jensen-Shannon 散度（Jensen-Shannon divergence）衡量精度。

Result: singlet-DALI 与 doublet-DALI 比精确后验快 20 至 1000 倍，增益随 inspiral 信噪比（SNR）增大而提高，在更完整波形如 IMRPhenomHM 上增益更显著；两者均系统性优于标准 Fisher 矩阵，而 triplet-DALI 变异性大，实用优势受限。

Conclusion: singlet-DALI 与 doublet-DALI 是兼顾效率与精度的可行近似方案，triplet-DALI 不宜作为稳健选择；同时提出的 SymDALI 代码可基于 Wolfram Language 符号化计算导数，为新实现提供工具支持。

Abstract: We investigate the accuracy and computational efficiency of posterior approximations for gravitational-wave (GW) parameter estimation based on Fisher Matrix and its higher-order extension, the Derivative Approximation for LIkelihoods (DALI), in the context of tests of General Relativity using parameterized deviations in the inspiral waveforms (the TIGER formalism). We adopt the \texttt{IMRPhenomD} waveform and compare the exact posterior sampling to the following approximations in increasing order of complexity: the traditional Fisher Matrix, the Fisher sampling (singlet-DALI), the doublet-DALI and the triplet-DALI. We find that both singlet and doublet-DALI are significantly faster than the exact posterior by factors between 20 and 1000, depending on the inspiral SNRs -- larger gains correspond to larger SNRs. These gains are considerably larger for more complete waveforms, such as \texttt{IMRPhenomHM}. To evaluate the accuracy of the approximations, we use Jensen-Shannon divergence. We conclude that both singlet and doublet-DALI systematically improves over the standard Fisher Matrix approximation, while triplet-DALI shows large variability, limiting its practical advantage. We also introduce a code with a new implementation of Fisher and DALI analysis (Symbolic DALI, \texttt{SymDALI}) which computes derivatives symbolically via \textit{Wolfram Language}.

</details>

### [6] [Quasi-exponentials and semi-classical limits for the chordal BPZ system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38192)
*Mo Chen,Hao Wu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由 chordal Belavin–Polyakov–Zamolodchikov（BPZ）方程在半经典极限下导出的非线性 Hamilton–Jacobi 系统，其中各方程共享同一特征值参数 λ。核心结论是确定该系统在模去加性常数意义下的全局实值解个数：λ>0 时给出解的计数与存在性，λ<0 时证明不存在此类解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BPZ 方程是共形场论中的核心对象，其半经典极限给出非线性 Hamilton–Jacobi 系统，理解该系统的实值解结构有助于刻画相应半经典动力学。摘要指出 λ=0 情形可由 Eremenko 建立的两类解与有理函数之间的联系得到解数，因此本文聚焦尚未解决的 λ≠0 情形。

Method: 对 λ>0，通过 Schwarzian 对应（Schwarzian correspondence）将梯度的代数方程与具有指定临界点的准指数函数（quasi-exponentials）相联系；借助 Mukhin–Tarasov–Varchenko 的枚举与横截性（transversality）结果获得光滑实分支，并用 Poisson 括号恒等式（Poisson bracket identity）证明其精确性。对 λ<0，则建立能量界（energy bound）以排除全局实值解。

Result: λ>0 情形得到光滑的实解分支，并确定其个数；这些分支经 Poisson 括号恒等式被证明是精确的。λ<0 情形不存在全局实值解。λ=0 情形的解数由既有的与有理函数的联系给出。

Conclusion: 该非线性 Hamilton–Jacobi 系统在模去加性常数意义下的全局实值解个数依赖于特征值参数 λ 的符号：λ>0 时存在有限个由准指数枚举所刻画的光滑实分支，λ<0 时无解；λ=0 情形可由与有理函数的联系处理。

Abstract: We study the nonlinear Hamilton--Jacobi system arising as the formal semi-classical limit of the chordal Belavin--Polyakov--Zamolodchikov equations with a common eigenvalue parameter $λ$. We determine the number of global real-valued solutions modulo additive constants. When $λ=0$, the number of solutions can be derived using the connection between the solutions and rational functions, due to Eremenko. We focus on the case when $λ\neq 0$ in this article. For $λ>0$, a Schwarzian correspondence relates the algebraic equations for the gradients to quasi-exponentials with prescribed critical points. Enumeration and transversality results of Mukhin, Tarasov, and Varchenko yield smooth real branches, and a Poisson bracket identity establishes their exactness. An energy bound rules out global real-valued solutions for $λ<0$.

</details>


### [7] [Para families of orthogonal polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38211)
*Stéphane Z. Beaulac,Nicolas Crampé,Quentin Labriet,Lucia Morey,Rafael I. Nepomechie,Luc Vinet*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文证明 para-Krawtchouk、para-Racah、\(q\)-para-Racah 与 para-Bannai–Ito 等正交多项式所承载的代数表示是可约的，并分解为两个不可约子模，从而在统一框架下解释其双格结构与本征值配对现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: para-Krawtchouk 多项式最初源于寻找具有完美态传输（perfect state transfer）的自旋链（spin chains），其后 para-Racah、\(q\)-para-Racah 与 para-Bannai–Ito 多项式亦相继通过 Askey 概型的奇异截断（singular truncations）得到，并用于设计自旋链。这些多项式均在双格（bi-lattices）上正交，其“para”前缀可追溯至 para-Krawtchouk 情形，其谱对应 parabose 振子的谱。因此有必要澄清它们在相应代数中的表示结构。

Method: 研究表明，这些多项式所给出的相应代数（Hahn、Racah、\(q\)-Racah、Bannai–Ito 及互补 Bannai–Ito）的表示是可约的（reducible）。每个表示分解为同一代数的两个不可约模（irreducible modules）的直和，分别附着于两个交错子格（interlaced sub-lattices），并给出各子模的参数。据此将 para 多项式用子模所承载的经典多项式表示，并解释本征值的配对关系 \(λ_n=λ_{N-n}\)。

Result: 每个 para 族对应的代数表示均分解为两个不可约子模，分别对应两个交错子格；子模参数被明确给出。para 多项式可由子模所承载的经典多项式表达，本征值配对 \(λ_n=λ_{N-n}\) 因此得到说明；同时识别出双格设定中支撑该约化的三个特征。并在此分解视角下统一呈现各 para 族的性质、其构造方式及应用场景。

Conclusion: 该工作以表示的可约分解为核心，统一刻画了 para-Krawtchouk、para-Racah、\(q\)-para-Racah 与 para-Bannai–Ito 族的代数结构、经典多项式表达与谱配对特征，并梳理了这些族的构造途径与实际应用。

Abstract: The para-Krawtchouk polynomials arose in the search for spin chains with perfect state transfer; the para-Racah, $q$-para-Racah and para-Bannai--Ito polynomials followed, obtained through singular truncations of families of the Askey scheme and used in turn to design spin chains. All are orthogonal on bi-lattices; the prefix goes back to the para-Krawtchouk case, whose spectrum is that of the parabose oscillator. The representations that these polynomials provide of the corresponding algebras --- Hahn, Racah, $q$-Racah, Bannai--Ito and complementary Bannai--Ito --- are shown to be reducible. Each decomposes into a direct sum of two irreducible modules of the same algebra, one attached to each of the two interlaced sub-lattices, and the parameters of the submodules are given. The para polynomials are thereby expressed in terms of the classical polynomials that the submodules carry, and the pairing $λ_n=λ_{N-n}$ of their eigenvalues is accounted for; the three features of the bi-lattice setting on which the reduction rests are identified. The properties of the para families are then presented in a unified way in the light of this decomposition, together with the constructions through which these families arise and the settings in which they have been put to use.

</details>


### [8] [Integer Sampling and the Identifiability of Mellin Residues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38234)
*Yifan He*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：对一类由慢变分量与可数多个载体构成的函数，所有 Mellin 残差（Mellin residues）均可由带误差的整数样本精确确定，且误差小于任意代数阶。存在一个例外零测集之外的单一参数 a>1 适用于整个函数类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该结果旨在为受量子引力中 Mellin 平均（Mellin averaging）启发的模型类提供插值不变性（interpolation-invariance）陈述，即样本数据是否足以唯一恢复解析不变量。

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限时间可达性问题——即两个概率向量能否在时间 t 内通过给定集合中的生成元相互转换——是马尔可夫动力学（Markovian dynamics）中的关键问题。该研究旨在借助微分几何工具处理归一化 Kolmogorov 生成元情形下该问题的结构刻画。

Result: 推导出两类可达集的解析边界，并通过其对应体积之比评估所得界；结果表明相对于均匀概率向量（uniform probability vector）的非对称性与 Kullback-Leibler 相对熵（Kullback-Leibler relative entropy）相关。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该讲义为数学家提供了多体局域化研究的入门路径，将物理直觉（稳定性与失稳机制）与严格数学技术（微扰旋转、重整化、概率估计）相结合。

Abstract: These notes are based on a mini-course delivered at the Les Houches Summer School, Quantum Theory on All Scales, in August 2026. The mathematical discussion deals with how perturbative rotations, renormalization, and probabilistic estimates are used in a rigorous approach to many-body localization.

</details>

Conclusion: 经符号修正后的计算表明重整化真空能在大质量下消失，满足物理要求，并揭示能量作为空腔半径函数的多次变号行为，为球形腔中标量场卡西米尔能的研究提供了更正后的结果。

Abstract: We reconsider the vacuum (Casimir) energy of a massive scalar field obeying Dirichlet boundary conditions inside a spherical cavity. Numerical results reveal that the energy changes sign several times as a function of the cavity radius.

</details>


### [13] [Cluster Attention Neural Operators for Solving Parametric Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39914)
*Ming Zhong,Antonio Colanera,Gianluigi Rozza,Zhenya Yan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出簇注意力神经算子（Cluster Attention Neural Operator, CANO），通过新型交叉注意力机制动态聚类查询，同时保留全分辨率键与值，避免切片压缩损失并消除权重共享限制，在多个偏微分方程基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统参数化偏微分方程（partial differential equations, PDEs）仿真需对每个参数重复计算，高保真设计不切实际；现有基于Transformer的神经算子（neural operators）虽能捕捉全局依赖，却面临二次注意力复杂度，而Transolver的切片空间投影虽快却损失精细空间信息并因共享权重限制建模灵活性。

Method: CANO重构注意力机制，采用新颖的交叉注意力（cross-attention）动态聚类查询（queries），同时保留全分辨率的键（keys）和值（values），从而避免切片压缩损失、移除权重共享限制，并在保持全局交互的同时维持计算速度。

Conclusion: CANO在保持计算效率与全局交互能力的同时，克服了切片压缩与权重共享的局限，验证了其在多样PDE场景下的有效性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Traditional simulations of parametric partial differential equations (PDEs) rely on repetitive computations for each parameter, which makes high-fidelity design impractical. Neural operators address this issue by learning solution operators, accelerating parameter-space mapping by orders of magnitude. Recent Transformer-based neural operators attempt to capture global dependencies, but often at the cost of quadratic attention complexity. Transolver resolves this problem by projecting physical states into a reduced slice space for attention computation. Although fast, this projection sacrifices fine spatial information. Moreover, by operating in this reduced space with shared weights across attention heads, it may constrain the model's flexibility, thereby limiting its capacity to capture complex phenomena. To address these issues, we propose the Cluster Attention Neural Operator (CANO), which reformulates attention via a novel cross-attention mechanism that dynamically clusters queries while preserving full-resolution keys and values. This avoids slice compression loss and removes weight-sharing limits. At the same time, the model remains fast without losing global interactions. Empirically, CANO achieves state-of-the-art performance across canonical PDE benchmarks, covering fluid and solid dynamics (e.g., Navier-Stokes, Airfoil, Plasticity), irregular unstructured geometries (e.g., Pipe Turbulence, Composites), and long-term temporal rollouts. Across solid deformation and turbulent flow benchmarks, CANO achieves lower errors than baselines and exhibits strong geometric adaptability and temporal consistency.

</details>

<details>

</details>

### [15] [Accidental accuracy and formal consistency in $GW$+BSE: Exact benchmarks and regime-dependent error cancellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38247)
*Michael O. Atambo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 基于扩展 Hubbard 二聚体（extended Hubbard dimer）的精确对角化（exact diagonalization），本文构建可控基准以量化 $GW$+BSE 中核（kernel）不自洽带来的代价。结果显示实际工作流（practical construction）的光学带隙准确性源自两个独立误差的偶然抵消，而非隐含自洽性，且该偶然准确性仅存在于弱束缚区（weak-binding regime）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体微扰理论（many-body perturbation theory）中，BSE 核在形式上应等于自能（self-energy）的泛函导数 $K=\delta\Sigma/\delta G$；但常规做法使用静态屏蔽的直接相互作用与裸交换相互作用构建核，破坏了这一自洽性，而其后果缺乏可控的量化评估。本文旨在分离并度量这种不自洽的具体代价，并界定标准工作流可被信任的参数范围。

Method: 采用扩展 Hubbard 二聚体的精确对角化，构造可控基准以隔离不一致性的影响；对比内部不自洽的实际 $GW$+BSE 构建与冻结屏蔽（frozen-$W$）导数核两种方案，并扫描包括最近邻相互作用（nearest-neighbor interaction）与激子（exciton）束缚强度在内的参数空间。

Result: 实际构建常通过被低估的 $GW$ 带隙与被过大的裸交换核之间的偶然抵消而给出准确光学带隙，并映射出该偶然准确性出现的参数区域；开启最近邻相互作用并束缚激子后，实际构建在弱束缚区始终准确，抵消最优处精度可达 $10^{3}$ 量级（parts in $10^{3}$），而冻结-$W$ 构建在任何束缚强度下均失效。超过某一束缚阈值后，两种静态构建均退化，表明深束缚激子需要超越静态核的物理。

Conclusion: 实际核的准确性是两个相互独立误差（被低估的准粒子带隙与过大的交换项）的偶然抵消，而非隐藏的自洽性；偶然准确性属于弱束缚区的属性，冻结-$W$ 导数核的失败可追溯至其对交换通道的过度屏蔽（overscreening）。该工作为 $GW$+BSE 的近似失效提供了可控分类体系（taxonomy），并为判定标准工作流何时可信给出了精确诊断。

Abstract: Standard many-body perturbation theory for optical excitations combines the $GW$ approximation for quasiparticle energies with the Bethe-Salpeter equation (BSE) for the electron-hole response. Formally, the BSE kernel must equal the functional derivative of the self-energy, $K=δΣ/δG$. In routine practice, however, the kernel is built from a statically screened direct interaction and a bare exchange interaction, which breaks this consistency. Using exact diagonalization of the extended Hubbard dimer, we construct a controlled benchmark that isolates the price of this inconsistency. We show that the internally inconsistent practical $GW$+BSE construction frequently yields accurate optical gaps through accidental cancellation between an underestimated $GW$ gap and an oversized bare-exchange kernel, and we map the regions of parameter space where this accidental accuracy occurs. Upon switching on the nearest-neighbor interaction and binding the exciton, the practical construction remains the accurate one throughout the weak-binding regime, with cancellation optima at parts in $10^{3}$, while the frozen-$W$ construction fails at every binding strength. Beyond a binding threshold both static constructions deteriorate, showing that deeply bound excitons require physics beyond any static kernel. Accidental accuracy is therefore a property of the weak-binding regime, and the systematic failure of the frozen-$W$ derivative kernel within the explored parameter space traces to its overscreening of the exchange channel. The accuracy of the practical kernel is an accidental cancellation of two independent errors, an underestimated quasiparticle gap and an oversized exchange term, not a hidden consistency. These results provide a controlled taxonomy of approximation failure in $GW$+BSE and a precise diagnostic for when standard workflows can be trusted.

</details>

*Xingyu Zhang,Daniel Vaz,Chi-Yi Kao,Marcus Tamura,Xiong Feng,Carlos A. Rios Ocampo,Bhavin J. Shastri,Nathan Youngblood*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成相变光子学（integrated phase-change photonics）可实现无静态功耗的非易失光开关，但可逆切换所需电能限制其实际缩放；以往提升能效的努力多集中于器件优化（如微加热器或波导重设计），本文转而采用以材料为中心的策略。

### [18] [Stress-based structure optimization for a range-separated hybrid van der Waals density functional](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38478)
*Per Hyldgaard,Yunqi Shao,Raul Quintero-Monsebaiz,Lars Öhrström*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将高精度范围分离杂化（range-separated hybrid, RSH）范德华密度泛函（van der Waals density functional, vdW-DF）AHBR 从 Quantum Espresso 移植至 VASP，以实现稳定的基于应力的胞优化，并验证其在分子相互作用、块体结构与复杂软物质预测中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂物质（complex matter）常具有部分软性与可塑性，需先依赖密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）预测原子结构，再表征其性质。AHBR 虽在结合能与结构预测上精度高，但其在 Quantum Espresso 中的现有实现难以稳健支持 RSH 的基于应力的单胞优化。

Result: 新实现被用于预测与理解扭曲岩盐（distorted-rocksalt）金属一氧化物中的原子与反铁磁（anti-ferromagnetic）结构；并用于预测 C2N 共价有机框架（covalent-organic framework, COF）体系中的层堆叠模式（motifs）。

Abstract: Complex matter, often partly soft and pliable, is the generic form of material systems. We must therefore generally call on density functional theory (DFT) to first predict the atomic structure before we can use it to also characterize (expected) properties. A recent range-separated hybrid (RSH) van Waals density functional (vdW-DF), `vdW-DF2-ahbr' (abbreviated AHBR) [PRX 12, 041003 (2022)], shows promise as a high-accuracy predictor of both binding energies and structure, from molecules to solids. However, the present implementation in Quantum Espresso (QE) does not, in our experience, support robust stress-based unit-cell optimization of RSHs. Here we document and illustrate work enabling practical AHBR-based complex-matter discovery: We port the AHBR XC functional to the Vienna Ab Initio Simulation Package (VASP) where use of stress-based DFT optimization is already stable also for RSHs. We test the implementation by comparing high-accuracy AHBR-QE and AHBR-VASP predictions of non-covalent molecular interactions and for structure and cohesion of simple bulk structures. We also illustrate and test the use of the new AHBR-VASP implementation to predict and understand (atomic and anti-ferromagnetic) structure in distorted-rocksalt metal monoxides. ### [20] [Single-particle detection of the Néel transition in LaCrO$_3$ microcrystals via widefield nitrogen-vacancy center magnetic imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38529)
*Carson O. Patterson,Ethan Q. Williams,Eden Tzanetopoulos,Benjamin C. Li,Rachel T. Smith,Matthew Chang,Hideyuki Watanabe,Daniel R. Gamelin,Kai-Mei C. Fu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用宽场氮-空位（NV）磁强计对熔盐法合成的单个 LaCrO₃ 微晶进行杂散磁场成像，在单颗粒水平解析反铁磁奈尔转变，并揭示被系综测量掩盖的颗粒间磁性异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土正铬酸盐（rare-earth orthochromites）具有可化学调控的磁性，在温度传感与温控开关中具应用潜力，但其实现依赖于理解合成条件与颗粒形貌如何影响 Cr³⁺ 磁有序。传统系综手段（中子衍射、SQUID 磁强计）仅给出平均磁性，无法获取颗粒级信息。

Method: 采用宽场氮-空位（nitrogen-vacancy, NV）磁强计对熔盐合成（molten salt synthesis）制备的单个 LaCrO₃ 微晶的杂散磁场（stray magnetic fields）进行成像，在 278–298 K 区间测量 18 个颗粒；并在奈尔温度以下施加旋转面内磁场，且进行重复热循环。

Result: 18 个颗粒中，5 个在系综奈尔温度附近出现明显磁化起始，4 个无可测磁信号，9 个呈温度无关信号，与系综测量未探测到的局域磁性杂质一致。奈尔温度以下，颗粒磁化在旋转面内磁场下保持不变，符合倾斜反铁磁（canted antiferromagnetic）有序而非顺磁性；重复热循环显示磁场图样在每次降温间变化，但部分特征在同一位置重现，符合微米尺度结构钉扎的反铁磁畴（antiferromagnetic domains），其弱铁磁矩符号在每次穿越奈尔转变时独立设定。

Conclusion: 单颗粒 NV 磁强计被确立为探测单个微晶磁有序与畴结构的灵敏手段，是将颗粒形貌与缺陷结构同正铬酸盐磁有序相关联的关键一步。

Abstract: Rare-earth orthochromites exhibit rich, chemically tunable magnetic behavior, making them attractive for temperature sensing and temperature-dependent switching applications. Realizing this potential requires understanding how synthesis conditions and particle morphology influence the underlying Cr$^{3+}$ magnetic ordering. Orthochromite magnetism has traditionally been characterized by ensemble techniques such as neutron diffraction and SQUID magnetometry, which report only averaged magnetic properties and cannot access particle-level information. Here we use widefield nitrogen-vacancy (NV) magnetometry to image the stray magnetic fields of individual LaCrO$_3$ microcrystals produced via molten salt synthesis, resolving the antiferromagnetic Néel transition in single particles. Of the 18 particles measured between 278 and 298 K, five show a clear onset of magnetization near the ensemble Néel temperature, four show no measurable magnetic signal, and nine display temperature-independent signatures consistent with localized magnetic impurities undetected by ensemble measurements. Below the Néel temperature, particle magnetization remains fixed under a rotating in-plane field, consistent with canted antiferromagnetic ordering rather than paramagnetism, while repeated thermal cycling shows that the magnetic field pattern varies between cooldowns with certain features recurring at the same locations, consistent with micron-scale structurally pinned antiferromagnetic domains in which the sign of the weak ferromagnetic moment is set independently each time they pass through the Néel transition. These results establish single-particle NV magnetometry as a sensitive probe of magnetic ordering and domain structure in individual microcrystals, a key step toward correlating individual particle morphology and defect structure with magnetic ordering in orthochromites.

</details>


### [21] [Enabling Domain-Specific Atomistic Models: A Machine Learning Potential for the Solid Acid Family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38531)
*Jonas Hänseroth,Rose Asuka Baroness von Stackelberg,Christian Dreßler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出面向“无水利固态氢键网络介导质子导体（water-free solid-state hydrogen-bond network mediated proton conductors）”这一特定化合物类别的专用机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potential），训练集包含跨 55 种材料的 440 万个第一性原理构型。该势在同域内超越主流通用势，能重现实测活化能（activation energy）与阴离子转动动力学（anion rotational dynamics）排序，并实现更高层级电子结构理论精度与超大体系模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用跨周期表势（universal potential）使原子尺度模拟普及，但普遍预期将势专门化到单一化合物类别可提升精度，然而此类成功案例稀少，且少有模型在速度上超越通用模型或达到更高层级电子结构理论（electronic-structure theory）精度。因此需要验证“类别内通用”路线的可行性与收益。

Method: 构建覆盖 55 种材料的 440 万个第一性原理构型数据库，训练一个在无水利固态氢键网络介导质子导体类别内通用（而非跨化学空间通用）的势；并提出紧凑变体（compact variant），参数量为原模型的五分之一。针对远离训练集的组分与结构近亲分别评估精度，并通过每种材料数百个额外构型提升电子结构理论层级。

Result: 该势在该域内超越领先的通用势，可重现实测活化能及通用模型缺失的阴离子转动动力学排序，表明静态结构（static structure）一致并不蕴含输运（transport）行为一致。远离训练集的组分精度下降，但结构近亲仍具竞争力；每材料增加数百构型即可达到更高层级电子结构理论精度。紧凑变体参数减至五分之一，速度更快，且精度优于所有测试的通用模型。

Conclusion: 紧凑模型可在单块图形处理器（GPU）上承载超过 25 万个原子，使晶界（grain boundary）与向高导相转变的模拟成为可能，并使质子的量子处理（quantum treatment）变得可负担。参考精度与可及体系尺寸由此基本解耦，为其他化合物类别提供了可复制的模板。

Abstract: Machine-learned interatomic potentials trained across the periodic table have made atomistic simulation broadly accessible, and specializing them to a single compound class is widely expected to improve accuracy. Yet examples remain scarce, and fewer still surpass universal models in speed or reach a higher level of electronic-structure theory. Here we present a potential that is universal within the class of water-free solid-state hydrogen-bond network mediated proton conductors rather than across chemistry and a database of 4.4 million first-principles configurations spanning 55 materials. It surpasses leading general-purpose potentials across this domain, recovering measured activation energies and the ordering of anion rotational dynamics that those models miss; agreement on static structure does not imply agreement on transport. Accuracy falls for compositions far from the training set but stays competitive for close structural relatives, and a higher level of electronic-structure theory is reached with a few hundred additional configurations per material. We further introduce a compact variant carrying a fifth of the parameters, faster still and yet more accurate than every general-purpose model tested. It sustains more than a quarter of a million atoms on a single graphics processor, placing grain boundaries and the transition into the highly conducting phase within reach, and making a quantum treatment of the protons affordable. Reference accuracy and accessible system size thus become largely independent, offering a template for other compound classes.

</details>


### [22] [Dense and porous phase transition study of SEI formation using phase-field method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38679)
*Joonyeob Jeon,Lukas Kobbing,Tejs Vegge,Jin Hyun Chang,Birger Horstmann,Ivano Eligio Castelli*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究采用相场框架（phase-field framework）在开路条件下考察固体电解质界面膜（solid electrolyte interphase, SEI）随荷电状态（state of charge, SoC）的演化，通过引入空间关联噪声（spatially correlated noise）描述随机输运扰动，揭示了SEI由致密相向多孔相转变的三种粗糙度演化机制，并量化了转变时间对SoC的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SEI对电池长期稳定性至关重要，因其影响电极与电解液之间的反应；然而，SEI从致密相向多孔相的演化过程此前仍未被充分理解，尤其是其与荷电状态的关联尚不清晰。

Method: 在开路条件下构建相场框架研究SoC依赖的SEI演化，引入空间关联噪声表征随机输运扰动，并由噪声诱导输运与表面弛豫（surface relaxation）竞争所产生的界面粗糙度（interface roughness）演化来评估致密-多孔转变时间。

Result: 模拟得到三种不同的粗糙度演化机制；预测转变时间总体从20% SoC时的约一个月增至80% SoC时的七个多月，并在55%至60%之间出现显著变化，该变化与石墨SoC-OCV关系相对应，后者通过能斯特条件（Nernst condition）控制界面Li0自由基浓度。在不可逆容量损失引起的动态SoC条件下，转变行为随存储过程中SoC的演化而改变。

Conclusion: SoC-OCV关系是决定开路存储条件下SEI相稳定性及致密-多孔转变时间的核心因素。

Abstract: The solid electrolyte interphase (SEI) is essential for the long-term stability of batteries because it influences the reactions between the electrode and electrolyte. Despite previous studies, the SEI evolution from dense to porous phases remains incompletely understood. Here, we investigate the SoC-dependent evolution of the SEI using a phase-field framework under open-circuit conditions. Spatially correlated noise is introduced to describe stochastic transport perturbations, and the dense-to-porous transition time is evaluated from the evolution of interface roughness arising from the competition between noise-induced transport and surface relaxation. The simulations reveal three distinct roughness evolution regimes. The predicted transition time generally increases from approximately one month at 20% SoC to more than seven months at 80% SoC, with a pronounced change between 55% and 60%. This change corresponds to the graphite SoC-OCV relation, which controls the interfacial Li0 radical concentration through the Nernst condition. Under dynamic SoC conditions caused by irreversible capacity loss, the transition behavior changes as the SoC evolves during storage. These results show that the SoC-OCV relation is central to determining SEI phase stability and the dense-to-porous transition time under open-circuit storage.

</details>


### [23] [Polymorph-Tunable Spin Texture and Excitonic Structures in Monolayer WSi2P4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38756)
*Xianbo Chenwei,Yu Zhou,Ke Wu,Gaofeng Xu,Ruixue Li,Yuan Li,Yabei Wu,Shaowen Xu,Fanhao Jia*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 单层 WSi2P4（MSi2X4 家族成员）经第一性原理 GW-BSE 计算被证明兼具直接 K 谷带隙与强自旋轨道耦合，其三种多型（alpha、beta、gamma）均为直接带隙并受约 1.3 eV 迁移势垒动力学锁定；自旋织构（spin texture）与激子亮度可由相结构调控，构成协同工程平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 兼具直接带隙与强自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）的二维半导体对量子光电子学（quantum optoelectronics）具有高度吸引力，而此类材料稀缺，故需寻找并表征可实验获取的新候选体系。

Method: 采用第一性原理 GW 加贝特-萨耳皮特方程（GW plus Bethe-Salpeter equation, GW-BSE）计算，对单层 WSi2P4 及其 alpha、beta、gamma 三种多型（polymorph）的能带、自旋织构与激子光学响应进行分析。

Result: 三种多型均为直接带隙半导体，被约 1.3 eV 迁移势垒（migration barrier）动力学锁定；alpha 与 gamma 相（D3h 点群）因 sigma_h 镜面而具有贯穿布里渊区（Brillouin zone）的持续自旋织构，gamma 相近区受带间 SOC 混合弱调制；极性 beta 相（C3v）呈现 Rashba 自旋劈裂。SOC 将最低亮激子的二重简并劈裂为暗态，并使原吸收峰分裂为 A、B 两峰，其相对亮度由 K 谷导带劈裂决定；alpha-WSi2P4 因异自旋支的微小能带交叉打开自旋允许辐射通道而具有最亮的 Peak A。

Conclusion: MSi2P4 家族可作为相可调平台，在单一材料体系内实现自旋织构、能带劈裂与激子亮度的协同工程（cooperative engineering）。

Abstract: Two-dimensional semiconductors that simultaneously possess a direct band gap and strong spin-orbit coupling (SOC) are highly attractive for quantum optoelectronics. Using first-principles GW plus Bethe-Salpeter equation (GW-BSE) calculations, we show that monolayer WSi2P4-an experimentally accessible member of the MSi2X4 family-hosts a direct K-valley gap together with strong SOC. Its three competing polymorphs (alpha, beta and gamma) are all direct-gap semiconductors and are kinetically locked behind 1.3 eV migration barriers. The sigma_h mirror plane of the D3h of alpha and gamma phases enforces a persistent spin texture across the Brillouin zone, weakly modulated near gamma by interband SOC mixing, whereas the polar beta phase (C3v) exhibits Rashba spin splitting. SOC splits the doubly-degenerate lowest bright exciton into dark states and splits the original absorption peak into two peaks (A and B), whose relative brightness is governed by the K-valley conduction-band splitting. alpha-WSi2P4 displays the brightest Peak A because a tiny band crossing of opposite-spin branches can open a spin-allowed radiative channel. These results establish the MSi2P4 family as a phase-tunable platform for the cooperative engineering of spin texture, band splitting, and excitonic brightness within a single material system.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卤化物钙钛矿中载流子寿命随注入水平变化可达数个数量级，使传统时间分辨光致发光分析及不同文献报道寿命之间的比较变得困难。因此需要一种能在明确注入条件下测得寿命的替代方法。

Method: 采用强度调制光致发光谱（intensity-modulated photoluminescence spectroscopy, IMPLS）作为频域替代方案，在工况条件下于特定注入水平（如等效一个太阳光照）测量寿命。针对钙钛矿/传输层堆叠结构，引入专用传递函数（transfer function）以解耦电荷转移与界面复合；在器件层面与强度调制光电压谱（intensity-modulated photovoltage spectroscopy, IMVS）进行对比。

Result: 在薄膜样品上，IMPLS与稳态光致发光（steady-state photoluminescence）给出可比的寿命；在钙钛矿/传输层堆叠中，IMPLS响应更复杂，传递函数可预测太阳能电池中载流子寿命的上限。在器件中，IMVS常因电容效应（capacitive effects）而无法测得寿命；IMPLS不仅呈现与IMVS相同的两个特征频率，还出现第三个特征频率，从而解析出寿命本身。

Conclusion: IMPLS可在工作条件下按注入水平定量给出载流子寿命，并通过传递函数与额外特征频率克服界面复合及电容效应对寿命测量的干扰，为钙钛矿材料与器件的寿命评估提供更可靠的分析途径。

Abstract: The charge-carrier lifetime in halide perovskites often varies by orders of magnitude with the injection level, complicating the analysis of conventional time-resolved photoluminescence and comparisons of reported lifetimes. We use intensity-modulated photoluminescence spectroscopy (IMPLS) as a frequency-domain alternative. Carried out under operating conditions, it yields the lifetime at a specific injection level, e.g., one-sun-equivalent illumination. On thin films, IMPLS and steady-state photoluminescence yield comparable lifetimes. For perovskite/transport-layer stacks, the IMPLS response becomes more complex. A dedicated transfer function disentangles charge transfer from interface recombination and predicts an upper limit on the charge-carrier lifetime in the solar cell. In devices, electrical methods such as intensity-modulated photovoltage spectroscopy (IMVS) often fail to measure the lifetime due to capacitive effects. IMPLS shows not only the same two characteristic frequencies as IMVS, but also a third one, thereby resolving the lifetime itself.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规莫尔体系（moiré systems）的局域堆叠描述无法适用于大角度扭转石墨烯：不等价旋转中心定义了不同的公度界面（commensurate interfaces），其低能层间杂化由间隙谷Umklapp隧穿（intervalley Umklapp tunneling）主导。因此需要建立超越局域堆叠框架的理论，以理解由不同晶体对称性界面组装成的三维扭转石墨中的拓扑行为。

Method: 构建受对称性约束的有效模型（symmetry-constrained effective model），分析非手性与手性隧穿（nonchiral and chiral tunneling）之间的竞争；进一步考察子晶格对称性破缺（sublattice-symmetry breaking）对相图的影响；并结合密度泛函理论（DFT）与原子尺度计算，对21.8°扭转石墨的平衡结构与压缩响应进行验证。

Result: 非手性与手性隧穿的竞争产生单环与双环节线相（one-ring and two-ring nodal-line phases），以及在子晶格（手性）对称性存在下的C₃保护高阶拓扑绝缘体（higher-order topological insulator）。节线环携带整数绕数（integer winding numbers），反向缠绕的环可湮灭进入有能隙相。DFT与原子尺度计算表明，21.8°扭转石墨的平衡结构为高阶拓扑绝缘体，压缩则驱动其进入拓扑Weyl半金属相（topological Weyl semimetal phase）。

Conclusion: 对称性不等价界面的堆叠顺序可作为三维扭转结构中能带拓扑（band topology）的工程化调控途径，拓展了扭转电子学中拓扑相设计的研究框架。

Abstract: Large-angle twisted graphene lies beyond the local-stacking description of conventional moiré systems: inequivalent rotation centers define distinct commensurate interfaces whose low-energy interlayer hybridization is governed by intervalley Umklapp tunneling. Density functional theory (DFT) and atomistic calculations for $21.8^\circ$ twisted graphite identify the equilibrium structure as a higher-order topological insulator, while compression drives it into a topological Weyl semimetal phase. These findings establish the stacking sequence of symmetry-inequivalent interfaces as a means of engineering band topology in three-dimensional twisted structures.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-MnTe 中 175 cm⁻¹ 拉曼模式长期被指认为唯一对称允许的声子（E₂g），但第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）将其置于 100 cm⁻¹ 以下，该矛盾引发三种竞争性解释：对称性降低（D₆h→D₃h）声子、本征空穴自掺杂导致的电子等离激元（plasmon）、或外在 MnTe₂ 杂质信号。论文旨在解决这一争议。

This direct, growth-controlled correlation identifies the mode as an extrinsic MnTe$_2$ impurity signature or as a distorted MnTe feature, rather than an intrinsic phonon or plasmon feature, providing a practical diagnostic for phase purity in MBE-grown $α$-MnTe and clarifying which Raman features can be reliably attributed to the intrinsic altermagnetic phase.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We use temperature-dependent Raman spectroscopy to investigate five configurations of atomically precise 9-armchair graphene nanoribbons (9-AGNRs) differing in substrate, alignment, and coverage. The $D$ and $G$ modes soften on heating at configuration-dependent rates. For the same unaligned high-coverage film before and after polymer-free transfer, measured $D$- and $G$-mode redshift rates are smaller on the Raman-optimised substrate than on Au by factors of 4.7 and 5.6, respectively. Between 80 and 290 K, the model gives $D$- and $G$-mode redshifts from $0.305$ to $6.322~\mathrm{cm}^{-1}$, whereas the zero-K-referenced Klemens-type contribution remains below $0.050~\mathrm{cm}^{-1}$. Under the adopted assumptions, thermal-expansion mismatch therefore dominates these shifts. The modelled RBLM change remains below $1~\mathrm{cm}^{-1}$ and cannot be robustly separated into its two contributions. *Christine Falter,Yurii Kutovyi,Nils von den Driesch,Denny Dütz,Nataliya Demarina,Prateek Kaul,Anjana Rajan,Jiayuan Zhang,Thomas J. Smart,Leqi Zhou,Lidia Kibkalo,András Kovács,Qing-Tai Zhao,Lars R. Schreiber,Alexander Pawlis*

Motivation: 宽禁带化合物半导体 ZnSe 因非简并导带（non-degenerate conduction band）与同位素核自旋纯化（isotopic nuclear spin purification）潜力，有望实现长自旋相干时间，是电子自旋量子比特的候选基质材料。此类器件需具备对 ZnSe 中电子的可靠静电控制和在低温下仍可工作的低阻欧姆接触，而这两点此前尚待实现。

Abstract: The wide-bandgap compound semiconductor ZnSe is a promising host material for the realization of electron spin-qubits. Its non-degenerate conduction band and the potential for isotopic nuclear spin purification promise long spin coherence times. Key requirements for such devices include reliable electrostatic control of electrons in ZnSe and low-resistance ohmic contacts that remain functional at cryogenic temperatures. In this work, we utilize a novel Shadow Wall technique for molecular-beam epitaxy combined with in-situ deposition of Al ohmic contacts to realize normally-off ZnSe-based field-effect transistors. The devices exhibit linear output characteristics and effective gate control of the channel from room temperature down to 5 K, confirming low-resistance ohmic contacts to the undoped ZnSe channel. The drain current can be modulated by several orders of magnitude through electrostatic gating, with threshold voltages of approximately 3 V and field-effect mobilities exceeding 100 cm2/Vs over the investigated temperature range. Self-consistent Schrödinger-Poisson and drift-diffusion simulations reproduce the measured transfer characteristics and provide insight into the role of interface electrostatics in determining the channel formation and threshold voltage. These results demonstrate the potential of gated ZnSe heterostructures for future spin-qubit applications.

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究提出以过渡金属（transition metal, TM）修饰的硼掺杂石墨烯（boron-doped graphene, BDG）与二维 MoTe2 作为基底，用于高效吸附捕获挥发性碘物种（I2、CH3I），并通过第一性原理计算阐明其吸附增强的电子结构起源与反应动力学可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射性碘物种（尤其 I2 与 CH3I）因高挥发性、化学稳定性及与传统基底和吸附材料相互作用较弱，构成显著的环境与技术危害，亟需高效的吸附捕获与缓解方案。

Abstract: Radioactive iodine species, particularly I$_2$ and CH$_3$I, pose significant environmental and technological hazards owing to their high volatility, chemical stability, and relatively weak interaction with traditional substrate and sorption materials. In this work, we proposed a series of transition-metal (TM) (Fe, Ni, Cu, Zn) decorated boron-doped graphene (BDG) and 2D-MoTe2 substrates for efficient adsorptive capture and mitigation of such volatile Iodine species. Using systematic first-principles density functional theory (DFT) calculations, we elucidate the microscopic origin of the enhanced adsorption by analyzing the changes in the electronic structure upon adsorption. More importantly, we analyzed the thermodynamic and kinetic feasibility of adsorption on these newly designed substrates using Climbing-Image Nudged Elastic band (CI-NEB) calculations and found that TM decoration serves as an effective catalytic center, thereby making the reaction thermodynamically and kinetically feasible. Conversely, the reaction becomes thermodynamically and kinetically unfavorable on pristine substrates in the absence of a TM atom as a catalytic center. This work deepens our understanding of the electronic origin of the enhanced adsorption and reaction kinetics, and the predictions made will be useful for experimental realization.

### [32] [An electromechanically coupled multiphase-field model with generalized kinetic relations for ferroelectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39671)
*Hsu-Cheng Cheng,Dennis M. Kochmann*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种机电耦合（electromechanically coupled）多相场（multiphase-field）框架，可将任意给定的动力学关系（kinetic relation）直接嵌入演化方程，从而突破传统 Allen–Cahn 定律隐含的线性速度–驱动力关系，实现对铁电体中非线性畴壁（domain wall）运动的模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典相场模型多采用 Allen–Cahn 演化律（Allen–Cahn evolution law），其本质为能量梯度下降，导致界面速度与驱动力之间呈线性动力学关系。然而铁电陶瓷（ferroelectric ceramics）等材料普遍表现出非线性动力学行为，需要替代性的演化律与模型框架。

Method: 构建应力驱动的机电耦合多相场框架，采用广义动力学表述（general kinetic formulation），将预设的动力学关系直接整合进演化律；多相形式允许对不同序参量对（order-parameter pairs）分别指定不同的动力学关系与界面能（interfacial energy）。

Result: 该模型能够按指定非线性动力学（如遵循 Merz–Stadler 定律的指数–幂律混合型）正确演化畴壁，并可区分不同类型铁电畴壁的差异化性质。

Conclusion: 所提框架适用于模拟材料中的动力学关系，应用范围不限于铁电体，具有较广的推广潜力。

Abstract: Classical phase-field models typically use the Allen--Cahn evolution law to model interface motion as a consequence of energy-gradient descent. This implies a linear kinetic relation between the velocity of an interface (e.g., a domain wall in a ferroelectric) and its driving force. When nonlinear kinetic relations are to be modeled, as commonly found in ferroelectric ceramics, an alternative evolution law and hence an alternative model is needed. Here, we propose an electromechanically (stress-driven) coupled multiphase-field framework with a general kinetic formulation, which integrates a prescribed kinetic relation directly into the evolution law. We show that this model correctly evolves domain walls with the assigned nonlinear kinetics, e.g., of mixed exponential-power law type, following the so-called Merz--Stadler law. The multiphase formulation further enables distinct kinetic relations and interfacial energies to be assigned to different order-parameter pairs, which reflects the distinguishable properties of the different types of ferroelectric domain walls. The proposed framework is broadly applicable for simulating kinetic relations in materials with applications beyond ferroelectrics.

</details>


### [33] [Second-order Real Nodal Lines in Nodal Surface Semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39744)
*Yunye Wang,Ruopu Zhao,Tianqi Zhang,Weikang Wu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有实节线（real nodal lines, RNLs）实现中，节线均由另一条节线连接，节线能否被其他类型能带简并（band degeneracies）连接尚属开放问题。

Result: 每条节线携带非平庸实陈数（real Chern number）$ν_R=1$，产生位于一对 $PT$ 相关铰链（hinges）上的拓扑铰链费米弧（hinge Fermi arcs）；IGN 中一对贯穿布里渊区（Brillouin zone）的节线由节面连接，各携带双重非平庸 $Z_2$ 荷，体-边对应表现为铰链费米弧与鼓面表面态（drumhead surface states）。

Conclusion: 该研究确立节面作为实节线的新型连接伙伴，并为在碳基及其他轻元素系统中探索高阶实拓扑（higher-order real topology）提供路线图。

Abstract: Real nodal lines (RNLs) featuring real Chern numbers and second-order boundary modes have attracted widespread attention. In all previously reported realizations, an RNL is linked by another nodal line, and whether an RNL can be linked by other types of band degeneracies has remained open. Here, we propose a second-order real nodal-line semimetal in which a pair of RNLs is linked by a nodal surface. We show that this state can be realized in spinless systems with both $PT$ and nonsymmorphic $S_{2z}T$ symmetries, where the $S_{2z}T$-enforced nodal surface prevents the pair of RNLs from annihilation. Each nodal line carries a nontrivial real Chern number $ν_R=1$, giving rise to topological hinge Fermi arcs located at a pair of $PT$-related hinges. Guided by this construction, we identify the interpenetrated graphene network (IGN) as a promising material realization. First-principles calculations confirm that a pair of nodal lines traversing the Brillouin zone are linked by a nodal surface and each nodal line carries double nontrivial $Z_2$ charges. The bulk-boundary correspondence of IGN manifests as a pair of hinge Fermi arcs together with drumhead surface states. Our work establishes nodal surfaces as a new linking partner for real nodal lines and provides a roadmap for exploring higher-order real topology in carbon-based and other light-element systems.

### [34] [A transferable full-band Monte Carlo framework for complex alloy avalanche photodiodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39795)
*Shafat Shahnewaz,Hannaneh Karimi,Joe C. Campbell,Avik W. Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一套基于物理的多尺度全带蒙特卡洛（full-band Monte Carlo）框架，用于模拟复杂合金雪崩光电二极管（avalanche photodiodes, APDs）中的雪崩倍增与过剩噪声，并在 Al$_{0.7}$InAsSb 四元体系上完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂合金 APD 的增益与电离统计同时受能带结构、合金无序与散射物理影响，需要将原子尺度材料结构与其器件级雪崩行为相联系的建模方法。现有方法难以在无结构改动的前提下迁移至任意合金组分体系。

Result: 在 \SI{1}{\micro\meter} Al$_{0.7}$InAsSb p-i-n APD 上，仅标定碰撞电离软度参数（impact-ionization softness parameters）即可复现实测增益与过剩噪声特性。框架可无需结构调整迁移至任意闪锌矿（zinc-blende）三元与四元合金。

Conclusion: 该框架为分析与设计复杂合金 APD 提供了可记录、可复现的途径，其中能带结构、无序与散射物理共同决定增益与电离统计。

Abstract: We present a physics-based multiscale full-band Monte Carlo framework for modeling avalanche multiplication and excess noise in complex alloy avalanche photodiodes (APDs), demonstrated on Al$_{0.7}$InAsSb as a representative quaternary system. The framework links atomistic material structure to device-level avalanche statistics: an environment-dependent $sp^3d^5s^\ast$ tight-binding calculation resolves the full conduction- and valence-band structure of the random- or digital-alloy configuration -- including $Γ$, X, and L valley ordering, non-parabolicity, anisotropy, and spin-orbit-induced valence-band splitting -- and supplies the band-structure inputs for stochastic high-field transport. A central element of the framework is a physics-derived treatment of alloy-disorder scattering, in which the quaternary disorder potential is constructed from atomic valence differences, covalent radii, and Thomas--Fermi screening through a composition-weighted decomposition into binary contributions, complemented by composition-interpolated polar-optical, acoustic, intervalley-phonon, impurity, and impact-ionization models. Because every material-dependent input is generated from the atomic composition and configuration by the same well-defined procedure, the framework transfers without structural modification to arbitrary zinc-blende ternary and quaternary alloys. Applied to a \SI{1}{\micro\meter} Al$_{0.7}$InAsSb p-i-n APD, the framework reproduces the measured gain and excess-noise characteristics with only the impact-ionization softness parameters calibrated. The approach provides a documented, reproducible route for analyzing and designing complex alloy APDs in which band structure, disorder, and scattering physics jointly determine gain and ionization statistics.

</details>

*Royi Ledermann,Rave Hanoch Saadon,Adam Herling,Ofer Neufeld*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该理论研究探讨了石墨烯（graphene）在圆偏振激光照射下、包含非唯象电子-声子（electron-phonon, e-ph）耦合时的 Floquet 能带（Floquet band）行为，指出声子效应会显著降低能隙可见度，并为缺失的 Floquet 拓扑能隙提供解释及缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Floquet 能带工程因可借激光调控材料性质而受关注，Floquet 拓扑态已在 Bi$_2$Se$_3$ 表面测得；但石墨烯中 Floquet 拓扑能带的原始预言始终未被观测到，已有工作仅测得非拓扑的 Floquet-Bloch 态或间接特征，因此需要解释这一缺失。

Method: 采用理论方法研究石墨烯在圆偏振激光照射下的行为，并引入非唯象的电子-声子（e-ph）耦合；分析 $Gamma$ 光学声子与非-$Gamma$ 声子对 Floquet 能隙及能带特征的影响，并进一步考虑仪器展宽（instrumentation broadening）的叠加效应。

Result: 由于石墨烯较大的零点运动（zero-point motion），e-ph 相互作用会显著展宽 Floquet 能带；较大的位移 $Gamma$ 光学声子即使在无仪器展宽时也会将 Floquet 能隙减小约一半，并诱发平带（flat bands）等额外特征，这些声子“进入能隙”模糊了光电子发射（photoemission）信号；非-$Gamma$ 声子则闭合能隙并大幅降低可见度，与合理预期的仪器展宽耦合后效应显著加剧。

Conclusion: 研究结果为缺失的 Floquet 拓扑能隙提出一种解释，并给出明确的缓解策略：（i）预抽运相干声子（coherent phonons）以抵消模糊效应，或（ii）转向声子统计更有利的狄拉克（Dirac）体系；同时提出 Floquet 拓扑物理在局域区域内依然存在，诸如输运（transport）等性质应可被探测，期望推动实验分析并促成石墨烯 Floquet 拓扑的直接观测。

Abstract: Floquet band engineering has been intensively studied for its potential to control material properties via laser driving. In particular, Floquet topological states have been measured on the surface of Bi$_2$Se$_3$. Nonetheless, the original prediction of Floquet topological bands in graphene remains unobserved, with works only measuring non-topological Floquet-Bloch states or indirect features. Here, we theoretically explore graphene irradiated by circular lasers with non-phenomenological electron-phonon (e-ph) coupling. We show that e-ph interactions substantially broaden Floquet bands due to graphene's large zero-point motion, as expected from phenomenological treatments. However, we further find that larger displacement $Γ$ optical phonons reduce Floquet gaps by about half, even in absence of instrumentation broadening, and can also induce additional features like flat bands. These phonons "enter the gap" and blur photoemission signatures. Including non-$Γ$ phonons closes the gap and greatly reduces visibility. When these modes couple to reasonably expected instrumentation broadening, the effect is significantly exacerbated. Our results propose an answer to the missing Floquet topological gaps and also lead to clear mitigation strategies: (i) Pre-pumping coherent phonons to counteract blurring, or (ii), transition to a Dirac system with more favorable phonon statistics. Moreover, our analysis proposes that Floquet topological physics is alive in localized regions, such that properties of interest like transport should be accessible. We expect this work to impact experimental analysis and lead to set-ups where graphene Floquet topology might finally be directly observed.

</details>


### [36] [Engineering Grain Boundary Commensurability for Ferroelectric Stabilization in Hafnia-Based Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39931)
*Chuqiao Shi,Xinyan Li,Xing He,Kaiji Zhao,Jesse Schimpf,Akash Surampalli,Adan Mireles,Sergio Puebla,Yi Jiang,Ramamoorthy Ramesh,Xiaofeng Qian,Lane W. Martin,Andrew M. Rappe,Yimo Han*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用深度分辨多切片电子叠层成像（depth-resolved multislice electron ptychography）以原子分辨率绘制不同晶界处的三维（3D）相分布；并结合蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations）揭示极性相稳定的原子与能量起源；据此以外延方式工程化 HZO 薄膜以促进共格晶界优先形成。

Result: 高共格晶界通过补偿晶格失配并缓解几何阻挫（geometric frustration），有效抑制非极性四方相（nonpolar-tetragonal phase）；模拟显示共格晶界比其他晶界更有利于极性正交相。外延工程化薄膜使铁电器件剩余极化（remanent polarization）提升约 60%。

Abstract: Grain boundaries fundamentally dictate the macroscopic properties of polycrystalline materials by breaking long-range symmetry. In ferroelectrics, these structural discontinuities are conventionally considered as detrimental features that induce depolarization fields and accumulate defects, thereby suppressing or pinning local polarization. Here, we demonstrate that the commensurability of grain boundaries inherently governs the polar-orthorhombic phase stability in polycrystalline Hf0.5Zr0.5O2 (HZO) thin films. Using depth-resolved multislice electron ptychography, we map the three-dimensional (3D) phase distribution across diverse boundaries with atomic resolution, revealing that highly commensurate grain boundaries effectively suppress the nonpolar-tetragonal phase by compensating for lattice mismatch while mitigating geometric frustration. Guided by this principle, we epitaxially engineer HZO thin films to promote the preferential formation of commensurate grain boundaries, resulting in an approximately 60% enhancement in the remanent polarization of ferroelectric devices. ### [37] [Cluster-based Structural Similarity for Dataset Visualization and Data Selection for Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39984)
*Yuto Iwasaki,Miguel A. Caro*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据高效的机器学习原子间势（machine learning interatomic potentials, MLIPs）训练依赖数据选择策略，而结构相似性评估面临保留单个原子环境信息与降低计算成本之间的权衡。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) are essential components for accelerating simulation-driven materials design. Data-efficient MLIP training relies on data-selection strategies that maximize structural diversity while limiting computationally expensive first-principles calculations. A key challenge in such strategies is evaluating structural similarity, which involves a trade-off between retaining information on individual atomic environments and reducing computational cost. Here, we propose similarity evaluation methods that achieve both representational fidelity and computational efficiency. Our method represents each structure using a small set of characteristic atomic environments identified by k-medoids clustering and computes pairwise similarity through optimal matching between these representatives or their distributions. Molecular benchmarks demonstrate that our method is approximately 50 times faster than the baseline method while also more clearly distinguishing structures with different chemical compositions. Similarity-based data-selection benchmarks demonstrate that our methods improve the data efficiency and stability of force prediction in MLIPs.

*Chloe W. Lindeman,Joshua D. Clugston,Justin C. Goodrich,Mark Sutton,James L. Harden,Robert L. Leheny*

TL;DR: 该研究利用X射线光子相关光谱（X-ray photon correlation spectroscopy）研究分形纳米颗粒凝胶（fractal nanoparticle gel）在确定性循环剪切与随机剪切训练下的记忆效应，发现两者均可诱导微观结构可逆性，但随机协议训练更慢且更不规则。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序材料（disordered materials）的记忆训练此前多集中于循环应变振幅，随机驱动（random driving）能否赋予类似训练尚存疑问，本文旨在探究该问题。

Method: 采用X射线光子相关光谱，对经确定性循环剪切或 strain 振幅介于 −γ_t 与 +γ_t 之间的随机剪切训练的分形纳米颗粒凝胶进行观测。

Conclusion: 随机驱动可像确定性循环剪切一样训练无序凝胶产生记忆，且该记忆可通过标准协议读出与量化，拓展了无序材料记忆训练协议的适用范围。

Abstract: A variety of disordered materials, including jammed particulate systems and crumpled paper, can be trained to exhibit memory of a cyclic strain amplitude. Only recently, however, have questions emerged around the whether random driving can impart similar training. Here, we employ x-ray photon correlation spectroscopy to study a fractal nanoparticle gel trained via either deterministic cyclic shear or random shear bounded by strain amplitudes $-γ_t$ and $+γ_t$. Both types of training lead to microstructural reversibility, with a slower and more irregular training for the random protocol. In both cases, the shear induces redistribution of internal stress in the gel with corresponding irreversible strain displacements whose magnitudes decrease during training. Finally, we show that memory of the random driving can be read out and quantified using a standard protocol.

</details>

TL;DR: 该文提出校准马氏外推等级（calibrated Mahalanobis extrapolation grade）γ，将其集成到 GRACE 基础模型（foundation models）中，与能量和力在同一次前向传播中评估，用于量化原子环境的外推可靠性。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: γ 由潜在特征空间（latent feature space）中最近簇的马氏距离（Mahalanobis distances）定义，并按元素与簇分别基于训练距离分布设定 γ=1；其为分段可微的逐原子量，无需额外推理即可与能量、力一并计算。对照实验表明，对不变多体基（invariant many-body basis）的归一化随机投影可检测结构与化学外推。

Conclusion: CALM 外推等级提供了一种低开销、可微的逐原子可靠性指标，可用于监控基础模型的外推行为并指导主动数据采集。

Abstract: Foundation machine-learning interatomic potentials cover broad configurational and chemical spaces, but their reliability can vary across the atomic environments encountered during a simulation. Here we introduce the calibrated Mahalanobis (CALM) extrapolation grade $γ$, a piecewise differentiable per-atom quantity integrated into GRACE foundation models and evaluated alongside energies and forces in a single model pass. We define $γ$ from nearest-cluster Mahalanobis distances in latent feature space, setting $γ=1$ from the training-distance distribution separately for each element and cluster. Controlled tests show that a normalized random projection of the invariant many-body basis detects structural and chemical extrapolation. On different foundation datasets, OMat24 and SMAX, $γ$ correlates with atomic force errors and separates structures with different error distributions. The CALM grade adds percent-level computational cost, and its spatial gradient guides uncertainty-biased data collection toward configurations with larger absolute force errors.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用溶液法制备 AgBiS₂ 基忆阻器，结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）识别本征缺陷与迁移势垒，并运用界面与厚度工程、电化学阻抗谱（electrochemical impedance spectroscopy）、脉冲测量及 operando 后结构分析进行表征。

Result: 器件表现出约 0.08 V 的超低 SET 电压与 >10⁴ 的高 ON/OFF 比；计算表明 Ag 间隙原子为最有利本征缺陷且 Ag 亚晶格中间隙与空位迁移势垒低；阻变行为可从突变数字模式系统调控为渐变模拟模式；低偏压下出现显著负电容（negative capacitance，感性）行为；脉冲序列下实现渐变电导调制并模拟突触响应。

Conclusion: 后操作结构分析显示，反复的导电细丝形成与断裂驱动开关层发生显著形貌演化；将结构动力学与开关变异性关联，为可靠、耐用且可持续的忆阻器设计提供了重要原则。

Abstract: Memristors have emerged as key building blocks of neuromorphic computing architectures due to their ability to integrate data storage and processing. While metal halide perovskites have recently shown significant promise owing to their mixed electronic-ionic conduction and low-cost solution processability, their reliance on toxic lead and limited stability presents critical challenges. Here, we report environmentally friendly, low-toxicity AgBiS2-based solution-processable memristors exhibiting an ultra-low SET voltage of ~0.08 V and a high ON/OFF ratio of >104. First-principles calculations identify Ag interstitials as the energetically most favourable native defect and reveal low migration barriers within the Ag sublattice, for both interstitials and vacancies, facilitating ionic transport in the AgBiS2 lattice. Through interface and thickness engineering, the resistive switching behaviour can be systematically tuned from abrupt digital to gradual analog modes. Notably, thicker switching layers promote the evolution of stable conductive pathways through intermediate metastable states, revealing a controllable filament evolution process. Electrochemical impedance spectroscopy reveals pronounced negative capacitance (inductive) behaviour at low bias voltages, arising from coupled electronic-ionic dynamics. Consistent with this behaviour, pulse measurements demonstrate gradual conductance modulation under pulse trains, emulating synaptic responses relevant for neuromorphic computing. Finally, post-operando structural analysis reveals substantial morphological evolution of the switching layer driven by repeated filament formation and rupture. Linking structural dynamics to switching variability provides important design principles for achieving reliable and durable sustainable memristors.

</details>


### [41] [Phase-Field Modeling of Liquid Phases with Ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40308)

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合焓（enthalpy of mixing）显著为负会导致液相中出现有序化（ordering）现象。CALPHAD 方法中已发展出多种液相热力学描述（如缔合模型（associate model）与改进准化学模型（modified quasichemical model）），但将这些液相模型及其自由能表达式直接纳入相场（phase-field）模拟尚缺乏可行路径。

Method: 关键在于识别液相内部的物理过程并恰当定义内部热力学变量（internal thermodynamic variables）。据此将液相自由能转化为组分成分（component compositions）与内部变量的函数，并通过 Allen-Cahn 型弛豫方程（Allen-Cahn-type relaxation equations）描述内部变量的演化，从而把 CALPHAD 型自由能（CALPHAD-type free energy）表述与相场模拟相耦合。

Result: 以 Na-Sb 和 Na-Sn 体系为对象，演示了该建模策略在液相向化学计量相（stoichiometric phases）转变过程中的应用，表明所提方法能够直接引入液相模型及其自由能表述。

Conclusion: 该研究为利用相场模拟预测具有有序化液相的微观组织演化（microstructure evolution）奠定了基础，拓展了 CALPHAD 液相模型在相场框架下的应用范围。

Abstract: A strong negative enthalpy of mixing can lead to ordering in liquid solutions. To describe ordering in liquids, different thermodynamic descriptions of liquid phases have been developed in the CALPHAD approach, including the associate model and the modified quasichemical model. In this study, we show that the key to directly incorporate these liquid phase models and their corresponding CALPHAD-type free energy formulations in phase-field simulations, is to identify the internal processes and to properly define the internal thermodynamic variables. The free energy of the liquid phase can then be converted to a function of component compositions and internal variables, and the evolution of the internal variables will be determined via Allen-Cahn-type relaxation equations. We demonstrate this modeling strategy using the formation of stoichiometric phases from liquid phases in Na-Sb and Na-Sn systems. This study will pave the way for predicting microstructure evolution in liquids with ordering using phase-field simulations.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 在x≈0.3附近，核效应在从氘到铅的广泛核靶中出现系统性抵消，表明该区域适合作为研究束缚核子部分子结构的参照点；其物理内涵可在微观模型中进一步阐释。

Abstract: Deep-inelastic scattering (DIS) data on nuclei, from deuterium to lead, indicate that nuclear effects on the partonic structure of bound nucleons are remarkably small around $x=0.3$, where valence-quark distributions peak. We present the results of a global analysis of DIS data on the cross-section ratios $σ^A/σ^{{}^2\text{H}}$ in the range $0.25 \leq x \leq 0.35$, revealing a remarkable cancellation of nuclear effects across the entire range of nuclear targets. We also discuss the interpretation of such an observation within a microscopic model of nuclear modifications of the structure functions.

</details>


### [43] [Effective Contact Theory for Exotic Loosely Bound States in Strongly Interacting Expanding Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38579)
*Xiaofeng Wang,Zebo Tang,Zhangbu Xu,Chi Yang,Wangmei Zha*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 提出有效接触理论（effective contact theory），将重离子碰撞中松散束缚态的产生与连续态两粒子关联统一描述，把常规联合（coalescence）的硬相对动量截断替换为正则化的Bethe–Peierls核，并给出首个束缚—连续一致性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短程关联可产生独立于微观细节的普适关系，Tan接触量（Tan's contact）在原子、凝聚态与核系统中已成功连接近对、高动量组分与束缚态形成；但相对论QCD物质（relativistic QCD matter）的非平衡冻结（freeze-out）是否受类似普适性支配尚属开放问题。

Method: 构建有效接触理论，用正则化的Bethe–Peierls核（regulated Bethe–Peierls kernel）替代传统联合模型的硬相对动量截断：冻结定域（freeze-out localization）确定其动量尺度，复合粒子产额确定其接触留数（contact residue），从而把束缚态与连续态观测量视为同一两粒子密度矩阵（two-particle density matrix）的投影。

Result: 库仑束缚的Kμ原子因原子、产生与源尺度高度分离而成为理想实现体系；对STAR dΛ关联的接触理论分析提供了首个束缚—连续一致性检验，并表明推断出的近阈参数可依赖于有限膨胀源。

Conclusion: 该框架为在强相互作用膨胀物质中统一关联联合、关联（correlations）与奇异束缚态建立了通用策略。

Abstract: Short-distance correlations generate universal relations that can be independent of microscopic details. Tan's contact is a prominent example, connecting close pairs, high-momentum constituents, and bound-state formation across atomic, condensed-matter, and nuclear systems. Whether an analogous universality governs the nonequilibrium freeze-out of relativistic QCD matter is an open question. We develop an effective contact theory that relates the production of loosely bound states to continuum two-particle correlations in heavy-ion collisions. The construction replaces the hard relative-momentum cutoff of conventional coalescence by a regulated Bethe--Peierls kernel: freeze-out localization fixes its momentum scale, while the composite yield fixes its contact residue. Bound and continuum observables then become projections of a common two-particle density matrix rather than independent phenomena. Coulomb-bound $Kμ$ atoms provide an ideal realization because their atomic, production, and source scales are widely separated. A contact-theory analysis of STAR $dΛ$ correlations supplies a first bound--continuum consistency test and demonstrates how inferred near-threshold parameters can depend on the finite expanding source. The framework establishes a general strategy for relating coalescence, correlations, and exotic bound states in strongly interacting expanding matter.

</details>

### [44] [Relativistic Spherical Accretion in Nonrotating Black Hole Spacetimes: Astrophysical Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38508)
*David Barrero-González,Alejandro Cruz-Osorio*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文将 Michel 解推广至任意对角球对称时空，系统分析十三种非旋转黑洞时空的球对称吸积流，并探讨对超大质量黑洞与 GW190521 前身黑洞的观测应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有吸积研究多局限于 Schwarzschild 等标准时空，缺乏对不同黑洞解（含荷电、正则、标量场、暗能量、修改引力等）吸积行为的统一比较，故需系统考察时空偏离对吸积流的影响。

Method: 将 Michel 解（Michel solution）推广至任意对角时空（diagonal spacetimes），计算密度、温度、速度、马赫数（Mach number）、质量吸积率与轫致辐射光度（bremsstrahlung luminosity）的平衡剖面，并给出吸积率与光度随时空参数的拟合公式。

Result: 以 Schwarzschild 解为参照，吸积流出现显著偏离：宇宙学常数与暗能量模型强烈抑制吸积，而正则黑洞（regular black holes）与类 Yakawa 时空总体增强吸积；并应用于 Sgr A*、M87* 及 GW190521 相关的中间质量黑洞前身质量推断。

Conclusion: 观测不确定性可能允许探测偏离 Schwarzschild 时空的印记，表明吸积观测有望成为区分不同黑洞时空模型的探针。

Abstract: We present a systematic analysis of spherically symmetric accretion flows onto thirteen non-rotating black hole spacetimes, including charged and regular black holes, scalar and dilaton fields, dark energy, cosmological constants, modified gravity, and parametrized metrics. We generalize the Michel solution to arbitrary diagonal spacetimes and investigate the density, temperature, velocity, Mach number, mass accretion rate, and bremsstrahlung luminosity equilibrium profiles. Using the Schwarzschild solution as a reference, we find significant deviations in the accretion flow, with cosmological-constant and dark-energy models strongly suppressing accretion, while regular and Yukawa-like spacetimes generally enhance it. We provide fitting formulas for the accretion rates and luminosities as functions of the spacetime parameters. We then explore two astrophysical applications: accretion onto supermassive black holes, such as Sgr A* and M87*, and the inferred progenitor masses of the intermediate-mass black holes associated with the GW190521 gravitational wave event. We find that observational uncertainties may allow us to probe signatures of deviations from the Schwarzschild spacetime.

</details>


### [45] [Improved Population and EOS Joint Inference for Binary Neutron Star Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39057)
*Marc Ebiri,Richard O'Shaughnessy*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该工作将 RIFT 算法（RIFT algorithm）的广义版本 Hyperpipe 扩展为可接入用户自定义先验（prior），以实现中子星状态方程（equation of state, EOS）与双中子星族群超参数（population hyperparameters）的联合推断（joint inference），并发现常用 EOS 族的经验性先验边界过于严格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星内部极端环境为探测高密度核状态方程提供了途径，但历史上 EOS 与中子星质量分布（mass distribution）常被分别推断，后者在 EOS 研究中往往被简单固定。由于 EOS 依赖中子星质量，二者理应联合推断。

Method: 在 Hyperpipe（RIFT 算法的广义版本）中实现与用户可提供灵活先验的接口，以支持 EOS 与双中子星族群超参数的联合推断；并联合若干已有观测（银河系大质量脉冲星、双中子星系统、毫秒 X 射线脉冲星、引力波事件 GW170817 与核对称能（nuclear symmetry energy）），采用广泛使用的参数化 EOS 族与简单高斯族群模型（Gaussian population model）。同时引入新的坐标变换（coordinate transformations）。

Result: 恢复出的参数与以往 EOS 及双中子星族群分析一致，族群拟合为二元正态分布（bivariate normal distribution），均值 $(μ_1,μ_2) = (1.39,1.27) M_{\odot}$、宽度 $σ= 0.08 M_{\odot}$。通过新的坐标变换发现该 EOS 族所用的经验性先验边界可能过于严格；采用放松但仍具物理性的先验边界后，超参数后验（posterior）显著改善，EOS 推断仅发生适度变化。

Conclusion: 基于 Hyperpipe 的框架可有效实现 EOS 与双中子星族群超参数的联合推断；分析表明现行 EOS 族的经验性先验边界可能过窄，放松且仍保持物理性的边界可带来明显更优的超参数后验，并对 EOS 推断产生温和影响。

Abstract: The extreme environment within neutron stars presents the opportunity to probe the nuclear equation of state at high densities while studying the properties of these stellar remnants. Historically, the equation of state and neutron star mass distribution have been inferred separately, with the latter often simply assumed to be fixed in studies of the former, but the dependence of the equation of state on neutron star mass indicates they should be inferred together. In this work, we extend the generalized version of the popular RIFT algorithm known as Hyperpipe to interface with flexible, user-provided priors that will facilitate joint inference of equation of state and binary neutron star population hyperparameters. We demonstrate this framework's utility via application to a combination of several existing observations, particularly including massive galactic pulsars, double neutron star systems, millisecond X-ray pulsars, the gravitational wave event GW170817 and the nuclear symmetry energy, within the context of a widely-adopted parametric EOS family and a simple Gaussian population model. We recover parameters consistent with previous analyses of the EOS and binary neutron star population, finding the latter to fit a bivariate normal distribution with mean $(μ_1,μ_2) = (1.39,1.27) M_{\odot}$ and width $σ= 0.08 M_{\odot}$. Furthermore, we implement novel coordinate transformations in our pipeline, with which we have discovered that the ad hoc prior boundaries used for this EOS family may be too restrictive. We present results with relaxed yet still physical prior boundaries, noting vastly improved hyperparameter posteriors and modestly different equation of state inferences.

</details>

### [46] [Theory of criticality-enabled $U(1)$ symmetry breaking in a class of 1+1D systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38581)
*E. S. Andriyakhina,A. S. Shankar,T. Senthil,Z. D. Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文对 1+1 维 Lifshitz 量子临界点处自发 $U(1)$ 对称性破缺现象提出解析理论，指出 Berry 相位耦合使 $\theta$ 与 $U(1)$ 电荷密度共轭，在大 $N$ 极限下带电顶点 $e^{i\beta\theta}$ 出现长程有序，并结合 DMRG 数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对近期提出的 1+1 维 Lifshitz 量子临界点自发 $U(1)$ 对称性破缺现象，需要一个解析理论来解释其机制与后果。

Method: 构建含两个周期标量 $\theta$、$\phi$ 的低能场论，Berry 相位耦合使 $\theta$ 与 $U(1)$ 电荷密度 $\partial_x\phi$ 正则共轭；在可控大 $N$ 极限下解析计算，并给出超越大 $N$ 极限的解析论证；使用有限尺寸与无限系统 DMRG 在具有 $U(1)\rtimes\mathbb{Z}_2$ 对称性的巡回费米子链上检验预言。

Conclusion: 解析与数值结果共同阐明了 1+1 维中临界性促成 $U(1)$ 对称性破缺的机制及其后果。

Abstract: We present an analytic theory of the recently proposed phenomenon of spontaneous $U(1)$ symmetry breaking at 1+1D Lifshitz quantum critical points. The low-energy field theory contains two periodic scalars $θ$ and $φ$, with a Berry phase coupling that makes $θ$ canonically conjugate to the $U(1)$ charge density $\partial_x φ$. Within a controlled large-$N$ limit, we demonstrate that the $U(1)$-charged phase vertex $e^{iβθ}$ develops long-range order, while the conjugate vertex $e^{iβφ}$ decays as a stretched exponential, $\log\langle e^{iβφ(x)} e^{-iβφ(0)}\rangle\propto-|β|^{4/3}|x|^{2/3}$. Going beyond the large-$N$ limit, we give an analytic argument that the Lifshitz field theory supports $U(1)$ long-range order provided its dynamical exponent satisfies $z\neq1$, a condition strongly supported by existing calculations. We test these predictions using finite-size and infinite-system DMRG in an itinerant-fermion chain with $U(1) \rtimes \mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry. At the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ ferromagnetic transition, the spin sector of the chain maps to the Lifshitz field theory. Consistent with analytic predictions, the $U(1)$-charged spin-nematic bond operator $S_i^+S_{i+1}^+$ exhibits long-range order, while the electron Green's function shows stretched-exponential decay. Together, these results elucidate the mechanism and consequences of criticality-enabled $U(1)$ symmetry breaking in 1+1D.

</details>

Conclusion: 相变信息可以保留在符号问题之中，即便直接符号采样（direct sign sampling）已变得不切实际。这为通过符号问题的行为获取相变信息提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Discussions of the sign problem usually focus on its role as the primary limitation of the applicability of quantum Monte Carlo methods for reliably solving quantum many-body models. Its behavior as a function of spatial lattice size, doping, temperature, and interaction strengths has been carefully characterized within the determinant quantum Monte Carlo algorithm. Here, we consider the much less well-explored question of the temperature- and size-dependence of the sign problem in the world-line quantum Monte Carlo method. We review how, in this algorithm, even sampling the partition function of free fermions yields a sign problem, which we connect to non-analytic behavior in the corresponding reference model, hard-core bosons on a lattice. The latter exhibits a finite-temperature Kosterlitz-Thouless phase transition in 2D, and we show how this non-analytic behavior is imprinted on the average sign of the weights for the corresponding free fermion 2D tight-binding model. Our results thus show how phase-transition information can remain encoded in the sign problem even after direct sign sampling becomes impractical.

Abstract: Clifford augmented methods have emerged as a powerful technique to simulate quantum circuits and many-body systems by exploiting the stabiliser structure found in said systems, whilst additionally enabling one to readily quantify the non-stabilizerness (i.e.\ magic) present. In this work, we utilise a qudit Clifford augmented simulation method to probe the presence of magic in the Affleck-Kennedy-Lieb-Tasaki (AKLT) model, finding that its ground state and scar states are highly magical as quantified by their Stabiliser Rényi Entropy (SRE). Nevertheless, owing to its low entanglement structure, these states are still readily expressible as a Clifford augmented Matrix Product State with low bond-dimension and moreover may be time-evolved using the Clifford augmented time-dependent variational principle (TDVP) algorithm. We utilise Clifford augmented TDVP to study the string order dynamics in the scar states of the AKLT model after a quench within the Haldane phase to the anti-ferromagnetic Heisenberg Hamiltonian. We observe that there is a decay of string order following the quench, which is more pronounced with increasing bimagnon number. This finding highlights the sensitivity of string order in the AKLT scar states to symmetry-preserving quenches, even when the initial and final Hamiltonians belong to the same topological phase.

Abstract: It is well-known that thermalization breaks down in presence of an extensive number of symmetries or conservation laws that are mutually compatible or commuting with each other. We ask here how an extensive number of mutually incompatible local symmetries govern eigenstate thermalization (ETH). We will investigate this through models within the class of bond-dependent $\mathbb{Z}_2$-symmetric quantum spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ Hamiltonians that naturally admit this structure. This leads to exponentially large degeneracies in the spectrum governed by the incompatible symmetry structure. Here, using spectral diagnostics, we show that non-integrability can survive despite extensively many conserved quantities when they are incompatible. Furthermore, we show that ETH behavior persists deep into the many-body spectral edge due to this structure. In other words, incompatible local symmetries enable a mechanism to obtain enough thermodynamic entropy at low excitation energy densities to make ETH operational or predictive near the ground state.

</details>

Temperature dependence of the monoclinic structure at ambient pressure presents complex evolution of lattice parameters with weak but clearly discernable anomalies at \SI{\sim 250} {\K} and \SI{\sim 110} {\K}, which are correlated with the second MIM transition and the linear to nonlinear temperature-dependent resistivity crossover, respectively. These anomalies are likely due to some charge order state causing a partially gapped Fermi surface. The magnetic phase diagram of PrIr$_3$B$_2$ is also investigated from anisotropic measurements. Abstract: Characterizing entanglement in quantum materials through experimentally accessible observables remains a central challenge. Extending this approach to local composite observables requires new entanglement bounds. Here we establish a rigorous bound for the total nearest-neighbour vector chirality of periodic spin-$1/2$ chains with an even number of sites. The quantum Fisher information per spin cannot exceed $k+1$ for any $k$-producible state, so exceeding this bound certifies entanglement depth of at least $k+1$. Combining infinite density-matrix renormalization group and thermal pure quantum-state calculations, we demonstrate multipartite entanglement encoded in vector-chiral correlations at zero and finite temperatures in an extended $J_1$-$J_2$ XXZ chain. Abstract: Moiré superlattices formed in transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) offer a versatile platform for exploring emergent quantum phases arising from strong electronic correlations. </details>

Conclusion: 熵驱动的不稳定性及其与磁序的共存得到三种方法确证，表明与足够软的物理模式耦合的补偿磁体可经由热效应产生交替磁性。

Abstract: We identify a route to altermagnetism driven by entropy upon heating and demonstrate it in a minimal square-lattice antiferromagnet. We derive an instability criterion for this exchange-modulated state, predict its temperature-driven reentrant behavior, and show that its overlap with Néel order defines a finite-temperature altermagnetic phase. Variational spin-wave theory, spin--lattice Monte Carlo, and cluster diagonalization establish the entropy-driven instability and its coexistence with magnetic order. The resulting state supports a transverse spin conductivity. The mechanism provides a route to thermally induced altermagnetism in compensated magnets coupled to sufficiently soft physical modes.

</details>


### [59] [Impurity-induced Friedel oscillations and Wigner crystallization in Luttinger liquids via Unified Field Bosonization Technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40053)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 获得强非均匀体系下费米子格林函数与密度-密度关联函数的闭式解析表达式；解析导出标度行为（scaling behaviour），发现在 $g=1/3$ 处出现交叉：$g>1/3$ 时 Friedel 振荡关联主导，$g<1/3$（强排斥相互作用区）时 Wigner 晶体样关联主导。
