Abstract: We present an analytical study of impurity-induced density correlations in interacting one-dimensional Luttinger liquids using the Unified Field Bosonization Technique (UFBT). Unlike conventional approaches that treat impurities perturbatively, UFBT incorporates localized impurities exactly at the free-fermion level while treating interactions non-perturbatively. Closed analytical expressions for fermionic Green functions and density-density correlation functions are obtained for strongly inhomogeneous systems, enabling a unified description of the impurity-induced $2k_F$ Friedel oscillations and the $4k_F$ Wigner-crystal like correlations. The corresponding scaling behaviour is derived analytically, revealing a crossover in the dominant long-distance correlations at $g=1/3$: Friedel correlations dominate for $g>1/3$, whereas Wigner-crystal like correlations become dominant for $g<1/3$, corresponding to the regime of sufficiently strong repulsive interactions. A reflection-coupled harmonic analysis is developed to account for the left-right mixing induced by impurity backscattering and to consistently reproduce the resulting density correlations. The spinless limit is also obtained as a direct reduction of the spinful formulation. These results provide an analytically controlled framework for describing impurity-induced and interaction-driven density correlations in strongly inhomogeneous one-dimensional quantum systems.

</details>


### [60] [Probing Collective and Individual Kondo Screening: Multi-Stage, Multi-Channel Kondo Effects in a $C_3$-Symmetric Four-Impurity Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40109)
*Danqing Hu,Jiangfan Wang,Zixiang Hu,Yi-feng Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作通过设计四杂质近藤（four-impurity Kondo, 4IK）模型并利用辅助浴数值重整化群（auxiliary-bath numerical renormalization group, AUNRG）方法，区分多杂质体系中的集体屏蔽（collective screening）与个体（局域）屏蔽（individual/local screening），揭示二者分别对应涌现的多阶段双通道近藤效应与不稳定的三通道近藤效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体屏蔽被认为是多杂质与晶格近藤系统（lattice Kondo systems）的基础物理机制，但如何确立该图像并使之与个体（局域）近藤屏蔽相区分，仍是一项重大挑战，因此需要设计可探测被屏蔽态本质的方案。

Method: 采用新近发展的辅助浴数值重整化群（AUNRG）方法，构造四杂质近藤（4IK）模型，其中探测杂质（probe impurity）仅与原有的三个近藤杂质耦合，以此探测被屏蔽态的性质；该辅助浴构造可推广至含额外杂质的模型。

Result: 在具有共享电子浴的 C3 对称三杂质近藤（3IK）模型中，小近藤耦合 J_K 下表现为对团簇自旋自由度的集体屏蔽并给出完全屏蔽的费米液体（Fermi liquid）基态，大 J_K 下则表现为对局域杂质的个体屏蔽。在 4IK 模型中，集体屏蔽与个体屏蔽分别导致涌现的多阶段双通道近藤效应（multi-stage two-channel Kondo effect）与不稳定的三通道近藤效应（unstable three-channel Kondo effect）。

Conclusion: 结果暗示被屏蔽态具有特殊的螺旋性（helicity）结构，并证实了多杂质近藤系统中集体屏蔽的图像；同时表明同一辅助浴构造可扩展至含额外杂质的模型，用于探索具有涌现团簇自由度的新奇多体量子态（many-body quantum states）。

Abstract: Collective screening has been proposed to underlie the basic physics of multi-impurity and lattice Kondo systems. But how to establish this picture and distinguish it from individual (local) Kondo screening remains a grand challenge. The recently developed auxiliary-bath numerical renormalization group (AUNRG) method provides a key step towards resolving this issue. Its application to the $C_3$-symmetric three-impurity Kondo (3IK) model with a shared electron bath reveals fully screened Fermi liquid ground states arising from collective screening of cluster spin degrees of freedom at small Kondo coupling $J_{\rm K}$ and individual (local) screening of local impurities at large $J_{\rm K}$. Here we design a four-impurity Kondo (4IK) model where the probe impurity couples only to the original three Kondo impurities to detect the nature of the screened states. We show that the collective and individual Kondo screenings give rise to emergent multi-stage, two-channel Kondo effect and unstable three-channel Kondo effect, respectively. This suggests a special helicity structure of the screened states and confirms the idea of collective screening in multi-impurity Kondo systems. Our work also demonstrates that the same auxiliary-bath construction can be readily extended to models with additional impurities to explore novel many-body quantum states with emergent cluster degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [61] [adapol: Adaptive pole-fitting for quantum many-body physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40150)
*Zhen Huang,Chia-Nan Yeh,Lin Lin,Nils Wentzell,Jason Kaye,Hugo U. R. Strand*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本工作提出 adapol，一个用于将松原格林函数（Matsubara Green's function）、自能与杂化函数拟合为简单极点紧凑求和的 Python 软件包，基于改进的 AAA 有理逼近与非凸优化实现稳健拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极点拟合（pole fitting）在量子杂质求解器（quantum impurity solver）的杂化拟合、极点展开压缩、图解方法以及松原格林函数的解析延拓（analytic continuation）等多类场景中均会用到，因此需要一个准确、紧凑且稳健的工具。

Method: adapol 采用改进版的 AAA 有理逼近算法（rational approximation algorithm）估计极点位置，并借助非凸优化（non-convex optimization）对极点进行精化；拟合结果由实极点位置与矩阵值留数（matrix-valued residues）构成。

Result: 该流程能够以黑箱（black-box）且抗噪（noise-robust）的方式获得准确而紧凑的拟合结果，并提供简洁自包含的接口、详尽文档与示例，以及与 TRIQS 软件包的接口。

Conclusion: adapol 为松原格林函数、自能与杂化函数的极点拟合提供了一个易用且通用的解决方案，适用于多种量子多体计算场景。

Abstract: We describe adapol, a Python package for fitting Matsubara Green's functions, self-energies, and hybridization functions by a compact sum of simple poles with real pole locations and matrix-valued residues. This pole-fitting step arises in many contexts, including hybridization fitting for quantum impurity solvers based on exact diagonalization, perturbation theory, and tensor networks, the compression of existing pole expansions such as the discrete Lehmann representation, diagrammatic methods based on discrete pole representations, and the analytic continuation of Matsubara Green's functions. adapol uses a modified version of the AAA rational approximation algorithm to estimate pole locations, and non-convex optimization to refine them. This procedure yields accurate and compact fits in a black-box and noise-robust manner. adapol provides a simple, self-contained interface with extensive documentation and examples, as well as an interface to the TRIQS package.

</details>


### [62] [Designing Strongly Correlated Quantum Phases of Matter with Foundation Neural-Network Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40209)
*Alessandro Sinibaldi,Luciano Loris Viteritti,Riccardo Rende,Federico Becca,Giuseppe Carleo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出基于基础神经网络量子态（Foundation Neural-Network Quantum States）的从头算逆设计（ab initio inverse design）框架，将目标物性表达为耦合的可微函数，并通过耦合空间中的梯度优化搜索哈密顿量；在阻挫海森堡（frustrated Heisenberg）模型上发现新的量子自旋液体（quantum spin liquid）候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计具有目标物性的材料本质上是逆问题：寻找其基态具备该物性的微观哈密顿量耦合。对强关联量子系统（strongly correlated quantum systems），高效求解此类问题仍基本不可行，故需新的通用逆设计框架。

Method: 采用基础神经网络量子态，将一族哈密顿量的基态编码于单一变分波函数（variational wave function）中；由于拟设（ansatz）显式依赖耦合，目标物性成为耦合的可微函数，哈密顿量搜索转化为耦合空间中的梯度优化。应用该方法搜索方格子阻挫海森堡模型的非磁相，逐步增加自由次近邻耦合数目，并用至 16×16 团簇的有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）检验最优模型。

Result: 单一耦合时，方法重现方格子 $J_1$-$J_2$ 海森堡模型已知的非磁窗口；两个对角耦合独立变化时，揭示出连接方格子与各向异性三角格子（anisotropic-triangular-lattice）区域的扩展非磁区；在含八个独立耦合的 $2\times2$ 原胞搜索空间中，优化自发收敛至 $J_1$-$J_2$-$δ$ 海森堡模型（对角耦合在相邻格间交替取两值，近期在交变磁性 altermagnetism 背景下提出）。对该最优模型的有限尺寸标度表明基态无磁序、二聚序（dimer order）或格序（plaquette order）。

Conclusion: 该最优模型被确立为新的量子自旋液体候选，且区别于此前在方格子上提出的候选体系，表明所提出的可微逆设计框架能有效发现新颖的强关联量子物态。

Abstract: Designing a material with a desired property amounts to solving an inverse problem: finding the couplings of a microscopic Hamiltonian whose ground state exhibits that property. For strongly correlated quantum systems, solving this problem efficiently remains largely out of reach. To address this challenge, we present a general framework for ab initio inverse design based on Foundation Neural-Network Quantum States, a recent approach in which the ground states of a family of Hamiltonians are encoded in a single variational wave function. Because the ansatz depends explicitly on the couplings, any target property is a differentiable function of them, and the search for the right Hamiltonian reduces to gradient-based optimization in coupling space. We apply this approach to search for nonmagnetic phases of frustrated Heisenberg models on the square lattice with an increasing number of free next-nearest-neighbor couplings, aiming to identify new quantum spin liquid candidates. With a single coupling, the method recovers the known nonmagnetic window of the square $J_1$-$J_2$ Heisenberg model, whereas letting the two diagonal couplings vary independently reveals an extended nonmagnetic region connecting the square-lattice and anisotropic-triangular-lattice regimes. In a search space of eight independent couplings within a $2\times2$ unit cell, which contains several paradigmatic frustrated spin models, the optimization spontaneously converges to the $J_1$-$J_2$-$δ$ Heisenberg model, in which the diagonal couplings alternate between two values on neighboring plaquettes, a model recently proposed in the context of altermagnetism. Finite-size scaling up to $16\times16$ clusters in this optimal model shows that the ground state has no magnetic, dimer, or plaquette order, establishing it as a new quantum spin liquid candidate, distinct from those previously proposed on the square lattice.

</details>

Conclusion: 结果给出了未来在小尺度 k_LISA ≈ 10^12 Mpc^-1 上对螺旋 PMFs 观测极限的有用判据。

Abstract: Primordial magnetic fields (PMFs) are anticipated to be a cosmological candidate for generating a stochastic gravitational wave background (SGWB) through their anisotropic stress. Using the planned sensitivity of the LISA-TAIJI network, we calculate the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) and perform a Fisher forecast to assess the feasibility of constraining the parameters of helical PMFs. We conclude that it is possible to estimate the helical-to-non-helical power ratio~$r_H$ with $\mathrm{SNR}^{V}>2$ if the PMF strength is $\mathcal{B} \gtrsim 10~\mathrm{nG}$ ($50~\mathrm{nG}$) in the case of the delta-function-type (scale-invariant-type) PMF spectrum. Our results offer a useful criterion that indicates the future observational limit on helical PMFs at the small scale $k_\mathrm{LISA} \approx 10^{12}~\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CMB 观测在探测原初功率谱（primordial power spectrum）尺度依赖方面存在尺度覆盖限制，因此需寻找能触及更小尺度的新探针以检验暴胀模型（inflationary models）。

Method: 以未来暗 Ages 21 cm 功率谱（Dark Ages 21 cm power spectrum）作为探测手段，考察其对原初功率谱尺度依赖的约束能力，并与 CMB 数据联合分析，同时评估不同枢轴尺度（pivot scale）选择的影响。

Conclusion: 暗 Ages 21 cm 观测是检验暴胀物理的有力探针，与 CMB 数据联合可提升约束能力；但模型区分能力取决于其尺度依赖形式，且分析中需明确枢轴尺度选择。

Abstract: We investigate the potential of future observations of the Dark Ages 21 cm power spectrum to probe the scale dependence of the primordial power spectrum and distinguish between inflationary models. While models predicting nearly identical running spectral indices may remain difficult to distinguish even with future 21 cm observations, models with unconventional scale dependence can be stringently tested. We further show that combining 21 cm observations with CMB data can significantly improve the constraints, even for a moderate observational configuration. We examine the impact of the choice of pivot scale on the resulting constraints.

</details>

### [67] [Catheter-modulated transient solute dispersion in micropolar annular flow with a retentive and absorptive arterial wall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38423)
*Sohel Ahmed,Nanda Poddar,Kajal Kumar Mondal,Subham Dhar*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文在同心环腔内建立微极流体（micropolar fluid）压力驱动流的瞬态溶质输运模型，耦合 Gill-Sankarasubramanian 广义弥散框架，求解交换系数与色散系数的时间演化及窄间隙渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的在于区分导管化动脉（catheterized artery）中受限几何与微极效应（hydrodynamic effects）和管壁动力学（kinetic wall effects）对溶质弥散的独立贡献，为理解溶质在动脉与导管间隙中的瞬时输运提供理论框架。

Method: 方法上采用精确稳态微极速度场，耦合 Gill-Sankarasubramanian 广义弥散理论，从而得到体相-表面（bulk-surface）层级方程，并用 Rannacher 阻尼 Crank-Nicolson 时间推进求解 $K_0(t)$、$K_1(t)$、$K_2(t)$；同时进行 $\varepsilon=1-\lambda$ 的窄间隙渐近分析。管壁条件为不可渗透导管的完全反射与动脉壁的不可逆吸收加可逆表面滞留。

Result: 结果显示交换系数与微极参数无关，满足 $-K_0(0^+)=2(\beta+\theta Da)/(1-\lambda^2)$；短时 $K_2-Pe^{-2}\sim\sigma_v^2t$，表明初期主导弥散为流体力学性质且与壁面动力学无关。弱反应区中微极性对对流近似线性、对剪切弥散（shear dispersion）呈二次影响；窄间隙分析给出第六次幂抑制关系，且 $\lambda$ 从 0.01 增至 0.30 时有效对流约降 2.45 倍、被动剪切弥散约降 24 倍，并满足精确分配 $\Phi_m+\Phi_s+\Phi_a=1$。

Conclusion: 结论表明该耦合公式能够重构反应调制的横向非均匀性，并将受限与微极引起的流体力学效应同壁面动力学效应清晰区分，同时给出溶质在可移动、可逆滞留与不可逆吸收三部分间的精确分配。

Abstract: We study transient solute dispersion in pressure-driven micropolar flow through a concentric annulus representing a catheterized artery. The catheter is impermeable to solute, whereas the arterial wall combines irreversible removal with reversible surface retention. The exact steady micropolar velocity field is coupled to the Gill-Sankarasubramanian generalized-dispersion framework; the resulting bulk-surface hierarchy is advanced with Rannacher-damped Crank-Nicolson time stepping to determine $K_0(t)$, $K_1(t)$ and $K_2(t)$. The exchange coefficient is independent of the micropolar parameters and satisfies $-K_0(0^+)=2(β+θDa)/(1-λ^2)$, whereas $K_2-Pe^{-2}\simσ_v^2t$ initially, so the leading short-time dispersion is hydrodynamic and independent of wall kinetics. In the weak-reaction regime, micropolarity modifies convection approximately linearly and shear dispersion quadratically through the velocity-amplitude factor. A narrow-gap analysis with $\varepsilon=1-λ$ gives $\bar v\sim(2-N_c)\varepsilon^2/6$ and $K_2-Pe^{-2}\sim(2-N_c)^2\varepsilon^6/7560$ for a fixed pressure gradient, revealing a sixth-power suppression of shear dispersion as the catheter approaches the arterial wall. Increasing $λ$ from $0.01$ to $0.30$ reduces effective convection by about a factor of $2.45$, while chemically passive shear-induced dispersion falls by a factor of $24$. The coupled formulation also yields the exact partition $Φ_m+Φ_s+Φ_a=1$, separating mobile, reversibly retained and irreversibly absorbed solute. The reconstructed field quantifies reaction-modulated transverse non-uniformity, while the results distinguish hydrodynamic effects of confinement and microrotation from kinetic wall effects.

</details>


### [68] [A macroscopic-shadow-corrected lattice Boltzmann method for fast, time-accurate simulation of low-Reynolds-number transient flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39474)
*Yanhui Ding,Yaolong Yu,Yuan Yu,Hao Zhang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文提出宏观阴影修正格子玻尔兹曼方法（macroscopic-shadow-corrected lattice Boltzmann method, MSC-LBM），通过对未分裂动力学残差施加缺陷修正（defect correction），在保留 BDF2 不动点与时间精度的前提下消除声学时间步限制，实现随网格加密单调增长的墙钟加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缓慢瞬态流的显式格子玻尔兹曼模拟（lattice Boltzmann simulation）受声学时间步（acoustic time step）制约；双时间步进（dual-time stepping）虽可解除该限制，但内层收敛缓慢，已报道的墙钟收益仅约四至十倍，且缺乏随网格加密而增强的机制。

Method: MSC-LBM 对未分裂动力学残差（unsplit kinetic residual）施加缺陷修正；在每个傅里叶波数（Fourier wavenumber）上对守恒残差矩精确求解类 Stokes 系统，同时保持二阶后向差分公式（BDF2）不动点与时间精度。沿双松弛时间（two-relaxation-time）轴 $Λ=1/4$，取 $ω^+=ω^-=1$ 使单次碰撞消除全部非流体力学扰动，$η(ν)=|6ν-1|/(6ν+1)$ 在 $ν=1/6$ 处消失。另构建与反弹格式一致的动力学粗求解器（kinetic coarse solver）并自适应重线性化，以扩展至全封闭腔体。

Result: 在计时实验中，$N=256$、$\mathrm{Re}=1$ 时，MSC-LBM 在匹配精度与 1% 门槛下分别取得 27.95 倍与 52.71 倍墙钟加速；匹配加速比随网格单调上升至 $N=1024$ 时的 122.02，且在 $\mathrm{Re}=10^{-4}$–$100$ 范围内持续有效。三维 D3Q19 扩展在 $N=128$、$\mathrm{Re}=1$ 同样门槛下达到 27.85 与 8.84 倍。全封闭腔体情形下，物理推进（setup-excluding physical-march）加速在 $\mathrm{Re}=10$ 与 $\mathrm{Re}=100$ 时分别为 26.86 与 3.84 倍（$N=128$）。逐波数符号精确求逆给出了可达的非设计收缩包络。

Conclusion: 收缩性、参数扫描与计时结果共同界定了已验证的适用区间：低至中等雷诺数（Reynolds number）的瞬态流动，其计算成本无需再受声学步进支配。

Abstract: Explicit lattice Boltzmann simulations of slow transient flows are constrained by the acoustic time step. Dual-time stepping can remove this restriction, but slow inner convergence has limited reported wall-clock gains to roughly four- to tenfold, without a mechanism that strengthens under refinement. We present the macroscopic-shadow-corrected lattice Boltzmann method (MSC-LBM), which applies defect correction to the unsplit kinetic residual. At each Fourier wavenumber, a Stokes-like system is solved exactly for the conserved residual moments while preserving the second-order backward-differentiation formula (BDF2) fixed point and temporal accuracy. Along the two-relaxation-time axis $Λ=1/4$, $ω^+=ω^-=1$ makes one collision eliminate all non-hydrodynamic perturbations; $η(ν)=|6ν-1|/(6ν+1)$ vanishes at $ν=1/6$, leaving hydrodynamic slow modes whose long-wave shadow is inverted by the corrector. In completed timing campaigns, MSC-LBM achieves 27.95- and 52.71-fold wall-clock speedups at $N=256$, $\mathrm{Re}=1$ under matched-accuracy and $1\%$ gates. The matched speedup rises monotonically to 122.02 at $N=1024$, with gains persisting across $\mathrm{Re}=10^{-4}$--$100$. The three-dimensional D3Q19 extension reaches 27.85 and 8.84 under the same gates at $N=128$, $\mathrm{Re}=1$. A bounce-back-consistent kinetic coarse solver with adaptive relinearisation extends MSC-LBM to fully enclosed cavities, yielding setup-excluding physical-march speedups of 26.86 at $\mathrm{Re}=10$ and 3.84 at $\mathrm{Re}=100$ for $N=128$. An exact per-wavenumber symbol inverse establishes the attainable off-design contraction envelope. The contraction, parameter-sweep, and timing results jointly delimit the demonstrated operating regime: low-to-moderate-Reynolds-number transients for which acoustic stepping need not dictate computational cost.

</details>


### [69] [Stable and Interpretable Multi-Mode Rheological Universal Differential Equations (mmRUDEs) for Data-Driven Constitutive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38470)
*Mohua Das,Nicholas King,Navid Azizan,Gareth H. McKinley*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种以稳定性与可靠性为核心的多模式 RUDE（Rheological Universal Differential Equation）框架，通过数值条件化改进、热力学约束与可解释性表示，实现复杂流变行为的稳定数据驱动建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 RUDE 框架虽具备框架无关的张量本构骨架并可嵌入神经网络，但其灵活性导致训练中的数值刚性问题、张量基项组合的非唯一性以及随非线性增强的外推能力差，尤其对具有宽松弛谱（broad relaxation spectra）的工业材料构成挑战。

Method: 采用输入不变量（input invariants）的对数压缩与分离网络架构（split-network architecture）改善训练数值条件；引入可微投影层（differentiable projection layer）在每个时间步强制满足 Clausius--Duhem 不等式；并提出 “rheome” 以紧凑表示主导的加权张量基贡献，提升可解释性。

Result: 框架以合成数据（多模式 Giesekus 模型）与缠结硅酮聚合物熔体（entangled silicone polymer melt）实验数据为例，两者均在 LAOS（Large Amplitude Oscillatory Shear）下训练，并在稳态剪切、瞬态应力增长与拉伸流动等未见流动条件下保持稳定与准确，而未约束模型可能出现数值发散。

Conclusion: 上述改进使 RUDE 框架训练得到的模型具备稳定性与可解释性，为在计算流体动力学（computational fluid dynamics）模拟中部署以及指导软材料加工工艺的理性设计奠定基础。

Abstract: The Rheological Universal Differential Equation (RUDE) framework embeds neural networks within a frame-indifferent tensorial constitutive backbone and so enables data-driven discovery of complex material rheological behavior. However, the flexibility that makes the RUDE framework attractive also makes it difficult to deploy: the learned neural network correction can make the constitutive equation numerically stiff during training, distribute corrections across non-unique combinations of tensor-basis terms, and extrapolate poorly with increasing nonlinearity, especially for industrially relevant materials with broad relaxation spectra. We develop a stability- and reliability-focused multi-mode RUDE framework that addresses three key challenges: thermodynamic admissibility, out-of-distribution prediction, and interpretability. Logarithmic compression of the input invariants and a split-network architecture improve numerical conditioning during training, while a differentiable projection layer enforces the Clausius--Duhem inequality at every time step. We illustrate the framework with both synthetic (a multi-mode Giesekus model) and experimental data on an entangled silicone polymer melt. Both models were trained on LAOS; they remain stable and accurate under unseen flow conditions, including steady shear, transient stress growth, and extensional flows, for which the unconstrained models may diverge numerically. To aid in interpretation of the contributions of each learned correction to the rheological behavior, we introduce the rheome, a compact way of representing the dominant weighted tensor-basis contributions to the overall rheological behavior. These improvements to the RUDE framework lead to stable and interpretable trained models, paving the way for implementation in computational fluid dynamics simulations and for guiding the rational design of soft material processing operations.

</details>


### [70] [Directional Control of Droplet Motion via Geometric Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38654)
*Manoj K. Chaudhury,Vanessa Elias,Michelle Fruge*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

Motivation: 在硅油溶胀（swelled with silicone oil）的PDMS基底上，水滴易于运动，但通过倾斜基底配合浅槽与陷阱来引导液滴轨迹的方式难以同时操控多个液滴，存在明显局限。

Result: 平凹基底可持续引导液滴沿弯曲测地线运动；嵌入刚性板并经溶胀形成的S形台阶能将液滴运动限制在单一方向；两种策略结合后可实现单液滴与多液滴的多种定向运动形式。

Abstract: We explore new methods for inducing the movement of water droplets on elastomeric substrates, such as polydimethylsiloxane (PDMS). Our approach builds upon the well-known phenomenon that water droplets move easily across these substrates when swelled w,ith silicone oil. While designing shallow open channels and traps on a PDMS film offers a simple way to control a droplet's trajectory by tilting the substrate in two orthogonal directions, this method is poorly suited for manipulating multiple droplets simultaneously. To address this limitation, we introduce two new methods. The first method utilizes a plano-concave PDMS platform with a radius of curvature significantly larger than the size of the droplet and its translation distance. When the system's configuration is altered, this curved substrate consistently guides the droplet along a curved geodesic. The second method incorporates diode-like gates on the PDMS. These gates are created on either flat or plano-concave PDMS films by embedding thin, rigid plates into the elastomer prior to crosslinking, followed by swelling in silicone oil. This process generates sigmoidal steps that restrict droplet motion to a single direction. Finally, we demonstrate how combining these two strategies enables various forms of directed motion for both single and multiple droplets.

Result: 同一普适态可由多条路径到达：提高活性、增强非线性对流、减弱非线性饱和或调节 Swift–Hohenberg 不稳定性。由已知活性控制转变标定出的单一临界值，能够预测其他路径上普适性的起始，并将它们折叠到共同阈值上。该普适统计包含参数无关的能量谱（energy spectrum）E(k)∼k^{-3/2}。

Conclusion: 研究识别出一个控制普适活性湍流（universal active turbulence）起始的动力学组织参数（dynamical organizing parameter），并为 Toner–Tu–Swift–Hohenberg 普适类（universality class）提供了一个类雷诺数度量，表明该转变可由单一控制参数统一刻画，而非仅依赖活性。

Abstract: Beyond a critical Toner--Tu drive, bacterial turbulence exhibits universal statistics, including a parameter-independent energy spectrum $E(k)\sim k^{-3/2}$. We show that the same universal state can be reached not only by increasing activity, but also by strengthening nonlinear advection, weakening nonlinear saturation, or tuning the Swift--Hohenberg instability. Motivated by the competition between these processes, we identify the independent dimensionless groups governing the dynamics and construct a Reynolds-like control parameter from them. A single critical value, calibrated from the known activity-controlled transition, predicts the onset of universality along the other parameter routes and collapses them onto a common threshold. These results identify a dynamical organizing parameter for the onset of universal active turbulence and provide a Reynolds-like measure for the Toner--Tu--Swift--Hohenberg universality class.

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 提出演化几何 Transformer（Evolving-Geometry Transformer, EGT），在编码器各层重建命中连通图并以相对命中坐标作为可学习注意力偏置，用于高亮度大型强子对撞机（High-Luminosity LHC）的带电粒子径迹重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高亮度大型强子对撞机下命中环境密集且存在组合歧义；现有图方法与 Transformer 方法依赖静态几何邻域，无法随径迹级表示演化而自适应调整。

Method: 构建图 Transformer，在连续编码器层中重建命中连通性图（hit-connectivity graph），并将相对命中坐标作为可学习的注意力偏置（attention bias）注入注意力计算。

Result: 在覆盖线性、螺旋及缩减 TrackML 事件的五个模拟基准（每条事件最多 200–500 条同时径迹）中，EGT 在五分之三设置上取得最高 FitAccuracy，最复杂设置达 79.6%，超出最强基线 1.6 个百分点；将演化拓扑替换为静态图使完美径迹率从 81% 降至 29%，移除几何偏置降至 73%；加入与信号命中数相当的合成背景后 FitAccuracy 下降 3.4 个百分点；单事件前向传播在 NVIDIA A100 上耗时 31 ms。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当仅凭局部几何不足以确定径迹归属时，对命中关联进行迭代精化（iterative refinement）可提升完整径迹的恢复能力。

Abstract: Charged particle track reconstruction at the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider is challenged by dense hit environments and combinatorial ambiguities. Existing graph-based and Transformer approaches commonly rely on static geometric neighbourhoods, which cannot adapt as track-level representations evolve. We propose the Evolving-Geometry Transformer (EGT), a graph Transformer that reconstructs the hit-connectivity graph at successive encoder layers and incorporates relative hit coordinates as learnable attention biases. Across five simulation benchmarks spanning linear, helical, and reduced TrackML events with up to 200--500 simultaneous trajectories, EGT achieves the highest FitAccuracy on three of five settings under the shared benchmark protocol, reaching 79.6\% on the most complex setting and exceeding the strongest baseline by 1.6 percentage points. Replacing the evolving topology with a static graph reduces the perfect-track rate from 81\% to 29\%, while removing the geometric bias reduces it to 73\%. Under synthetic background injection equal to the number of signal hits, FitAccuracy decreases by 3.4 percentage points. The neural-network forward pass requires 31~ms per event on an NVIDIA A100 GPU. These results indicate that iterative refinement of hit associations improves complete-track recovery when local geometry alone is insufficient to determine trajectory membership.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气中微子分析通常仅使用向上事例（$\cos\theta_\mu\in[-1,0]$）以压制宇宙线缪子本底。作者受 CJPL 极低地下缪子本底这一条件启发，检验扩展天顶角接受度能否提升大气中微子振荡测量灵敏度。

Result: 对基准振荡参数 $\Delta m^2_{32}=2.4\times10^{-3}\ \mathrm{eV}^2$、$\sin^2\theta_{23}=0.5$，周围岩石产生的中微子诱导缪子通量估计为 $(3.65\pm1.00)\times10^{-13}$ cm$^{-2}$ s$^{-1}$ sr$^{-1}$，对应 1725 m³ 液氮体积中缪子产额 $(0.13\pm0.034)$ day$^{-1}$。10 年曝光下，扩展可用天顶角范围在 90% 置信水平上使投影振荡灵敏度明显提升。

Conclusion: 在 CJPL 低本底条件下，将天顶角接受范围向向下方向适度扩展可行且有效，可在 10 年曝光内显著改善大气中微子振荡参数的投影灵敏度（90% 置信水平）。

Abstract: In atmospheric neutrino analyses, the usable zenith-angle range is generally restricted to upward-going events to suppress the cosmic-ray muon background. Motivated by the exceptionally low underground muon background at the China Jinping Underground Laboratory (CJPL), we investigate whether extending the zenith-angle acceptance can improve the sensitivity of atmospheric neutrino oscillation measurements. An existing 1725 m$^{3}$ liquid-nitrogen volume is adopted as the basis of a simplified detector model, in which the accepted zenith-angle range is extended from $\cosθ_μ\in[-1,0]$ to $\cosθ_μ\in[-1,0.3]$, and the projected oscillation sensitivity is evaluated for a 10-year exposure. The atmospheric neutrino flux is estimated by interpolating standard flux predictions as a function of geomagnetic latitude. Neutrino interactions in the surrounding rock and the liquid-nitrogen volume are simulated for neutrino energies of $E\ge0.1$ GeV, followed by secondary-particle transport and geometry-based event selection. For the benchmark oscillation parameters $Δ$m$^{2}_{32}$ = $2.4\times10^{-3}\ \mathrm{eV}^{2}$ and $\sin^{2}θ_{23}=0.5$, the neutrino-induced muon flux from the surrounding rock is estimated to be $(3.65 \pm 1.00)\times10^{-13}$ cm$^{-2}$ s$^{-1}$ sr$^{-1}$, corresponding to a muon yield of $(0.13 \pm 0.034)$ day$^{-1}$ in the 1725 m$^{3}$ liquid-nitrogen volume. Three-flavor neutrino oscillations are incorporated into a Poisson-likelihood $χ^{2}$ analysis to evaluate the sensitivity to the oscillation parameters. Extending the usable zenith-angle range leads to a clear improvement in the projected oscillation sensitivity at the 90\% confidence level over a 10-year exposure.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该测量旨在利用所选观测量对质心系散射角（scattering angle in the center-of-mass frame）与相互作用部分子动量分数（fractional momenta of the interacting partons）的敏感性，以刻画质子部分子结构（partonic structure of the proton）并测定强耦合（strong coupling）。

Method: 使用 CMS 实验在 CERN LHC 于 2016–2018 年采集的 13 TeV 质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度 138 fb⁻¹），测量 Z/γ*(→μμ)+jet 事件的三重微分截面。观测量为 μ 子对横动量（transverse momentum）、μ 子对质心系相对于实验室系的快度助推（boost in rapidity）与领头喷流（leading jet）、以及二者绝对快度间距的一半；并对探测器效应在所有三个维度上同时进行解卷。

Result: 测量得到的三重微分截面经三维同时解卷后，与微扰量子色动力学（perturbative quantum chromodynamics）次次领头阶（next-to-next-to-leading order, NNLO）并修正电弱（electroweak）与非微扰（nonperturbative）效应的预言进行了比较。

Conclusion: 该测量为表征质子部分子结构以及确定强耦合提供了有价值的信息。

Abstract: A measurement is presented of the triple-differential production cross section of Z/$γ^*$($\to$ $μμ$)+jet events using proton-proton collision data recorded at a center-of-mass energy of 13 TeV by the CMS experiment at the CERN LHC. The data, collected in the years 2016$-$2018, correspond to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. The cross section is measured as a function of the transverse momentum of the muon pair, the boost in rapidity of the center-of-mass system of the muon pair and the leading jet with respect to the laboratory frame, and half of their absolute rapidity separation. This choice of observables ensures sensitivity to the scattering angle in the center-of-mass frame and the fractional momenta of the interacting partons. After unfolding for detector effects in all three dimensions simultaneously, the results are compared with predictions at next-to-next-to-leading order in perturbative quantum chromodynamics corrected for electroweak and nonperturbative effects. This measurement provides valuable information towards characterizing the partonic structure of the proton and determining the strong coupling.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为未来重离子（heavy-ion）碰撞中 ${\rm D}^{\rm *+}$ 自旋排列研究提供精确基线，并验证与扩展此前测量。

Conclusion: 结果确认并扩展了先前测量，为未来重离子碰撞中 ${\rm D}^{\rm *+}$ 自旋排列研究提供了精确基线。

Abstract: A precise measurement of prompt and non-prompt ${\rm D}^{\rm *+}$-meson spin alignment with respect to the helicity and production axes in proton-proton (pp) collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 13.6$ TeV is presented, using data collected with the ALICE experiment at the LHC. The spin alignment is quantified by measuring the diagonal spin density matrix element $ρ_{\rm 00}$ for ${\rm D}^{\rm *+}$ mesons at midrapidity ($|y|<0.8$). The measurement is performed in the transverse-momentum ($p_{\rm T}$) range $3<p_{\rm T}<100$ GeV/$c$ for prompt ${\rm D}^{\rm *+}$ mesons and $3<p_{\rm T}<30$ GeV/$c$ for non-prompt ${\rm D}^{\rm *+}$ mesons, respectively. The measured $ρ_{\rm 00}$ values for prompt ${\rm D}^{\rm *+}$ mesons are consistent with the unpolarised expectation of $1/3$ over the entire $p_{\rm T}$ range, indicating no evidence of spin alignment. In contrast, $ρ_{\rm 00}$ of non-prompt ${\rm D}^{\rm *+}$ mesons exhibit a deviation from $1/3$, consistent with expectations from weak beauty-hadron decays and well described by PYTHIA 8 simulations coupled with the EvtGen decay package. These results confirm and extend previous measurements, providing a precise baseline for future studies of ${\rm D}^{\rm *+}$ spin alignment in heavy-ion collisions.

In this method, the dependence of the amplitudes on the dihadron and dimuon invariant masses is extracted model-independently through a fit to the helicity angles and a subsequent calculation of sPlot weights. In this paper, we show that this method still performs well when applied to data with experimental realism, namely in the presence of detection efficiencies and background candidates, both of which can have non-factorising dependencies between the fitted helicity angles and extracted two-particle masses. This work suggests that the method is suitable for application to real experimental data, where it could provide model-independent decay-amplitude shapes for direct comparison with theoretical predictions. Wresilo,W. Wu,E. Yandel,T. Yang,L. E. Yates,H. W. Yu,G. P. Zeller,J. Zennamo,C. Zhang,Y. Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: MicroBooNE 首次在氩（argon）靶上测量了无介子（no mesons）、含一个及以上质子（$N_{p}\geq 1$）的缪子中微子带电流（charged-current）双微分截面（double-differential cross-section），并发现现有事件生成器（event generator）对末态相互作用（final-state interactions）的建模均不充分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子-核相互作用中末态相互作用与质子末态建模存在不确定性，此前缺乏针对无介子、多质子末态的双微分截面测量。该工作旨在提供此类精确测量，以检验并约束事件生成器模型。

Method: 使用费米国家加速器实验室（Fermi National Accelerator Laboratory）MicroBooNE 探测器采集的完整 Booster Neutrino Beam 数据集，对应 $1.30 \times 10^{21}$ 质子打靶数（protons on target）。测量量涵盖质子多重数（proton multiplicity）、领头及次领头质子（leading and subleading protons，至第四领头质子）的动量与缪子-质子张角（muon-proton opening angle），以及强子系统的总可用动能（total available kinetic energy）。统计与系统不确定度（statistical and systematic uncertainties）的处理纳入了不同分布间的关联性（correlations）。

Result: 测得的截面与多个采用不同初级及末态相互作用模型的事件生成器预言进行了比较，结果显示对末态相互作用建模具有强烈敏感性，且所测试的生成器均无法完全恰当地描述数据。数据与预言在质子多重数分布上存在显著分歧，尤其体现在反向张角（backward opening angles）与次领头质子运动学（subleading proton kinematics）方面。

Conclusion: 该测量表明当前事件生成器在描述无介子多质子末态的中微子-氩相互作用时存在明显不足，尤其是末态相互作用与次领头质子相关观测量，可为后续模型改进提供关键约束。

Abstract: We present the first double-differential cross-section measurements of muon neutrino charged-current interactions on argon with no mesons and one or more protons ($N_{p} \geq 1$) in the final state as a function of proton multiplicity and kinematics. The analysis uses the full Booster Neutrino Beam dataset collected by the MicroBooNE detector at Fermi National Accelerator Laboratory, corresponding to a total of $1.30 \times 10^{21}$ protons on target. We measure cross sections as a function of the proton multiplicity, the momenta and muon-proton opening angles of the leading and subleading protons up to and including the fourth-leading proton, as well as the total available kinetic energy of the hadronic system. The treatment of the statistical and systematic uncertainties includes correlations between the different extracted distributions. The measured cross sections are compared to predictions from several event generators using different primary and final-state interaction models. The results reveal strong sensitivity to the modeling of final-state interactions, and none of the generators tested provides a fully adequate description of the data. Significant disagreement between data and predictions is observed in the proton multiplicity distributions, particularly at backward opening angles and for subleading proton kinematics.

</details>


### [78] [Climate, Science, and the FCC: Think Clearly, Choose Wisely, Act Swiftly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30180)
*Patrick Janot,Christophe Grojean*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该文回应2026年夏季法国物理学会粒子与场分部内部关于CERN未来环形对撞机（Future Circular Collider, FCC）的争议，逐条审视针对该项目的批评，主张气候因素本身不足以决定FCC的命运，关键在于以实际后果评判可选方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在2026年夏季，法国物理学会（French Physical Society）粒子与场分部（Particles and Fields Division）的讨论中，对FCC的反对意见集中呈现为碳排放、两座EPR核反应堆、五百人死亡、数十亿资金需重新分配以及科学目标不明确等指控。作者意在回应这些情绪化批评，并服务于CNDP组织的公众辩论及瑞士相关磋商。

Method: 文章以明确的科学愿景为基础，逐条审查上述指控，通过量级比较（orders of magnitude）面对情绪化的意象，区分项目的碳足迹（footprint）与完整资产负债表（full balance sheet），并考察推迟或放弃项目的代价，同时探讨降低影响的途径，包括尚不确定的天然氢（natural hydrogen）前景。

Result: 摘要未报告具体研究数据或定量结果，仅说明该文以逐条回应方式处理对FCC的各类指控，并将影响评估拓展至推迟或放弃方案的代价与减排可能性。

Conclusion: 气候问题本身不能决定FCC的未来，而应依据实际后果来评判可用的选择；文章将FCC之争提升为关于社会形态的选择：是以放弃与不断收缩的视野来组织社会，还是改变生产方式并选择一个值得期待的未来。

Abstract: Could a collider become the symbol of everything that must be abandoned to save the climate? In the summer of 2026, this question ran through discussions within the Particles and Fields Division of the French Physical Society. An unacceptable carbon footprint, two EPR nuclear reactors, five hundred deaths, billions to be reallocated and, to top it all, unclear scientific objectives: the objections to CERN's Future Circular Collider (FCC) appeared to form an overwhelming indictment. This essay examines each charge in turn. Grounded in a clear and compelling scientific vision, it confronts emotionally charged imagery with orders of magnitude, distinguishes a project's footprint from its full balance sheet, considers the costs of postponing or abandoning it, and explores ways of reducing its impacts-including the still uncertain prospect of natural hydrogen. The climate alone does not decide the future of the FCC; it requires us to judge the available choices by their real consequences. Developed for the public debate organised by France's National Commission for Public Debate (CNDP) and for the Swiss consultation, this essay is intended for the broadest possible readership, well beyond particle physics and the scientific community. It frames the FCC debate as a choice about the kind of society we want to build: should we organise society around renunciation and ever-shrinking horizons, or transform how we produce and choose a future worth wanting?

</details>

### [79] [Dear Quantizers, Stochastic $\approx$ Brane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38209)
*Justin Kulp*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文以超对称（supersymmetry）形式化结合莫尔斯理论（Morse theory），将随机量子化重新表述为体拓扑场论对相对量子场论的“绝对化”，并给出其与BV/BRST复形、A模型及非相对论与Carrollian极限的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机量子化（stochastic quantization）将d维欧氏量子场论视为(d+1)维Langevin系统的平衡极限，但其结构性质（如边界数据与规范对称性）尚缺乏拓扑场论层面的统一刻画，本文旨在填补该缺口。

Method: 利用随机系统的超对称表述（supersymmetric formulation），对Langevin动力学应用莫尔斯理论（Morse theory）技术；并将随机量子化解释为通过体上同调拓扑平衡场论（bulk cohomological topological Equilibrium TFT, "EqmTFT"）对相对量子场论（relative QFT）的绝对化（absolutization）。

Result: 体EqmTFT的丰富Neumann边界数据（enriched Neumann boundary data）与d维BV/BRST复形（BV/BRST complex）相关联；解析延拓（analytic continuation）揭示其与A模型（A-model）以及非相对论极限（non-relativistic limit）和Carrollian极限（Carrollian limit）存在联系。

Conclusion: 随机量子化可被组织为体拓扑场论与边界BV/BRST数据的对应框架，并借由解析延拓与多种已知物理极限（A模型、非相对论、Carrollian）建立桥梁，暗示其具有更广泛的拓扑与几何结构意义。

Abstract: Stochastic quantization formulates $d$-dimensional Euclidean QFT as the equilibrium limit of a $(d+1)$-dimensional Langevin system. Using the supersymmetric formulation of stochastic systems, we apply techniques from Morse theory to study Langevin dynamics. We interpret stochastic quantization as the absolutization of a relative QFT by a bulk cohomological topological Equilibrium TFT ("EqmTFT"), and relate its enriched Neumann boundary data to the $d$-dimensional BV/BRST complex. Analytic continuation reveals connections to the A-model and non-relativistic and Carrollian limits.

</details>


### [80] [Construction of mirror pairs of Berglund-Hubsch Calabi-Yau orbifolds of loop-type polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38273)
*Maxim Malyutin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出并应用了针对环型多项式（loop-type polynomials）的Berglund-Hubsch-Krawitz（BHK）型Calabi-Yau流形 orbifold（orbifold）及其镜像对（mirror pairs）的构造算法，枚举出216个满足Calabi-Yau条件的环型多项式，并通过分析发现两个恰好给出三代费米子（N_gen=3）的构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于构造具有现象学意义的粒子物理模型，特别是寻找夸克与轻子代数（generations）恰好为三的Calabi-Yau orbifold构型。

Method: 方法为提出并应用BHK型Calabi-Yau流形orbifold及镜像对的构造算法；对环型多项式进行完全枚举，计算Euler示性数（Euler characteristics）、代数与单态（singlets）数目，并采用Roan广义组合构造（generalized combinatorial construction）分析twisted sector态。

Result: 共获得216个满足Calabi-Yau条件的唯一环型多项式；所有研究构型中均不存在Roan对（Roan pairs），表明在解orbifold奇点（orbifold singularities）时twisted sector对Hodge数之差的贡献为零；发现对角指数（diagonal exponents）为{74, 3, 3, 5, 3}与{74, 3, 5, 3, 3}的两个构型，其夸克与轻子代数恰为三（N_gen=3）。

Conclusion: 本文表明在BHK型环型多项式框架下不存在Roan对，twisted sector对Hodge数差贡献为零；所发现的两个N_gen=3构型对构建现象学上真实的粒子物理模型具有重要价值。

Abstract: In this paper, an algorithm for constructing orbifolds of Calabi-Yau manifolds of the Berglund-Hubsch-Krawitz (BHK) type and their mirror pairs for loop-type polynomials is proposed and applied. A complete enumeration of loop polynomials satisfying the Calabi-Yau condition was carried out, yielding 216 unique polynomials. For each polynomial, the Euler characteristics, the numbers of generations and singlets were computed, and the twisted sector states were analyzed using Roan's generalized combinatorial construction. It was established that Roan pairs are absent for all studied configurations, indicating a zero contribution of the twisted sectors to the difference in Hodge numbers upon resolving the orbifold singularities. The primary physical result of this work is the discovery of two configurations with diagonal exponents $\{74, 3, 3, 5, 3\}$ and $\{74, 3, 5, 3, 3\}$, for which the number of quark and lepton generations is precisely three ($N_{\text{gen}} = 3$), which is of key interest for constructing phenomenologically realistic models of particle physics.

</details>


### [81] [Detectors in Nonrelativistic Conformal Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38301)
*Cyuan-Han Chang,Subham Dutta Chowdhury,Ian Moult,Dam Thanh Son*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在非相对论共形场论（nonrelativistic conformal field theories, NRCFTs）中引入探测器算符（detector operators）研究，定义速度微分的能量通量算符 $\mathcal{E}_v(\hat{n})$，并计算其在多体态与三体态中的单点函数，进而导出非相对论版本的共形碰撞机束缚（conformal collider bounds）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近通量（asymptotic fluxes）关联是研究物理系统的主要实验手段之一；相对论量子场论（relativistic QFT）中探测器算符形式化已取得显著进展，但非相对论共形场论中尚无相应研究。本文受此成功激励，旨在启动 NRCFTs 中探测器算符的系统研究。

Method: 在 NRCFTs 中，由于通量在角度与速度上均有分布，自然探测器算符取为速度微分的能量通量 $\mathcal{E}_v(\hat{n})$；本文给出该算符的非微扰定义，研究其对称性与代数结构，并在代表性 NRCFT 的少体与多体态中计算其单点函数，随后通过论证 $\mathcal{E}_v(\hat{n})$ 的正定性，将其施加于自旋 1 态以导出 Hofman-Maldacena 共形碰撞机束缚的非相对论推广。

Result: 计算了自由费米子（free fermions）以及幺正性极限费米子（fermions at unitarity）等少体与多体态中探测器可观测量的单点函数；在相互作用三体态中，其行为异常丰富，反映了 Efimov 三体波函数，并因共振相互作用而形成增强的“喷注”（jet）结构。基于正定性论证，得到了非相对论共形碰撞机束缚。

Conclusion: 研究勾勒了 NRCFTs 中探测器研究的若干未来方向，并讨论其可在从冷原子到低能中子实验等多种实验平台上被测量。

Abstract: Correlations in asymptotic fluxes provide one of the primary experimental means of studying physical systems. In the context of relativistic QFT, the formalization of such experiments in terms of detector operators has led to significant progress. Motivated by these successes, in this paper we initiate a study of detector operators in nonrelativistic conformal field theories (NRCFTs). In NRCFTs, fluxes are distributed in both angle and velocity, making the natural detector operator the energy flux differential in velocity, $\mathcal{E}_v(\hat{n})$. We provide non-perturbative definitions of these operators in NRCFTs, and study their symmetries and algebra. We calculate one-point functions of these detector observables in a variety of few and many body states of representative NRCFTs, including free fermions, as well as fermions at unitarity. In interacting three-body states, they exhibit a remarkably rich behavior reflective of the Efimov three-body wave-function, including the formation of an enhanced "jet" structure due to the presence of resonant interactions. We use these detectors to derive a nonrelativistic generalization of the Hofman-Maldacena conformal collider bounds by arguing for positivity of $\mathcal{E}_v(\hat{n})$, and imposing this in spin 1 states. We outline a number of future directions for the study of detectors in NRCFTs, and discuss how they can be measured in a variety of experimental platforms from cold atoms to low energy neutron experiments.

</details>


### [82] [Scale-Covariant Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38302)
*Nikolay Bobev,Pieter Bomans,Fridrik Freyr Gautason*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨由 D$p$-膜反作用产生的大尺度协变弦背景，利用全息方法研究其对偶强耦合非共形规范场论动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的在于揭示 D$p$-膜反作用所生成的标度协变背景结构，并借助该结构打开理解对偶强耦合非共形规范理论动力学的窗口。

Method: 在便利的 Weyl 标架中，度规含有 AdS 因子、膨胀子具有非平凡剖面；作者利用四维规范超引力、D$p$-膜包裹紧致流形及十维直接构造，提出多类超引力解并构建全息重整化群流。

Result: 发现多类新的超对称背景，包括由 D2-膜、紧致流形上 D$p$-膜及十维无限族构造的背景；并构建了这些背景间的全息重整化群流，计算了低能局域算符谱。

Conclusion: 全息方法确定了热自由能与标量两点函数等可观测量的形式，这些预言可在格点计算中检验。

Abstract: We explore a vast landscape of scale-covariant string backgrounds that arise from the backreaction of D$p$-branes. In a convenient Weyl frame the metric has an AdS factor and the dilaton has a non-trivial profile. This structure provides a notion of scale covariance that facilitates a holographic analysis and provides a window into the dynamics of the dual strongly coupled non-conformal gauge theory. We present different classes of supergravity solutions of this kind and discuss their dual field theory interpretation. We find several new supersymmetric backgrounds arising from D2-branes by utilizing four-dimensional gauged supergravity, D$p$-branes wrapped on compact manifolds, and infinite families constructed directly in ten dimensions. We construct holographic RG flows between some of the new supersymmetric backgrounds and compute the spectrum of low-lying local operators. Holography determines the form of observables such as the thermal free energy and scalar two-point functions, predictions which could be tested on the lattice.

</details>


### [83] [Quantum Gravity Principles for 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ Supergravity Theories and Asymptotic Hodge Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38307)
*Lukas Kaufmann,Timo Weigand*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维 N=2 超引力（supergravity）在矢量多重态模空间（vector multiplet moduli space）无穷远边界处的行为，借助极化霍奇结构（polarised Hodge structure）的实变分及数学新进展进行分类，并区分仅由超引力决定的性质与需量子引力 UV 完备（UV completion）的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清四维 N=2 超引力模空间无穷远处哪些性质仅源于超引力本身，哪些依赖于量子引力（quantum gravity）中的一致 UV 完备，从而识别超引力与量子引力约束的界限。

Method: 以支配 N=2 超引力矢量多重态模空间的极化霍奇结构实变分为分析基础，结合数学文献的最新进展，对无穷远退化（infinite distance degenerations）进行分类，并构造特定 Kähler 基以考察预势（prepotential）的三重耦合。

Result: 所得无穷远退化分类在规范耦合（gauge couplings）层面与“退紧化到五维或六维极限”或“弱耦合弦极限”的解读一致；该结论推广了 Calabi–Yau 紧化中的既有结果，且对任意四维 N=2 超引力成立，无论是否存在 UV 完备。同时证明存在使预势三重耦合非负的 Kähler 基，与超引力的几何起源无关。

Conclusion: 提出四维 N=2 超引力与量子引力相容的两个非平凡约束：霍奇结构的整性（integrality）作为完备性假设（completeness hypothesis）的必要条件，以及模空间存在简单正规交叉紧化（simple normal crossing compactification）以保证弱耦合渐近引力对偶标架（gravitational duality frames）的相容性。

Abstract: We study the behaviour of general four-dimensional N=2 supergravity theories near boundaries at infinity of the vector multiplet moduli space, with a focus on a clear distinction between properties that follow from supergravity alone rather than from a consistent UV completion in quantum gravity. The basis for our analysis is the appearance of a real variation of polarised Hodge structure governing the vector multiplet moduli space of N=2 supergravity, combined with very recent advances in the mathematics literature. The resulting classification of infinite distance degenerations exactly agrees, at the level of gauge couplings, with a possible interpretation of the limits as decompactification limits to five or six dimensions or as weakly coupled string limits, as expected from a quantum gravity perspective. This observation extends previous results found in the context of string compactifications on Calabi--Yau varieties and, remarkably, holds for general four-dimensional N=2 supergravity theories regardless of a UV completion. We furthermore show the existence of a Kähler basis in which the triple couplings of the prepotential are non-negative, irrespective of a geometric origin of the supergravity theory. Finally we propose two non-trivial constraints for a four-dimensional N=2 supergravity to be compatible with quantum gravity: Integrality of the Hodge structure as a necessary condition for the completeness hypothesis and the existence of a simple normal crossing compactification of the moduli space to ensure compatibility of the weakly coupled asymptotic gravitational duality frames.

</details>


### [84] [Bootstrapping Weakly Broken Gauge Theories in (A)dS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38308)
*Daniel Baumann,Kurt Hinterbichler,Callum R. T. Jones,Nathan Meurrens*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文提出一种针对(反)德西特空间((anti-)de Sitter space)中弱破缺规范理论(weakly broken gauge theories)的自举方法(bootstrap approach)，通过积分相应Ward恒等式(Ward identity)导出含固定非局域贡献的赝荷守恒恒等式(pseudo-charge conservation identities)，并将其应用于杨-米尔斯理论、双AdS引力希格斯机制及德西特共形引力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象聚焦于对偶边界流(dual boundary current)守恒被双迹算符(double-trace operator)破坏的情形，旨在为弱破缺规范理论建立可施加一致性约束的边界自举框架。

Method: 对相应Ward恒等式进行积分，推导出包含三点函数(three-point functions)积分形式的固定非局域贡献的赝荷守恒恒等式，并将其作为允许理论空间的一致性条件。

Abstract: Energy transport across conformal interfaces is universal in two spacetime dimensions, but its higher-dimensional counterpart has remained largely unexplored. In particular, we show that the transmission is independent of the incidence angle and of the profile of the incident perturbation. We also derive bounds on transmission controlled by central-charge-like quantities characterizing the number of degrees of freedom of the theories on the two sides of the interface.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用固定角速度 Ω 的巨正则系综（grand canonical ensemble）下的双变量约化模型（two-variable reduced model）分析单转 Kerr–AdS₅ 黑洞鞍点；通过复 Hessian（complex Hessian）的 Takagi 奇异值判定软模，并使用 Airy 型与 Pearcey 型渐近近似，以及 KSW 相界约束。

Result: 小/大黑洞鞍点在临界点合并，对应一个 Takagi 奇异值为零；该合并在 |Ω|<1 全域内与 Hawking–Page 相变分离。对标准测度的约化积分，当牛顿常数 G 充分小时，热 AdS（thermal AdS）在每个实合并点的复邻域内一致占优，故该邻域内配分函数无零点，尽管局部由 Airy 型行为描述。Fisher 零点（Fisher zeros）反而在 Hawking–Page 共存线附近以 O(G) 间距堆积，源于热 AdS 与大黑洞鞍点的干涉；实准欧几里得 Kerr–AdS₅ 族满足严格的渐近 KSW 相界，合并点位于允许域内。在固定角动量扩展系综中，两个合并点组合成尖点，并给出依赖围道的 Pearcey 近似。

Conclusion: 结果表明确切的局部鞍点合并并不必然意味着配分函数零点或全局相变；Takagi 模、相变与 Fisher 零点在复黑洞鞍点结构中具有可区分的角色。

Abstract: We study the complex saddle structure of singly rotating Kerr--AdS$_5$ black holes using a two-variable reduced model in the grand canonical ensemble at fixed angular velocity, $Ω$. The small and large black hole saddles merge at a critical point where one Takagi singular value of the complex Hessian vanishes, identifying the soft mode associated with the merger. This merger remains distinct from the Hawking--Page transition throughout the physical domain $|Ω| < 1$. For the reduced integral with the standard measure, we show that thermal AdS dominates uniformly in a complex neighborhood of each real merger point when Newton's constant $G$ is sufficiently small. Consequently, this neighborhood contains no zeros of the partition function, even though the local saddle merger is described by Airy-type behavior. The Fisher zeros instead accumulate near the Hawking--Page coexistence line, with spacing of order $G$, through interference between thermal AdS and the large black hole saddle. We also show that the real quasi-Euclidean Kerr--AdS$_5$ family satisfies a strict asymptotic Kontsevich--Segal--Witten (KSW) phase bound for $|Ω| < 1$, with the merger point lying inside the allowed domain. In the fixed angular momentum extended ensemble, two such merger points combine into a cusp with a contour-dependent Pearcey approximation. These results distinguish local saddle degeneracies from global phase coexistence and clarify the different roles of Takagi modes, phase transitions, and zeros of the partition function in the complex black hole saddle structure.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文利用外尔张量（Weyl tensor）的位置空间分析，在四维渐近平坦时空中推导大质量粒子散射的经典对数软引力子定理（logarithmic soft graviton theorem），并独立验证了 Laddha 与 Sen 的频率空间结果。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 位置空间分析可在经典层面重现领头与对数软引力子定理，并为相应渐近对称性提供匹配关系层面的支撑，从而独立验证频率空间方法的结果。

Abstract: We derive the classical logarithmic soft graviton theorem for the scattering of massive particles in four-dimensional asymptotically flat spacetime using a position-space analysis of the Weyl tensor. Starting from the Iyer-Damour multipole solution in harmonic gauge, we obtain an infinite tower of antipodal matching relations across spatial infinity for all five Newman-Penrose Weyl scalars, at linear order in $G$ and for the leading matter-induced logarithms at order $G^2$. We connect the relation relevant to the logarithmic soft theorem to the radiative-gauge formulation of Boschetti-Campiglia and Compère-Robert. We then compute the required asymptotic fields directly from scattering data, including matter contributions and nonlinear effects responsible for graviton drag. Combining these results with the Newman-Penrose evolution equations yields position-space proofs of the classical leading and logarithmic soft graviton theorems, independently confirming the frequency-space derivation of the latter originally performed by Laddha and Sen.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞视界与宇宙学视界此前多在各自框架下讨论软定理，缺乏统一描述。该工作旨在将两类视界纳入同一软结构表述，揭示不同视界几何背后的共同性质。

Abstract: We develop a unified formulation of leading soft photon theorems for nondegenerate static spherical horizons. By fixing the photon mode normalization from the horizon symplectic form, we obtain a common angular soft factor whose sign follows from the boundary orientation. We determine the geometric coefficients in the local angular and momentum representations for a chosen radial normalization. The charge balance includes horizon endpoint data and contributions from other boundaries. This formulation brings black hole and cosmological horizons into a common description. Applying the same construction to Yang-Mills fields yields the corresponding tree-level soft gluon theorem. These results identify the common soft structure underlying different horizon geometries.

</details>


### [96] [Soliton like solutions of $λφ^4$ theory in de Sitter space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39459)
*D. V. Diakonov*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注德西特时空中带四次自相互作用标量场的经典解结构，试图以嵌入空间方法系统刻画不同因果类型所对应的解族，并考察其物理效应（经典作用量有限性、真空跃迁及信号传播）。

Method: 采用拟设（ansatz）$\varphi=F(X\cdot\xi)$，其中 $X_\alpha$ 为嵌入空间（ambient space）坐标，通过常矢量 $\xi$ 的因果性质（类时/类空）对解进行分类；并计算所得孤子（soliton）的经典作用量，同时评估 $D=4$ 全局真空跃迁解背景下的延迟格林函数（retarded Green's function）。

Result: 对类时（time-like）$\xi$，解全局正则（globally regular），描述围绕极小值的全局振荡或全局真空跃迁；对类空（space-like）$\xi$，解在全局坐标片中奇异，但在开切片（open slicing）中正则，在庞加莱坐标片（Poincaré patch）中对应收缩气泡（contracting bubbles）。经典作用量仅在时空维度 $D \le 5$ 时有限；$D=4$ 全局真空跃迁解具有辐射物态方程（equation of state）$w=1/3$。

Conclusion: 在 $D=4$ 全局真空跃迁解上，延迟格林函数相对真空背景上的格林函数出现增长的尾部（growing tail），表明该跃迁在晚期放大了信号。

Abstract: We study classical solutions of a scalar field with quartic potential in de Sitter space-time. Using the ansatz $φ=F(X\cdotξ)$, via embeding coordinate $X_α$ of the ambiend space we find several families of analytic solutions classified by the causal character of the constant vector $ξ$. For time-like $ξ$, the solutions are globally regular and describe either global oscillations around a minimum or global vacuum transition; for space-like $ξ$, they are singular in the global patch but regular in the open slicing. In the Poincaré patch they correspond to contracting bubbles. We compute the classical actions of these solition which are finite only in spacetime dimensions $D \le 5$. We show that the $D=4$ global vacuum transition solution has the radiation equation of state $w=1/3$. In $D=4$ global vacuum transition solution, the retarded Green's function acquires a growing tail as compared to the Green's function on top of vacuum, implying that the transition amplifies the signal at late times.

</details>


### [97] [Dispersion relation, propagation features and canonical gauge structure of GNLED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39470)
*Abedennour Dib,José A. Helayël-Neto,Tomislav Prokopec,Alessandro D. A. M. Spallicci,Youssef Temmam*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究带非动力学背景场的广义非线性电动力学（Generalised Non-Linear Electrodynamics, GNLED）的传播性质与正则规范结构，通过场重定义将背景诱导的导数耦合转化为质量型贡献，证明其对应一个有质量间隙的非极化模式，并保持两个物理自由度与形变阿贝尔U(1)规范对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解非动力学背景场如何影响非线性电动力学的光子传播与规范结构，尤其是背景诱导的导数耦合能否被有效视为质量项，以及这种有效质量是否会引入额外极化自由度，从而检验理论的物理自洽性。

Method: 从理论的二次光子扇区（quadratic photon sector）出发，实施场重定义，将背景诱导的导数耦合转化为由矢量 V_μ=∂_μ α/α 描述的质量型贡献，其中 α 由拉格朗日量对不变量 F 的导数在背景处取值定义；进而推导色散关系（dispersion relation）与传播子（propagator），并进行完整哈密顿量分析。

Result: 在常量背景下，对纯类空 V_μ，传播模式满足 ω²=||k||²+||V||²，定义有效质量标度 m_eff=|V| 与有限静止频率，相速度超光速而群速度亚光速，呈典型有质量间隙模式；有质量极点位于横向扇区，纵向奇异性与规范固定相关；哈密顿量分析给出两个第一类约束（first-class constraints）和仅两个物理自由度，有效质量未引入额外极化。

Conclusion: 理论表现为形变的阿贝尔 U(1) 对称性（deformed Abelian U(1) symmetry）规范理论；对于纯类空背景，哈密顿量为正定，表明该方法在物理上是自洽的，有效质量不改变物理自由度数目。

Abstract: We investigate the propagation properties and canonical gauge structure of Generalised Non-Linear Electrodynamics (GNLED) in the presence of a non-dynamical background field. Starting from the quadratic photon sector of the theory, we perform a field redefinition that converts the background-induced derivative coupling into a mass-like contribution characterized by the vector \(V_μ=\partial_μα/α\) where $α= \sqrt{\left.\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}}{\partial \mathcal{F}}\right|_B}$. For a constant background, we derive the corresponding dispersion relation and show that, for a purely space-like \(V_μ\), the propagating mode obeys \(ω^2=\|\vec k\|^2+\|\vec V\|^2\), defining an effective mass scale \(m_{\rm eff}=|\vec{V}|\) and a finite rest frequency. The resulting phase and group velocities exhibit the characteristic dispersive behaviour of a gapped mode, with subluminal group velocity and superluminal phase velocity. We then construct the propagator and show that the massive pole resides in the transverse sector, while the longitudinal singularity is associated with gauge fixing. A complete Hamiltonian analysis yields two first-class constraints and only two physical degrees of freedom, demonstrating that the effective mass does not introduce an additional polarisation. The associated gauge transformation is a deformed Abelian \(U(1)\) symmetry. Finally, we show that the Hamiltonian is positive definite for purely space-like backgrounds.

</details>


### [98] [Nonperturbative Dyson--Schwinger equations in QCD: a first approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39506)
*Vladimir Dzhunushaliev,Vladimir Folomeev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于无限非微扰戴森–施温格方程组（nonperturbative Dyson--Schwinger equations）的非微扰量子化（nonperturbative quantization）方案，并针对静态情形给出该无限方程组的首个截断近似，讨论了规范选取、非线性狄拉克方程导致的质量间隙以及“量纲嬗变”等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非微扰量子化问题，无限戴森–施温格方程组难以直接求解，因此需要构造可行的截断近似方案。

Conclusion: 作者论证，出现在有限截断中并导致“量纲嬗变”的“闭合常数”（closure constants）在过渡到无限戴森–施温格方程组时应仍然保留；并将非微扰量子化与湍流的随机理论（stochastic theory of turbulence）进行了类比。

Abstract: We consider a procedure for nonperturbative quantization based on an infinite system of nonperturbative Dyson--Schwinger equations. We propose a first approximation for truncating this infinite system of equations in the static case. The main ideas underlying this approximation are as follows: (a) the two-point Green's functions of vacuum gauge fields are factorized by introducing scalar fields; (b) in the non-vacuum case, the degrees of freedom of the $SU(3)$ gauge field can be divided into ``almost classical'' and ``almost quantum'' degrees of freedom; (c) the four-point Green's functions of the gauge fields are represented as bilinear combinations of two-point Green's functions; (d) an approximation for the three-point Green's function describing the interaction between quarks and gauge fields is proposed, which leads to the splitting of the original Dirac equation for the quark-field operators into two equations, one describing the expectation value of the fermion field and the other acquiring a nonlinear term and serving to describe a condensate composed of sea quarks bound by the vacuum gauge field. We point out that the emergence of a nonlinear Dirac equation within this approximation may lead to a mass gap in the energy spectrum of solutions of the corresponding systems of equations. The issue of the emergence of ``dimensional transmutation'' within this approximation is also discussed. We provide arguments in favor of the statement that the ``closure constants'' appearing in the finite truncation and giving rise to ``dimensional transmutation'' should survive in the transition to the infinite system of Dyson--Schwinger equations. An analogy between nonperturbative quantization and the stochastic theory of turbulence is also discussed.

</details>

*Dhruva K. S*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明酉超对称洛伦兹共形场论（unitary supersymmetric Lorentzian conformal field theory）的光线代数（light-ray algebra）包含一个普适的超对称广义 $\mathfrak{bms}$ 代数。作者构造了由超对称流、$R$-对称流与能动张量支撑于余维度一零超曲面（co-dimension one null hypersurface）上的平行光线算符，并在 $d=3,4$ 中验证其分次对易关系，进而给出平均零能条件（ANEC）的超对称推导。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于揭示共形场论光线代数中可能存在的普适超对称结构，并借助该结构为平均零能条件（averaged null energy condition, ANEC）提供基于超对称的独立论证。该构造仅依赖整体超对称与微观因果性、幺正性、洛伦兹不变性等基本原理，因此可适用于强耦合理论。
