Result: 结果表明，酉超对称洛伦兹共形场论的光线代数确实包含普适的超对称广义 $\mathfrak{bms}$ 代数，且分次对易关系在 $d=3,4$ 中得到验证。作为推论，利用零面（null sheet）上的局域超对称代数可证明平均零能算符为正算符，从而给出 ANEC 的超对称推导；该结果在自由无质量超对称理论中得到验证。

Conclusion: 结论指出，局域超对称可保证 ANEC 得到满足，作者进一步给出论证支持该结论可推广至一般超对称量子场论。该框架因不依赖微扰或弱耦合假设，对强耦合超对称理论同样适用。

Abstract: We show that the light-ray algebra of unitary supersymmetric Lorentzian conformal field theories contains a universal supersymmetric generalized $\mathfrak{bms}$ algebra. We construct parallel light-ray operators using the supersymmetry current, $R-$symmetry current and the stress tensor supported on a co-dimension one null hypersurface and determine their graded commutators in $d=3,4$ to verify this claim. Our construction only relies on global supersymmetry and fundamental physical principles such as microcausality, unitarity and Lorentz invariance and thus applies to strongly coupled theories. As a corollary, we show that the averaged null energy operator is a positive operator using the local supersymmetry algebra on the null sheet, providing a supersymmetric derivation of the averaged null energy condition (ANEC). Finally, we verify our results in free massless supersymmetric theories and present arguments to support that the local supersymmetry continues to ensure the ANEC is satisfied even in general supersymmetric quantum field theories.

</details>


### [100] [Fractional Superfluid Vortices and Worldsheet Wess--Zumino--Witten Models in Two-Color QCD and $Sp(2N)$ Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39760)
*Muneto Nitta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究双色QCD（two-color QCD）及一般赝实（pseudoreal）规范理论中有限重子密度下的超流涡旋，揭示其分数化最小涡旋、零模局域化及体WZW项的降维结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限重子密度下，反对称双夸克凝聚（antisymmetric diquark condensate）允许携带分数重子相位绕数的涡旋，但此类涡旋的零模结构、核心对称性增强及其与体Wess–Zumino–Witten（WZW）项的关系尚缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 在双色QCD及更一般的赝实规范理论中，取偶数味数N_f=2m，分析重子相位绕数ν=1/m的分数最小涡旋；考察嵌入相互正交味平面的涡旋的手征作用量因子化，并追踪偏离对角味零模在分离与重合时的行为；进而推导Sp(2N)规范理论中基本费米子情形下体WZW项的降维归属。

Result: 最小涡旋各局域一个Sp(2)≃SU(2)核心扇区；正交味平面涡旋的手征作用量在领头阶精确因子化；偏离对角味零模在有限间距下不可归一化，但在重合时局域化，使可归一化扇区由[Sp(2)]^m增强至Sp(2m)。n个重合最小涡旋的丛集携带Sp(2n)_N WZW理论，味对称涡旋（重子相位绕数ν为正整数）携带Sp(2m)_{Nν}；单位绕数时对应Sp(2m)_N映射，WZW不动点处中心荷（central charge）为c=Nm(2m+1)/(N+m+1)。

Conclusion: 结果给出涡旋到Sp(2m)_N的映射并提示一种更普遍的非超对称四维/二维对应（nonsupersymmetric four-dimensional/two-dimensional correspondence）。

Abstract: We study superfluid vortices in two-color QCD and more general pseudoreal gauge theories with even flavor number $N_f=2m$ at finite baryon density. The antisymmetric diquark condensate admits fractional minimal vortices with baryon-phase winding $ν=1/m$, corresponding to $1/m$ of the conventional circulation quantum, each localizing an $Sp(2)\simeq SU(2)$ core sector. For vortices embedded in mutually orthogonal flavor planes, the leading-order chiral action factorizes exactly. Off-diagonal flavor zero modes are non-normalizable at finite separation but become localized at coincidence, enhancing the normalizable sector from $[Sp(2)]^m$ to $Sp(2m)$. For fundamental fermions of an $Sp(2N)$ gauge theory, the bulk Wess--Zumino--Witten (WZW) term descends compatibly: a cluster of $n$ coincident minimal vortices carries an $Sp(2n)_N$ WZW theory, while a flavor-symmetric vortex with positive integer baryon-phase winding $ν$ carries $Sp(2m)_{Nν}$. At unit winding this yields the vortex map to $Sp(2m)_N$, with central charge $c=Nm(2m+1)/(N+m+1)$ at the WZW fixed point, and suggests a broader nonsupersymmetric four-dimensional/two-dimensional correspondence.

</details>


### [101] [AI for BMN Strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39769)
*Min-xin Huang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 借助人工智能（Artificial Intelligence, AI）工具，重新审视此前关于 IIB 型弦理论（type IIB string theory）pp-wave 背景下 BMN（Berenstein-Maldacena-Nastase）弦研究中若干数学表述明确的问题；这些问题具有重要物理意义，部分取得显著进展，部分仍未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 所研究的数学问题具有重要物理意义，涉及高亏格临界超弦振幅（higher-genus critical superstring amplitudes）的计算以及宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）可能的估计。

Method: 在 AI 工具辅助下，对先前 BMN 弦研究导出的、数学上已明确表述的问题进行重新审视与推进。

Result: 在部分问题上取得显著进展（significant progress），而另一些问题仍处于未解决状态（remain unresolved）。

Conclusion: AI 辅助的问题重访在部分议题上有效推进了研究，但仍有开放问题有待解决。

Abstract: With the assistance of AI (Artificial Intelligence) tools, we revisit some mathematically well-formulated problems from our previous studies of BMN (Berenstein-Maldacena-Nastase) strings on type IIB string theory pp-wave background. These mathematical problems have important physical implications, relating to the computations of higher-genus critical superstring amplitudes, possible estimate of the cosmological constant. We make significant progress on some problems, while others remain unresolved.

</details>


### [102] [Light non-vacuum BMS$_3$ blocks at order $1/c_{M}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39893)
*Boyang Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文推导了四轻外部原初场（generic weights）与非真空交换情形下 BMS$_3$ 最高权块（highest-weight BMS$_3$ blocks）的 $1/c_{M}$ 修正闭合表达式，并证明至该阶仅需全局扇区及含单个非全局生成元的后代项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 BMS$_3$ 块在 $1/c_{M}$ 阶的修正尚缺乏闭合表达式，且需厘清渐近对称代数下计算所需的最小算符截断。研究同时旨在与 Virasoro 情形及超相对论收缩建立一致性检验。

Method: 在 $c_L$ 与所有算符权重固定下，证明至该阶仅需全局扇区及含一个非全局生成元 $L_{-n}$ 或 $M_{-n}$（$n\geq2$）的后代项，从而将计算约化为全局块及其导数，并对非零交换 boost 权重 $\xi_{p}$ 求和。对成对相同外部算符，采用 shadow representation 中归一化三点函数引入 boundary gravitons 独立复现修正；并借助 Virasoro 块的形式化超相对论收缩（含一个翻转手征表示）作进一步检验。

Result: 获得 $1/c_{M}$ 修正的闭合表达式，其贡献对 $\xi_{p}\neq0$ 已重求和；对成对相同外部算符，通过 boundary gravitons 独立复现该修正，同时保持交换两点函数固定，并复现已知的 $1/c$ 阶非真空 Virasoro 修正；超相对论收缩给出相同修正，且证明收缩与按固定后代层级提取 $1/c_{M}$ 系数可交换。

Conclusion: $\xi_{p}=0$ 的退化交换（degenerate exchange）需独立的商构造（quotient construction），不能由一般块取光滑极限得到，相关讨论置于附录。

Abstract: We derive a closed expression for the $1/c_{M}$ correction to highest-weight BMS$_3$ blocks with four light external primaries of generic weights and non-vacuum exchange. With $c_L$ and all operator weights held fixed, we prove that the global sector and descendants containing exactly one non-global generator $L_{-n}$ or $M_{-n}$ with $n\geq2$ suffice through this order. This truncation reduces the intrinsic calculation to global blocks and their derivatives, whose contributions we resum for nonzero exchanged boost weight $ξ_{p}$. For pairwise identical external operators, we independently reproduce the correction through the introduction of boundary gravitons in the normalized three-point functions in the shadow representation, while keeping the exchanged two-point function fixed. The analogous prescription also reproduces the known non-vacuum Virasoro correction at order $1/c$. For the same external configuration, a formal ultra-relativistic contraction of Virasoro blocks with one flipped chiral representation provides a further check: it yields the same correction, and we prove that contraction commutes with extraction of the $1/c_{M}$ coefficient at each fixed descendant level. The degenerate exchange with $ξ_{p}=0$ requires a separate quotient construction and is not obtained as a smooth limit of the generic block, which we discuss in the appendix.

</details>


### [103] [Large gaps and BTZ entropy in modular spectra with positive integer degeneracies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39930)
*Chi-Ming Chang,Reiko Liu,Wen-Jie Ma*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过递归修复（recursively repair）Virasoro 真空的 Maloney--Witten--Keller 模完成（modular completion），构造了具有唯一真空、离散能级与正整数简并度的模不变环面配分函数（modular-invariant torus partition function）。该构造在每个充分大的实数中心荷 c 下实现特定的原初维度间隙（primary dimension gap），并重现 Bekenstein--Hawking 熵及其修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于构造同时具备唯一真空、离散能级与正整数简并度的模不变配分函数，以检验纯引力/全息中 BTZ 鞍点（BTZ saddle）预测与严格模不变谱之间的相容性。摘要未进一步展开物理背景，此处仅依据所述构造目标归纳。

Method: 方法为：对 Virasoro 真空的 Maloney--Witten--Keller 模完成进行递归修复，通过精确模修复（exact modular repairs）与有限阶矩匹配（finite moment matching）逐带离散化连续谱（discretize successive spectral bands），同时保持已构造能级不变并控制收敛性。

Result: 在 c_L=c_R=c、a=(c-1)/12 下，构造给出原初维度间隙 Δ_1=(1+κ)a（充分小的固定 κ>0）以及 Δ_1=a+δ（任意固定 δ≥0），对每个充分大的实数 c 成立；所有非真空原初场满足 h,ḣ≥(c-1)/24。在固定 δ 族中，可选择谱使其态密度在固定非负归一化紧支光滑核平滑后，逐固定有限阶 1/c 匹配单个微扰 BTZ 鞍点的平滑预测，计数包含全部自旋与 Virasoro 后代。其对数在每固定正 E/c（E=Δ-c/12）处重现 Bekenstein--Hawking 熵及其修正，包括热 AdS 主导正则系综的 0<E<c/12 区间。

Conclusion: 结论为：存在此类模不变配分函数构造，可在保持唯一真空与正整数简并度的同时实现受控的原初间隙谱，并在固定阶 1/c 与固定 E/c 意义下与单一微扰 BTZ 鞍点的态密度预测及 Bekenstein--Hawking 熵修正相符，覆盖热 AdS 主导区域。

Abstract: We construct modular-invariant torus partition functions with a unique vacuum, discrete energy levels and positive integer degeneracies by recursively repairing the Maloney--Witten--Keller modular completion of the Virasoro vacuum. Exact modular repairs and finite moment matching discretize successive spectral bands while preserving earlier levels and controlling convergence. For $c_L=c_R=c$ and $a=(c-1)/12$, the construction realizes primary dimension gaps $Δ_1=(1+κ)a$ for sufficiently small fixed $κ>0$, and $Δ_1=a+δ$ for any fixed $δ\ge0$, at every sufficiently large real $c$. Every nonvacuum primary satisfies $h,\bar h\ge(c-1)/24$. In the fixed-$δ$ family, spectra can be chosen whose densities of states, smoothed with a fixed nonnegative normalized smooth kernel of compact support, match the correspondingly smoothed prediction of a single perturbative BTZ saddle through every fixed finite order in $1/c$. The count includes all spins and Virasoro descendants. Its logarithm reproduces the Bekenstein--Hawking entropy and its corrections at every fixed positive $E/c$, where $E=Δ-c/12$. This includes $0<E<c/12$, where thermal AdS dominates the canonical ensemble.

</details>


### [104] [Greybody factors of large Gauss-Bonnet d-dimensional black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39944)
*Filipe Moura,João Rodrigues*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在高频极限下解析计算了含高斯-博内（Gauss-Bonnet）修正的 d 维大球对称黑洞的灰体因子（greybody factors），涵盖程函极限（eikonal limit）与强阻尼（highly damped）两种情形，并给出完整的透射与反射散射系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将灰体因子的解析计算推广至含高斯-博内修正的高维球对称黑洞，并在高频极限下系统处理不同频率区间的散射问题。

Method: 采用解析方法，在高频极限下计算 d 维大球对称高斯-博内黑洞的灰体因子；同时考虑程函极限（频率实部远大于虚部）与强阻尼情形（虚部远大于实部），涉及引力子与试探标量场（test scalar fields）的发射，并涵盖张量（tensorial）、矢量（vectorial）与标量（scalar）引力扰动。

Result: 计算得到了完整的透射与反射散射系数（transmission and reflection scattering coefficients）。在强阻尼极限下，张量、矢量与标量三类引力扰动给出相同结果；而在程函极限下三者结果不同。

Conclusion: 结果表明，引力扰动类型的差异仅在程函极限下显现，而在强阻尼极限下消失，体现了两种高频区间中散射行为的结构性差异。

Abstract: We compute analytically greybody factors for d-dimensional large spherically symmetric black holes with Gauss-Bonnet corrections in the high frequency limit. Our calculations include both the eikonal limit, where the real part of the frequency of the scattered wave is much larger than the imaginary part, and the highly damped case,where the imaginary part of the frequency is much larger than the real part, addressing the emission of gravitons and test scalar fields, and yielding full transmission and reflection scattering coefficients. We consider tensorial, vectorial and scalar gravitational perturbations, obtaining the same result for the three cases in the highly damped limit, but not in the eikonal limit.

</details>


### [105] [Nilpotent Symmetries and Conserved Charges: BRST-Quantized Version of the 4D Abelian 2-Form Gauge Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40025)
*R. P. Malik*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维自由阿贝尔2-形式规范理论的BRST量子化版本，推导其在幂零对称变换下不变性的修正Noether守恒荷，并证明其线性独立性及CF型约束的代数起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BRST量子化的自由阿贝尔2-形式规范理论中，Noether荷在幂零对称变换下的不变性问题尚未被系统处理，且电荷间线性独立性的证明需要从代数结构出发加以澄清。

Method: 在(3+1)维自由阿贝尔2-形式规范理论的BRST框架下，讨论无穷小、连续、离壳幂零的(反-)BRST与(反-)co-BRST对称变换，推导相应Noether守恒荷，并利用对称变换与其生成元（即守恒荷）之间的数学关系构造修正荷。

Result: 原始Noether荷在幂零对称变换下并不不变；经系统修正后的荷具有不变性，且两类荷彼此反对易，从而证明其线性独立；同时由守恒荷的代数结构导出(反-)BRST与(反-)co-BRST不变的Curci-Ferrari型约束。

Conclusion: 通过对称变换与守恒荷生成元的深层数学联系，可获得BRST量子化阿贝尔2-形式理论中不变Noether荷的修正形式，并揭示荷的线性独立性与CF型约束的代数来源。

Abstract: We focus on the Becchi-Rouet-Stora-Tyutin (BRST) quantized version of the four (3 + 1)-dimensional (4D) free Abelian 2-form gauge theory and (i) discuss its infinitesimal, continuous and off-shell nilpotent (anti-)BRST and (anti-)co-BRST symmetry transformations, and (ii) derive the corresponding Noether conserved (anti-)BRST and (anti-)co-BRST charges. We demonstrate that these charges are not invariant under the above nilpotent symmetry transformations. We systematically derive the modified versions of the above Noether charges which are found to be invariant under the above symmetry transformations. As a consequence, both types of the above charges anticommute with each-other which proves their linear independence. We also derive the (anti-)BRST and (anti-)co-BRST invariant Curci-Ferrari (CF) type restrictions from the algebraic structures of the Noether conserved charges. In all the above proofs and derivations, we take into account the deep mathematical relationship that exists between the infinitesimal and continuous symmetry transformations and their generators as the Noether conserved charges.

</details>


### [106] [Quantum corrections to the Casimir effect in a scalar Hořava-Lifshitz theory with rough plates at low temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40081)
*Claudio Bórquez,Byron Droguett*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在 (3+1) 维 Hořava-Lifshitz 型理论框架下，研究大质量自相互作用实标量场在粗糙 Dirichlet 边界下的 Casimir 效应，利用有效作用量与广义 ζ-函数方法给出重整化有效势、拓扑质量与 Casimir 能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将 Casimir 效应拓展至具有各向异性标度（anisotropic scaling）的 Hořava-Lifshitz 型理论，并同时纳入边界粗糙度（boundary roughness）与温度效应，以考察这些因素对量子真空效应的联合影响。

Method: 采用有效作用量（effective action）与广义 ζ-函数（generalized ζ-function）方法，在低温区（low-temperature regime）推导含各向异性标度、边界粗糙度与温度的重整化有效势（renormalized effective potential），并在单圈阶（one-loop order）确定拓扑质量、在双圈阶（up to two loops）计算 Casimir 能量。

Result: 在无质量极限（massless limit）下，当 z>1 取奇数值时，拓扑质量与双圈 Casimir 贡献出现红外发散（infrared divergences），这与缺少内禀质量标度（intrinsic mass scale）相关；而单圈 Casimir 能量保持有限。当 z=1 时，在零温度与零粗糙度极限下可还原标准相对论性 Dirichlet 结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明 Hořava-Lifshitz 型各向异性标度会显著改变 Casimir 效应的圈阶结构：拓扑质量与双圈贡献对奇 z 值敏感并出现红外发散，而单圈 Casimir 能量仍然稳定；同时该框架在 z=1 极限下与已知相对论结果一致，构成对标准 Casimir 理论的自洽推广。

Abstract: We investigate the Casimir effect of a massive, self-interacting real scalar field with quartic coupling in a $(3+1)$-dimensional Hořava-Lifshitz-type theory, subject to rough boundaries obeying Dirichlet boundary conditions. Using the effective action and generalized $ζ$-function approach, we derive the renormalized effective potential, incorporating anisotropic scaling, boundary roughness, and temperature in the low-temperature regime. We determine the topological mass at one-loop order and the Casimir energy up to two loops. In the massless limit, the topological mass and the two-loop Casimir contribution exhibit infrared divergences for odd values $z>1$, associated with the absence of an intrinsic mass scale, while the one-loop Casimir energy remains finite. For $z=1$, the standard relativistic Dirichlet result is recovered in the zero-temperature and vanishing-roughness limits.

</details>


### [107] [Lie Algebra Saddles in the IKKT Matrix Model and Criteria for the Emergence of Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40183)
*Henry Liao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在 IKKT 矩阵模型中，将十维 Hermitian 矩阵实现有限维实李代数的经典解（李代数鞍点）分类，导出时空签名（signature）的判据：空间可半单，时间必可解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IKKT 矩阵模型中时空的维度、几何与度规签名（metric signature）须由矩阵动力学自发涌现，因此需要刻画哪些经典解能够给出特定签名的涌现时空，并给出时间涌现的判据。

Method: 将经典解限定为“李代数鞍点（Lie-algebraic saddles）”：十个 Hermitian 矩阵构成有限维实李代数的酉表示。在此类中运动方程化为代数上对称双线性形式 $h$ 的单一代数方程，涌现时空的签名由 $h$ 读出；分析借助复化代数的单性带来的 Casimir 阻碍，以及半单代数需经由不同单理想间的耦合获得鞍点。

Result: 复化代数为单（simple）时，Casimir 阻碍只允许 $h=0$，解释了模糊球（fuzzy spheres）与膨胀宇宙解为何需要质量项或红外调节器。半单代数（semisimple algebras）仅在单理想相互耦合时才有鞍点，六维才首次出现，十二维起才可达所有签名。至多十一维的非退化 Lorentzian 鞍点（故十维模型的所有 Lorentzian 背景）必含非零可解根（solvable radical）；半单部分唯一非平凡可能是纯类空的 Lorentz 代数 $\mathfrak{so}(1,3)$，每个类时方向均含根分量。半单部分自十维起才对根作用；十维时根须为阿贝尔并承载 Weyl 旋量表示，十一维才出现非阿贝尔根。

Conclusion: 李代数鞍点分类给出时间涌现的必要结构条件：空间可为半单，而时间必须是可解的（space can be semisimple; time must be solvable）。

Abstract: In the IKKT matrix model, the dimension, geometry, and metric signature of spacetime must emerge from the matrix dynamics. We classify the Lie-algebraic saddles of the model: classical solutions in which the ten Hermitian matrices realize a finite-dimensional real Lie algebra in a unitary representation. For this class the equations of motion collapse to a single algebraic equation for a symmetric bilinear form $h$ on the algebra, and the signature of the emergent spacetime can be read off from $h$. Two facts control the outcome. First, when the complexified algebra is simple, a Casimir obstruction allows only $h=0$; this explains why fuzzy spheres and the expanding-universe solutions need mass terms or infrared regulators. Second, semisimple algebras admit saddles only through couplings between distinct simple ideals; such saddles first appear in six dimensions and reach every signature only from twelve. These facts yield criteria for the emergence of time. Every nondegenerate Lorentzian saddle in at most eleven dimensions---hence every Lorentzian background of the ten-dimensional model---has a nonzero solvable radical. The only nontrivial possibility of the semisimple part is the Lorentz algebra $\mathfrak{so}(1,3)$, which is purely spacelike, and every timelike direction has a component in the radical. When the semisimple part is nontrivial, it acts on the radical only from ten dimensions on; at ten dimensions, the radical must be abelian and must carry the Weyl-spinor representation, which solves the Lorentzian equations of motion; a nonabelian radical first appears in eleven dimensions. As a result, space can be semisimple; time must be solvable.

</details>


### [108] [The classical limit of the Magnus expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40291)
*Andreas Brandhuber,Graham R. Brown,Sebastien MacDonald,Paolo Pichini,Gabriele Travaglini,Pablo Vives Matasan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文揭示了量子场论中 Magnus 展开（Magnus expansion）的经典极限与排列的 Malvenuto-Reutenauer Hopf 代数（Malvenuto-Reutenauer Hopf algebra）之间的联系。通过 Schwinger 参数化，证明超经典贡献在积分前即抵消，经典 Magnus 振幅与世界线量子场论预期一致，并由此给出一般 Murua 系数的新公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于建立量子场论经典极限与组合 Hopf 代数之间的深层联系，并理解经典散射（如引力与标量 QED）中相关的传播子结构。经典 Magnus 振幅与世界线量子场论之间的关系此前缺乏明确的代数解释。

Method: 采用三次标量理论（cubic scalar theory），描述大质量粒子与无质量标量量子的耦合，并对大质量传播子使用 Schwinger 参数化（Schwinger parametrisation）。在此理论中于树图层次（tree level）计算 Magnus 振幅的经典极限，并借助 Hopf 代数分析非连通图（disconnected diagrams）的抵消。

Result: 证明所有超经典贡献（hyperclassical contributions）在 Schwinger 固有时被积函数层面、积分之前即完全抵消；经典 Magnus 振幅与世界线量子场论（worldline quantum field theory）的预期精确吻合。两项结果均依赖于非连通图的抵消，该抵消与 Hopf 代数中伴随第一欧拉投影子（adjoint first Eulerian projector）的作用相关，其湮灭与非连通贡献相联系的洗牌积（shuffle products）。由此得到一个用更简单的有向链系数表达一般 Murua 系数的新公式。

Conclusion: 世界线图包含一般的有向树拓扑（directed-tree topologies），而场论图仅包含有向链（directed chains），二者之间的对应关系导出了 Murua 系数的新表达。此外，观察到 Magnus 振幅与支配多重 Wilson 线（multiple Wilson lines）指数化的网混合矩阵（web-mixing matrices）之间存在启发性的联系。

Abstract: We uncover a connection between the classical limit of the Magnus expansion in quantum field theory and the Malvenuto-Reutenauer Hopf algebra of permutations. We study a cubic scalar theory describing a massive particle coupled to massless scalar quanta, which captures the propagator structure relevant to classical scattering in gravity and scalar QED. Using a Schwinger parametrisation of the massive propagators, we compute the classical limit of Magnus amplitudes at tree level in this theory. First, we prove that all hyperclassical contributions cancel at the level of the Schwinger proper-time integrand, before integration. Then, we show that the classical Magnus amplitude precisely matches expectations from worldline quantum field theory. Both results rely on cancellations of disconnected diagrams which we relate to the Hopf algebra, specifically to the action of the adjoint first Eulerian projector, which annihilates the shuffle products associated with disconnected contributions. Moreover, the worldline diagrams include generic directed-tree topologies, whereas the field-theory diagrams only include directed chains. The correspondence we establish between the two therefore leads to a new formula for generic Murua coefficients in terms of simpler directed chain coefficients. Finally, we observe a suggestive connection between Magnus amplitudes and the web-mixing matrices governing the exponentiation of multiple Wilson lines.

</details>


### [109] [Four-loop beta function in N=2 supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory, dimensional reduction, and treatment of $γ_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40326)
*M. A. Bezuglov,B. A. Kniehl,V. N. Velizhanin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在四圈（four loops）水平上检验降维方案（dimensional-reduction scheme）与γ₅处理方法的实现细节，通过计算N=2超对称杨-米尔斯理论（N=2 supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory）的贝塔函数（beta function）来验证相关技术，该贝塔函数在一圈以上已知为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N=2超对称杨-米尔斯理论的一圈以上贝塔函数已知为零，使其成为检验降维方案和D维γ₅ Dirac矩阵处理方式的理想试金石（testbed）。同时，四圈水平上标准模型（Standard Model）规范耦合贝塔函数计算所涉及的实现细节需要严格验证。

Method: 采用降维方案，以图形化（diagrammatically）方式在四圈阶计算N=2超对称杨-米尔斯理论的贝塔函数。在γ₅的处理上使用读出点方法（reading-point method），该方法因便利性而更受青睐。

Result: 在四圈阶，读出点方法的具体实现细节开始变得重要；作者因此对先前为四圈标准模型规范耦合贝塔函数计算所主张的这些实现问题进行了严格验证，并确认贝塔函数在四圈阶仍为零。

Conclusion: 四圈计算验证了降维方案及γ₅读出点方法实现细节的有效性，支持了此前在标准模型四圈规范耦合贝塔函数计算中所主张的处理方式。

Abstract: Adopting the dimensional-reduction scheme, we diagrammatically compute, at four loops, the beta function of N=2 supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory, which is well known to vanish beyond one loop. This provides a formidable testbed for both the treatment of the problematic $γ_5$ Dirac matrix in D dimensions and the implementation of the dimensional-reduction scheme. In the use of the reading-point method of $γ_5$, being preferable for convenience, specific details of implementation start to matter at four loops. We thus rigorously validate these implementation issues, which have previously been advocated for the calculation of the gauge-coupling beta functions at four loops in the Standard Model.

</details>

### [110] [Hidden Angular Momentum Loop Currents in Symmetric Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38317)
*Rule Yi,Vincent P. Flynn,Benedetta Flebus*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出环流（loop current）可源于晶格本身，由量子与热涨落维持，而非仅由电子集体动力学驱动。在保持时间反演、空间反演与四重旋转对称的二维弹性晶格中，邻近格胞出现方向相反的电流环，而各格点平均角动量为零。结果表明弹性几何可将涨落组织为持续角动量环流，无需电子序、磁序、外驱或自发对称破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环流序（loop-current order）此前多用于解释非常规电子相，晶格通常被视为被动的集体动力学载体。作者意在检验环流能否源自晶格自身，并由此拓展平衡态环流物理至晶格动力学范畴。

Method: 建立角动量交换的微观描述，将格点间转移与晶格回复力矩（crystalline restoring torques）分离，从而说明在对称性保持的平衡态中环流如何涌现。具体在保持时间反演（time-reversal）、空间反演（inversion）与四重旋转对称性的二维弹性晶格中进行刻画。

Result: 在相邻格胞（plaquettes）上发现方向相反的电流环，同时每个格点的平均角动量消失。这些电流由声子真空（phonon vacuum）的零点涨落（zero-point fluctuations）维持，并在有限温度下持续存在。其起源被追溯至局域对称性降低环境中的方向性弹性相互作用，而更高晶体对称性将电流组织为补偿式环流图样。

Conclusion: 研究将平衡态环流物理扩展至晶格动力学，表明弹性几何可将涨落组织成持续的角动量环流。该机制不需要电子序或磁序、外部驱动或自发对称破缺（spontaneous symmetry breaking）。

Abstract: Loop-current order has been invoked to explain unconventional electronic phases, with the crystal lattice usually regarded as a passive host for the underlying collective dynamics. Here we show that circulating currents can originate in the lattice itself, sustained by quantum and thermal fluctuations. We develop a microscopic description of angular momentum exchange that separates transfer between sites from crystalline restoring torques and reveals how circulation can emerge in a symmetry-preserving equilibrium state. In a two-dimensional elastic lattice preserving time-reversal, inversion, and fourfold rotational symmetries, we uncover opposite current loops on neighboring plaquettes while the mean angular momentum vanishes at every site. These currents are sustained by zero-point fluctuations in the phonon vacuum and persist at finite temperature. We trace their origin to directional elastic interactions in locally symmetry-lowered environments, with the higher crystal symmetry organizing the resulting currents into a compensated pattern of circulation. Our results extend the physics of equilibrium loop currents to lattice dynamics, revealing that elastic geometry can organize fluctuations into persistent angular momentum circulation without electronic or magnetic order, external driving, or spontaneous symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [111] [Three-Dimensional Shankar Skyrmions in Frustrated Antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38624)
*Vladyslav M. Kuchkin,Ricardo Rama-Eiroa,Carlos Saji,Alvaro S. Nunez,Roberto E. Troncoso*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文为受阻手性反铁磁体（frustrated chiral antiferromagnets）建立三维 Shankar 斯格明子（Shankar skyrmion）的连续场理论，并给出亚稳态有限尺寸 π₃(SO(3)) 孤子的形成条件与动力学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维非阿贝尔拓扑织构（non-Abelian topological textures）的微观起源与稳定机制尚不清晰，需要统一的理论框架来解释其有限尺寸平衡态。

Method: 构建连续理论并结合 Derrick–Hobart 标度分析（scaling analysis），考察交换作用、Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya 相互作用（DMI）、各向异性与阻挫的平衡；提出两条微观路径：非共线反铁磁体（noncollinear AFs）中的内禀旋转参考系序，以及振幅软化 Néel 场（Néel field）的 S³ 扩展；并进行数值极小化与有效动力学分析。

Result: Derrick–Hobart 分析表明各能量项可相互平衡从而确定有限平衡尺寸；S³ 织构投影到含一对空间分离、电荷相反的 Bloch 点（Bloch points）的 Néel 场；数值极小化得到亚稳态单极子（monopole）织构，有效动力学识别出相干呼吸振荡（breathing oscillations）作为其特征有限频率集体模。

Conclusion: 研究提供了 Shankar 斯格明子静态与动力学的微观框架，并指出受阻手性反铁磁体是三维非阿贝尔拓扑织构的有前景宿主材料。

Abstract: We formulate a continuum theory for three-dimensional (3D) Shankar skyrmions in frustrated chiral antiferromagnets (AFs) and derive the conditions for metastable finite-size $π_3(SO(3))$ solitons. A Derrick--Hobart scaling analysis shows that exchange, Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interaction (DMI), anisotropy, and frustration can balance to set a finite equilibrium size. We identify two microscopic routes to this common topological and stabilization framework: intrinsic rotation-frame order in noncollinear AFs and an $\mathbb{S}^3$ extension of an amplitude-softened Néel field. In the latter, the smooth four-component texture projects onto a Néel field containing a spatially separated pair of oppositely charged Bloch points. Numerical minimization yields metastable monopole textures, while the effective dynamics identifies coherent breathing oscillations as their characteristic finite-frequency collective mode. Our results provide a microscopic framework for the statics and dynamics of Shankar skyrmions and identify frustrated chiral AFs as promising hosts of 3D non-Abelian topological textures.

</details>


### [112] [Optical observation of interlayer spin correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38825)
*Akiyoshi Park,Pranshoo Upadhyay,Andrey Grankin,Emil Viñas Boström,Mahdi Ghafariasl,Hassan Alnatah,Masoud Mohammadi-Arzanagh,Gautam Nambiar,Beini Gao,Alireza Alvandi,Sakthi Rajmano Madhan Kumar,Ghadah Alshalan,Isaac Sherwood,Mahmoud Jalali Mehrabad,You Zhou,Arun Ramanathan,Xavier Roy,Angel Rubio,Mohammad Hafezi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作首次实现层间自旋关联（interlayer spin correlations）的直接光学探测：在范德华反铁磁体（van der Waals antiferromagnet）CrSBr 中利用双磁振子拉曼散射（two-magnon Raman scattering），结果与无拟合参数的微观自旋波模型高度吻合，并可通过激子共振增强信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层间自旋关联决定层状材料的磁基态与跨界面自旋输运，是二维磁性最基本的量之一，但长期缺乏直接测量手段，处于实验不可及的状态。

Method: 采用双磁振子拉曼散射作为探测手段，对象为范德华反铁磁体 CrSBr；并将实验与不含任何拟合参数的微观自旋波模型（spin-wave model）进行对比，同时发展激子介导（exciton-mediated）的增强方案。

Result: 双磁振子信号仅在反铁磁态出现，当磁场将晶体驱动至铁磁态时消失；实验与微观自旋波模型在无拟合参数条件下高度一致；将激光调至激子共振附近时，因激子的大振子强度，信号增强约十倍。

Conclusion: 磁振子对拉曼光谱（magnon-pair Raman spectroscopy）为范德华磁体中层间自旋关联提供了直接的光学窗口，并可拓展至扭转双层（twisted bilayers）与近邻耦合异质结构（proximity-coupled heterostructures）。

Abstract: Spin correlations govern numerous collective behaviors of quantum materials, underpinning exotic phenomena such as unconventional superconductivity and topological magnetism. In layered materials, the interlayer spin correlation is important because it characterizes the magnetic ground state and determines spin transport across the interface. Yet interlayer spin correlations have remained hard to measure directly, leaving one of the most basic quantities of two-dimensional magnetism out of experimental reach. Here we provide the first direct optical probe of interlayer spin correlations, using two-magnon Raman scattering in the van der Waals antiferromagnet (AFM) CrSBr, in remarkable agreement with a microscopic spin-wave model without any fitting parameter. Moreover, the two-magnon channel switches on only in the AFM state and vanishes when a magnetic field takes the crystal to a ferromagnetic state. We furthermore establish an exciton-mediated variant of the technique, where tuning the laser near the exciton resonance enhances the signal roughly tenfold due to the exciton's large oscillator strength. Magnon-pair Raman spectroscopy thus opens a direct optical window into interlayer spin correlations in van der Waals magnets, extendable to twisted bilayers and proximity-coupled heterostructures.

</details>


### [113] [Spin Birefringence and Spin Polarized Transmission across p-wave Altermagnetic Heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39174)
*Yong-Mei Zhang,Zhi-Ping Niu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文基于哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）与波函数匹配方法，研究N/AM界面及N/AM/N异质结（heterojunction）中的电子输运与自旋极化（spin polarization），揭示支路分裂、全内反射（total internal reflection）及周期振荡传输特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索交替磁体（altermagnet, AM）异质结构中的自旋选择性输运机制，为自旋电子学（spintronics）器件的实验研究提供理论基础。

Method: 方法为针对三个区域的哈密顿量提出一般波函数，并构建线性方程组，据此求解反射系数、透射系数及自旋极化。

Result: 电子由普通金属入射至AM时被散射并分裂为+支与_支，以不同折射角进入AM区，+支可能发生全内反射，临界角由入射条件与AM材料性质决定；在异质结中，自旋向上与向下电子传播方向相同但透射概率不同，透射与极化随结长周期性变化，交换场（exchange field）与自旋劈裂强度影响显著，自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）影响较小。

Conclusion: 可通过调节AM材料参数获得优化系统，本研究为自旋选择性输出及自旋电子学器件的实验研究奠定理论依据。

Abstract: This paper investigates electron transport properties across N/AM interface or N/AM/N heterojunction. Based on Hamiltonians in the three regions, general wave functions are proposed and combined linear equations are constructed. Reflection and transmission coefficients as well as spin polarizatioin of the altermagnet heterostructure are obtained. When electron incident from normal metal to altermagnet, it is scattered and split into + branch and _ branch transmitting into altermagnet zone with separate refractive angles. Total internal reflection might happen to + branch. Critical angle of total reflection is determined by incident conditions and properties of AM material. In the heterostructure case, spin-up and spin-down electrons transmitted with same propagation directions but different transmission probabilities. Transmission and polarization periodically change with length of the junction. Exchange field and spin-splitting strength have pronounced effect on spin-polarized transmittance while the effect of spin-orbit coupling is quite small. Optimized system can be found by tuning parameters of the AM material. This study will lay the theoretical groundwork for experimental research on spin-selective outputs and spintronic devices.

</details>


### [114] [Giant PhotoMagnetoDiode Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39435)
*V. L. Korenev,S. A. Tarasenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在0.1 T磁场下观测到整流比高达10的光磁二极管效应（photomagnetodiode effect），表现为光电流对电压的二极管式非互易依赖，并由双极性漂移与表面复合理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在报道并解释在外磁场中出现的强非互易（nonreciprocity）光电输运现象，即在磁场下光电流对偏压呈现类似二极管的依赖关系。

Method: 在低温、带间光激发（interband photoexcitation）条件下，对高质量VPE生长的GaAs样品施加外磁场并测量光电流与电压的关系，同时观测光致发光行为。

Result: 在0.1 T磁场下获得高达10的巨整流比（rectification ratio）；光导在电场与磁场极性同时反转时保持不变；光致发光（photoluminescence）中观测到更高的不对称比。

Conclusion: 该现象可由电场与磁场中载流子的双极性漂移（ambipolar drift）结合快速表面复合（surface recombination）理论很好地描述。

Abstract: We report the observation of a photomagnetodiode effect with a giant rectification ratio of 10 at a magnetic field of 0.1 T. The effect consists of a diode-like dependence of the photocurrent on the applied voltage that emerges in an external magnetic field. Such a pronounced nonreciprocity is observed in high-quality VPE-grown GaAs samples at low temperatures at interband photoexcitation. The photoconductivity is invariant upon reversing the polarity of both electric and magnetic fields. Similar behavior with even higher asymmetry ratio is observed in photoluminescence. The findings are well described by the theory of ambipolar drift of carriers in electric and magnetic fields together with fast surface recombination.

</details>


### [115] [Emergent Quantum Geometric Phases in Holey Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39500)
*Pablo Canteli,Yuriko Baba,Juan Salvador-Sánchez,Jorge Estrada-Álvarez,Ana Pérez-Rodríguez,Carlos Sánchez-Sánchez,Vito Clericò,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Francisco Domínguez-Adame,Rafael A Molina,Enrique Diez,Elena Díaz,Mario Amado*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用超高精度图形化反点阵（antidot lattices）封装单层石墨烯（monolayer graphene）开展磁输运测量，系统调控晶格尺寸后观察到经典可公度性特征层级与Brown-Zak振荡，并证明可在无扭转、无晶格失配、无多层堆叠条件下几何化生成类莫尔电子谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯等二维材料中电子密度的周期性调制可显著改变能谱与输运性质，但要在保持材料本征电子性质的同时实现可控、可设计的周期性调制仍具挑战，尤其难以在单层体系中以几何方式获得类莫尔（moiré）物理。

Method: 采用封装单层石墨烯与超高精度、光刻定义的周期性反点阵结构，保留材料本征电子性质；通过系统调节晶格尺寸，开展磁输运测量以研究可公度性（commensurability）与超晶格（superlattice）现象。

Result: 观测到源自回旋轨道（cyclotron orbits）多重经典轨迹的经典可公度性特征层级，表现为超出传统单轨道图像的展宽电阻峰；其上叠加显著的Brown-Zak振荡，源自磁通量子与工程化Bravais晶格单胞间的量子可公度性；并可直接测量体系的内禀几何相位（geometric phase），实现磁场周期性与光刻周期性图案的精确匹配。

Conclusion: 纳米图形化石墨烯（nanopatterned graphene）可作为干净、可调、可扩展的平台，通过按需实空间设计实现与探索莫尔物理，无需依赖扭转、晶格失配或多层堆叠。

Abstract: In graphene and other two-dimensional materials, periodic modulations of the electron density can significantly alter the energy spectrum and transport properties. Here, we report magnetotransport measurements in encapsulated monolayer graphene with ultra-high-quality patterned periodic antidot lattices that preserve the intrinsic electronic properties of the material. This lithographically defined platform enables controlled access to commensurability and superlattice phenomena at length scales otherwise difficult to achieve. By systematically tuning the lattice dimensions, we reveal a hierarchy of classical commensurability features arising from cyclotron orbits with comparable radii that follow multiple classical trajectories, resulting in broadened resistance peaks beyond the conventional single-orbit picture. Superimposed on these features, we observe pronounced Brown-Zak oscillations arising from the quantum commensurability between the magnetic flux quantum and the unit cell of the engineered Bravais lattices. We demonstrate that the intrinsic geometric phase of our system is directly measurable and show a precise matching of the magnetic field periodicity to the lithographic periodic patterning, where moiré-like electronic spectra can be geometrically generated in single-layer graphene without the need for twist, lattice mismatch, or multilayer stacking. Our results establish nanopatterned graphene as a clean, tunable, and scalable platform for realizing and exploring moiré physics through on-demand real-space design

</details>


### [116] [Anisotropic wavevector-dependent damping of thickness-quantized magnons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39612)
*Tamara Azevedo,Rostyslav O. Serha,Yannik Kunz,Matthias R. Schweizer,Vitaliy I. Vasyuchka,Mathias Weiler,Andrii V. Chumak,Burkard Hillebrands,Mikhail Kostylev,Alexander A. Serga*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究利用高分辨参量不稳定谱学技术，在微米厚钇铁石榴石（yttrium iron garnet, YIG）薄膜中探测厚度量子化的自旋波（spin wave），波长低至约百纳米，揭示了随波矢各向异性变化的磁振子阻尼（magnon damping）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁振子阻尼是决定自旋波输运与多模磁振子系统非线性动力学的关键因素。然而既有描述多依赖“模式无关的有效参数”假设，这一简化会掩盖依赖于传播几何与模式剖面的波长相关弛豫过程，从而限制对真实阻尼行为的理解。

Method: 采用高分辨参量不稳定谱学（parametric-instability spectroscopy）探测微米厚 YIG 薄膜中的厚度量子化自旋波，波长可达约一百纳米；通过观测不稳定阈值随磁场的依赖行为，并与偶极—交换理论（dipole–exchange theory）进行对比分析。

Result: 不稳定阈值随磁场呈现规则的锯齿状依赖，源于离散厚度模式之间的切换；与偶极—交换理论的对比揭示出各向异性的、依赖于波矢的阻尼，其随模式数增加而增大，且对面内与面外波矢分量呈现不同的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 研究结果否定了模式无关有效阻尼参数的简化假设，表明磁振子阻尼具有显著的波矢各向异性与模式依赖性，为多模磁振子系统的自旋波输运与非线性动力学描述提供了更精细的实验依据。

Abstract: Magnon damping is a key factor governing spin-wave transport and nonlinear dynamics of multimode magnon systems. However, many descriptions rely on the assumption of an effective mode-independent parameter, which can mask wavelength-dependent relaxation processes that depend on propagation geometry and mode profile. Here, we employ high-resolution parametric-instability spectroscopy to probe thickness-quantized spin waves with wavelengths down to about a hundred nanometers in micrometer-thick yttrium iron garnet films. The instability threshold exhibits a regular sawtooth dependence on the magnetic field, arising from switching between discrete thickness modes. Comparison with dipole--exchange theory reveals anisotropic wavevector-dependent damping that increases with mode number and depends differently on the in-plane and out-of-plane wavevector components.

</details>


### [117] [Magnetization relaxation of interacting chains of nanomagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39811)
*Denis Ledue,Renaud Patte,Francois Vernay,Hamid Kachkachi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文研究一维偶极耦合纳米磁体链中从单粒子行为到集体行为的磁化动力学（magnetization dynamics）交叉，结合解析方法与时间量化蒙特卡洛（time-quantified Monte Carlo, TQMC）模拟，给出弛豫速率与磁化弛豫曲线的半解析表达，并揭示由偶极稳定化空间不均匀性驱动的场控交叉机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解偶极耦合纳米磁体组装体中，单粒子翻转与集体行为之间的转变机制，并寻求可高效预测其弛豫行为的理论框架。

Method: 方法上采用基于朗格理论（Langer's theory）的中高阻尼（intermediate-to-high damping, IHD）解析方法，以及时间量化蒙特卡洛（TQMC）模拟，对一维具单轴各向异性（uniaxial anisotropy）的偶极耦合纳米磁体链进行对比分析。

Result: 主要结果包括：(i) 计入（弱）偶极相互作用（dipolar interactions）的弛豫速率闭式表达式；(ii) 相互作用链磁化动力学 m(t) 的双指数半解析公式；(iii) 与 TQMC 模拟的系统比较，在较广参数范围内吻合良好。

Conclusion: 分析揭示出由偶极稳定化（dipolar stabilization）空间不均匀性驱动的、场控的从均匀（类宏自旋，macrospin-like）翻转到边缘成核传播（edge-nucleation propagation）的交叉；所推导表达式为预测偶极耦合纳米磁体组装的弛豫行为提供了计算高效的框架。

Abstract: We investigate the magnetization dynamics crossover from single-particle to collective behavior in a one-dimensional chain of dipolar-coupled nanomagnets with uniaxial anisotropy. Using both an intermediate-to-high damping (IHD) analytical approach based on Langer's theory and time-quantified Monte Carlo (TQMC) simulations, we derive and validate semi-analytical expressions for the relaxation rate and the magnetization relaxation curves. Our main results include: (i) a closed-form expression for the relaxation rate accounting for (weak) dipolar interactions, (ii) a two-exponential semi-analytical formula for the magnetization dynamics $m(t)$ of an interacting chain, and (iii) a systematic comparison with TQMC simulations, showing good agreement for a wide range of parameters. The analysis reveals a field-controlled crossover from uniform (macrospin-like) reversal to edge-nucleation propagation, driven by the spatial inhomogeneity of dipolar stabilization. The derived expressions provide a computationally efficient framework for predicting the relaxation behavior of dipolar-coupled nanomagnetic assemblies.

</details>


### [118] [Multi-Branch Transport in a Back-gated WS$_2$ Transistor at Deep-Cryogenic Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39878)
*Megan Powell,Vilas Patil,Hazel Neill,Stephen O'Sullivan,Paul K. Hurley,Lida Ansari,Farzan Gity,Alessandro Rossi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究了背栅多层二硫化钨（WS₂）晶体管在深低温（低至20 mK）下的电学性能，观察到超过10⁵的开关电流比与可重复的“肩状”开启特征，并用多有效导电支路并联的唯象模型加以描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料（two-dimensional materials）被视为突破传统半导体技术工作极限的电子应用候选者，其中过渡金属硫族化合物（transition-metal dichalcogenides）适用于场效应晶体管（field-effect transistor）操作，而二硫化钨（tungsten disulphide, WS₂）在低温工作方面尤具潜力。因此有必要考察其在深低温区的电学表现。

Method: 对背栅（back-gated）多层WS₂晶体管在深低温（deep cryogenic temperature，低至20 mK）下进行电学性能测量，并针对低温开启特性提出包含多个并联有效导电支路（effective conduction branches）的唯象模型（phenomenological model）进行描述。

Result: 器件在整个低温区间保持强栅极可调性（strongly gate-tunable），有效开关电流比（on/off current ratio）超过10⁵；低温开启特性呈现可重复的肩状特征（shoulder-like features）。

Conclusion: 多层WS₂晶体管在深低温下仍具备良好栅控与高开关比，其可重复的肩状开启行为可由多有效导电支路并联的唯象模型解释，显示WS₂在低温电子学应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Two-dimensional materials are promising candidates for electronic applications beyond the operating limits of conventional semiconductor technologies. Within this class, transition-metal dichalcogenides offer attractive properties for field-effect transistor operation, with tungsten disulphide (WS$_2$) emerging as a particularly promising material for operation at cryogenic temperatures. Here, we investigate the electrical performance of a back-gated multilayer WS$_2$ transistor at deep cryogenic temperature, down to 20 mK. The device remains strongly gate-tunable throughout the cryogenic regime, with an effective on/off current ratio exceeding $10^{5}$. Most notably, the low-temperature turn-on characteristics exhibit reproducible shoulder-like features, which we describe using a phenomenological model comprising multiple effective conduction branches operating in parallel.

</details>


### [119] [Thermal magnon transport in FM/AFM bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39947)
*Moumita Kundu,Ulrich Nowak*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了铁磁体（ferromagnet）与反铁磁体（antiferromagnet）双层结构中磁振子（magnon）的传播，通过施加空间变化的温度分布探测自旋塞贝克效应（spin Seebeck effect），发现反铁磁体中可由热触发自旋电流，并为磁振子手性选择性自旋输运的异质结构设计提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息处理技术的进步依赖自旋电子学（spintronics），其中磁态可作为数据存储与传输的高效载体。块体反铁磁体（antiferromagnet）因服从时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）通常不表现此类自旋电流，因此需要探索异质结构中的新机制。

Method: 在铁磁体与反铁磁体构成的双层（bilayer）结构中进行磁振子传播的理论研究；通过引入空间变化的温度分布来探测自旋塞贝克效应，由此产生局部磁振子激发及从热区到冷区的连续磁振子流，并以非平衡磁振子积累（non-equilibrium magnon accumulation）量化该磁振子流。

Result: 基于磁振子模式的手性（chirality），识别出磁振子从铁磁体进入反铁磁体或反向传播时的特定约束条件；关键发现是在反铁磁体中观察到热触发的自旋电流（spin current），该现象在服从时间反演对称性的块体反铁磁体中通常缺失。

Conclusion: 研究结果可为面向磁振子手性选择性自旋输运（magnonic chirality-selective spin transport）的异质结构（heterostructure）设计提供重要见解。

Abstract: Progress in information processing relies on spintronics, where magnetic states serve as efficient carriers for data storage and transfer. In this work, we theoretically study magnon propagation in a bilayer composed of a ferro-magnet and an antiferromagnet. For this purpose, we probe the spin Seebeck effect by introducing a spatially varying temperature profile. This generates a local magnon excitation and a continuous magnon flux from hot to cold regions which we quantify through the resulting non-equilibrium magnon accumulation. Based on the chirality of these modes, we identify specific constraints for magnon modes traveling either from the ferromagnet into the antiferromagnet or vice versa. A key finding is the observation of a thermally triggered spin current in the antiferromagnet, a phenomenon typically absent in bulk antiferromagnets that obey time-reversal symmetry. These results provide important insights into the design of heterostructures for magnonic chirality-selective spin transport.

</details>


### [120] [Atomic-Scale Imaging of Lattice Relaxation and Topological Flat Bands in Helical Trilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39986)
*Shudan Jiang,Zonglin Li,Yu Gu,Liang Liu,Dandan Guan,Yaoyi Li,Hao Zheng,Canhua Liu,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Shengwei Jiang,Xiaoxue Liu,Zhiwen Shi,Guorui Chen,Jinfeng Jia,Tingxin Li,Can Li,Shiyong Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究结合扫描近场光学显微镜与低温扫描隧道显微镜，对螺旋三层石墨烯（helical trilayer graphene, HTG）进行实空间研究，直接观测到超莫尔晶格弛豫、平坦能带及畴壁一维边界态，并实验确定魔角约为1.9°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HTG作为高度可调的莫尔量子材料（moire quantum material），具有强电子关联（electronic correlations）与非平庸能带拓扑（band topology），但其原子尺度晶格结构与局域电子性质此前基本未被探索。

Method: 采用扫描近场光学显微镜（scanning near-field optical microscopy）与低温扫描隧道显微镜（low-temperature scanning tunneling microscopy）相结合的方式进行实空间研究，并系统改变扭转角（twist angle）。

Result: 直接成像了超莫尔晶格弛豫（supermoire lattice relaxation），揭示出被尖锐畴壁（domain wall）分隔的大三角畴以及由平滑变化边界连接的条纹畴；原子尺度谱学发现具有蜂窝状电子纹理（honeycomb electronic texture）的平坦能带以及受限畴壁的一维边界态；实验识别出魔角（magic angle）约为1.9°，显著大于理论预测的1.6°。

Conclusion: 结果在HTG中建立了晶格弛豫与平坦能带之间的直接微观联系。

Abstract: Helical trilayer graphene (HTG) has emerged as a highly tunable moire quantum material that hosts strong electronic correlations and nontrivial band topology. However, the atomic-scale lattice structure and local electronic properties have remained largely unexplored. Here we present a comprehensive real-space study of HTG using a combination of scanning near-field optical microscopy and low-temperature scanning tunneling microscopy. We directly image supermoire lattice relaxation, revealing large triangular domains separated by sharp domain walls, as well as stripe domains connected by smoothly varying boundaries. Atomic-scale spectroscopy uncovers flat bands with a honeycomb electronic texture and one-dimensional boundary states confined to domain walls. By systematically varying the twist angle, we identify a magic angle of approximately 1.9°, substantially larger than the 1.6 degree predicted by theory. Our results establish a direct microscopic link between lattice relaxation and flat bands in HTG.

</details>


### [121] [Fingerprints of unconventional pairing in superconductor-hole-gas heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40059)
*Serafim S. Babkin,Karsten Flensberg,Jeroen Danon,Maksym Serbyn*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究提出通过面内磁场依赖的超流刚度（superfluid stiffness）与自旋磁化率（spin susceptibility）的非解析行为，以及刚度张量场角依赖的傅里叶谐波（Fourier harmonics），来探测超导体/二维空穴气异质结中诱导的能隙结构与非常规配对（unconventional pairing）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导体与二维空穴气（two-dimensional hole gas）异质结是量子器件的潜力平台，但其内禀性质（如非常规配对）难以通过实验获取。

Method: 分析面内磁场（in-plane field）下超流刚度与自旋磁化率的依赖关系，并利用动力学电感（kinetic-inductance）测量获取刚度张量场角依赖的傅里叶谐波。

Result: 超流刚度与自旋磁化率的面内磁场依赖呈现非解析性（nonanalyticities），可编码空穴气中诱导的能隙结构；刚度张量场角依赖的傅里叶谐波可提供非常规配对与立方 Rashba 耦合（cubic Rashba coupling）的特征指纹。

Conclusion: 所提测量方案可作为探测该类异质结中非常规配对与自旋轨道耦合特征的可及实验手段。

Abstract: Superconductor and two-dimensional hole gas heterostructures are promising platforms for quantum devices, but their intrinsic properties, e.g., unconventional pairings, are hard to access experimentally. Here, we show that the in-plane-field dependence of the superfluid stiffness and spin susceptibility exhibits nonanalyticities encoding the induced gap structure in the hole gas. Additionally, Fourier harmonics of the stiffness tensor's field-angle dependence, accessible via kinetic-inductance measurements, provide fingerprints of unconventional pairing and cubic Rashba coupling.

</details>


### [122] [Topological Transverse Transport without a Gap in Critical Topological Flat Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40065)
*Chang-geun Oh,Tomoki Ozawa,Jun-Won Rhim*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文构建了临界拓扑平带（critical topological flat bands, CTFBs）在低温下的本征电学、热电与热霍尔响应理论。结果表明，宏观平带简并导致化学势出现奇异 Lambert-W 漂移，并以代数-对数层级修正取代有能隙陈绝缘体的激活式热保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子化霍尔输运（quantized Hall transport）依托体能谱隙（bulk spectral gap）隔离占据子空间并指数压制热偏差。新发现的临界拓扑平带（CTFBs）中平带与色散连续谱接触却仍具确定整数陈数（Chern number），但其有限温度横向输运性质此前完全未被探索。

Method: 发展 CTFBs 中本征电霍尔、热电霍尔与热霍尔响应的低温理论；分别在固定粒子数与外部钉扎化学势至平带能量两种情形下分析化学势行为与占据。

Result: 固定粒子数时，宏观平带简并迫使化学势发生奇异 Lambert-W 漂移，产生代数-对数低温修正层级：电霍尔为 T·ln(1/T)，热电霍尔为 T·[ln(1/T)]²，热霍尔电导率为 T·[ln(1/T)]³。若将化学势钉扎至平带能量，则任意非零温度下平带被锁定在半占据，阻碍 T→0⁺ 时完全填充拓扑基态的恢复。

Conclusion: 零温霍尔量子化（Hall quantization）并不需要体能谱隙，但指数级热保护则不可或缺地依赖于能隙。

Abstract: Quantized Hall transport is traditionally anchored to a bulk spectral gap, which isolates the occupied subspace and exponentially suppresses thermal deviations. Recently discovered critical topological flat bands (CTFBs) challenge this paradigm: an exactly flat band touches a dispersive continuum while retaining a well-defined integer Chern number. However, their finite-temperature transverse transport properties remain entirely unexplored. Here, we develop a low-temperature theory of the intrinsic electrical, thermoelectric, and thermal Hall responses in CTFBs. At fixed particle number, the macroscopic flat-band degeneracy forces a singular Lambert-$W$ drift of the chemical potential, generating an algebraic-logarithmic hierarchy of low-temperature corrections: $T\ln(1/T)$ for electrical Hall, $T[\ln(1/T)]^2$ for thermoelectric Hall, and $T[\ln(1/T)]^3$ for thermal Hall conductivity, replacing the activated thermal protection of a gapped Chern insulator. By contrast, externally pinning the chemical potential to the flat-band energy locks the flat band to half occupation at any nonzero temperature, obstructing the recovery of the fully filled topological ground state as $T\to0^+$. Our results establish that a bulk spectral gap is unnecessary for zero-temperature Hall quantization, but indispensable for its exponential thermal protection.

</details>


### [123] [Long-lived divergence from equilibrium of electrons, nuclear spins and lattice for a solid state ion trap at low temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40268)
*Guy Matmon,Manuel Grimm,Markus Mueller,Byron J. Villis,Andrew J. Fisher,Gabriel Aeppli*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究以掺 Ho³⁺ 的 LiYF₄ 为模型体系，利用光学方法建立电子-核能级图并追踪布居数，发现在低温下核自旋激发可不依赖声子扩散并实现平衡，且获得高于电子自旋与晶格的有效温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理与工程中的基本问题是平衡化（equilibration），即电子、核自旋、声子等子系统如何在自身及彼此之间达到平衡；能级交叉（level crossing）因可最大化纠缠（entanglement）进而加速弛豫，在耦合自由度动力学中具有特殊地位。

Method: 采用光学方法确定包含回避交叉（avoided crossing）与非回避交叉（unavoided crossing）的电子-核能级方案；以稀土离子 Ho³⁺ 掺杂于 LiYF₄ 盐中的体系为对象，通过横向于四方晶格长轴的外磁场调控量子涨落，并以扫描纵向场下能级布居数的监测来追踪系统状态。

Result: 低温下，不同实验方案产生显著不同的非平衡态；证据表明核自旋激发在无声音子协助下扩散并平衡，且倾向于获得高于电子自旋与更冷晶格的有效温度。

Conclusion: 热辅助隧穿（thermally assisted tunneling）理论排除了声子辅助隧穿的标准图像，转而提示能级反交叉处的快速动力学完全由核自旋扩散促成，为稠密多组分相互作用量子系统中的热化（thermalization）与相关慢弛豫现象提供了新认识。

Abstract: A fundamental problem in physics as well as engineering is equilibration. For example, the regulation of thermal and quantum fluctuations enables thermal and quantum annealing of complex systems. A key question is how different subsystems, such as electrons, nuclear spins and phonons equilibrate on their own as well as with each other. Level crossings play a special role in the dynamics of coupled degrees of freedom, for it is here that entanglement can be maximized to speed up relaxation. Here we use optical methods to establish the electronuclear level scheme, including avoided and unavoided crossings, and to examine equilibration of a rare earth ion (Ho$^{3+}$) in a salt (LiYF$_4$), a model system with quantum fluctuations which can be tuned via an external magnetic field transverse to the crystallographic long axis of the tetragonal host. We track the state of the system by monitoring the populations of the levels as a function of swept longitudinal fields, and discover that at low temperatures, depending on the experimental protocol, vastly different non-equilibrium states arise. We find evidence that nuclear spin excitations diffuse and equilibrate without the assistance of phonons, and tend to acquire higher effective temperatures than the electronic spins and the yet cooler lattice. A theory of thermally assisted tunneling rules out the standard scenario of phonon -assisted tunneling, and instead suggests that fast dynamics at level anti-crossings are facilitated entirely by nuclear spin diffusion. This provides a new understanding of thermalization and related slow relaxation phenomena in dense, multi-component interacting quantum systems.

</details>


### [124] [Quantization through Dissipation and the Optical Quantum Hall Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40337)
*Zhenisbek Tagay,Ahmed Abouelkomsan,Yugo Onishi,Adbhut Gupta,Loren Pfeiffer,Liang Fu,N. P. Armitage*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出理解整数量子霍尔效应（integer quantum Hall effect）量子化的新路径：直流霍尔电导的精确量子化不仅由规范不变性、边缘通道或体拓扑不变量解释，还隐藏于体材料的有限频率耗散电动力学中——杂质态贡献的微弱低频耗散项经Kramers-Kronig变换后才恢复量子化平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整数量子霍尔效应中霍尔电导σ_xy被固定为e²/h的整数倍，且独立于无序与相互作用，已有Laughlin规范论证、边缘通道图像与体拓扑不变量等多种解释，各自捕捉不同物理侧面，但缺少从有限频率耗散光响应角度出发的统一理解。

Method: 采用高精度太赫兹法拉第旋转（terahertz Faraday rotation）测量结合数值计算，考察回旋共振（cyclotron resonance）在Kramers-Kronig变换下能否给出量子化平台，并检验杂质态所贡献的低频耗散项的作用。

Result: 回旋共振单独存在时，经Kramers-Kronig变换无法得到量子化平台；只有当引入杂质态产生的微弱、低频、由拓扑强制的耗散贡献后，量子化才得以恢复。

Conclusion: 该动力学机制将直流电导值与有限频率光学响应联系起来，为通过光学霍尔效应（optical Hall effect）理解量子化提供了新途径，表明量子化的信息"隐于显处"地存在于耗散响应之中。

Abstract: The integer quantum Hall effect remains the most paradigmatic and remarkable example of exact quantization in condensed matter physics. Its Hall conductivity $σ_{xy}$ is fixed to $e^2/h$ times an integer to a precision limited only by measurement and independent of disorder or interactions. This robustness has several explanations, each capturing a strikingly different piece of the physics. In Laughlin's gauge argument, flux insertion pumps an integer charge between edges, so quantization follows from gauge invariance alone. The edge-channel picture instead attributes transport to chiral, ballistic 1D channels at the sample boundary, one per filled Landau level. Bulk arguments tie $σ_{xy}$ to a topological invariant of the disordered system, with extended states compensating exactly for the current not carried by localized ones. Here we demonstrate an additional route to understanding quantization, rooted in the finite-frequency dissipative electrodynamics of the bulk. Using high-precision terahertz Faraday rotation and numerics, we show that the cyclotron resonance alone does not give quantized plateaus under Kramers-Kronig transformation. Quantization is recovered only once a faint, low-frequency, topologically enforced dissipative contribution from impurity states is included. This dynamical mechanism, hiding in plain sight within the dissipative response, ties the DC value to the finite-frequency optical response and offers a new route to quantization through the optical Hall effect.

</details>


### [125] [Resonances control when multiterminal Josephson currents reduce to two-terminal couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40346)
*A. Barış Özgüler*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文检验多端约瑟夫森结（multiterminal Josephson junctions）中"成对近似"（pairwise approximation）的准确性，发现电流误差中位数达 8.0%–21.2%，约为能量误差的两倍；误差由接近费米能级、耦合三个及以上端口的正常区模式控制，通过栅极失谐可使其降至 1% 以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多端约瑟夫森结被视为拓扑安德列夫能带（topological Andreev bands）、多对超电流与可调电路元件的载体，其电流既可用两端耦合的成对网络建模，也可用四重态（quartets）等真正的多端过程描述。核心问题是成对描述的准确度如何、以及什么因素控制其误差。

Method: 采用平面结的微观散射模型（microscopic scattering model），将精确电流与最优成对近似（允许任意非正弦耦合）对比；在无序的三端、四端结系综中统计误差，并扩展至设计器件的十六端；通过栅极对单一正常态模式进行失谐（detuning），并结合解析单能级模型与有限能隙（finite-gap）计算进行验证。

Result: 成对项几乎捕获了全部能量变化，但电流误差中位数为 8.0%–21.2%，约为能量误差的两倍，原因在于电流对多端谐波（multiterminal harmonics）的权重更高。接近费米能级且耦合三个及以上端口的正常区模式会产生大的非成对电流；仅依据正常态性质选择并移动该模式，即可在无需拟合电流的情况下预测八个三端、四端器件的误差随栅极的变化。两个洁净器件的有限能隙计算确认误差从共振附近数十个百分点降至 1% 以下。

Conclusion: 平面结继承了量子点（quantum dots）中已知的共振与共隧穿（cotunneling）两种机制，且正常态性质可识别决定二者切换的模式；解析单能级模型给出了任意端口数下实现成对近似精度所需的充分失谐量。

Abstract: Multiterminal Josephson junctions, in which one weak link couples three or more superconductors, are studied as hosts of topological Andreev bands, multi-pair supercurrents and tunable circuit elements. Their currents are modeled both as pairwise networks of two-terminal couplings and through genuinely multiterminal processes such as quartets. We ask how accurate the pairwise description is and what controls its error. In a microscopic scattering model of planar junctions, we compare exact currents with the best pairwise approximation, which allows arbitrary nonsinusoidal couplings. In ensembles of disordered three- and four-terminal junctions, the pairwise terms capture nearly all of the energy variation, yet the median current error ranges from 8.0 to 21.2 percent, about twice the energy error, because currents weigh the multiterminal harmonics more strongly. Designed devices extend the comparison to sixteen terminals. We identify a mechanism that controls this error. A normal-region mode near the Fermi level that couples to three or more terminals produces large nonpairwise currents, and detuning it with a gate suppresses them. Shifting only this mode, selected from normal-state properties, predicts the gate dependence of the error in eight three- and four-terminal devices without fitting currents. Finite-gap calculations in two clean devices confirm a drop from tens of percent near resonance to below one percent. An analytic single-level model gives a sufficient detuning for pairwise accuracy at any terminal count. Planar junctions thus inherit the resonant and cotunneling regimes known from quantum dots, and normal-state properties identify the mode that selects between them.

</details>
