### [126] [Searching for BSM Experimental Signatures with Large Lagrangian Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38309)
*Ibrahim Elsharkawy,Victoria Knapp-Perez,Wahid Bhimji,Aishik Ghosh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: hAIthem 是一个将强化学习（reinforcement learning, RL）的自引导探索与大语言模型（large language model, LLM）的文献知识相结合的框架，用于在超越标准模型（Beyond the Standard Model, BSM）的高维理论参数空间中寻找未被现有约束排除的区域，并生成可检验的实验信号。在单暗标量多重态模型的受限空间中，其性能超过进化算法基线，并提出将晕无关的运动学比值应用于古探测器（paleo-detectors）等新颖观测组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BSM 物理搜索的主要瓶颈在于缺乏具有判别力的实验观测，而非理论描述的匮乏；暗物质即为典型例证，其引力证据难以在庞大理论空间中区分不同模型。探索可检验的模型信号空间有助于发现被忽视的实验可观测量并评估未来实验的价值。核心难点在于如何设计超出既有文献范围的模型信号搜索。

Method: 构建 RL 智能体，通过与一套现象学工具进行类"海战棋"（Battleship）博弈，学习识别理论高维参数空间中在某约束子集下尚未被排除的部分。智能体实现为 Large Lagrangian Model（LLaM），即一个自回归 transformer，读取词元化的拉格朗日量，并在约 10,000 个拉格朗日量的约 10 亿词元上大规模预训练，随后在实时环境中微调。框架进一步用已有工具计算的可观测量构建决策树以分离 RL 发现的区域，并将剩余简并区域交由一组相互竞争的 LLM 智能体生成现实信号。

Result: 在该概念验证中，RL 搜索优于进化算法基线，找到更多可行区域且物理多样性更高。在单暗标量多重态模型的受限空间内，hAIthem 提出了此前已研究可观测量的有趣组合，例如将晕无关的运动学比值（halo-independent kinematic ratio）应用于古探测器。

Conclusion: 将强化学习的自引导探索与 LLM 的文献派生知识相结合的 hAIthem 框架，能够有效探索 BSM 可检验模型信号空间，识别被忽视的实验观测量，并提示未来实验的潜在效用；其概念验证显示优于进化基线，并可产生跨观测手段的新颖组合。

Abstract: The search for physics Beyond the Standard Model (BSM) is generally limited not by the supply of theory descriptions but by the lack of discriminating experimental observations. A case in point is dark matter, where the overwhelming gravitational evidence only goes so far in distinguishing between models within a vast theory space. Exploring the space of testable model signatures may help identify overlooked experimental observables and indicate the utility of future experiments. A challenge is designing a search through model signatures outside what is found in the literature. Our primary contribution is hAIthem, a framework that combines the self-guided exploration of reinforcement learning (RL) with the broad literature-derived knowledge of LLMs. We build an RL agent that learns to find which portions of a theory's high-dimensional parameter space are not excluded under some subset of constraints by playing a Battleship-style "game" against a suite of phenomenology tools. The agent is built as a Large Lagrangian Model (LLaM), an autoregressive transformer that reads a tokenized Lagrangian, is pretrained at scale (here on ~1 billion tokens from ~10,000 Lagrangians), and is fine-tuned in a live environment. The framework then constructs a decision tree that separates RL-found regions using observables computed with established tools, and passes the remaining degenerate regions to a set of LLM agents that compete to produce realistic signatures. In this proof of concept, RL-search outperforms an evolutionary-algorithm baseline, finding more viable regions with greater physical diversity. In a restricted space of single dark scalar multiplet models, we find that hAIthem proposes interesting combinations of previously studied observables, such as the application of a halo-independent kinematic ratio to paleo-detectors.

</details>


### [127] [Constraints on Complex Electroweak WIMPs from Solar Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38316)
*Juan P. Luengas,Maxim Pospelov,Harikrishnan Ramani*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析电弱多重态（electroweak multiplet）标量与费米型暗物质的太阳俘获、能量损失与湮灭过程，将早期 Higgsino 结果推广至其他表示。结果显示高阶多重态行为自相似，IceCube 将质量劈裂（mass splitting）约束至 δ≳350 keV，从而排除大部分电弱暗物质模型作为 LZ 单次高反冲事件的解释，并指出大麦哲伦云诱导的高速尾部会改变该事件的偏好参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电弱多重态模型是粒子暗物质最可信的实现之一，其现象学由暗物质质量与质量劈裂两个参数支配；亚 MeV 区间的劈裂尤为重要，因为它允许大的非弹性散射截面（inelastic scattering cross section）。此前分析多集中于 Higgsino，需推广至更一般的表示以检验其普适性。

Method: 针对标量与费米型电弱多重态暗物质，系统分析其太阳俘获（solar capture）、能量损失与湮灭过程，将先前针对 Higgsino 的结果推广至其他电弱表示，并比较不同多重态（doublet 与更高表示）的行为差异。同时考虑热冻结（thermal freeze-out）与标准晕模型（Standard Halo Model）速度分布，并探讨大麦哲伦云（Large Magellanic Cloud）诱导的高速尾部对结果的影响。

Result: 双重态（doublet）情形较为特殊，而更高表示表现出自相似行为。对于更高多重态，IceCube 结果将质量劈裂约束至 δ≳350 keV，适用于所有直至 100 TeV 的暗物质质量。在热冻结与标准晕模型速度分布假设下，这排除了大部分电弱暗物质模型作为 LZ 合作组报告的单次高反冲事件（high-recoil event）来源的可能性。此外，大麦哲伦云诱导的高速尾部会将 LZ 事件偏好的劈裂值提高至太阳约束之上。

Conclusion: 电弱多重态暗物质在太阳中的俘获、能量损失与湮灭分析可对质量劈裂施加严格约束，其中 IceCube 的约束有效排除了大部分此类模型对 LZ 单次高反冲事件的解释；但若暗物质速度分布存在高速尾部（如大麦哲伦云所致），则 LZ 事件所偏好的劈裂可高于太阳约束，从而影响该排除结论的稳健性。

Abstract: One of the most convincing realizations of particle dark matter is provided by models based on electroweak multiplets. The lightest neutral state of such a multiplet can be a stable Majorana fermion or a real scalar, and its phenomenology is governed by two parameters: the dark matter mass and the mass splitting $\{m_χ,δ\}$. The sub-MeV range for $δ$ is particularly important as it allows for a large inelastic scattering cross section. In this paper, we analyze solar capture, energy loss, and annihilation of scalar and fermionic electroweak multiplet dark matter, generalizing earlier results for the Higgsino to other representations. We find that, while the case of a doublet is somewhat special, higher representations behave in a self-similar way. For the higher multiplets, IceCube results constrain the splitting to $δ\gtrsim 350$ keV for all dark matter masses up to $100$ TeV. Assuming thermal freeze-out and a Standard Halo Model velocity distribution, this rules out most models of electroweak dark matter as a possible origin of the single high-recoil event reported by the LZ collaboration. We also show that a high-velocity tail in the dark matter distribution, such as that induced by the Large Magellanic Cloud, raises the splitting preferred by the LZ event above the solar bounds for many of the multiplets.

</details>


### [128] [QCD corrections to light-quark dipole sensitivity via transverse spin asymmetries at the Electron-Ion Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38535)
*Sally Dawson,Zhite Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究未来电子-离子对撞机（EIC）上极化电子与质子深度非弹性散射（DIS）中，依赖于轻夸克偶极算符（dipole operators）的横向自旋不对称（TSA）在次领头阶（NLO）QCD 修正下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在评估 NLO QCD 修正对横向自旋不对称的影响，并探讨其对超出标准模型新物理（new physics）的敏感性，该敏感性与 LHC 的 Drell-Yan 测量及中子电偶极矩（neutron electric dipole moment）给出的限制具有互补性。

Method: 在 EIC 极化电子与质子束的深度非弹性散射框架下，计算包含轻夸克偶极算符贡献的横向自旋不对称的次领头阶（NLO）QCD 修正，并借助宇称（parity）、时间反演不变性（time reversal invariance）及手征对称性（chiral symmetry）约束其解析形式。

Result: 横向自旋不对称在纳入 NLO QCD 效应后保持稳健；其主要不确定性来源于当前对下夸克（down quark）横向度部分子分布函数（transversity parton distribution function）的测定精度不足。

Conclusion: 该 TSA 可作为对新物理具互补敏感性的探针，而提升下夸克横向度分布的测定精度是降低理论不确定性的关键。

Abstract: We consider the effects of next-to-leading order (NLO) QCD corrections to transverse spin asymmetries (TSAs) in deep inelastic scattering at the future Electron-Ion Collider (EIC) with polarized electron and proton beams that depend on light quark dipole operators. The TSAs are robust under the inclusion of the NLO QCD effects and provide sensitivity to new physics that is complementary to limits extracted from the Drell-Yan measurements at the LHC and from neutron electric dipole moments. The major uncertainty on our results is from the poor existing determination of the transversity parton distribution function for the down quark. Interesting relations from parity and time reversal invariance, along with chiral symmetry, constrain the analytic form of the TSAs.

</details>


### [129] [Central exclusive production of $η$ and $η'$ mesons in diffractive proton-proton collisions at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39503)
*Piotr Lebiedowicz,Otto Nachtmann,Antoni Szczurek*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文采用张量-坡密子（tensor-pomeron）方法研究高能质子-质子碰撞中η与η'(958)介子的中心排他产生（CEP），在振幅层面引入吸收效应，并以WA102数据约束模型参数，进而给出LHC 13 TeV下的截面与微分分布预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于利用衍射性味道单态赝标量介子（flavour singlet pseudoscalar meson）产生过程探测坡密子（pomeron）的本质，并检验CEP在LHC高能条件下的可行性。

Method: 方法上采用张量-坡密子（tensor-pomeron）方法描述$pp \to pp\,\eta$与$pp \to pp\,\eta'$过程，在振幅层面（amplitude level）计入吸收效应（absorption effects），并利用$\sqrt{s}=29.1$ GeV的WA102实验数据约束模型参数。

Result: 对$pp \to pp\eta$，在$|\eta_{M}|<1$与$2<\eta_{M}<5$的赝快度区间内总截面上限分别为2.5 μb与5.6 μb；对$pp \to pp\eta'$，相应区间预测截面为0.3–0.7 μb与0.9–2.1 μb，同时给出LHC $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV下的截面与微分分布。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在LHC上通过衍射味道单态赝标量介子产生研究坡密子性质具有可行性。

Abstract: We discuss central exclusive production (CEP) of $η$ and $η'(958)$ mesons in high-energy proton-proton collisions using the tensor-pomeron approach, incorporating absorption effects at the amplitude level. Model parameters are constrained using WA102 experimental data measured at $\sqrt{s} = 29.1$ GeV. We present cross sections and differential distributions for the LHC energy of $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV. For $pp \to pp η$, the upper limit of the total cross section is 2.5 $μ$b for $|η_{M}| < 1$ and 5.6 $μ$b for $2 < η_{M} < 5$. For $pp \to pp η'$, the predicted cross sections are 0.3--0.7 $μ$b and 0.9--2.1 $μ$b in the respective pseudorapidity regions. These results demonstrate the feasibility of studying diffractive flavour singlet pseudoscalar meson production to probe the nature of the pomeron at the LHC.

</details>


### [130] [Exclusive production of $π^+ π^-$ pairs in diffractive $γp$ and in $pp$ collisions within the tensor-pomeron approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39520)
*Piotr Lebiedowicz,Otto Nachtmann,Antoni Szczurek*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文在张量-坡密子（tensor-pomeron）框架下研究高能衍射 γp 与 pp 碰撞中 π⁺π⁻ 对的独家产生，综合共振与非共振（Drell-Söding）贡献，解释 H1 数据并给出 LHC 能量下的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能衍射过程（diffractive process）中 π⁺π⁻ 对的产生机制及其共振线形（resonance line shape）尚需澄清，尤其是非共振贡献的作用，且需为 LHC 上强子-强子碰撞的独家产生测量提供理论依据。

Method: 在张量-坡密子方法（tensor-pomeron approach）下，同时考虑共振贡献（ρ(770)、ω、f₂(1270)）与非共振的 Drell-Söding 机制，计算 γp → π⁺π⁻p 与 pp → ppπ⁺π⁻ 反应的微分截面。

Result: 模型在 M_{ππ} ≲ 1.2 GeV 区间很好地描述 H1 数据；Drell-Söding 机制对共振线形的塑造起重要作用；并预测了 √s = 13 TeV 下（至少一个质子发射虚光子）的 pp → ppπ⁺π⁻ 微分截面。

Conclusion: 结果对 LHC 上 ALICE、ATLAS、CMS、LHCb 在仅使用快度间隙（rapidity-gap）条件筛选下的中心独家产生研究具有参考价值，也适用于超周边 pA/AA 碰撞的 π⁺π⁻ 产生以及未来 EIC、LHeC 的光致与电致产生。

Abstract: We discuss exclusive production of $π^+ π^-$ pairs in diffractive $γp$ and in $pp$ collisions at high energies, considering resonant ($ρ(770)$, $ω$, $f_2(1270)$) and non-resonant (Drell-Söding) contributions within the tensor-pomeron approach. For the $γp \to π^+ π^- p$ reaction, the model describes well the H1 data in the region $M_{ππ} \lesssim 1.2$ GeV. We discuss the important role of the Drell-Söding mechanism in shaping the resonance line. We also predict differential cross sections for the $pp \to pp π^+ π^-$ reaction at $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV where at least one proton emits a virtual photon. These findings are relevant for the central exclusive production of $π^+ π^-$ pairs in the context of ALICE, ATLAS, CMS, and LHCb measurements in hadron-hadron collisions at the LHC, especially under experimental selections using only rapidity-gap conditions. Additionally, our results are also applicable to $π^+ π^-$ production in ultraperipheral $p$A/AA collisions at the LHC and to photo- and electroproduction of $π^+ π^-$ at future electron-proton/ion colliders (EIC, LHeC).

</details>


### [131] [Supermassive charged gravitinos and the Lux-Zeplin event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39529)
*Krzysztof A. Meissner,Hermann Nicolai*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对 Lux-Zeplin 合作组报告的单一异常事件（被解释为 WIMP 的首个证据）提出替代解释，即暗物质可能由带分数电荷的超大质量引力微子（supermassive gravitinos）构成，并给出区分两种假说的可检验判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lux-Zeplin 合作组将某一异常事件解释为弱相互作用大质量粒子（WIMP）的首个证据，但该解释并非唯一。作者基于其早先提出的暗物质由带分数电荷超大质量引力微子构成的假设，寻求一种替代性说明。

Method: 通过对两种解释的物理特征进行对比分析：WIMP 预期小截面（cross section）与大通量率（flux rate），而超大质量引力微子预期大电磁截面与极低通量率；并细化此前估算，提出可增强实验判别能力的额外步骤。

Result: 观测到的约 250 keV 核反冲能量（nuclear recoil energy）以及一年内出现一个事件，与 Meissner 与 Nicolai（Eur. Phys. J. C 84 (2024) 3, 269）给出的估算吻合良好。区分引力微子假说与 WIMP 假说的关键特征是：沿引力微子直线轨迹在数微秒内出现两次或多次序列反冲。

Conclusion: 数微秒内的序列反冲测量可作为判别两种假说的明确判据，且此类测量还有助于克服“中微子本底”（neutrino floor）；作者据此细化先前估算并提出额外步骤以支持实验判别。

Abstract: The Lux-Zeplin collaboration recently reported one unusual event which was interpreted as first evidence for a WIMP (arXiv:2609.02823[hep-ex]). In this note we offer an alternative explanation of this event, following our earlier proposal that dark matter could consist of fractionally charged supermassive gravitinos. A main difference between the two interpretations is that one expects small cross sections and large flux rates for WIMPs, whereas it is the opposite for supermassive gravitinos, namely large (electromagnetic) cross sections and very low flux rates. The observed nuclear recoil energy of about 250 keV and the occurrence of one event in one year fit well with the estimates quoted in (K.A. Meissner, H.~Nicolai, Eur.Phys.J.C 84 (2024) 3, 269). A main characteristic of the new interpretation distinguishing it unequivocally from the WIMP hypothesis would be the sequential occurrence of two or more recoils within a few microseconds along the gravitino's straight trajectory, a prediction that remains to be checked. Such a measurement would also allow to overcome the `neutrino floor'. In this note we refine our previous estimates and propose some extra steps so as to enable the experiment to discriminate between the WIMP and gravitino hypotheses.

</details>


### [132] [PINNing the pion: conformal deep learning for $F_π(s)$ and the $(g-2)_μ$ hadronic contribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40008)
*Mayank Goel,Subhadip Mitra,Monalisa Patra*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出嵌入共形 z 平面的物理信息神经网络（Physics-Informed Neural Network, PINN），从第一性原理构造 π 介子电磁形状因子 F_π(s)，并以数据作为约束，给出模型无关的电荷半径、ρ 极点参数与 μ 子反常磁矩的双 π 贡献估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统通过唯象曲线拟合提取 F_π(s) 会引入模型依赖、非物理赝像与运动学不一致。作者希望让基本 S 矩阵（S-matrix）原理主导形状因子行为，而将实验数据降格为约束条件，从而避免拟合模型的任意性。

Method: 采用嵌入共形 z 平面的 PINN，直接跨类空与类时区域构造 F_π(s)：电荷归一化与 Schwarz 反射（Schwarz reflection）由构造自动满足，Cauchy-Riemann 解析性、色散关系（dispersion relations）、Watson 定理与微扰 QCD 渐近行为通过损失泛函引入。除 e^+e^- 散射数据外，通过开关原生纳入 τ 衰变数据以隔离纯同位旋矢量形状因子，绕过模型依赖的同位旋破缺预修正。

Result: 网络自发给出内部无零点的形状因子，并可用解析性与色散约束检验 ρ(770) 峰附近的实验张力。所得模型无关结果为：π 电荷半径 ⟨r_π^2⟩ = 0.435 ± 0.008_stat ± 0.007_cali fm^2，第二黎曼面极点参数 m_ρ^{pole} = 761.72 ± 1.04 MeV、Γ_ρ^{pole} = 135.99 ± 1.20 MeV，以及双 π 对 μ 子反常磁矩的贡献 a_μ^{ππ} = (506.48 ± 2.02_stat ± 1.70_cali) × 10^{-10}。

Conclusion: 将割裂复平面映射到单位圆可界定 Hessian 范数并避免神经正切核（Neural Tangent Kernel）谱饥饿，这两者是深度学习优化的已知失效模式，表明共形映射对训练的稳定性具有关键作用。整体框架实现了以 S 矩阵原理驱动、数据约束的模型无关形状因子提取。

Abstract: Extracting the pion electromagnetic form factor $F_π(s)$ through phenomenological curve-fitting models introduces model dependence, unphysical artefacts, and kinematic inconsistencies. We introduce a Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) embedded in a conformal $z$-plane that constructs $F_π(s)$ directly from first principles across spacelike and timelike domains: charge normalisation and Schwarz reflection are enforced by construction, while Cauchy-Riemann analyticity, dispersion relations, Watson's theorem, and perturbative QCD asymptotics enter through the loss functional. Thus, the fundamental S-matrix principles dictate the form factor's behaviour while data act as constraints. Mapping the cut complex plane onto the unit disk bounds the Hessian norm and prevents Neural Tangent Kernel spectral starvation, two known failure modes of deep-learning optimisation. Besides $e^+e^-$ scattering data, we also incorporate $τ$-decay data through a switch that isolates the pure isovector form factor natively, bypassing model-dependent isospin-breaking pre-corrections. The network organically yields an interior zero-free form factor, while the framework tests experimental tensions around the $ρ(770)$ peak against analyticity and dispersion constraints. We obtain model-independent estimates of the pion charge radius, $\langle r_π^2 \rangle = 0.435 \pm 0.008_{\text{stat}} \pm 0.007_{\text{cali}}$ fm$^2$, the second-sheet pole parameters, $m_ρ^{\text{pole}} = 761.72\pm 1.04$ MeV and $Γ_ρ^{\text{pole}} = 135.99 \pm 1.20$ MeV, and the two-pion contribution to the muon anomalous magnetic moment, $a_μ^{ππ} = (506.48 \pm 2.02_{\text{stat}} \pm 1.70_{\text{cali}}) \times 10^{-10}$.

</details>


### [133] [Production Shape Effects Beyond Rate Rescaling in Dark Photon Searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40052)
*X. Zhong,H. Y. Qiu,X. Q. Shen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文通过归一化比较暗光子轫致辐射（proton bremsstrahlung）的两种产生谱描述，分离并量化了长寿命粒子（long-lived particle）搜寻中由产生模型导致的接收度（acceptance）差异。在 NA62 束流收集器（beam dump）几何下，中心计算给出分数接收度偏移 δ_NF > 0.3，且该结论在多种有效 QRA 离壳能标与孔径变化下稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长寿命粒子搜寻中的产生模型不确定性会同时影响总产额与实验接收度所采样的运动学分布，二者常被混同处理。该文旨在将后者（分布形状引起的接收度效应）单独隔离出来，并检验速率因子化（rate factorization）假设是否成立。

Method: 在共同物理区间上对修正的 Weizsäcker–Williams 描述与有效准实近似（effective quasireal approximation, QRA）进行归一化后比较其暗光子轫致辐射谱。在固定该区间产额的前提下，以接收度差异直接度量对速率因子化的偏离；并在 NA62 几何中考察不同 QRA 离壳能标（1.0、1.5、2.0 GeV）与孔径变化，另用公开 NA62 全选择接收度数据检验双缪子（dimuon）接收度比。

Result: 在 NA62 显示灵敏度重叠区内每一数值收敛网格点上，中心计算给出分数接收度偏移 δ_NF > 0.3；该结论在 QRA 离壳能标 1.0、1.5、2.0 GeV 及所考虑的孔径变化下均持续成立。公开 NA62 全选择接收度显示，Mixing 与 Bremsstrahlung 两类产生类别的双缪子接收度比并非常数，范围约为 0.58 至 4.23，相对 1 的中位绝对偏离约 0.79。

Conclusion: 公开的全选择接收度依赖于产生类别，为接收度存在产生模型依赖性提供了互补证据。更一般地，所提出的投影判据（projection criterion）可对速率因子化做定量检验：归一化后的产生形状变化在所需精度下对接收度响应的投影必须可忽略，否则产生模型依赖性须在接收度计算中传播，或作为接收度不确定性处理。

Abstract: Production model uncertainties in long-lived particle searches can affect both the inclusive yield and the kinematic distributions sampled by the experimental acceptance. We isolate the latter effect by comparing the modified Weizsäcker--Williams description with the effective quasireal approximation for dark photon production via proton bremsstrahlung after normalizing both spectra on a common physical domain. At fixed production yield within this domain, the resulting acceptance difference directly measures the departure from rate factorization. In the NA62 beam dump geometry, the central calculation gives a fractional acceptance shift $δ_{\rm NF}>0.3$ at every numerically converged grid point within the displayed NA62 sensitivity overlap. This conclusion persists for effective QRA off-shell scales of 1.0, 1.5, and 2.0~GeV and under the aperture variations considered. Separately, the public NA62 full selection acceptances give a nonconstant dimuon acceptance ratio between the Mixing and Bremsstrahlung production categories, ranging from approximately 0.58 to 4.23, with a median absolute departure from unity of approximately 0.79 over the analyzed HEPData grid. This provides complementary evidence that the published full selection acceptance depends on the production category. More generally, the projection criterion provides a quantitative test of rate factorization. The normalized production shape variation must have negligible projection onto the acceptance response at the required accuracy. When this condition is not satisfied, production model dependence must be propagated through the acceptance calculation or treated as an acceptance uncertainty.

</details>


### [134] [New Physics in $ b \to s τ^+τ^-$ Processes and Correlations with $R(D^{(*)})$ and $B\to K^{(*)}ν\barν$: An (SM)EFT Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40072)
*Guillermo Baltà,Andreas Crivellin,Joaquim Matias,Martín Novoa-Brunet*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重访并更新了 \(b\to s\tau^+\tau^-\) 过程的预言，在弱有效理论（Weak Effective Theory）中给出分支比（branching ratio）的半解析表达式，并开展维度-6 SMEFT 分析，指出当前味物理数据偏好可显著增强该过程分支比的参数区域，且未来 LHCb、CMS、Belle II 的极限将探测其可及参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: \(b\to s\tau^+\tau^-\) 是探测与第三代夸克和轻子相关新物理（New Physics）的敏感探针，且受 \(R(D^{(*)})\) 持续反常与 \(B^+\to K^+\nu\bar\nu\) 速率增强迹象的驱动。鉴于此，作者认为有必要重新审视并更新该过程的预言。

Method: 在先前数据驱动方法（data-driven approach）纳入主导 \(\psi(2S)\) 共振的基础上，给出 \(B\to K^{(*)}\tau^+\tau^-\)（\(B_s\to\phi\tau^+\tau^-\)）全运动学范围的预言；在弱有效理论内提供相应分支比的半解析表达式，并进行维度-6 SMEFT（Standard Model Effective Field Theory）分析，假设新物理出现在 TeV 能标。

Result: 识别出当前数据偏好的参数空间区域，其中 \(b\to s\tau^+\tau^-\) 过程分支比可被提升若干数量级。当前实验灵敏度仍远高于标准模型（SM）速率，但现有味数据所暗示的增强意味着即将到来的 LHCb、CMS 与 Belle II 极限将探测相关 SMEFT 参数空间的重要区域。

Conclusion: 不同情景可能对 \(B\to K^{(*)}\nu\bar\nu\) 分支比给出相同预言，但通过测量 \(B\to K^{(*)}\tau^+\tau^-\)、\(B_s\to\tau^+\tau^-\) 与 \(B_s\to\phi\tau^+\tau^-\) 能够将它们区分开来。

Abstract: Tauonic flavour-changing neutral-current transitions ($b\to sτ^+τ^-$) are sensitive probes of New Physics connected to third-generation quarks and leptons. Motivated by the persistent anomalies in $R(D^{(*)})$ and the indications of enhanced $B^+\to K^{+}ν\barν$ rates, we revisit and update the predictions for $b\to sτ^+τ^-$ processes. Building upon a previous work on a data-driven approach to include the dominant $ψ(2S)$ resonances, we provide predictions for $B\to K^{(*)}τ^+τ^-$ ($B_s\toφτ^+τ^-$) over the full kinematic range. To include possible New Physics contributions within the Weak Effective Theory, we provide semi-analytic expressions for the corresponding branching ratios. We then perform a comprehensive dimension-6 SMEFT analysis of New Physics realised at the TeV scale. We identify regions of parameter space preferred by current data where the branching ratios of $b\to sτ^+τ^-$ processes can be enhanced by several orders of magnitude. While current experimental sensitivities remain far above the SM rates, the enhancements suggested by present flavour data imply that forthcoming limits from LHCb, CMS, and Belle~II will probe significant regions of the relevant SMEFT parameter space. Furthermore, we highlight that while different scenarios can lead to the same predictions for $B\to K^{(*)}ν\barν$ branching ratios, they can be disentangled by measuring $B\to K^{(*)}τ^+τ^-$, $B_s\toτ^+τ^-$ and $B_s\toφτ^+τ^-$.

</details>


### [135] [Interpreting the High-Recoil LUX-ZEPLIN Event with Bino-/Singlino-like and Higgsino Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40234)
*Subhojit Roy,Pedro Schwaller,Carlos E. M. Wagner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 针对 LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验近期报道的约 248 keV 核反冲候选事件，本文研究了两种基于中性微子（neutralino）与 Z 玻色子不同耦合的超对称（supersymmetry）解释：弹性散射情形与采用性（inelastic）散射情形。前者可由 bino 或 singlino 主导的中性微子通过轴矢 Z 耦合实现，后者由赝狄拉克 Higgsino（pseudo-Dirac Higgsino）的吸热散射实现，两者预言了偏离候选点处不同的反冲谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ 实验新近报告了一个位于 E_R≃248 keV 的核反冲候选事件，需要从超对称框架出发评估其对暗物质模型的约束与启示。动机在于区分中性微子与 Z 玻色子耦合的两种不同机制，并检验其与现有实验限制（如 Higgs 质量、太阳中微子搜寻）的一致性。

Method: 分为两种解释：其一为弹性情形，bino 或 singlino 主导的中性微子经由对角轴矢 Z 耦合散射，耦合强度由 Higgsino 不对称度 X=|N_13|^2-|N_14|^2 决定，而非总 Higgsino 分量；利用自旋相关（spin-dependent）与自旋无关（spin-independent）盲点不重合，可在保持 X∼10^{-2} 时压低 Higgs 媒介散射。其二为采用性情形，赝狄拉克 Higgsino 发生吸热散射，其质量劈裂由 PeV 能标的 gaugino 产生，并可通过相反符号的 bino-wino 抵消或弱的 singlino 混合来降低。

Result: 弹性情形在 MSSM 中倾向低 tanβ，与观测到的 Higgs 质量在几 TeV 标量夸克谱下存在张力，而 NMSSM 的树级四重耦合可缓解该张力；给出的 NMSSM 基准点（benchmark）取 m_χ≃500 GeV、X≃0.007，低于当前 LZ 限制，其遗迹丰度（relic abundance）来自略高于阈值的 singlino 型赝标量共振，同时压低当下的湮灭。采用性情形的最小热产生实现受到太阳中微子搜寻的强约束。两种机制在候选点之外预言了不同的反冲谱。

Conclusion: 两种超对称解释均可对应 LZ 候选事件，并可通过偏离候选点处的反冲谱形状加以区分。弹性情形在 NMSSM 中可自然容纳，采用性情形则受太阳中微子搜寻限制较强。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has recently reported a nuclear-recoil candidate at $E_R\simeq248$ keV. We study two supersymmetric interpretations based on distinct neutralino couplings to the $Z$ boson. In the elastic case, a bino- or singlino-like neutralino scatters through a diagonal axial $Z$ coupling set by the Higgsino asymmetry $X=|N_{13}|^2-|N_{14}|^2$ rather than by the total Higgsino fraction. Since the spin-dependent blind spot does not coincide with the spin-independent one, Higgs-mediated scattering can be suppressed while retaining $X\sim10^{-2}$. In the MSSM this favors low $\tanβ$, in tension with the observed Higgs mass for a few-TeV squark spectrum, a tension relieved by the tree-level quartic of the NMSSM. An explicit NMSSM benchmark with $m_χ\simeq500$ GeV and $X\simeq0.007$, below current LZ limits, obtains its relic abundance from a singlet-like pseudoscalar resonance just above threshold, which also suppresses present-day annihilation. In the inelastic case, a pseudo-Dirac Higgsino scatters endothermically, with a splitting of a few hundred keV generated by gauginos at the PeV scale which can be lowered by opposite-sign bino-wino cancellation, or by weak singlino mixing; the minimal thermal realization is strongly constrained by solar-neutrino searches. The two mechanisms predict distinct recoil spectra away from the candidate.

</details>


### [136] [The holographic QCD critical point is sensitive to quark flavors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39598)
*Niko Jokela,Matti Järvinen,Toshali Mitra,Aleksi Piispa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将味依赖的全息 V-QCD（holographic V-QCD）模型应用于两种不同物理环境，研究致密 QCD 物质（dense QCD matter）的相结构，发现相图对模型拟合方式与物理环境均高度敏感，并据此讨论 RHIC 束流能量扫描（Beam Energy Scan）净质子累积量测量的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期 RHIC 束流能量扫描第二阶段（Phase II of the RHIC Beam Energy Scan）的净质子累积量（net-proton cumulant）测量尚未显现清晰的非单调涨落信号，需要一个能解释该现象并联系致密 QCD 相结构的理论框架。模型还需同时纳入真实夸克质量谱（两个无质量轻夸克与一个重奇异夸克）以刻画不同实验环境。

Method: 采用味依赖的全息 V-QCD 模型，其夸克部分包含两个无质量轻夸克与一个重奇异夸克的质量层级（mass hierarchy），并将模型分别应用于电荷中性、β 平衡（beta-equilibrated）物质与奇异数中性、固定电荷-重子数比 n_Q/n_B = 0.4 的重离子碰撞（heavy-ion collisions）条件。沿化学冻结曲线（chemical freeze-out curve）计算高阶重子数累积量比（baryon number cumulant ratios），并将 V-QCD 与 Thermal-FIST 包中的强子共振气体（Hadron Resonance Gas, HRG）模型匹配以获得全局相图。

Result: 相图显著依赖于将模型对格子 QCD（lattice QCD）热力学进行调参（tuning）的具体流程以及所选物理环境。在作者偏好的拟合流程下，β 平衡物质呈现一级相变（first-order phase transition），其临界终点（critical endpoint）位于比早期全息研究显著更低的密度；但该相变在重离子碰撞条件下完全消失。高阶重子数累积量比被用于定量对比模型预测与实验数据。

Conclusion: 相变对物理环境的强烈依赖性可能解释为何近期 RHIC 束流能量扫描第二阶段的净质子累积量测量尚未显示清晰的非单调涨落特征。将 V-QCD 与 Thermal-FIST 中的 HRG 模型匹配所得的全局相图，进一步支持仅由 V-QCD 模型得出的结论。

Abstract: We study the phase structure of dense QCD matter by applying the flavor-dependent holographic V-QCD model to two distinct physical environments: charge-neutral, beta-equilibrated matter, and strangeness-neutral matter with a fixed charge-to-baryon number ratio, n_Q/n_B = 0.4, relevant for heavy-ion collisions. The quark sector of the model incorporates the realistic mass hierarchy of two massless light quarks and a massive strange quark. We find that the resulting phase diagram depends sensitively on both the procedure used to tune the model against lattice QCD thermodynamics and the choice of physical environment. Using our preferred fitting procedure, we find that beta-equilibrated matter exhibits a first-order phase transition terminating at a critical endpoint located at significantly lower densities than in earlier holographic studies. However, this phase transition disappears entirely under heavy-ion conditions. This pronounced environmental dependence may help explain why recent net-proton cumulant measurements from Phase II of the RHIC Beam Energy Scan have not yet revealed clear non-monotonic fluctuation signatures. We calculate higher-order baryon number cumulant ratios along the chemical freeze-out curve to quantitatively compare the model predictions with experimental data. Finally, we also present global phase diagrams obtained by matching V-QCD to Hadron Resonance Gas models implemented in the Thermal-FIST package, and argue that this comparison further supports the conclusions drawn from the V-QCD model alone.

</details>


### [137] [Schur elimination of brane-localised Higgs mass spectra, and the criteria for the $ε\to0$/$N\to\infty$ non-commutativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39910)
*Roberto Barceló,Subhadip Mitra*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对五维标准模型扩展中边界调节与 Kaluza-Klein 塔求和的次序问题，利用 Schur 补（Schur complement）精确消去整个 KK 塔，将两种求极限次序的不一致归结为单一求和的不连续性，并给出了直接证明及两个出现条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在众多五维标准模型扩展中，希格斯玻色子局域于额外维度上的一点并与体费米子耦合，计算费米子质量谱需要边界调节子（boundary regulator）；如 Ref.~[Barcelo:2014kha] 所示，在求和 KK 塔之前或之后移除调节子可能给出不同结果，这一次序依赖性问题需要刻画其来源。

Method: 利用耦合仅通过两个重叠数（overlap numbers）到达任一 KK 模这一性质，以 Schur 补精确消去整个 KK 塔，从而把两种求极限次序的分歧化为单一求和，并直接证明该求和的不连续性。

Result: 证明该不连续性出现需满足两个条件：耦合足够尖锐以使求和条件收敛（conditionally convergent），以及存在一个在接触点上具有互补行为的第二通道；这两个条件均不依赖于额外维度是否存在，甚至不依赖是否存在可求和的离散塔。

Conclusion: 同样的判据可应用于普通散射中的接触相互作用、光学定理（optical theorem）背后的虚部，以及跷跷板（seesaw）、门户（portal）和钟表（clockwork）构造；两个条件是否满足决定类似歧义是否起作用。摘要未给出定量结果或数值验证。

Abstract: In many 5D extensions of the Standard Model, the Higgs boson is localised to a single point along the extra dimension, where it couples to bulk fermions. Computing the fermion mass spectrum then requires a boundary regulator, and removing it before or after summing the Kaluza-Klein tower can give different answers, as shown in Ref.~\cite{Barcelo:2014kha}. Because the coupling reaches any KK mode through only two overlap numbers, the whole tower can be eliminated exactly by a Schur complement; the disagreement between the two orders of limit then reduces to a single sum, whose discontinuity we prove directly. This discontinuity needs two conditions to appear: a coupling sharp enough to leave the sum conditionally convergent, and a second channel with complementary behaviour at the point of contact. Neither condition depends on having an extra dimension, or even a discrete tower to sum. The same test applies to contact interactions in ordinary scattering, the imaginary part behind the optical theorem, and seesaw, portal, and clockwork constructions. Whether the two conditions are met determines whether a similar ambiguity plays any role.

</details>


### [138] [Electromagnetic probes of concurrent minijet-hydrodynamics evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39939)
*Soham Banerjee,Mayank Singh,Charles Gale,Sangyong Jeon,Daniel Pablos,Jean-François Paquet*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在联合小喷注-流体动力学（minijet-hydrodynamics）框架下计算直接光子（direct photon）与双轻子（dilepton）产生，发现小喷注诱导的流体演化修正会在电磁探针中留下可测量效应，表明电磁辐射是探测硬部分子与夸克-胶子等离子体相互作用的灵敏探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小喷注比体介质热化更慢并延续到流体阶段，其间向等离子体沉积能量与动量并产生尾迹（wakes），从而改变时空演化。强子观测量对这类修正不敏感（可被剪切黏度与初态能量动量张量归一化的调整吸收），因此需要寻找对底层时空历史直接敏感的探针。

Method: 采用同时演化小喷注与体介质的并发框架，逐事件纳入初态涨落与动力学小喷注-介质相互作用。小喷注由 PYTHIA 产生，背景等离子体采用 IP-Glasma 初态条件，流体演化用 MUSIC，强子输运用 UrQMD，并对直接光子与双轻子产生进行综合计算。

Result: 小喷注对强子多重数与动量各向异性的修正在很大程度上可通过调整剪切黏度与初态能量-动量张量归一化来吸收；而电磁探针在整个演化过程中发射，始终直接敏感于底层时空历史，其光子与双轻子观测量均出现由小喷注诱导的流体演化修正带来的可测量效应。

Conclusion: 电磁辐射可作为探测硬部分子（hard partons）与夸克-胶子等离子体（quark-gluon plasma）相互作用的灵敏探针，为研究小喷注与介质耦合提供了强子观测量之外的补充途径。

Abstract: We investigate direct photon and dilepton production in a concurrent minijet-hydrodynamics framework that consistently incorporates event-by-event initial-state fluctuations and dynamical minijet-medium interactions. Produced in initial hard scatterings, minijets thermalize more slowly than the bulk medium and persist into the hydrodynamic stage, where they deposit energy and momentum into the evolving plasma, generating wakes that modify its spacetime evolution. Minijets modify the hadronic multiplicity and momentum anisotropy in a manner that can largely be absorbed by adjusting the shear viscosity and the normalization of the system's initial energy-momentum tensor. In contrast, electromagnetic probes are emitted throughout the evolution and remain directly sensitive to the underlying spacetime history. We perform a comprehensive calculation of direct photon and dilepton production with the minijets and bulk medium evolved simultaneously, using PYTHIA to produce minijets, and describing the underlying plasma with IP-Glasma initial conditions, hydrodynamic evolution with MUSIC and hadronic transport with UrQMD. We find that the minijet-induced modification of the hydrodynamic evolution produces measurable effects in both photon and dilepton observables, establishing electromagnetic radiation as a sensitive probe of the interplay between hard partons and the quark-gluon plasma.

</details>


### [139] [$\mathcal{O}(αα_s^2)$ corrections to the Top Quark mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40229)
*Daniele Gaggero,Ciaran Williams*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了顶夸克质量的三圈 \(\mathcal{O}(\alpha\alpha_s^2)\) 混合 QCD-EW 修正，给出极点质量与 \(\overline{\rm{MS}}\) 质量在该阶的关系，并指出椭圆曲线周期对最终物理结果有贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顶夸克质量的精确确定需要高阶微扰修正，而三圈混合 QCD-EW 修正此前尚未完整给出。

Method: 将三圈自能表示为三圈主积分（master integrals, MIs）的函数，所选 MIs 满足 \(\varepsilon\)-因子化形式的微分方程，从而可写成迭代积分（iterated integrals）；并讨论所遇微分形式的类型，包括椭圆曲线（elliptic curves）的周期。

Result: 获得了极点质量 \(M_t\) 与 \(\overline{\rm{MS}}\) 质量 \(m_t(\mu)\) 在此阶的关系；对 \(M_t=172.3\) GeV，\(m_t(M_t)\) 的位移为 \(-216\) MeV，与当前实验不确定度相当。

Conclusion: 该三圈混合修正的位移量级可与实验精度相比，表明其在精确顶夸克质量测定中不可忽略；椭圆曲线周期亦参与最终物理结果。

Abstract: We calculate the three-loop $\mathcal{O}(αα_s^2)$ mixed QCD-EW corrections to the top quark mass and obtain the relationships between the pole ($M_t$) and $\overline{\rm{MS}}$ ($m_t(μ)$) masses at this order. In performing our calculation, we express the three-loop self-energies as a function of three-loop master integrals (MIs). These MIs are defined so as to solve a differential equation in $ε$-factorized form, allowing for their solution to be expressed as iterated integrals. We discuss the types of differential forms that we encounter, which include periods of elliptic curves, and their role in the final physical result. Using our results, we calculate the shift in $m_t(M_t)$ arising at this order. For $M_t = 172.3$ GeV, the shift is -216 MeV, which is comparable to the currently quoted experimental uncertainty.

</details>

### [140] [MyTm: An Automated Melting Temperature Calculation Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39686)
*Y. S. Huang,H. X. Song,Y. Sun,J. L. Li,Y. L. Xu,F. C. Wu,Y. C. Gan,Y. F. Wang,H. Wang,Hua Y. Geng*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: MyTm 是一个基于分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）的全自动化熔点计算工具包，集成了多种常用方法，并引入机器学习（machine learning, ML）方法识别固/液类原子，从而实现高通量熔点计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算材料科学的高通量筛选与人工智能辅助材料设计中，需要快速、自主地评估目标材料的熔点。然而传统 MD 熔点模拟涉及大量繁琐的人工操作，使大规模熔点计算难以实现。

Method: 提出 MyTm 工具包，采用完全模块化设计，通过组合模块实现全自动熔点计算。其支持直接加热法（direct-heating method）、空位法（void method）、改进空位法（modified void method）、固液共存法（solid-liquid coexistence method）与 Z 方法（Z method）。同时提出一种机器学习方法对固类原子与液类原子进行识别与分类。

Result: 所提出的 ML 分类方法有效解决了传统分类方法精度偏低的问题，使自动化高通量熔点计算流程成为可能。MyTm 的稳健性与有效性已通过若干研究充分的体系得到验证。

Conclusion: MyTm 提供了一种自动化、可模块化组合的 MD 熔点计算方案，结合 ML 原子分类可支撑高通量熔点计算流程，并在多个已充分研究的体系中验证了其稳健性与有效性。

Abstract: Melting temperature calculation is one of the important topics in computational materials science. In high-throughput in silico screening and artificial intelligence assisted design of materials, it usually requires a rapid and autonomous assessment of the melting temperature of the target. Unfortunately, molecular dynamics (MD) simulations of the melting point require many cumbersome and manual operations, making large-scale calculation of the melting point challenging. In this work, we introduce MyTm, a toolkit that employs MD to automatically determine the melting point. The method is fully modularized, and by combining these modules, the program enables fully automated melting calculations by using commonly adopted approaches, including the direct-heating method, the void method, the modified void method, the solid-liquid coexistence method, and the Z method. Moreover, a machine learning (ML) method is proposed and employed to recognize and classify the solid like and liquid like atoms, which effectively resolve the low accuracy issue in conventional classification approaches, thus making the automated high throughput pipeline of melting-point calculation possible. The robustness and efficacy of MyTm have been demonstrated by several well studied systems.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Reference-Measure Freedom of the Effective Hamiltonian: From Stochastic Thermodynamics to the Macroscopic Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38994)
*Ryuichi Okamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文指出，有效哈密顿量（EH）的坐标变换问题源于参考测度（gauge）的隐含改变；在慢变量流形上内蕴地定义平衡测度与 FPE 后，EH 是相对于所选 gauge 的标量函数，不同 EH 仅为同一内蕴平衡测度与随机动力学的不同 gauge 表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有效哈密顿量在化学与软物质体系中广泛用于描述平衡性质与慢动力学，但被质疑在坐标变换下不按标量变换，因而定义不良。本文旨在澄清这一表观问题的来源。

Method: 作者将平衡测度与 Fokker--Planck 方程（Fokker--Planck equation, FPE）内蕴地在慢变量流形（manifold of slow variables）上表述，并引入参考测度（gauge）的视角分析 EH 的定义；同时讨论不同约束协议（restraint protocols）与参考测度选择的关系。

Result: 研究表明，表观问题源自平衡概率密度所依赖的参考测度（gauge）被隐含改变；EH 是相对于所选 gauge 定义的标量函数，传统 EH 与近期在黎曼表述（Riemannian formulation）中提出的扩散依赖 EH 均为同一内蕴平衡测度与随机动力学的不同 gauge 表示；不同约束协议对应不同参考测度。当宏观极限下平衡测度满足大偏差原理（large-deviation principle）时，对应次主导 gauge 的 EH 差异相对大偏差速度（large-deviation speed）为次主导，EH 的主导部分由 gauge 无关的大偏差率函数（large-deviation rate function）决定，相应确定性动力学也 gauge 无关。

Conclusion: 有效哈密顿量的坐标变换问题可由参考测度（gauge）的隐含改变解释；EH 应被理解为相对于所选 gauge 的标量函数，而非唯一不变量。不同 EH 对应同一内蕴平衡测度与随机动力学的不同 gauge 表示；在满足大偏差原理的宏观极限下，EH 的主导部分及相应确定性动力学均为 gauge 无关。

Abstract: The effective Hamiltonian (EH), also referred to as the potential of mean force or free-energy landscape, is widely used to describe equilibrium properties and slow dynamics in chemical and softmatter systems. However, the conventional EH has been argued to be ill-defined because it does not transform as a scalar under a change of coordinates. Here, we show that this apparent problem originates from an implicit change of the reference measure (gauge) with respect to which the equilibrium probability density is defined. We formulate the equilibrium measure and the Fokker--Planck equation (FPE) intrinsically on the manifold of slow variables and show that an EH is a scalar function defined with respect to a chosen gauge. Different EHs, including the conventional EH and the diffusion-dependent EH recently proposed in a Riemannian formulation, thus provide different gauge representations of the same intrinsic equilibrium measure and stochastic dynamics. We further show that the gauge choice has an operational meaning: different restraint protocols for the slow variables select different reference measures. Finally, when the equilibrium measure obeys a large-deviation principle in a macroscopic limit, differences among EHs associated with subleading gauges become subleading relative to the large-deviation speed. The leading part of the EH is then determined by the gauge-independent large-deviation rate function, and the corresponding deterministic dynamics is likewise gauge independent.

</details>


### [142] [Interdependent-Network Criticality without a Second Network: Cascading Collapse of the Joule-Coupled Insulator-Metal Transition in $VO_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39155)
*Ouriel Gotesdyner,Michal Wasserman,Hodaya Kaster,Yosi Abulafia,Avital Fried,Bnaya Gross,Shlomo Havlin,Amos Sharoni*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在二维VO₂晶格中，电驱动的绝缘体-金属转变（insulator-metal transition, IMT）表现为单一结构实体却具有相互依赖网络（interdependent network）行为：焦耳热耦合引发级联反应，观测到长达数千秒的亚稳孕育平台及混合阶（mixed-order）突变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对IMT孕育延迟（incubation delay）的分析主要依赖宏观电-热建模（electro-thermal modeling），缺乏对转变动力学的统计洞察。本文旨在从统计与网络角度理解电驱动IMT的延迟与突变机制。

Method: 在二维VO₂晶格中研究电驱动IMT，结合时间分辨电阻测量（time-resolved resistance measurements）、焦耳耦合电阻网络模型（Joule-coupled resistor-network model）与相互依赖网络理论（interdependent-network theory）进行分析。

Result: 金属畴（metallic domains）耗散更多焦耳热，每次开关事件提升网络焦耳热，产生网络依赖并触发级联链式反应。观测到一阶（first-order）突变前存在长达数千秒的异常孕育平台（incubation plateau）；在临界点附近有效分支因子（effective branching factor）趋近1，平台寿命以指数ζ=1/2发散，序参量（order parameter）以指数β=1/2标度。

Conclusion: 这些特征与相互依赖网络的预测一致，表明仅耗散焦耳耦合即可在单一网络的关联氧化物（correlated-oxide）体系中驱动混合阶灾难性级联（mixed-order catastrophic cascades）。

Abstract: When driven by a large enough electric field or current, the insulating state of vanadium dioxide ($VO_2$) typically collapses abruptly into the metallic state (the insulator-metal transition, IMT) following a brief incubation delay. These incubation delays, typically lasting microseconds or less, have been analyzed predominantly through macroscopic electro-thermal modeling, providing little statistical insight into the transition dynamics. Here, we investigate the electrically driven IMT in a two-dimensional $VO_2$ lattice and demonstrate that the material behaves as a complex, interdependent network despite being a single structural entity. Because metallic domains dissipate significantly more Joule heat than insulating regions, each switching event raises the Joule heating of the network, resulting in network dependency and triggering a cascading chain reaction. Combining time-resolved resistance measurements with a Joule-coupled resistor-network model and interdependent-network theory, we observe an abrupt (first-order) transition preceded by an anomalously long incubation plateau lasting thousands of seconds. During this metastable plateau, an effective branching factor of the switching activity approaches unity at criticality, and the plateau lifetime diverges with exponent $ζ=1/2$; the order parameter scales with exponent $β=1/2$. These signatures coincide with those predicted for interdependent networks, indicating that dissipative Joule coupling alone can drive mixed-order catastrophic cascades in a single-network correlated-oxide system.

</details>


### [143] [Excursion-Resolved Thermodynamic Inference without Observing Reverse Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39205)
*Jie Gu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过有向可见事件之间的等待时间分布（waiting-time distributions），可在不观测每条跃迁逆过程的情况下重建边界动力学，并在源-靶闭合条件（source-target closure condition）下给出总熵产生（entropy production）的严格下界；仅观测一条生成有向环（spanning directed cycle）即足以识别完整马尔可夫生成元（Markov generator）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验通常只能探测到部分跃迁，导致底层状态动力学与耗散（dissipation）被隐藏。现有基于等待时间的熵产生估计方法依赖逆事件的可用性，因此需要一种在逆过程不可观测时仍能重建动力学并约束耗散的方法。

Method: 利用有向可见事件之间的等待时间分布重建边界动力学，无需观测每条被探测跃迁的逆过程。在简单的源-靶闭合条件下，由这些分布确定可见速率（visible rates）以及连接已观测状态的隐藏迂回（hidden excursions）。

Result: 该方法给出总熵产生的严格下界，且在逆事件可获得时优于标准的等待时间估计。此外，仅观测一条生成有向环即可识别完整马尔可夫生成元，即使所有逆向环边及其他微观跃迁均未被观测到。

Conclusion: 等待时间分布可作为重建隐藏边界动力学与约束耗散的有效工具，显著降低对完整微观跃迁观测的依赖；生成有向环的观测足以唯一确定马尔可夫生成元。

Abstract: Experiments often detect only selected transitions, leaving the underlying state dynamics and dissipation hidden. We show that waiting-time distributions between directed visible events can reconstruct the boundary dynamics without observing the reverse of every detected transition. Under a simple source-target closure condition, they determine visible rates and the hidden excursions connecting observed states, yielding a rigorous lower bound on total entropy production that improves the standard waiting-time estimate when reverse events are available. Remarkably, observing only a spanning directed cycle is sufficient to identify the full Markov generator, even when all reverse cycle edges and other microscopic transitions remain unseen.

</details>


### [144] [Logarithmic Quasi-Long-Range Order and Novel Continuous Phase Transition in the Two-Dimensional $XY$ Model with $1/r^4$ Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39267)
*Xianzhi Pan,Zhijie Fan,Youjin Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究相互作用按 \(1/r^{2+\sigma}\) 衰减的二维经典 XY 模型相图与临界性质，重点解析边缘情形 \(\sigma=2\)：低温相表现为对数准长程序（logarithmic quasi-long-range order, log-QLRO），其转变超出 Ginzburg-Landau-Wilson 范式与 Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless 机制，并指出 \(\sigma=2\) 为长程与短程普适类（universality class）的跨界边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在刻画长程相互作用下二维 XY 模型的临界行为，特别是澄清边缘情形 \(\sigma=2\) 的低温有序性质与相变机制，并检验近期理论提议。其关切点在于长程与短程普适类之间的分界，以及短程代数准长程序对弱长程扰动的稳定性。

Method: 方法组合三类手段：近乎精确的高斯自旋波理论（Gaussian spin-wave theory）、绝热重整化群分析（adiabatic renormalization-group analysis）以及大规模蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations）。分析聚焦于 \(1/r^4\) 相互作用对自旋波刚度核（spin-wave stiffness kernel）的对数修正。

Result: 结果显示低温相为对数准长程序，自旋关联按距离对数的幂衰减 \(C(r)\sim(\ln r)^{-\eta_\ell}\)，其中 \(\eta_\ell\propto T\)。\(1/r^4\) 相互作用以 \(\gamma(k)\simeq\kappa k^2\ln(1/k)\) 形式对数修正刚度核，抑制涡旋增殖（vortex proliferation），并给出非普适关联长度指数 \(\nu\propto1/\sqrt{2\pi\kappa}\)。模拟进一步表明，短程代数准长程序相对弱长程扰动在 \(\sigma\le2\) 时不稳定。

Conclusion: 结论确立 \(\sigma=2\) 为长程与短程普适类之间的跨界边界，并对近期理论提议作出批判性评估。该相变被描述为超出 Ginzburg-Landau-Wilson 范式与 Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless 机制的连续转变。

Abstract: We investigate the phase diagram and critical properties of the two-dimensional classical XY model with interactions decaying as $1/r^{2+σ}$. At the marginal case $σ=2$, we show that the low-temperature phase is characterized by logarithmic quasi-long-range order (log-QLRO), where spin correlations decay as a power of the logarithm of distance, $C(r)\sim(\ln r)^{-η_\ell}$ with $η_\ell\propto T$, and that the transition into this phase is a continuous transition beyond both the Ginzburg-Landau-Wilson paradigm and the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless mechanism. Our analysis combines a near-exact Gaussian spin-wave theory, an adiabatic renormalization-group analysis, and large-scale Monte Carlo simulations. We find that the $1/r^4$ interaction logarithmically modifies the spin-wave stiffness kernel as $γ(k)\simeqκk^2\ln(1/k)$, which suppresses vortex proliferation and gives rise to a nonuniversal correlation-length exponent $ν\propto1/\sqrt{2πκ}$. Crucially, our simulations reveal that the short-range algebraic QLRO phase is unstable against a weak long-range perturbation for $σ\le2$. These results establish $σ=2$ as the crossover boundary between the long-range and short-range universality classes and provide a critical evaluation of recent theoretical proposals.

</details>


### [145] [Phase Separation in Fractonic Fluids: Coarsening, Interfaces, Nucleation, and Hyperuniformity in and out of Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39299)
*Raphael Maire*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文构建了守恒标量密度高阶多极矩（higher-order multipole moments）的流体相分离（phase separation）理论，考察粗化动力学（coarsening dynamics）、成核（nucleation）与界面涨落（interfacial fluctuations），并预测高阶守恒律减慢弛豫、非平衡涨落—耗散定理（fluctuation–dissipation theorem）破缺抑制涨落与成核，模拟结果支持上述预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有相分离理论多关注密度守恒（零阶矩）情形，而高阶多极矩守恒对相分离各过程的影响尚不清楚。该工作旨在填补这一空白，刻画高阶守恒律与非平衡效应如何共同调控相分离行为。

Method: 发展描述标量密度高阶多极矩守恒流体中相分离的理论框架，并据此分析粗化动力学、成核及界面涨落的演化规律，辅以数值模拟进行验证。

Result: 高阶守恒律使所考察的全部现象弛豫变慢；非平衡条件下涨落—耗散定理的破缺既抑制密度涨落与界面涨落，又阻碍成核过程。模拟结果与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 高阶守恒律与非平衡涨落—耗散破缺是调控相分离动力学、界面涨落及成核的关键因素，所提出的理论框架可描述此类系统中相分离的普适行为。

Abstract: We develop a theory of phase separation in fluids conserving higher-order multipole moments of a scalar density, and investigate the resulting coarsening dynamics, nucleation, and interfacial fluctuations. We find that higher-order conservation laws slow the relaxation of all phenomena investigated, whereas nonequilibrium violations of the fluctuation--dissipation theorem suppress density and interfacial fluctuations while hindering nucleation. Simulations support these predictions.

</details>


### [146] [Square-root growth of operator entanglement in an integrable brickwork circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39401)
*Balázs Pozsgay*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文给出可积多体系统中局域算符冯·诺依曼纠缠（von Neumann entanglement）对数增长的普适预期之反例：在一个四态砖墙线路（brickwork circuit）中，若门为满足常数杨–巴克斯特方程（Yang–Baxter equation）的置换矩阵，则算符熵（operator entropy）按 $\left(\log2\right)\sqrt{t/\pi}+O(\log t)$ 增长，而非对数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前普遍预期：在无限体积的可积多体系统（integrable many-body systems）中，局域算符（local operator）的冯·诺依曼纠缠至多呈对数增长。该文旨在检验这一预期是否普遍成立，并构造反例。

Method: 采用四态砖墙线路（four-state brickwork circuit），其门为求解常数杨–巴克斯特方程（constant Yang–Baxter equation）的置换矩阵（permutation matrix）；分析该线路中算符的冯·诺依曼熵与固定指标雷尼熵（Rényi entropies），并用矩阵乘积算符（matrix product operator）模拟考察固定相对希尔伯特–施密特误差下的键维数（bond dimension）。

Result: 冯·诺依曼算符熵呈 $\left(\log2\right)\sqrt{t/\pi}+O(\log t)$ 增长；固定指标雷尼熵在指标小于 1 时线性增长、大于 1 时对数增长。在固定低于 1 的相对希尔伯特–施密特误差（relative Hilbert–Schmidt error）下，矩阵乘积算符模拟所需键维数至少按 $\exp(c\sqrt t)$ 增长（某 $c>0$）。

Conclusion: 该四态置换门线路构成可积框架下算符纠缠非对数增长的反例，表明“可积即至多对数纠缠”的预期并不普遍成立；同时其算符纠缠结构导致矩阵乘积算符表示效率随 $\sqrt t$ 指数恶化。

Abstract: It is widely expected that the von Neumann entanglement of a local operator grows at most logarithmically in integrable many-body systems in infinite volume. We give a counterexample in a four-state brickwork circuit whose gate is a permutation matrix solving the constant Yang--Baxter equation. The von Neumann operator entropy grows as $(\log2)\sqrt{t/π}+O(\log t)$, whereas fixed-index Rényi entropies grow linearly below index one and logarithmically above it. At fixed relative Hilbert--Schmidt error below one, matrix product operator simulations require a bond dimension growing at least as $\exp(c\sqrt t)$ for some $c>0$.

</details>


### [147] [The Hidden Topology of Network Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39461)
*Debasish Sarker*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文指出物理网络中拓扑（topology）与几何（geometry）不可分割。在剔除依赖拓扑的可交换边对数后，边对（edge pairs）的平均交叉倾向（mean crossing propensity）呈现精确的图无关性（graph independent），而拓扑信息保留在涨落（fluctuations）中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于物理网络中拓扑与几何相互耦合、难以分离这一问题，需要一种能在二者耦合情形下刻画网络响应（response）的理论框架。

Method: 方法上，先对依赖拓扑的合格边对数量进行因子分解（factoring out），利用可交换性（exchangeability）论证平均交叉倾向与图无关，并据此推导出一个微观、无参数（parameter-free）的理论以描述拓扑与几何耦合时的响应。

Result: 结果显示，合成（synthetic）与真实三维网络均证实：被归一化均值所掩盖的结构信息，仍留存于决定响应的相关性（correlations）之中。

Conclusion: 结论为：拓扑与几何在物理网络中不可分离，仅关注归一化均值会遗漏结构信息，而涨落与相关性是刻画网络响应的关键所在。

Abstract: Topology and geometry are inseparable in physical networks. Here we show that, after factoring out the topology-dependent number of eligible edge pairs, exchangeability makes the mean crossing propensity of those pairs exactly graph independent while preserving topology in fluctuations. We derive a microscopic, parameter-free theory for the response when topology and geometry are coupled. Synthetic and real three-dimensional networks confirm that structure hidden from the normalized mean survives in correlations that determine response.

</details>


### [148] [CYNAR: a trajectory-based estimator of entropy production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39654)
*Marco Baiesi,Ivan DI Terlizzi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了 CYNAR，一种从轨迹数据估计随机热力学中熵产生率（entropy production rate）σ 的流水线方法，基于 σ 的动力学分解（kinetic decomposition）为交通项（traffic term）T 与流入率（inflow rate）G，并在部分观测条件下给出下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从记录的轨迹推断熵产生率是随机热力学（stochastic thermodynamics）中的关键挑战。实验中驱动力通常未知，且往往只能观测到系统的部分自由度，因此需要一种在未知力与部分观测下仍可靠的方法。

Method: CYNAR 建立在近期提出的方法（Di Terlizzi, PRL 135, 237101 (2025)）之上，利用 σ 的动力学分解：交通项 T 由关联函数（correlation functions）的短时曲率推断，流入率 G 由稳态得分（steady-state score，即对数密度的梯度）计算。该分解在任意观测坐标子集上评估时均对 σ 给出下界，适用于任意扩散张量（diffusion tensor）且无需知晓隐藏自由度。作者将 CYNAR 与基于稳态概率流（steady-state probability currents）估计 σ 的标准方法在四个复杂度递增的系统上进行比较。

Result: CYNAR 表现更可靠：在接近平衡时无可检测偏差，对空间离散化依赖弱，且对测量噪声具显著鲁棒性。它同样易适用于高维系统，此时仅交通项即可给出接近 σ 的下界；在部分观测下仍具信息量，包括基于通量（flux）的估计恒为零的情形。

Conclusion: CYNAR 为从轨迹估计熵产生率提供了可靠、抗噪且适用于高维与部分观测的流水线，并已作为开源 Python 包提供。

Abstract: Inferring the entropy production rate $σ$ from recorded trajectories is a key challenge in stochastic thermodynamics. In experiments, the forces governing the dynamics are usually unknown and often only some of the system's degrees of freedom can be observed. Building on a recently introduced method [I. Di Terlizzi, Phys. Rev. Lett. 135, 237101 (2025)], we develop and validate CYNAR (computation yields nonequilibrium analysis and reconstruction), a pipeline that estimates $σ$ from trajectories. It exploits a kinetic decomposition of $σ$ into a traffic term ${\cal T}$, inferred from the short-time curvature of correlation functions, and an inflow rate ${\cal G}$, computed from the steady-state score, i.e., the gradient of the log-density. We show that the same decomposition, evaluated on any subset of observed coordinates, always bounds $σ$ from below, for any diffusion tensor and without knowledge of the hidden degrees of freedom. We then compare CYNAR with a more standard approach that estimates $σ$ from steady-state probability currents, on four systems of increasing complexity. CYNAR proves to be more reliable, with no detectable bias close to equilibrium, weak dependence on spatial discretization, and marked robustness to measurement noise. It is also readily applicable to high-dimensional systems, where the traffic alone provides a lower bound close to $σ$, and remains informative under partial observation, including cases where the flux-based estimate vanishes identically. CYNAR is provided as an open-source Python package.

</details>


### [149] [Minimizing capture time with many small traps in heterogeneous media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39990)
*Denis S. Grebenkov,Theodore Kolokolnikov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究非均匀介质中大量小吸收陷阱（absorbing traps）的最优放置问题，以最小化粒子的平均首次通过时间（MFPT）。作者区分弱捕获与强捕获两种情形，给出最优陷阱密度的解析形式，并用均匀化方法统一处理，数值优化验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子在含空间依赖扩散系数（diffusivity）D(x) 与给定初始分布 ω(x) 的异质介质中扩散，如何放置大量小吸收陷阱以最小化平均首次通过时间（mean first-passage time, MFPT）是核心问题。经典 Green 函数方法（Green's function approach）仅适用于弱捕获情形，存在适用范围局限，需发展能覆盖两种捕获情形的统一框架。

Method: 作者在二、三维中依据陷阱群对粒子的耗竭程度区分弱捕获与强捕获两种区域，并重新审视经典 Green 函数方法以界定其适用条件。随后发展均匀化方法（homogenization approach），同时捕捉两种区域；并分析一维情形及带空间依赖漂移（drift）与变截面薄域（thin domains）的推广。

Result: 弱捕获（陷阱较少）时最优陷阱密度 ρ 为归一化逆扩散率 μ∝1/D 与初始粒子分布 ω 的算术平均 ρ=½(μ+ω)；强捕获（陷阱较多）时 ρ∝√(μω)，即二者几何平均。二维中 Green 函数方法要求陷阱尺度随陷阱数指数级小，故多数实际情形落入强捕获区域。当 ω∝1/D（对应无陷阱时 Itô 解释下的平衡分布）时，最优陷阱分布即平稳粒子分布本身，且使 MFPT 在整个区域恒定，同时最小化最坏捕获时间。一维情形为 ρ∝(μω)^{1/3}。

Conclusion: 最优陷阱放置随捕获强度呈不同标度律：弱捕获为算术平均、强捕获为几何平均，二维中经典 Green 函数方法适用性受限。均匀化方法可统一描述两种情形，且 ω∝1/D 时最优分布即平衡分布本身。一维标度指数为 1/3，结果可推广至空间依赖漂移与变截面薄域，并经直接数值优化验证。

Abstract: We study the problem of optimally placing a large number of small absorbing traps to minimize the mean first-passage time (MFPT) of particles diffusing in a heterogeneous medium with space-dependent diffusivity $D(x)$ and prescribed initial particle distribution $ω(x)$. In two and three dimensions we identify two distinct regimes, depending on how strongly the trap ensemble depletes the particles. In the weak trapping regime (fewer traps) the optimal trap density $ρ$ is the arithmetic average $ρ= \frac12(μ+ω)$ of the normalized inverse diffusivity $μ\propto 1/D$ and the initial particle distribution $ω$. In the strong trapping regime (more traps), it is proportional to their geometric average, $ρ\propto \sqrt{μω}$. We revisit the classical Green's function approach and show that it applies to weak trapping only; in 2D this requires the trap size to be exponentially small in the number of traps, so that most applications of interest fall in the strong trapping regime instead. We therefore develop a homogenization approach that captures both regimes. A special choice of initial particle distribution is $ω\propto 1/D$, which corresponds to the equilibrium particle distribution in the absence of traps according to the Itô interpretation. In this case we show that the optimal trap distribution is the stationary particle distribution itself, regardless of the trapping strength, and this makes the MFPT constant throughout the domain; the same trap distribution also minimizes the worst-case capture time. Finally, in one dimension we find instead $ρ\propto\left(μω\right) ^{1/3}$, and a similar analysis covers space-dependent drift and thin domains of variable cross-section. Direct numerical optimization confirms the analytical results.

</details>


### [150] [Many-body topology in parity-preserving tensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40004)
*Maksimilian Usoltcev,Nguyen Hanh Dung,Carolin Wille,Matteo Rizzi,Alexander Altland*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究平面宇称守恒张量网络（planar parity-preserving tensor networks, ppTNs）在费米子表述下由局域相互作用诱导的高斯可收缩极限形变，并以最小双参数模型为平台，借助多种互补方法刻画其相图与临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ppTNs 的费米子表述中，高斯（Gaussian）可高效收缩极限会被局域相互作用破坏，如何在多体框架下分析此类收缩尚缺乏系统理解，故需以一个可控的最小模型为切入点。

Method: 构建最小双参数 ppTN，并利用其多重等价表述——相互作用费米子配分函数（partition function）、圈气体（loop gas）、形变环面码范数（deformed toric-code norm）与四次 Ising 模型——分别采用费米子微扰论、张量网络数值方法以及圈与自旋图像进行分析；同时引入边界扭转 $\mathbb Z_2$ 指标刻画高斯极限之外的性质。

Result: 相图中心存在一个“拓扑岛”（topological island），其超越高斯极限的特征由边界扭转 $\mathbb Z_2$ 指标刻画，该指标退化为 Chern 数奇偶性，并在对偶下具有圈绕数（loop-winding）解释。费米子微扰论预言相互作用相边界，张量网络数值确定其临界行为，圈与自旋描述揭示出一条含 $c=1$ 四态 Potts 多重临界不动点的自对偶线。

Conclusion: 多体方法可有效分析此类 ppTN 收缩：不同等价表述相互印证，共同确定相图、相边界、临界行为及自对偶线与多重临界不动点，为超越高斯极限的费米子张量网络研究提供了可操作范例。

Abstract: Planar parity-preserving tensor networks (ppTNs) admit a fermionic formulation in which the Gaussian, efficiently contractible limit is deformed by local interactions. We investigate how many-body methods can be used to analyze such contractions in a minimal two-parameter ppTN. Its contraction is simultaneously an interacting fermionic partition function, a loop gas, a deformed toric-code norm, and a quartic Ising model, allowing the same phase diagram to be approached with complementary tools. At its center lies a `topological island', characterized beyond the Gaussian limit by a boundary-twist $\mathbb Z_2$ indicator that reduces to Chern-number parity and admits a loop-winding interpretation under duality. Fermionic perturbation theory predicts the interacting phase boundaries, tensor-network numerics establish their critical behavior, and the loop and spin descriptions reveal a self-dual line with a $c=1$ four-state-Potts multicritical fixed point.

</details>


### [151] [Spectral theory for dynamical large deviations in non-Markov self-interacting processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40329)
*Francesco Coghi,Juan P. Garrahan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文为非马尔可夫跳跃过程（non-Markov jump processes）与非马尔可夫链（non-Markov chains）的动力大偏差（dynamical large deviations）建立了谱理论（spectral theory），通过多尺度 WKBJ 拟设将 Feynman–Kac 方程约化为一个倾斜算符（tilted operator）的本征值问题，并用双稳态自诱导 East 模型（bistable self-induced East model）进行示例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象是动力学依赖历史（通过状态依赖与跳跃依赖的经验观测量）的非马尔可夫过程，此类系统中的涨落量化缺乏高效的理论工具，因此需要发展相应的谱理论框架。

Method: 采用多尺度 Wentzel–Kramers–Brillouin–Jeffreys（WKBJ）拟设，将快速的构型弛豫（configurational relaxation）与缓慢的记忆演化（memory evolution）分离；由此把描述占据与通量统计的 Feynman–Kac 方程约化为一个与 Hamilton–Jacobi 特征线（characteristics）耦合的新型倾斜算符的本征值问题。

Result: 该约化给出了一个计算高效的框架，可用于量化一大类非马尔可夫系统（non-Markovian systems）中的涨落；并以双稳态自诱导 East 模型为例展示了相关一般性结果。

Conclusion: 多尺度 WKBJ 拟设可有效分离快慢自由度，从而将非马尔可夫大偏差问题转化为可求解的本征值问题，为广泛非马尔可夫系统的涨落分析提供了可行且高效的理论途径。

Abstract: We develop a spectral theory for dynamical large deviations in non-Markov jump processes and non-Markov chains, whose dynamics depends on the past through state- and jump-dependent empirical observables. We demonstrate that a multiscale Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin-Jeffreys (WKBJ) Ansatz separates fast configurational relaxation from slow memory evolution, reducing the Feynman--Kac equation for occupation and flux statistics to an eigenvalue problem for a new tilted operator coupled to Hamilton--Jacobi characteristics. This provides a computationally efficient framework for quantifying fluctuations in a broad class of non-Markovian systems. We illustrate our general results with a bistable self-induced East model.

</details>

### [152] [Measurement of the $^{39}$Ar specific activity in atmospheric argon with DArT at the Canfranc Underground Laboratory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39400)
*P. Agnes,H. O. Back,S. Bharat,W. M. Bonivento,M. Boulay,M. Caboni,N. Canci,N. Cargioli,P. Castello,S. Cebrián,L. Consiglio,R. Crampton,D. Diaz Mairena,D. Franco,I. Fuente-Ortega,F. Gabriele,D. Gahan,C. Galbiati,P. Garcia Abia,T. Hessel,S. Horikawa,A. Ianni,M. Kuźniak,L. Luzzi,M. Martínez,P. Organtini,A. Ortiz de Solórzano,R. Pavarani,P. Pegoraro,V. Pesudo Fortes,M. Razeti,O. U. Rehman,L. Romero,D. Sablone,E. Sánchez García,R. Santorelli,C. Seoane,J. Sosiak,S. Sulis,C. Tierney,S. Tullio,G. Vera Díaz*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: DArT 探测器在西班牙 Canfranc 地下实验室（LSC）测量了大气氩中 $^{39}$Ar 的比活度，结果为 $0.955 \pm 0.008~\text{Bq}/\text{kg}$，是目前最精确的测定值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气氩中 $^{39}$Ar 的比活度是低本底液氩实验（如暗物质与无中微子双贝塔衰变探测）的关键本底参数，需要更精确的测定；同时该测量也用于验证 DArT 作为 DArTInArDM 实验核心部件的能力。

Method: 使用低本底单相液氩探测器 DArT，读出采用低温硅光电倍增管（cryogenic silicon photomultipliers），位于西班牙 Canfranc 地下实验室（LSC）。探测器先充入大气氩，再充入来自 DarkSide-50 实验的已知放射性地下氩样品作为本底参照，从而避免依赖本底模拟；数据分析同时使用 cut-and-count 方法与分箱最大似然拟合（binned maximum-likelihood fit）。

Result: 两种分析方法给出一致结果，测得大气氩中 $^{39}$Ar 比活度 $a_{\text{AAr}}= 0.955 \pm 0.008~\text{Bq}/\text{kg}$，与其他实验的先前测量一致，是迄今对大气氩中 $^{39}$Ar 比活度最精确的测定。

Conclusion: 该结果验证了 DArT 作为 LSC 的 DArTInArDM 实验关键部件的性能；DArTInArDM 旨在测量从美国科罗拉多深井提取氩的 $^{39}$Ar 活度，为意大利 Gran Sasso 国家实验室（Laboratori Nazionali del Gran Sasso）的 Darkside-20k 与 LEGEND-1000 实验服务。

Abstract: We report the measurement of the $^{39}$Ar specific activity of atmospheric argon using DArT, a low-background single-phase liquid-argon detector read out by cryogenic silicon photomultipliers, at the Canfranc Underground Laboratory (LSC) in Spain. DArT was first filled with atmospheric argon and then with a sample of underground argon of known radioactivity from the DarkSide-50 experiment. The underground argon serves as a background reference in this measurement, making the result robust because we do not rely on background simulations. We measure the $^{39}$Ar specific activity with both a cut-and-count method and a binned maximum-likelihood fit, yielding consistent results. We obtain $a_{\text{AAr}}= 0.955 \pm 0.008~\text{Bq}/\text{kg}$. This result agrees with previous measurements from other experiments and provides the most precise determination of the $^{39}$Ar specific activity in atmospheric argon to date. It also validates DArT as a key component of the DArTInArDM experiment at LSC, which aims to measure the $^{39}$Ar activity of the argon extracted from deep underground wells in Colorado (USA) for the Darkside-20k and LEGEND-1000 experiments at Laboratori Nazionali del Gran Sasso in Italy.

</details>


### [153] [Performance and quality testing of CBM RICH front-end electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40318)
*Pavish Subramani,Christian Pauly,Joerg Foertsch,Dennis Pfeifer,Karl-Heinz Kampert*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本笔记介绍德国伍珀塔尔大学（University of Wuppertal）的实验装置，用于测量电荷共享串扰（charge sharing crosstalk），并在真实探测器配置下对 MAPMT-DIRICH 读出电子学（readout electronics）进行高计数率与高占据率测试，同时分析用于 CBM RICH 读出链的新一代 DIRICH 电源模块（power module）的电子噪声特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAPMT-DIRICH 读出电子学需在实际探测器条件下验证其高计数率（high rate）与高占据率（high occupancy）表现，并评估电荷共享串扰（charge sharing crosstalk）与电源噪声对读出链性能的影响。CBM RICH 读出链中所采用的新一代 DIRICH 电源模块（power module）的噪声特性亦需表征。

Method: 依托伍珀塔尔大学（University of Wuppertal）搭建的实验室测试平台（laboratory setup），在真实探测器配置下开展测量，包括电荷共享串扰测量、高计数率与高占据率测试，以及对新一代 DIRICH 电源模块诱导电子噪声（induced electronic noise）特性的分析。

Result: 摘要未给出具体测量数值或定量结果，仅说明该装置被用于完成上述测量与测试，并实现对新一轮 DIRICH 电源模块噪声特性的分析。

Conclusion: 该实验室装置可用于 MAPMT-DIRICH 读出电子学在真实探测器条件下的串扰、高计数率与高占据率评估，并为 CBM RICH 读出链所用新一代 DIRICH 电源模块的电子噪声特性提供分析手段。

Abstract: This note describes the laboratory setup at the University of Wuppertal and its use to measure charge sharing crosstalk, perform high rate and high occupancy tests of MAPMT-DIRICH readout elec- tronics using a realistic detector setup, and also includes the analysis of induced electronic noise characteristics of a new iteration of the DIRICH power module to be used in the CBM RICH readout chain.

</details>

### [154] [Spectroscopic Evidence for Nontrivial Band Topology in Superconducting FeTe/MnTe Heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39161)
*Shiwu Su,Yu Liang,Tongrui Li,Zhen Wang,Yuzhe Wang,Xianglin Li,Sen Liao,Pengxu Ran,Jiexiong Sun,Shengtao Cui,Zhe Sun,Zhengtai Liu,Jishan Liu,Mao Ye,Jing Tao,Donglai Feng,Juan Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 近化学计量比（near-stoichiometric）FeTe经高阶外延（higher-order epitaxy）稳定后呈现约13 K超导转变，ARPES与第一性原理计算共同揭示其非平凡Z2拓扑与狄拉克表面态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FeTe长期被视为非超导的反铁磁金属（antiferromagnetic metal）且带拓扑（band topology）平凡，但近期化学计量比（stoichiometry）控制的进展开始挑战这一图景。

Method: 在闪锌矿（zinc-blende）MnTe上进行高阶外延（higher-order epitaxy），以稳定间隙Fe被强烈抑制的近化学计量比FeTe；采用角分辨光电子能谱（angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, ARPES）与第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）表征其电子结构。

Result: 超导转变起始温度接近13 K；ARPES显示准粒子相干性（quasiparticle coherence）显著增强、定义良好的Fe来源空穴带（Fe-derived hole bands）以及费米能级附近近二维的类狄拉克锥态（Dirac-cone-like state）。计算识别出奇宇称与偶宇称能带（odd- and even-parity bands）之间的反转，给出非平凡Z2拓扑及与实验一致的狄拉克表面态（Dirac surface state）。

Conclusion: 结果阐明了化学计量比超导FeTe的本征电子结构，并提供了非平凡带拓扑的证据，使FeTe/MnTe成为探索拓扑超导（topological superconductivity）的有前景平台。

Abstract: FeTe has long been regarded as a nonsuperconducting antiferromagnetic metal with trivial band topology, but recent advances in stoichiometry control have begun to challenge this picture. Here we use higher-order epitaxy on zinc-blende MnTe to stabilize near-stoichiometric FeTe with strongly suppressed interstitial Fe and a superconducting transition onset near 13 K. Angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy reveals markedly enhanced quasiparticle coherence, well-defined Fe-derived hole bands, and a nearly two-dimensional Dirac-cone-like state near the Fermi level. First-principles calculations identify an inversion between odd- and even-parity bands, yielding nontrivial $Z_2$ topology and a Dirac surface state consistent with experiment. These results elucidate the intrinsic electronic structure of stoichiometric superconducting FeTe and provide evidence for nontrivial band topology, positioning FeTe/MnTe as a promising platform for exploring topological superconductivity.

</details>


### [155] [A Different Perspective on Superconductivity in Crystalline Graphene: Exploiting Energetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40104)
*Ke Wang,Shicong Song,K. Levin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出"双亲（two-parent）"能量框架，在不指定配对吸引细节的前提下，解释晶态石墨烯（crystalline graphene）超导为何普遍却常局限于不同同位旋有序金属（isospin-ordered metals）边界附近的狭窄区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶态石墨烯超导的分布异常：既广泛存在，又常被限制在相界附近的窄条区域，这一现象缺乏不依赖配对机制细节的统一解释。

Method: 采用"双亲（two-parent）"能量框架，以两个正常态同位旋有序金属为出发点，结合自由能简并的一级相变（first-order transition）分析，不预设配对吸引的具体形式。

Result: 当两个同位旋有序正常态在自由能上简并时，即便净超导能量增益很小，也可能稳定平衡超导态；该增益虽被重构正常金属（normal metal）的代价所削减，仍可实现稳定；一级简并还使超导可在两个正常态"亲本"间选择，倾向于净自由能增益最大者。

Conclusion: 该框架表明，晶态石墨烯超导的边界受限特征可由一级简并与双亲竞争解释，而无需明确配对吸引的微观细节。

Abstract: A central mystery of crystalline graphene is why superconductivity is so widespread yet often confined to strange slivers near boundaries between distinct isospin-ordered metals. In this paper, we apply a ``two-parent'' energetic framework which we show can explain this unusual form of superconductivity \textit{without} specifying the details of the necessarily present pairing attraction. First-order transitions are crucial here: when two normal isospin-ordered states are degenerate in free energy, even a small net superconducting energy gain may stabilize an equilibrium superconductor. We demonstrate how this is possible even though the small energy gain from pairing is reduced by the expense of reconstructing the normal metal, which is needed to achieve superconducting compatibility. The first order degeneracy also gives superconductivity a choice between two normal state parents, favoring the state with the largest net free energy gain.

</details>


### [156] [Electronic Reconstruction across the Tilt-Free Transition in La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40139)
*Mengjie Kong,Gergely Németh,Yingpeng Yu,Bosen Wang,Jianping Sun,Jinguang Cheng,Ferenc Borondics,Bastien Michon*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在常压下对 La₃Ni₂O₇ 的无倾斜转变（tilt-free transition，T_st≈544 K）进行宽谱红外-可见光反射率测量，发现极宽能区内的谱重（spectral weight）重分布及两个低能带间激发（interband excitations）的收敛合并，表明双层 Ni 3d_z² 电子态发生显著重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加压 La₃Ni₂O₇ 中高 T_c 超导（high-T_c superconductivity）的出现与抑制 NiO₆ 八面体（octahedra）倾斜的结构转变密切相关，但该转变对电子结构的影响仍缺乏理解。

Method: 在常压下利用宽带红外至可见光反射率（infrared-to-visible reflectivity），覆盖 15 meV 至 3.2 eV 的光子能量，探测 T_st≈544 K 处无倾斜转变前后的电子响应。

Result: 观察到异常宽能区内的显著谱重（spectral weight）重分布，谱重从 1–3 eV 的激发转移至 1 eV 以下的低能激发；最显著的是，进入无倾斜相后两个低能带间激发（interband excitations）逐步收敛并合并。

Conclusion: 结果将无倾斜转变确立为重构与高 T_c 超导相关电子自由度的直接途径，并为超导态的电子结构提供了常压参考。

Abstract: The emergence of high-$T_c$ superconductivity in pressurized La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ is intimately linked to a structural transition that suppresses the tilts of the NiO$_6$ octahedra, yet its impact on the electronic structure remains poorly understood. Here, we probe the electronic response across this tilt-free transition at $T_{st}\simeq 544$ K using broadband infrared-to-visible reflectivity at ambient pressure, covering photon energies from 15 meV to 3.2 eV. We observe a pronounced redistribution of spectral weight over an exceptionally broad energy range, with spectral weight transferred from excitations between 1 and 3 eV toward low-energy excitations below 1 eV. Most strikingly, two low-energy interband excitations progressively converge and merge upon entering the tilt-free phase, revealing a substantial reconstruction of the finite-energy electronic structure. These changes point to a reconstruction of the bilayer Ni $3d_{z^2}$-derived electronic states, whose interlayer coupling is central to proposed mechanisms of superconductivity in La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$. Our results establish the tilt-free transition as a direct route to reorganizing the electronic degrees of freedom implicated in high-$T_c$ superconductivity and provide an ambient-pressure reference for the electronic structure of the superconducting state.

</details>

### [157] [Synchronous Monte Carlo method and its application to a mean-field spin glass model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38790)
*Yoshiyuki Kabashima*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种全变量同步更新的蒙特卡洛方法，借助辅助高斯场保持细致平衡，从而适配GPU等大规模并行硬件；并结合动力学平均场理论对平均场自旋玻璃体系进行精确刻画，在随机正交模型中揭示了动力学转变温度以下广义涨落耗散定理的双斜率行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（Markov chain Monte Carlo）方法逐变量更新以满足细致平衡（detailed balance），因而无法充分利用GPU等大规模并行硬件。论文旨在构建一种可在成对相互作用（pairwise interactions）体系中实现全变量同步更新、同时仍严格满足细致平衡的蒙特卡洛方法。

Method: 通过高斯积分恒等式（Gaussian integral identity）引入辅助高斯场（auxiliary Gaussian fields），使所有变量可同时更新；该方法满足细致平衡，因而可保证收敛至正则分布（canonical distribution）。针对平均场自旋玻璃（mean-field spin glasses），采用动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT）对其动力学进行精确解析，并将方法与理论应用于呈现随机一阶转变（random first-order transition）的随机正交模型（random orthogonal model）。

Result: 在随机正交模型中，发现动力学转变温度（dynamical transition temperature）以下涨落耗散定理（fluctuation-dissipation theorem, FDT）被明显违背；响应与关联之间的关系呈现广义FDT的双斜率（two-slope）形式。模拟结果与理论预测在定量上一致。

Conclusion: 同步更新的蒙特卡洛方法在保持细致平衡与收敛保证的同时适用于并行硬件；结合DMFT可对平均场自旋玻璃动力学给出精确描述，随机正交模型中的广义FDT双斜率结构获得理论与模拟的定量相互验证。

Abstract: Standard Markov chain Monte Carlo methods update variables one at a time to satisfy detailed balance, which prevents them from fully exploiting massively parallel hardware such as graphics processing units (GPUs). We study a Monte Carlo method for systems with pairwise interactions in which all variables are updated simultaneously, made possible by auxiliary Gaussian fields introduced through the Gaussian integral identity. The method satisfies detailed balance and is therefore guaranteed to converge to the canonical distribution. For mean-field spin glasses, its dynamics can be analyzed exactly by dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT). Applying the method and the DMFT to the random orthogonal model, which exhibits a random first-order transition, we find that the fluctuation-dissipation theorem (FDT) is clearly violated below the dynamical transition temperature, and that the relation between response and correlation takes the two-slope form of a generalized FDT, with simulations and theory in quantitative agreement.

</details>


### [158] [On the Relation Between the Boson Peak and the Vibrational Phases in Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39541)
*Philip Rasmussen,Søren S. Sørensen*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究揭示玻色峰（boson peak）与振动模式从局域态到扩展但非波状（extended but non-wave-like）态的转变同时出现，并与相位相干性（phase coherence）及空间周期性（spatial periodicity）的极大值相吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色峰是玻璃态与无序体系中常见的振动特征，通常被定义为对德拜模型（Debye model）标度关系（g(E)∝E²）的偏离；尽管受到广泛关注，其振动的微观本质此前仅有零散的讨论。

Method: 摘要未提供具体方法细节；作者通过分析玻色峰出现时振动模式的性质变化，考察其与局域—扩展转变、相位相干性及空间周期性特征之间的关联。

Result: 分析显示，玻色峰的出现伴随振动模式由局域态向扩展但非波状态的转变（crossover），并与相位相干性和空间周期性的极大值相吻合。

Conclusion: 该分析为理解多种玻璃体系中玻色峰的微观起源提供了新的见解。

Abstract: The boson peak is a vibrational feature commonly found in glassy and disordered systems described as the deviation from the scaling of the Debye model ($g({E}){\propto}{E}^2$). Despite much interest, the microscopic nature of the vibrations is only anecdotally discussed. Here we report how the boson peak appears in conjunction with a crossover from localized to extended but non-wave-like and coincide with a maximum in phase coherence and spatial periodicity. Our analyses provide new insights into the microscopic origin of the boson peak across a wide range of glasses.

</details>

### [159] [On Lorentzian Lie groups of Nonzero Constant Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40017)
*Mohamed Boucetta*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有非零常截面曲率（constant sectional curvature）左不变洛伦兹度量（left-invariant Lorentzian metric）的李群，通过中心与导理想（derived ideal）的因果性质给出洛伦兹李代数的描述，并对低维情形完成分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 李群上常曲率左不变度量在黎曼情形已有经典理论（如Heintze、Nomizu、Barnet的结果），但向伪黎曼（pseudo-Riemannian）、特别是洛伦兹情形推广仍不完整，本文旨在填补该缺口并刻画相关李代数结构。

Method: 重新审视Heintze理论以刻画负常曲率黎曼李群的李代数；将Nomizu与Barnet的经典结果推广到伪黎曼情形，构造大量携带非完备常曲率左不变度量的李群；并按中心与导理想的因果性质描述非零常曲率洛伦兹李代数。

Result: 当导理想为洛伦兹型时描述尚不完整，本文在四维情形给出完全分类；证明SL(2,R)上每个非零常曲率左不变洛伦兹度量都是双不变且完备的；半单李群（semisimple Lie group）存在此类完备度量当且仅当它局部同构于SL(2,R)；并通过非中心类空左不变Killing向量场（Killing vector field）的存在性刻画SL(2,R)。

Conclusion: 论文在四维及以下完成了非零常曲率洛伦兹李代数的分类，并将半单情形下完备左不变洛伦兹常曲率度量的存在性归结为SL(2,R)的局部同构类。

Abstract: We investigate Lie groups endowed with left-invariant Lorentzian metrics of nonzero constant sectional curvature. We first revisit Heintze's theory and obtain a characterization of the Lie algebras of Riemannian Lie groups of negative constant curvature. We also extend classical results of Nomizu and Barnet to the pseudo-Riemannian setting by constructing a large family of Lie groups carrying incomplete left-invariant metrics of constant sectional curvature.
  We then describe Lorentzian Lie algebras of nonzero constant curvature according to the causal nature of their center and derived ideal. This description is complete except when the derived ideal is Lorentzian, for which we obtain a complete classification in dimension four.
  We show that every left-invariant Lorentzian metric of nonzero constant curvature on \(\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb R)\) is bi-invariant and complete. Moreover, a semisimple Lie group admits a complete left-invariant Lorentzian metric of nonzero constant curvature if and only if it is locally isomorphic to \(\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb R)\). We also characterize \(\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb R)\) through the existence of a noncentral spacelike left-invariant Killing vector field. Finally, we classify Lorentzian Lie algebras of nonzero constant curvature in dimensions at most four.

</details>

### [160] [Geometry and Mechanics of Ribbon Gridshells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38365)
*Daniel Castro,Joo-Won Hong,Hillel Aharoni,Étienne Reyssat,José Bico,Benoît Roman*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出一种由长而窄的带状条（ribbons）按预定角度交叉构成的薄片超材料（metamaterial），即带状网格壳（ribbon gridshells），并给出其粗粒化（coarse-grained）控制方程与解的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械超材料（mechanical metamaterials）的反常性质源自非平凡的介观组分（mesoscopic constituents）；受建筑与工业结构启发，作者希望引入一种可作为模型薄片超材料的结构，以在几何约束驱动下获得可控且可求解的力学行为。

Method: 将长而窄的带状条按预定角度交叉排布构成带状网格壳；在几何约束（geometric constraints）驱动下推导显式的粗粒化控制方程，并按带状条的交叉模式（intersection patterns）对解进行分类；进一步建立可调高斯曲率（Gaussian curvature）分布，构造逆设计（inverse design）问题，并在适当定义的局部条件下求解；同时分析软模（soft modes），并以实验与数值方法验证。

Result: 该结构表现出高度非线性行为，但控制方程可显式写出且解可按交叉模式分类；结构可实现任意、可调的高斯曲率分布，逆设计问题在特定局部条件下可解；软模分析经实验与数值确认，揭示出既包含刚性（rigid）又呈现反常软化（anomalously soft）行为的丰富力学特性。

Conclusion: 带状网格壳构成一类几何约束主导、却仍可解析刻画的模型薄片超材料，其可调高斯曲率与可解逆设计为定制曲率分布提供途径，软模所揭示的刚性与反常软化共存特征表明其力学内涵丰富。

Abstract: Mechanical metamaterials exhibit anomalous properties induced from non-trivial mesoscopic constituents. Inspired from architectural and industrial structures, we introduce arrangements of long, narrow ribbons intersecting at prescribed angles as a model thin-sheet metamaterial. These ribbon gridshells are shown to display highly non-linear behavior driven by the geometric constraints, nonetheless unlike most complex mechanical systems, we are able to explicitly write out the coarse-grained governing equations and to classify their solutions in terms of the intersection patterns of the ribbons. It is shown that the structure can assume arbitrary, tunable Gaussian curvature distributions, allowing us to formulate an inverse design problem, solvable under a suitably defined local condition. An analysis of the soft modes, confirmed by experiment and numerics, reveals rich mechanics which may exhibit both a rigid and an anomalously soft behaviors.

</details>


### [161] [Morphodynamics of lipid vesicles under osmotic forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38464)
*Nicholas Broussinos,David Saintillan*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该类论文通过可渗透、面积不可压缩的连续介质膜模拟，研究球形脂质囊泡在渗透冲击下的动力学，揭示由单一无量纲参数Γ主导的三阶段演化过程及其标度规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解渗透驱动下脂质囊泡膜崩塌与内陷形态形成的动力学机制及不同时间尺度，为实验观察到的囊泡套囊泡结构前体提供连续介质层面的描述。

Method: 采用具有弯曲弹性与表面黏度的可渗透、面积不可压缩流体膜的连续介质模拟，并配合线性稳定性分析与标度分析，以单个无量纲参数Γ（渗透压力与弯曲力相对强度）刻画体系动力学。

Result: 模拟显示三阶段演化：快速屈曲与崩塌、褶皱与脊线的平滑化、缓慢折叠为内陷形态；线性稳定性分析预测初始屈曲失稳的波长与增长率，屈曲时间按Γ^{-2}标度，折叠时间按Γ^{-2/3}标度，均与模拟定量吻合；能量预算显示崩塌期累积的弯曲能在弛豫期释放并由膜黏度耗散。

Conclusion: 该研究给出了渗透驱动膜崩塌与内陷的机制及彼此分离的时间尺度的连续介质描述，表明动力学由单一无量纲参数Γ统一控制并符合理论标度预测。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of spherical lipid vesicles subjected to an osmotic shock using continuum simulations of a permeable, area-incompressible fluid membrane with bending elasticity and surface viscosity. The dynamics are governed by a single dimensionless parameter $\varGamma$, which measures the relative strength of osmotic pressure and bending forces. Osmotic forcing drives a three-stage evolution comprising rapid buckling and collapse, smoothing of the resulting wrinkles and ridges, and slow folding into an invaginated morphology resembling the precursor to experimentally observed vesicle-in-vesicle structures. Linear stability analysis predicts the wavelength and growth rate of the initial buckling instability and shows that the dimensionless buckling time scales as $\varGamma^{-2}$, in quantitative agreement with simulations. At longer times, the energy budget reveals a reversal in elastic energy transfer: bending energy accumulated during buckling is released during relaxation and dissipated by membrane viscosity. A scaling analysis of the folding dynamics predicts that the dimensionless folding time scales as $\varGamma^{-2/3}$, again in excellent agreement with simulations. These results provide a continuum description of the mechanisms and widely separated timescales governing osmotically driven membrane collapse and invagination.

</details>


### [162] [Measures of Chaotic Advection in Simulations of Active Nematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38509)
*Md Mainul Hasan Sabbir,Brandon Klein,Daniel A. Beller,Kevin A. Mitchell*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文研究由ATP驱动、动力蛋白交联的微管束组成的二维活性向列相流体（active nematic fluid），关注±1/2拓扑缺陷及其编织运动引发的混沌平流（chaotic advection）。通过对比传统Beris-Edwards（BE）模型与增强向列锁定（BENL）模型，发现BENL模型能更准确复现实验中的自混合测量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该系统的+1/2拓扑缺陷如同"虚拟搅拌棒"，其编织运动产生宏观尺度混沌平流与自混合，可用拓扑熵（topological entropy）和李雅普诺夫指数（Lyapunov exponent）量化。研究旨在检验连续介质模型能否复现这些测量，以厘清描述该类流体的合适理论框架。

Method: 采用两种连续介质模型：传统Beris-Edwards（BE）模型，以及新近发展的带增强向列锁定（enhanced nematic locking, BENL）的Beris-Edwards模型。两者差异在于是否采用"向列锁定原理"（nematic locking principle），即密集细长微管束因空间位阻（steric interactions）无法独立于邻近束旋转，BENL仅在材料断裂的局部区域（如拓扑缺陷生灭附近）例外。研究中运用多种数值方法估计混沌平流的度量。

Result: 基于数值方法对两种模型估算的混沌平流度量进行比较，结果表明BENL模型比传统BE模型更准确地复现了微管基活性向列相流体中由混沌平流驱动的自混合实验测量。

Conclusion: 向列锁定原理的引入对准确描述微管基活性向列相流体的混沌平流与自混合至关重要，BENL模型在再现实验测量方面优于传统BE模型。

Abstract: Active nematics are non-equilibrium fluids composed of rod-like self-propelled units that collectively generate large-scale coherent flows. Here, we focus on a canonical experimental system: an active nematic fluid in $2D$ driven by ATP, composed of densely packed, extended microtubule (MT) bundles cross-linked by kinesin motors.
