An intriguing feature of this system is the creation and annihilation of topological defects with topological charge $\pm1/2$, due to the fracturing of the material. Experiments confirm that the positive ($+1/2$) defects serve as "virtual stirring rods" that move around each other in a complex braiding pattern. This collective braiding motion of positive defects stretches and folds the fluid itself, i.e., produces macroscale chaotic advection. The degree of self-mixing due to the chaotic advection can be measured using topological entropy and the Lyapunov exponent. Our goal is to determine whether continuum models of MT-based active nematic fluid can reproduce these measurements. To this end, we use two continuum models: the traditional Beris-Edwards (BE) model and the more recently developed Beris-Edwards model with enhanced nematic locking (BENL). The difference between the two models is the adoption of the "nematic locking principle", which states that an individual MT bundle cannot rotate independently of its neighbors due to steric interactions among elongated dense MT bundles. This principle holds in the BENL model except in small localized areas of the material domain where the material fractures, specifically near the creation and annihilation of topological defects. We employ several numerical methods to estimate measures of chaotic advection using both models. Our study shows that the BENL model more accurately reproduces experimental measures of self-mixing driven by chaotic advection in MT-based active nematic fluids.

</details>


### [163] [Modeling density variations in two-dimensional microtubule-based active nematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38520)
*Kevin A. Mitchell,Sean Ricarte,Md Mainul Hasan Sabbir,Brandon Klein,Daniel A. Beller*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出一种显式包含密度变化（density variations）的微管（microtubules, MTs）活性向列材料建模新方法，替代传统恒定密度假设的 Landau-de Gennes 模型，模拟结果更贴近实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密排列微管层是活性材料（active materials）的经典实验模型，也是细菌湍流、有丝分裂纺锤体与形态发生等生物系统的合成类似物，但现有理论模型假设微管密度恒定，无法刻画实验中显著的密度变化——而密度变化正是向列结构本身的实验特征。

Method: 传统做法采用两个场：Q-张量（Q-tensor，编码向列相有序度与取向）与流体速度，密度设为常数，源自经典 Landau-de Gennes 液晶理论。本文提出根本不同的方法，显式纳入密度变化，并给出不依赖经典各向同性-向列相变（isotropic-nematic phase transition）的拓扑缺陷（topological defects）产生与湮灭机制。

Result: 新模型模拟结果与实验视频高度相似，包括特征性的条纹图案（striation patterns）；并复现了系统被限制在圆形井（circular well）中时的重要实验行为，而现有理论无法捕捉该行为。

Conclusion: 显式考虑密度变化可显著提升对微管基活性向列材料（MT-based active nematics）的描述能力，同时提供了一条超越 Landau-de Gennes 理论、无需依赖各向同性-向列相变的缺陷动力学建模范式。

Abstract: A dense two-dimensional layer of aligned microtubules (MTs), powered by molecular motors, is a canonical laboratory model of active materials and a synthetic analog of biological systems such as bacterial turbulence, mitotic spindles, and morphogenesis. This material exhibits nematic ordering and associated topological defects, which display complex emergent dynamics, including the creation and annihilation of defects and the braiding of defects around one another in a complicated chaotic dance. Despite its prominent role in research, the MT-based active nematic material lacks a well established theoretical model that accurately captures the rich density variations prominently seen in experiments---density variations that are, in fact, the experimental signature of the nematic structure itself. The MT-system is typically modeled using two fields: the Q-tensor (encoding the order and orientation of the nematic phase) and the fluid velocity; critically, the microtubule density is assumed to be constant. This traditional model is adopted from classical Landau-de Gennes liquid crystal theory. Here, we present a fundamentally different approach to modeling MT-based active nematics that explicitly incorporates density variations, producing simulations that strongly resemble experimental videos, including the characteristic striation patterns. It also reproduces important behavior of the system confined to a circular well---behavior seen experimentally, but not captured by current theory. In crafting our model, we present an alternative to Landau-de Gennes theory for the creation and annihilation of topological defects that does not rely on the classic isotropic-nematic phase transition.

</details>


### [164] [Moving against the odds with a cyclic active Brownian ratchet: current enhancement and reversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39255)
*Theo Spornhauer,Benoît Mahault,Johannes Zierenberg*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 在非对称势（asymmetric potential）中对活性粒子（active particles）的自驱动（self-propulsion）进行周期性调制，可同时增强定向输运（rectified transport）并实现方向反转，且驱动速度本身无需改变符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索仅通过周期性调制活性强度（而非改变推进方向）来动态调控活性粒子的定向输运，并理解其背后的整流（rectification）机制。

Method: 采用理论分析（theoretical analysis）方法，刻画活性输运对推进强度的非线性响应，并分析活性诱导扩散（activity-induced spreading）与被动弛豫（passive relaxation）之间的相互作用。

Result: 理论分析界定了由两种不同整流机制主导的参数区间（regimes），并预测了电流反转（current reversal）发生的有限驱动周期范围（finite range of driving periods）。

Conclusion: 循环活性（cyclic activity）可作为动态控制定向活性输运的手段，并有望应用于活性粒子的选择性分选（selective sorting）。

Abstract: We show that periodically modulating the self-propulsion of active particles in an asymmetric potential can both enhance rectified transport and reverse its direction, even when the propulsion speed itself never changes sign. Current enhancement results from the nonlinear response of active transport to propulsion strength, whereas current reversal emerges from the interplay between activity-induced spreading and passive relaxation. Our theoretical analysis identifies the regimes governed by these distinct rectification mechanisms and predicts the finite range of driving periods over which current reversal occurs. These results establish cyclic activity as a means of dynamically controlling rectified active transport, with potential applications to the selective sorting of active particles.

</details>


### [165] [Liquid crystalline order and its impact on shape evolution of fluid lipid membranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40113)
*Ingo Nitschke,Maik Porrmann,Axel Voigt*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作将流体脂质膜的经典曲率弹性自由能动态最小化方法扩展为Surface Beris–Edwards–Helfrich模型，显式纳入膜的液晶结构，实现脂质层面局部力学与宏观生物功能尺度间的多尺度耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典曲率弹性自由能模型未显式处理构成膜的脂质分子所导致的液晶结构，而局部液晶序变化会影响弯曲刚性与表面黏度，进而影响形状演化，因此需要引入显式液晶描述以刻画多尺度耦合。

Method: 基于Lagrange–d'Alembert原理推导Surface Beris–Edwards–Helfrich模型，并在单常数近似（one-constant approximation）下给出数值算法求解相应方程。

Result: 数值求解展示了平衡形状的影响因素：除经典曲率弹性自由能所依赖的守恒表面积与封闭体积外，平衡形状还依赖弹性参数。动态演化显示出切向流动、形状演化与液晶序之间的紧密耦合。

Conclusion: 该模型揭示了脂质液晶序与膜形状及流动的强耦合关系，并为向脂质相分离（lipid phase separation）与不对称脂质双分子层（asymmetric lipid bilayers）的扩展指明了方向。

Abstract: Dynamic approaches to minimize the classical curvature-elasticity free energy for a fluid lipid membrane, are extended towards a Surface Beris--Edwards--Helfrich model. This extension explicitly treats the liquid crystal structure of the membrane, which results from the lipid molecules, that are, on average, oriented normal to the surface. Locally varying liquid crystal order leads to local variations in bending rigidities and surface viscosity and thus influences the shape evolution. This provides a multiscale coupling between the local membrane mechanics on the level of the lipids and the mesoscopic length and time scales of biological functions. The model is derived using the Lagrange--d'Alembert principle. We provide a numerical algorithm to solve the equations in the one-constant approximation and demonstrate the impact on the emerging equilibrium shapes, which not only depend on the specified conserved surface area and enclosed volume, as for the classical curvature-elasticity free energy, but also the elastic parameter. Exploring the dynamic evolution shows a tight coupling of tangential flow, shape evolution and liquid crystalline order. We further point to extensions towards lipid phase separation and asymmetric lipid bilayers.

</details>


### [166] [Fully developed active turbulence defined through a non-equilibrium phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39348)
*Lasse Bonn,Tianxiang Ma,Olga Bantysh,Wei Feng,Martin Cramer Pedersen,Guangyin Jing,Jordi Ignés-Mullol,Francesc Sagués,Nuno A. M. Araujo,Amin Doostmohammadi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究提出活性流体中湍流式集体流动的起始（onset）可由一个活性驱动的相变来刻画，该相变以系统尺度涡量节线（vorticity nodal lines）临界骨架的出现为标志。结合两类实验（微管-驱动蛋白活性向列相、游泳细菌稠密悬浮液）与活性向列相及涨落向列流体力学的大规模模拟，作者在临界活性阈值处同时识别出几何上的逾渗（percolation）转变与力学上的刚性逾渗（rigidity percolation）转变，并证明二者在满足细致平衡（detailed balance）的平衡系统中不存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡系统对平衡统计物理（equilibrium statistical physics）中相与相变的标准定义构成挑战；活性流体中微观尺度的持续能量注入使系统本质上远离平衡，并产生类湍流的集体流动，但其起始点一直缺乏明确定义。因此需要为“充分发展的活性湍流（fully developed active turbulence）”建立可实验检验的定义，并将微观活性与宏观几何及力学联系起来。

Method: 方法上结合两类独立实验——微管-驱动蛋白（microtubule kinesin）活性向列相与游泳细菌稠密悬浮液——以及活性向列相和涨落向列流体力学（fluctuating nematohydrodynamics）的大规模模拟。分析围绕涡量场节线的几何结构与涡旋中心（vortex centres）的力学连通性展开，并以逾渗统计作为刻画手段。

Result: 结果表明该转变发生在临界活性阈值处：阈值以下，涡量结构呈碎片化，涡旋中心仅形成有限的、力学上柔软的（floppy）网络；阈值以上，涡量场节线发生逾渗，涡旋中心形成刚性且贯穿整个系统的团簇。几何转变以涡量节线的临界逾渗统计为特征，力学转变则体现为涡旋中心满足刚性逾渗统计的构型出现。

Conclusion: 两类转变在服从细致平衡的平衡系统中均不存在，说明该相变本质上是非平衡的。这些结果给出了充分发展活性湍流的可实验获取的定义，将微观活性与生命系统的宏观几何和力学联系起来。

Abstract: Non-equilibrium systems challenge the standard definitions of phases and phase transitions from equilibrium statistical physics. For example, in active fluids continuous energy injection at the microscale drives the system intrinsically out of equilibrium. This process gives rise to collective turbulent-like flows whose onset remains poorly defined. Here we show that the onset corresponds to an activity-driven phase transition, marked by the emergence of a system-spanning critical backbone of vorticity nodal lines. Combining two independent experiments, microtubule kinesin active nematics and dense suspensions of swimming bacteria, with large-scale simulations of active nematics and fluctuating nematohydrodynamics, we show that this transition occurs at a critical activity threshold. Below this threshold, vorticity structures are fragmented and vortex centres form only finite, mechanically floppy networks. Above this threshold, the nodal lines of the vorticity field percolate and the vortex centres form a rigid, system-spanning cluster. The geometric transition is characterised by the emergence of critical percolation statistics for the vorticity nodal lines, while the mechanical transition reflects the emergence of a vortex configuration that shows rigidity percolation statistics for vortex centres. We show that both transitions are absent in equilibrium systems that obey detailed balance, demonstrating that the transition is intrinsically non-equilibrium. These results establish an experimentally accessible definition of fully developed active turbulence, linking microscopic activity to macroscopic geometry, and mechanics of living systems.

</details>


### [167] [The hidden side of paintbrushes: a geometry shaped by power laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39410)
*Raphaelle Taub,Jérôme Crassous,Thomas Salez,Frédéric Restagno,Christophe Poulard*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 对数百支画笔的实测研究揭示出普适标度律（universal scaling laws）：刷毛长度与直径的关系受刷毛末端形状约束，并共同决定画笔在有无颜料时的力学响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绘画工具自史前时期即由动物与植物材料制成，其制作技术在演化中趋向优化形状；该研究旨在揭示画笔设计背后是否存在可定量描述的普适物理原理。

Method: 对数百支画笔进行实验测量，分析刷毛长度与直径之间的关系，并考察刷毛末端形状对其力学响应的调控作用；分别在施加颜料与不施加颜料的条件下测试其机械行为。

Result: 发现刷毛长度与直径之间的标度关系与刷毛末端形状相关联；对于圆头画笔（round brushes），颜料印记尺寸随压缩距离（compression distance）按标度关系变化，即仅取决于画家施加的力，而与画笔自身的力学响应无关。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了支配画笔设计及其对艺术技法影响的普适物理原理。

Abstract: Since prehistoric times, painters have used brush-like tools made from animal and plant materials to apply pigments to rock surfaces. Over time, techniques for creating brushes evolved, and converged towards optimized shapes. Our study performed over hundreds of brushes reveals universal scaling laws. In particular, we uncover a relation between bristle length and diameter linked to the shape of the bristle ends, which governs their mechanical response with and without paint. For round brushes, we found that the size of the paint stain scales with the compression distance, meaning it only depends on the force applied by the painter and not on the mechanical responses of the brushes. These results shed light on the underlying universal physical principles governing brush design and their impact on artistic techniques.

</details>


### [168] [Active spinners drive passive matter into chiral rotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39439)
*Dennis Schorn,Anpuj Nair S,Stijn van der Ham,Benno Liebchen,Hanumantha Rao Vutukuri*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验与数值模拟证明，纯旋转的磁性自旋体（magnetic spinners）可将手性（chirality）与边缘电流（edge currents）动力学传递给被动胶体物质，并在组成与吸引强度改变时出现"内外翻转"的互补手性态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性自旋体（active spinners）注入角动量但不产生持续平移，构成不同于沿弯曲轨迹运动的自驱动粒子的手性活性物质（chiral active matter）。已有研究显示自旋体流体可自组织为带环流边缘电流的旋转团簇，但角动量注入本身能否单独将手性与边缘电流动力学传递给普通被动物质，仍是未解问题。

Method: 采用实验与数值模拟相结合的方式，使用纯旋转的磁性自旋体与被动胶体（passive colloids）混合体系，通过改变被动粒子分数（passive-particle fraction）、体系组成及自旋体间吸引力来考察组织行为，并构建极简模型（minimal model）加以验证。

Result: 在高被动粒子分数下，活性自旋体组装成被被动物质包围的旋转团簇；改变组成与自旋体吸引力后，组织方式发生"内外翻转"，被动胶体形成被手性自旋体流体包围的旋转团簇。被动转子表现出边缘电流，并与活性对应物具有相同的角速度标度关系 |Ω| ~ R^{-2}。极简模型表明，自旋体介导的横向（transverse）与非互易（non-reciprocal）相互作用足以重现这些互补手性态及其边界驱动动力学。

Conclusion: 研究确立了活性自旋体的角动量注入可作为一条途径，使原本被动的物质获得手性、集体旋转与边界输运（boundary transport）能力，从而将手性功能赋予未被驱动的材料组分。

Abstract: Active spinners inject angular momentum into their surroundings without persistent translation, providing a route to chiral active matter distinct from self-propelled particles moving along curved trajectories. Recent studies have shown that spinner fluids can self-organize into rotating clusters with circulating edge currents characteristic of odd-viscous fluids. Whether angular-momentum injection alone can transmit chirality and edge-current dynamics to ordinary passive matter - and thus serve as a generic mechanism for imparting chiral functionality to undriven material components - remains an open question. Here, we show experimentally and numerically that purely rotational magnetic spinners can transfer chirality and edge-current dynamics to passive colloidal matter. At high passive-particle fraction, active spinners assemble into rotating clusters surrounded by passive matter. Remarkably, upon changing composition and spinner attraction, this organization turns inside out: passive colloids form rotating clusters surrounded by a chiral spinner fluid. These passive rotors exhibit edge currents and the same angular-velocity scaling, $|Ω|\sim R^{-2}$, as their active counterparts. A minimal model shows that spinner-mediated transverse and non-reciprocal interactions are sufficient to reproduce these complementary chiral states and their boundary-driven dynamics. Our results establish angular-momentum injection by active spinners as a route to endow otherwise passive matter with chirality, collective rotation and boundary transport.

</details>


### [169] [Curvature-induced migration of small domains on lipid membranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39772)
*Jaime Agudo-Canalejo*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种小畴（small-domain）描述，针对形状被固定的膜，将局部 Helfrich 能量与小面积等周展开结合，得到由局部平均曲率与高斯曲率决定的位置相关能量，从而区分弯曲刚度、自发曲率、高斯弯曲模量与线张力等难以分离的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 膜曲率已知会影响脂质或蛋白畴的位置，但弯曲刚度（bending rigidity）、自发曲率（spontaneous curvature）、高斯弯曲模量（Gaussian bending modulus）与线张力（line tension）的作用在一般情况下难以分离。作者希望建立可在固定膜形状下解析描述畴定位的框架。

Method: 假设膜形状固定（例如通过强黏附于弯曲基底），结合局部 Helfrich 能量与小面积等周展开（small-area isoperimetric expansion），得到仅由局部平均曲率（mean curvature）与高斯曲率（Gaussian curvature）决定的位置依赖能量。随后识别出四种曲率偏好（curvature preference）区制，其边界只依赖两个无量纲参数。

Result: 在具有复杂曲率的特定膜形状上，该曲率偏好决定小畴所经历的能量景观（energy landscape），且由于线张力贡献，能量景观强烈依赖畴的尺寸。例如在扁球形（oblate）膜上，极小畴倾向定位于极点，而较大畴偏好定位于赤道；该转变是连续还是非连续，强烈依赖畴的自发曲率。

Conclusion: 该尺寸依赖的定序表明一种机制：小畴可先在一个位置聚集与融合，随后在其生长过程中再迁移到其他位置。

Abstract: Membrane curvature has been shown to bias the location of lipid or protein domains, but the roles of bending rigidity, spontaneous curvature, Gaussian bending modulus, and line tension are difficult to separate in general. We develop a small-domain description for a membrane whose shape is held fixed, e.g. by strong adhesion to a curved substrate. Combining a local Helfrich energy with the small-area isoperimetric expansion gives a position-dependent energy determined by the local mean and Gaussian curvatures. We identify four generic regimes of curvature preference whose boundaries depend on only two dimensionless parameters. On specific membrane shapes with complex curvature, we show that this curvature preference determines the energy landscapes experienced by small domains, which are strongly dependent on the size of the domain through the line tension contribution. For example, on an oblate-shaped membrane, very small domains tend to localize at the poles, whereas larger domains prefer to localize at the equator. Whether this transition is continuous or discontinuous is found to strongly depend on the spontaneous curvature of the domain. This ordering suggests a mechanism by which small domains can collect and coalesce at one location before relocating as they grow.

</details>


### [170] [Fluctuation-induced ionic friction at solid-electrolyte interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40142)
*Damien Toquer,Baptiste Coquinot,Nikita Kavokine,Lydéric Bocquet*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文结合分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations）与理论建模，研究固体内部涨落（internal fluctuations）如何影响固-电解质界面（solid–electrolyte interface）输运，并量化固体激发（solid excitations）对离子摩擦（ionic friction）的贡献。结果表明离子摩擦源于多体效应（many-body effects）主导的集体离子模式，而非单个拖曳力，且高频分子模式具有离子特异性，可用于离子光谱分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解固体自身内部涨落对固-电解质界面输运的影响，并定量刻画固体激发在离子摩擦中所扮演的角色，这一机制此前未被充分阐明。

Method: 方法上，将分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations）与理论建模（theoretical modeling）相结合，用于分析界面处电解质对固体涨落的动力学响应及摩擦来源。

Result: 结果显示，与纯水相比，离子的存在增强了电解质的动力学响应（dynamical response），从而产生涨落诱导的界面摩擦（fluctuation-induced interfacial friction）中的离子贡献；集体离子模式（collective ionic modes）主导耗散，说明离子摩擦来自多体效应（many-body effects）而非单个拖曳力；此外，高频分子模式（high-frequency molecular modes）表现出离子特异性（ion-specific）。

Conclusion: 结论指出，离子摩擦的多体起源与离子特异性高频模式为离子的光谱分离（spectral separation of ions）提供了可能路径，并对海水淡化（desalination）、过滤（filtration）与能量转换（energy conversion）具有潜在意义。

Abstract: In this article, we explore how transport at a solid--electrolyte interface is affected by the internal fluctuations of the solid and quantify the role of solid excitations on ionic friction. Combining molecular dynamics simulations with theoretical modeling, we show that the presence of ions enhances the dynamical response of the electrolyte compared to pure water. This leads to an ionic contribution to the fluctuation-induced interfacial friction. We show that collective ionic modes dominate the dissipation, such that ionic friction arises from many-body effects rather than from individual drag forces. Furthermore, high-frequency molecular modes are found to be ion-specific, suggesting possible routes toward spectral separation of ions, with implications for desalination, filtration, and energy conversion.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Exact Operator Complexity Measures from Random Matchgate Unitaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38300)
*Gregory A. L. White,Jens Eisert,Neil Dowling*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究了随机 matchgate 系综（random matchgate ensembles）中多种海森堡表象（Heisenberg-picture）复杂度量的典型行为，核心工具是建立在马约拉纳基（Majorana basis）下的可处理自由费米子幺正 Weingarten 演算（free-fermionic unitary Weingarten calculus）。结果表明，即便电路经典可模拟，算子纠缠（operator entanglement）与算符稳定子熵（operator stabiliser entropy）仍满足体积律（volume laws），乱序关联子（OTOC）亦可指数衰减，说明晚期饱和值本身并不见证计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Matchgate 电路描述物理相关情形下的经典可处理动力学，却又展现复杂量子系统特征（如高 magic 量），由此引出问题：典型复杂度量在平凡动力学下被饱和到何种程度。该文旨在厘清量子资源（quantum resources）与模拟复杂度（simulation complexity）之间的区别及其对所用表象的依赖。

Method: 提出一种可处理且可推广的自由费米子幺正 Weingarten 演算，完全以马约拉纳基表述，专用于计算算子矩，并化简为依赖算子泛函所规定边界条件的组合学问题。基于该框架推导典型局域算子纠缠、算符稳定子熵与高阶乱序关联子的闭式表达。

Result: 对扩展型初始马约拉纳串（extensive Majorana strings），算子纠缠与稳定子熵均服从体积律，尽管底层电路经典可模拟；乱序关联子同样可变为指数小，构成涌现自由独立性（emergent free independence）的经典可处理实例。此外，超出高斯基线（Gaussian baseline）的算子纠缠为掺杂 matchgate 电路中非高斯门数量的下界。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了计算随机 matchgate 精确平均的实用工具箱，并阐明量子资源与模拟复杂度之间的区别，以及二者对所用表示方式的依赖性；晚期 OTOC 饱和值不能单独作为计算困难的判据。

Abstract: Matchgate circuits describe classically tractable dynamics in a physically relevant setting while nonetheless exhibiting features characteristic of complex quantum systems, such as high amounts of magic. This invites the broader question: to what extent are typical complexity measures saturated under trivial dynamics? Here, we address this by studying the behaviour of different Heisenberg-picture complexity measures in random matchgate ensembles. Our main technical contribution is a tractable and generalisible free-fermionic unitary Weingarten calculus formulated entirely in the Majorana basis. This method is tailored to computing moments of operators, and simplifies to a combinatorics problem which depends on the boundary conditions prescribed by a given operator functional. Using this framework, we derive closed-form expressions for the typical local-operator entanglement, operator stabiliser entropy, and higher-order out-of-time-ordered correlators. For extensive initial Majorana strings, both operator entanglement and stabiliser entropy obey volume laws, even though the underlying circuits remain classically simulable. Out-of-time-ordered correlators can likewise become exponentially small, showing that their late-time saturation value alone does not witness computational hardness and constituting a classically tractable example of emergent free independence. Finally, we relate these separations to operator resource theories, showing how operator entanglement beyond the Gaussian baseline lower-bounds the number of non-Gaussian gates in doped matchgate circuits. Our results present a practical toolbox for computing exact averages over random matchgates, while clarifying the distinction between quantum resources versus simulation complexity, and their dependence on the representation being exploited.

</details>


### [172] [Excitation gap of a blockade structure with $\mathbb{Z}_2$ topological order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39475)
*Simon Fell,Tobias F. Maier,Hans Peter Büchler,Nicolai Lang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了里德堡（Rydberg）平台启发下某类仅含两体阻塞（blockade）相互作用哈密顿量在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下存在有限激发能隙，并推广至实现非阿贝尔量子双重（non-Abelian quantum double）相的更广泛模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统在热力学极限下能隙（spectral gap）的严格数学证明极为困难，但对物质量子相（quantum phases of matter）的分类具有基础性意义。既有工作提出了与里德堡平台相关的具体模型，但其能隙是否存在尚待严格论证。

Method: 证明基于已知的能隙稳定性（gap stability）结果，并利用所研究模型的局域对称性（local symmetry）。

Result: 证明了 Maier 等人提出的哈密顿量存在有限激发能隙；该模型仅含两能级系统间的两体阻塞相互作用，其基态处于环面码（toric code）相并具有拓扑序（topologically ordered）。该结论亦适用于 Büchler 等人提出的、实现非阿贝尔量子双重相的一类更广泛的阻塞哈密顿量。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在具有局域对称性的阻塞相互作用模型中，可通过能隙稳定性论证严格建立热力学极限下的有限能隙，从而为相关拓扑有序相的分类提供数学支撑。

Abstract: Mathematically rigorous statements on the spectral gap of quantum many-body systems in the thermodynamic limit are notoriously difficult to prove -- yet they are of fundamental importance for classifying quantum phases of matter. Here we prove the existence of a finite excitation gap for a particular Hamiltonian which was proposed in [T. F. Maier et al., PRX Quantum 6, 030340 (2025)] and is motivated by the Rydberg platform. The Hamiltonian exhibits only two-body blockade interactions between two-level systems and has a topologically ordered ground state in the toric code phase. We show that our result also applies to a broader class of blockade Hamiltonians which realize non-Abelian quantum double phases and were proposed in [H. P. Büchler et al., Phys. Rev. B 114, 065113 (2026)]. The proof builds on known gap stability results and exploits the local symmetry of the studied models.

</details>


### [173] [Sharp Quantum Entropy Mixing Rates and the Operator Layer Cake Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39814)
*Alexander Stottmeister*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了关于二元量子系综混合速率的 Bravyi 猜想的最优常数 $c=1$，通过算符层蛋糕定理（operator layer cake theorem）给出的对易子精确积分表示完成证明，并由此得到维度无关的熵变化极限及纠缠速率的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：哈密顿演化下量子系综的冯·诺依曼熵（von Neumann entropy）以何种速率变化。Bravyi 提出小增量混合猜想（small incremental mixing conjecture），其后被归约为矩阵不等式并猜测最优常数为 $c=1$，此前尚未得证，故本文旨在完成该证明。

Method: 采用由 Cheng 和 Liu 提出的算符层蛋糕定理（operator layer cake theorem），获得对易子 $[A,\log B]$ 的精确积分表示，据此证明关于正迹类算符（positive trace-class operators）$A\leq B$ 的矩阵不等式，最优常数为 $c=1$。

Result: 证得最优常数 $c=1$。由此得到对一元演化改变二元量子系综熵的速率给出与维度无关的尖锐极限；混合速率满足最优常数下的小增量混合；二分哈密顿量（bipartite Hamiltonians）的纠缠速率被 $(2\log d+1)\|H\|$ 所界定。

Conclusion: 该结果证明并确立了 Bravyi 猜想的最优常数形式，给出维度无关的熵变速率上界，同时修正了 Lieb 与 Vershynina 关于一般系综混合速率的猜想。

Abstract: At what rate does the von Neumann entropy of an ensemble of quantum states change under Hamiltonian evolution of its constituents? Bravyi proposed the small incremental mixing conjecture controlling the mixing rate of a binary ensemble $\{(1\!-\!p,ρ_1),(p,ρ_2)\}$ by $c\,\|{H}\|h_{2}(p)$ (with the binary entropy $h_{2}$). The proof of Bravyi's conjecture was subsequently reduced to the matrix inequality $\|[A,\log B]\|_{1}\!\leq\! c\,h_{2}(p)$ for positive trace-class operators $A\!\leq\! B$ with $\text{Tr} A\!=\!p$ and $\text{Tr} B\!=\!1$ on separable Hilbert spaces by Mariën, Audenaert, Van Acoleyen, and Verstraete, and they conjectured the optimal constant to be $c\!=\!1$. Here, I prove the latter conjecture using an exact integral representation of the commutator $[A,\log B]$ obtained from the operator layer cake theorem, due to Cheng and Liu. The result gives a sharp dimension-independent limit on how rapidly unitary evolution of one component can change the entropy of a binary quantum ensemble. It follows that mixing rates satisfy small incremental mixing with the optimal constant, and entangling rates of bipartite Hamiltonians are bounded by $(2\log d\!+\!1)\|H\|$. Moreover, the result corrects a conjecture by Lieb and Vershynina for mixing rates of general ensembles.

</details>


### [174] [Haah's 3D cubic code thermalizes rapidly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39959)
*Sebastian Stengele,Libor Caha,Ángela Capel,Simone Warzel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作严格证明 Haah 的三维立方码（cubic code）在任意正温度下均快速热化，从而否定其作为自纠错量子存储器（self-correcting quantum memory）的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立方码被提出作为三维自纠错量子存储器的候选方案，其依据是缺乏弦状逻辑算符（string logical operators）以阻碍热错误。然而其是否为自纠错量子存储器长期悬而未决，数值上虽探讨过记忆时间，却缺乏解析上界。

Method: 证明依托于既有框架（Commun. Math. Phys. 407, 195 (2026)），将关联衰减条件（decay-of-correlations condition）转化为 Davies 生成元（Davies generator）的修正对数 Sobolev 不等式（modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality）。

Result: 立方码在每个正温度下都快速热化，即具有快速的混合（rapid mixing）行为，因此不存在自纠错所需的稳定性。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个针对任意分形子码（fracton code）在全部正温度下快速混合的解析证明，解决了立方码自纠错性质的长期开放问题。

Abstract: Haah's 3D cubic code was introduced as a candidate for a self-correcting quantum memory in three dimensions, using the absence of string logical operators to obstruct thermal errors. It has been a long-standing open question whether it is a self-correcting quantum memory. Even though the memory time was explored numerically, no analytical upper bound was known. Here, we settle this question rigorously and show that the cubic code thermalizes rapidly at every positive temperature. Our proof relies on the framework of [Commun. Math. Phys. 407, 195 (2026)] to turn a decay-of-correlations condition into a modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality for the Davies generator. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first analytic proof of rapid mixing for all positive temperatures for any fracton code.

</details>


### [175] [Predicting properties of Scrooge ensembles with high accuracy and low sample complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39992)
*Yue Wu,Yuzhi Tong,Helen Propson,Yihui Quek,Liang Mao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对 Scrooge 系综（Scrooge ensemble）高阶矩（higher-order moments）难以解析与制备的问题，提出多项式近似方法，并据此给出高效的量子算法与块编码（block encoding）构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Scrooge 系综在量子多体动力学与量子信息中有重要应用，其统计性质由高阶矩刻画，但因含非多项式 Haar 积分核（non-polynomial Haar integrand）而难以分析；既有采样机制依赖昂贵的后选择（postselection），在大系统尺度下效率低下。

Method: 引入对 Scrooge 第 k 阶矩的多项式近似，误差可调至某阈值；基于该近似给出估计可观测期望值的量子算法；在已知 ρ 制备电路时构造 Scrooge 第 k 阶矩的块编码；并提出高效实现向 ρ^{⊗k} 的 k 重对称子空间（k-fold symmetric subspace）投影的子程序。

Result: 近似误差阈值相较既有结果由 1/||ρ||_∞ 的逆多项式改进为指数级；算法使用 Õ(k^3/ε^5) 份 ρ 副本估计期望值；对称子空间投影子程序仅需 O(k^3/ε^2) 份 ρ 样本，避免了朴素的 k! 后选择代价。

Conclusion: 该工作为 Scrooge 系综性质的理论分析与实际预测提供了新工具，并有望应用于量子多体动力学与量子信息处理。对称子空间投影子程序本身亦具独立价值。

Abstract: The Scrooge ensemble associated with a density matrix $ρ$ is the maximally random ensemble whose average density matrix is $ρ$. It has attracted growing interest for its applications to quantum many-body dynamics and quantum information. The intrinsic statistical properties of the Scrooge ensembles are encoded in their higher-order moments, yet they are nontrivial to analyze and prepare for they feature a non-polynomial Haar integrand, blocking direct use of Haar integration machinery. Existing physical mechanisms to sample multiple copies of states from the Scrooge ensembles, meanwhile, typically require costly postselection, rendering them inefficient for large system size. In this work, we develop efficient and accurate methods for analyzing and implementing Scrooge moments. We first introduce a polynomial approximation to the Scrooge $k$-th moments whose error is tunable down to a threshold that improves from inverse polynomial to exponential in $1/||ρ||_\infty$ over previous results. Building on our approximation, we then give the first efficient quantum algorithm that estimates observable expectation values using $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(k^3/ε^5)$ copies of $ρ$ for any target error $ε$ above this threshold. We further provide a construction of a block encoding of the Scrooge $k$-th moment when the preparation circuit of $ρ$ is known. Of independent interest could be a key subroutine in our algorithms, which efficiently implements projection onto the $k$-fold symmetric subspace for $ρ^{\otimes k}$ using $\mathcal{O}(k^3/ε^2)$ samples of $ρ$, avoiding the naive $k!$ postselection cost of such a projection. These results provide new tools for both the theoretical analysis and practical prediction of properties of Scrooge ensembles, with potential applications to quantum many-body dynamics and quantum information processing.

</details>


### [176] [Transmitting algebras through quantum channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40054)
*Robert Salzmann,Satvik Singh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立有限维 $C^*$-代数（$C^*$-algebra）经量子信道（quantum channel）精确传输的理论，将零误差信息论（zero-error information theory）与算子代数纠错（operator-algebraic error correction）统一于按嵌入排序的代数类型传输集合中，并研究该集合是否存在支配代数（dominating algebra）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限维 $C^*$-代数可描述经典-量子混合信息（hybrid classical-quantum information），其中量子系统维数可依赖经典消息，因而需要一套统一框架刻画可精确传输的代数类型。核心动机是判断可传输代数集合是否存在一个支配代数，使所有可传输代数均可嵌入其中。

Method: 允许任意编码与解码（encoding and decoding），构造按嵌入关系排序的代数类型传输集合；引入混合容量（hybrid capacities）以重构唯一可能的支配代数类型；并用极小禁代数类型（minimal forbidden algebra types）给出支配性的完整有限刻画。

Result: 支配性在小维数下即可失效；其缺失时会出现多个不可比较的极大代数，对应同一信道的不同最优使用方式。对于算子系统（operator system）与 $*$-代数图同构的信道（含高度可分信道）以及单次零误差量子容量为零的信道，可识别支配代数。张量积（tensor product）下联合编码产生新代数类型，形成 Shannon 理论超可加性（superadditivity）的代数类比；两信道各自存在支配代数时其联合使用仍可能失败。还构造出 $n$ 重张量幂具有双指数多个极大代数类型的信道。

Conclusion: 新可传输代数类型可在任意大块长（block-length）处出现，故该信道的完整传输结构无法由任何有限块长码集合生成，表明零误差量子信息传输具有本质上的非有限可生成性。

Abstract: We develop a theory of exact transmission of finite-dimensional $C^*$-algebras through quantum channels. These algebras describe hybrid classical-quantum information, allowing the dimension of the quantum system to depend on the classical message. Allowing arbitrary encodings and decodings yields a transmission set of algebra types, ordered by embedding, that unifies zero-error information theory with operator-algebraic error correction. We ask if this set admits a dominating algebra into which every transmittable algebra embeds. Our central finding is that domination can fail even in small dimensions. In its absence, several incomparable maximal algebras can describe different optimal uses of the same channel, forcing the user to select one depending on the operational task and the type of information to be preserved. We introduce hybrid capacities that reconstruct the only possible dominating algebra type and present a complete finite characterization of domination using minimal forbidden algebra types. We identify dominating algebras for channels whose operator systems are graph-isomorphic to $*$-algebras, including highly divisible channels, and for channels with zero one-shot zero-error quantum capacity. Under tensor products, we prove that joint coding can produce new algebra types, giving an algebraic analogue of superadditivity from Shannon theory. Domination can fail for the joint use of two channels, even when both channels separately admit dominating algebras. Finally, we construct a channel whose $n$-fold tensor powers have doubly exponentially many maximal algebra types, attaining the largest possible growth scaling for finite-dimensional channels. Consequently, new transmittable algebra types appear at arbitrarily large block-lengths for this channel, so its full transmission structure cannot be generated from any finite collection of bounded-block-length codes.

</details>


### [177] [Generalized Reimpell-Werner Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40203)
*Shihao Ru,Bikun Li,Weibo Gao,Liang Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将 Reimpell-Werner 迭代（Reimpell-Werner iteration）推广至具有任意 Hermitian 代价矩阵（Hermitian cost matrices）的线性目标，在满足支撑重叠（support overlap）条件的初始化下证明迭代收敛到全局最优，并给出渐近 O(1/ε) 的迭代复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子测量与信道（quantum measurements and channels）决定量子协议中信息的提取、编码与传输，其性能优化需要随 Hilbert 空间维度（Hilbert space dimensions）增长仍具实用性的数值方法。Reimpell-Werner 迭代以尊重约束的重复矩阵更新提供实用途径，但其收敛性的理论保证与适用问题类别尚需拓展。

Method: 将 Reimpell-Werner 迭代推广到以任意 Hermitian 代价矩阵表示的线性目标（linear objectives），并通过分析迭代格式的收敛性，在适当的支撑重叠初始化条件（support overlap conditions）下进行理论证明。

Result: 证明当初始化满足合适的支撑重叠条件时，迭代点收敛到全局最优（global optimum）；对每个固定问题、迭代参数选择与可容许初始化，渐近地 O(1/ε) 次迭代足以使目标值进入最优值 ε 邻域。

Conclusion: 该工作为 Reimpell-Werner 迭代提供了严格的理论基础（rigorous foundation），并扩大了其收敛保证所适用的优化问题类别。

Abstract: Quantum measurements and channels determine how information is extracted, encoded, and transmitted in quantum protocols. Optimizing their performance often requires numerical methods that remain practical as Hilbert space dimensions increase. The Reimpell-Werner iteration offers a practical approach to these tasks through repeated matrix updates that respect the constraints. Here, we generalize this iteration to linear objectives with arbitrary Hermitian cost matrices. We prove that the iterates converge to a global optimum whenever the initialization satisfies suitable support overlap conditions. For each fixed problem, choice of iteration parameters, and admissible initialization, $\mathcal{O}(1/\varepsilon)$ iterations suffice asymptotically to bring the objective value within $\varepsilon$ of the optimum. These results provide a rigorous foundation for the iteration and broaden the class of optimization problems to which its convergence guarantees apply.

</details>


### [178] [Quantum Markov State Models for Metastable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40214)
*Hao-En Li,Lin Lin,Michael Ragone*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作构建了量子马尔可夫状态模型（quantum Markov state models, QMSM），在系统缓慢演化假设下，将开放量子系统的长期动力学压缩到包含经典扇区与量子矩阵块的小型物理状态空间中，并给出最优误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统长期会丢失大部分微观信息，仅剩少数自由度主导其动力学。经典马尔可夫状态模型（classical Markov state models, MSMs）虽能描述亚稳态（metastable）动力学，但其相位标签无法刻画亚稳态间可能残留的量子相干（quantum coherence）；且即便已知慢模，其谱投影（spectral projection）未必产生合法量子态。

Method: 假设演化信道（channel）$\mathcal C$ 二次作用时变化极小，缺陷 $\eta=\|\mathcal C^2-\mathcal C\|_\diamond$ 足够小，且慢自由度数目与全系统尺寸无关。在此假设下构造压缩与重构信道，二者复合可精确恢复所有约化态，反向复合给出精确幂等（idempotent）信道以逼近 $\mathcal C$。

Result: 所构造方案在所有输入态上给出最优的 $\mathcal{O}(\eta^{1/3})$ 菱形范数（diamond-norm）界，对由 $\mathcal C$ 制备的亚稳态则改进为 $\mathcal{O}(\eta^{1/2})$，其常数仅依赖慢维度。约化转移信道可迭代以预测微观动力学且误差可控，并回答了 Kitaev 的精确舍入（exact-rounding）问题。以弱驱动耗散自旋链（weakly driven dissipative spin chain）为例，验证其可支持亚稳态逻辑量子比特（logical qubit）或长寿命经典相。

Conclusion: QMSM 为开放量子系统的长期动力学提供了兼具经典扇区与量子矩阵块的有效约化描述，在给定假设下实现了可控误差的压缩、重构与信道幂等化，并可迭代预测微观动力学。

Abstract: Open quantum systems can rapidly lose most microscopic information, leaving only a few degrees of freedom to govern their long-time dynamics. Classical Markov state models (MSMs) describe such metastable dynamics as transitions between a few representative phases and are a well-established tool in condensed matter and chemical physics. However, phase labels alone cannot capture quantum coherence that may persist between metastable states. Even when the slow modes are known, their spectral projection need not produce valid quantum states. We construct quantum Markov state models (QMSMs) that describe the surviving information and its evolution on a small physical state space containing classical sectors and quantum matrix blocks. We assume that the evolution channel $\mathcal C$ changes little when applied a second time, with sufficiently small defect $η=\|\mathcal C^2-\mathcal C\|_\diamond$, and that the number of slow degrees of freedom is bounded independently of the full system size. Under these assumptions, we construct compression and reconstruction channels whose composition recovers every reduced state exactly, while the reverse composition gives an exactly idempotent channel approximating $\mathcal C$, answering Kitaev's exact-rounding question \cite{Kitaev2025}. Our constructions give an optimal diamond-norm bound of $\mathcal{O}(η^{1/3})$ on all input states, as well as an improved bound of $\mathcal{O}(η^{1/2})$ on metastable states prepared by $\mathcal C$, with constants depending only on the slow dimension. The reduced transition channel can be iterated to predict the microscopic dynamics with controlled error. We illustrate the QMSM through a weakly driven dissipative spin chain supporting either a metastable logical qubit or long-lived classical phases.

</details>


### [179] [Compatibility of quantum instruments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40266)
*Chloe Kim,Yujie Zhang,Eric Chitambar,Marius Junge,Felix Leditzky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将量子测量的兼容性（compatibility）推广为 $d$-兼容性，即由维度为 $d$ 的父过程配合无限经典信息经后处理模拟测量、信道与仪器族，并将其刻画为算子空间与算子系统的共同线性分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始测量兼容性只关注是否存在单一父测量，未限制父过程所传递的量子维度；本文意图在量子资源受限的框架下细化该概念，用维度 $d$ 刻画父过程可传递的量子系统大小。

Method: 将 $d$-兼容性表述为算子空间与算子系统的共同线性分解，证明其构成闭集；定义兼容性泛函（compatibility functional）为经 $d$ 维寄存器分解时完全有界范数（completely bounded norm）乘积的最小值；借助算子空间分解理论、信道-态对偶（channel-state duality）进行分析。

Result: 在无穷多设置与可分的无穷维输入下 $d$-兼容族仍为闭集；以互无偏基（mutually unbiased bases）定义的测量-制备信道与以 Heisenberg-Weyl 算子定义的 pinching 信道在一定 $d$ 区间内为 $d$-不兼容；多仪器的 $d$-兼容性与 Choi assemblage 的可制备性相关联，可用于认证 Schmidt 数及单向量子通信（one-way LOCC）可实现性。

Conclusion: 兼容性泛函同时给出兼容性广义鲁棒性（generalized robustness）的上界，并通过有记忆限制的非瞬态制备博弈（memory-bounded nontransient preparation game）给予解释，为量化与认证量子资源受限下的不兼容性提供统一框架。

Abstract: Measurement compatibility asks whether a family of quantum measurements can be simulated by one parent measurement followed by classical post-processing. We extend this measurement compatibility to the notion of \emph{$d$-compatibility} for measurements, channels, and instruments whose parent passes a $d$-dimensional quantum system and unlimited classical information to the post-processing. We formulate $d$-compatibility as a common linear factorization of operator spaces and operator systems and show that $d$-compatible families form a closed set, even with infinitely many settings and a separable infinite-dimensional input. To certify $d$-incompatibility, we define the compatibility functional as the smallest product of completely bounded norms over common factorizations through a $d$-dimensional register. Using factorization theory of operator spaces, we show that measure-and-prepare channels defined in terms of mutually unbiased bases and pinching channels defined in terms of Heisenberg-Weyl operators are $d$-incompatible in certain regimes of $d$. We also link $d$-compatibility of multi-instruments to the preparability of Choi assemblages using channel-state duality, and use this connection to show that for bipartite states the (in)compatibility of instruments can be used to certify Schmidt numbers and the implementability of the instrument using one-way LOCC protocols. Finally, our compatibility functional also bounds the generalized robustness of compatibility, which we interpret through a memory-bounded nontransient preparation game.

</details>


### [180] [Refined sample complexities from the tomographic rate function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40271)
*Arick Grootveld,Alexander Maloney,Jason Pollack,Peixue Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文建立协变量子态层析协议的分析框架，用速率函数比较不同协议的渐近性能，发现即便各协议样本复杂度均为阶最优，其速率函数仍呈严格排序，说明速率函数是比样本复杂度更精细的性能指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态层析（quantum state tomography）协议通常仅以样本复杂度（sample complexity）衡量，但该指标无法区分阶最优协议之间的性能差异。作者希望通过大偏差（large deviation）框架下的速率函数（rate function）提供更细粒度的比较手段。

Method: 建立协变（covariant）协议的分析框架，推导若干协议的速率函数，包括基于随机纯化（random purification）的协议与Haah等人的样本最优（sample-optimal）协议；速率函数用一类新的散度（divergence）族表达，涵盖反向夹层Rényi散度（reverse sandwiched Rényi divergence）与Keyl退火量子相对熵（annealed quantum relative entropy）。此外引入基于Wasserstein距离而非迹距离（trace distance）的样本复杂度变体。

Result: 所推导的速率函数服从严格排序，尽管各协议的样本复杂度均为阶最优。基于Wasserstein距离的样本复杂度也因速率函数排序而呈现类似的严格不等式。

Conclusion: 层析速率函数（tomographic rate function）比常用的样本复杂度更能精细刻画量子态层析协议的性能，可作为协议比较的更优指标。

Abstract: Protocols for quantum state tomography can be characterized either by their sample complexity or by the tomographic rate function that governs the large deviation behaviour of error estimates. We establish a framework for the analysis of covariant protocols which allows us to compare these measures. First, we derive the rate functions for several protocols, including random purification-based protocols and the sample-optimal protocol of Haah et al. These rate functions are expressed in terms of a new family of divergences which includes the reverse sandwiched Rényi divergence and Keyl's annealed quantum relative entropy as special cases. We show that these rate functions obey a strict ordering, even though the sample complexity of each protocol is order optimal. We consider a different version of sample complexity based on Wasserstein distance rather than trace distance, and show that the ordering of the rate functions implies analogous strict inequalities for the Wasserstein-type sample complexity. Thus the tomographic rate function is a more fine-grained indicator of the performance of a tomography protocol than the usual sample complexity.

</details>


### [181] [Memory dimension detection in open quantum dynamics via pseudo-control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40281)
*Chang Liu,Alexander Yosifov,Ximing Wang,Zhenhuan Liu,Jinzhao Sun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种伪控制协议（pseudo-control protocol），将记忆维度（memory dimension）信息编码于实验可及的干涉矩阵（interference matrix）中，通过其秩（rank）给出记忆维度的下界认证，无需直接访问环境或进行完整的过程张量层析（process-tensor tomography）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画开放量子动力学（open quantum dynamics）需要识别影响系统演化的环境自由度；记忆维度作为复现该影响所需最小有效记忆的规模，可量化动力学相关的环境资源，并为比较不同多体环境提供共同基准。现有方法通常依赖对环境的直接访问或完整过程张量层析，存在操作上的困难。

Method: 引入一种伪控制协议，将关于记忆维度的信息编码进实验可访问的干涉矩阵中；该矩阵的秩可在不直接访问环境、也不进行完整过程张量层析的条件下，认证记忆维度的下界。将该协议应用于量子多体环境（quantum many-body environments），并允许灵活调节相互作用时间与间隔时间。

Result: 该协议揭示了相互作用与间隔时间、环境动力学以及系统-环境相互作用如何共同塑造可探测的记忆；矩阵的秩提供记忆维度的下界认证。摘要未给出具体数值或实验数据。

Conclusion: 该工作为刻画开放量子动力学提供了一种新的操作性技术，并可通过记忆特征（memory signatures）探测系统-环境相互作用的结构，为比较多体环境资源提供了统一途径。

Abstract: Characterising open quantum dynamics requires identifying the environmental degrees of freedom that shape the system's evolution. The memory dimension, defined as the size of the smallest effective memory needed to reproduce this influence, quantifies the dynamically relevant environmental resources and provides a common basis for comparing different many-body environments. Here we introduce a pseudo-control protocol that encodes information about the memory dimension in an experimentally accessible interference matrix, whose rank certifies a lower bound on this dimension without direct access to the environment or full process-tensor tomography. Applied to quantum many-body environments, the protocol allows flexible adjustment of the interaction and interval times, revealing how these parameters, environmental dynamics, and system-environment interactions shape detectable memory. Our approach provides a new operational technique for characterising open quantum dynamics and probing the structure of system-environment interactions through their memory signatures.

</details>


### [182] [More mutually unbiased bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40311)
*Mateo Cárdenes Wuttig,Joseph Tindall*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种构造互不偏基（mutually unbiased bases, MUBs）的拟设（ansatz），在非素数幂维度中给出超过既有结果的基组数目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MUBs 的最大数目在非素数幂维度中仍属未知；已有构造方法的产出有限，需要新的构造途径以在该类维度中取得更多基组。

Method: 拟设中每个基由对角相位矩阵作用于固定酉矩阵构成，该酉矩阵为傅里叶矩阵（Fourier matrices）的张量积，并可选择地包含任意实哈达玛矩阵（real Hadamard matrix）；此外利用 Paley 实哈达玛矩阵构造特定维度下的基组。

Result: 在维度 12 得到 5 个 MUBs，维度 48、96、192 得到 6 个，维度 36、108 得到 7 个，维度 648 得到 10 个；这些基可在每第十八个维度渐近地超过张量积上界（tensor product bound）。对素数幂 q≡3 (mod 4)，在维度 d = q(q+1) 上构造出 q+1 个基，其数目按 √d 增长，且不能被更小集合的张量积超越。

Conclusion: 所提构造在多个非素数幂维度中提升了已知 MUBs 数目，并可通过张量积延伸在渐近意义上突破张量积上界，同时给出基于 Paley 矩阵的渐近为 √d 的构造。

Abstract: Mutually unbiased bases (MUBs) describe quantum measurements for which certainty in one basis gives uniform outcomes in the others. Their maximum number remains unknown in dimensions that are not powers of a prime. We introduce an ansatz that, in many dimensions, allows us to construct more MUBs than have previously been found. Each basis in the ansatz consists of a diagonal phase matrix applied to a fixed unitary, which is a tensor product of Fourier matrices and, optionally, any real Hadamard matrix. This construction yields five MUBs in dimension 12, six in dimensions 48, 96, and 192, seven in dimensions 36 and 108, and ten in dimension 648. These bases can then be extended to exceed the tensor product bound, asymptotically, in every eighteenth dimension. Moreover, we show that Paley's real Hadamard matrix can be used to construct $q+1$ bases in dimension $d = q(q+1)$ for every prime power $q\equiv3\pmod4$. This count grows as $\sqrt d$ and cannot be exceeded by tensor products of smaller sets.

</details>


### [183] [Quantum hypothesis testing of non-mixed-unitarity: A multifaceted hierarchy of quantum channel discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40355)
*Pratik Ghosal,Pritam Halder,Ayan Patra,Aditi Sen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将“判别未知量子信道为非混合酉信道还是混合酉信道”形式化为复合信道假设检验问题，刻画了在探测态与辅助记忆不同限制下可达到的斯坦指数（Stein exponent），并建立了一个多层次的严格分离层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对未知量子信道的多次使用，需要判定其属于特定非混合酉信道（non-mixed-unitary channel）还是混合酉信道集合（set of mixed-unitary channels），这一判别问题在信道资源理论中具有基础意义。

Method: 将其表述为复合信道假设检验问题，考察并行策略可达的斯坦指数；引入块独立同分布（block-i.i.d.）探测态（块内允许任意关联、块间保持独立同分布），给出相应斯坦指数的有限字母（finite-letter）表达式，并变化块大小、块内关联限制及辅助记忆可得性以构建层级。

Result: 无辅助记忆时，完全独立同分布探测态对任意 unital 非混合酉信道的斯坦指数为零；三个乘积探测态组成的块即可使 qutrit Werner-Holevo 信道获得严格正指数。块大小为二时，乘积探测态对 O(3)-协变信道似不足，但纠缠探测态可使 qutrit Werner-Holevo 信道获得正指数，而所有最大纠缠探测态均失败。有辅助记忆时，完全独立同分布探测态即足够，对任意非混合酉信道给出严格正指数且无需输入-参考纠缠，其下界由到混合酉集合的钻石范数（diamond-norm）距离决定；对奇数维 Werner-Holevo 信道该指数为无穷，任意满施密特秩（full-Schmidt-rank）纯态均为最优。

Conclusion: 探测态相关性结构、块大小与辅助记忆共同构成一个多方面层级，层内存在若干严格分离；辅助记忆可显著提升判别能力并使指数与信道到混合酉集合的钻石范数距离相联系。

Abstract: Given multiple uses of an unknown quantum channel, we determine whether it is a specified non-mixed-unitary channel or belongs to the set of mixed-unitary channels. Formulating this as a composite channel hypothesis testing problem, we characterize the Stein exponents achievable by parallel strategies under progressively weaker restrictions on probe states and auxiliary memory. In particular, we consider block-i.i.d. probes, which allow arbitrary correlations within blocks of fixed size while remaining i.i.d. across blocks, and derive finite-letter expressions of the corresponding Stein exponents. Varying the block size, which interpolates between fully i.i.d. and arbitrary probes, together with further restrictions on intra-block correlations and access to auxiliary memory, results in a multifaceted hierarchy. We establish several strict separations within this hierarchy. Without auxiliary memory, we prove that fully i.i.d. probes yield a vanishing Stein exponent for every unital non-mixed-unitary channel, while blocks of three product probes suffice to obtain a strictly positive exponent for the qutrit Werner-Holevo channel. For block size two, although product probes seem to be insufficient for O(3)-covariant channels, an entangled probe achieves a strictly positive exponent for the qutrit Werner-Holevo channel, even though every maximally entangled probe fails. In contrast, auxiliary memory makes fully i.i.d. probes sufficient by yielding a strictly positive exponent for every non-mixed-unitary channel, even without requiring input-reference entanglement, and this exponent admits a lower bound determined by the diamond-norm distance from the mixed-unitary set. Moreover, for odd-dimensional Werner-Holevo channels, we find that this exponent is infinite, with every full-Schmidt-rank pure state being optimal.

</details>


### [184] [Pauli instability in arbitrary states: detecting magic in physical correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40032)
*Tanner Jackson,Alexey Milekhin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出利用非时序关联函数（out-of-time-order correlators, OTOC）在任意态下度量量子演化中的非稳定子性（non-stabilizerness，即“魔法”magic），并证明对满秩态（尤其吉布斯态）该量仅在演化为 Clifford 时为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子演化的“魔法”度量刻画其在经典计算机上模拟的难度，核心问题是能否从有限温度或基态等物理关联函数中提取魔法的信息。

Method: 通过计算任意量子态下的非时序关联函数（OTOC）来构造魔法的度量；并借助 Choi 态的稳定子熵（stabilizer entropy）建立联系，同时分析使用单一固定探针算符的情形。

Result: 对每个满秩态（full-rank state），特别是每个吉布斯态（Gibbs state），所得量当且仅当演化为 Clifford 时为零；即使在任意线路架构中仅用单个固定探针算符，该量仍给出实现该动力学所需 T 门数量的下界。

Conclusion: OTOC 可作为从物理关联函数中提取魔法信息的有效工具，其性质与 Choi 态稳定子熵相联系，并为量子线路中 T 门数量提供可操作的下界估计。

Abstract: Measures of non-stabilizerness (``magic'') of a quantum evolution quantify how hard it is to simulate on a classical computer. A natural question is how to extract information about magic from physical correlators, for example at finite temperature or in the ground state. In this paper we show how to measure magic using out-of-time-order correlators evaluated in an arbitrary state. For every full-rank state, and in particular for every Gibbs state, the resulting quantity vanishes if and only if the evolution is Clifford. We establish its basic properties, relate it to the stabilizer entropy of the Choi state, and prove that, even with a single fixed probe operator, it gives a lower bound on the number of $T$ gates needed to implement the dynamics in any circuit architecture.

</details>


### [185] [Collective Dynamics in Spin Chains with Constrained Dissipation: Classically Fragile but Quantum Robust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38333)
*Pietro Brighi,Gianluca Frazzei,Igor Lesanovsky,Juan P. Garrahan,Alberto Biella*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究以动力学受限自旋链“耗散远东模型（dissipative far-East model）”为平台，考察量子涨落与经典涨落对相共存（phase coexistence）的竞争影响，并刻画其稳态相图与弛豫动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体系统中的集体非平衡现象（collective non-equilibrium phenomena）受涨落强烈影响，尤其在相变附近，经典效应与量子效应的相互作用会改变合作行为，但其竞争机制尚需具体模型加以厘清。

Method: 采用团簇平均场方法（cluster mean-field approach）绘制稳态相图，并使用随机张量网络方法（stochastic tensor-network methods）刻画系统动力学。

Result: 无涨落时系统呈现涌现集体动力学与相共存；该现象对量子涨落具有鲁棒性并被其进一步稳定，而经典涨落则在长时间尺度上抑制相共存。在双稳相（bistable phase）内，局域磁化与纠缠动力学中均出现动力学异质性（dynamical heterogeneity）特征。

Conclusion: 量子涨落与经典动力学约束的竞争共同塑造了多体系统的弛豫动力学与相结构，为理解经典-量子竞争下的非平衡集体行为提供了机制性认识。

Abstract: Collective non-equilibrium phenomena in many-body systems are strongly influenced by fluctuations, particularly in the vicinity of phase transitions, where the interplay between classical and quantum effects can alter cooperative behavior. Here, we investigate this classical-quantum competition in a kinetically constrained spin chain, the dissipative far-East model. In absence of fluctuations, the system exhibits emergent collective dynamics, resulting in phase coexistence. We find that this phenomenon is robust to quantum fluctuations, which further stabilize it, whereas classical fluctuations suppress phase coexistence at long times. We map out the steady-state phase diagram using a cluster mean-field approach and characterize the dynamics with stochastic tensor-network methods. Within the bistable phase we identify signatures of dynamical heterogeneity in both the local magnetization and the entanglement dynamics. This highlights how competing quantum fluctuations and classical kinetic constraints shape the relaxation dynamics and the phase structure of many-body systems.

</details>


### [186] [Interferometric Readout of Momentum-Space Topology in a Programmable Dissipative Photonic Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38352)
*Andrea Cataldo,Emil J. Bergholtz,Daniel Leykam,Jun Gao,Ali W. Elshaari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作利用可编程光子集成电路在合成动量空间中实现非厄米动力学，并通过相移干涉恢复被强耗散压制的体演化相位，从而读出非厄米拓扑不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米动力学中的拓扑信息编码于体演化的相位中，但强耗散会抑制携带该相位的振幅，使得动量空间拓扑的直接探测困难。

Method: 借助幺正膨胀（unitary dilation）在合成动量空间中实现非厄米动力学，并采用相移干涉（phase-shifted interferometry）恢复相位；在非厄米 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger 模型与经 Rice-Mele 泵浦构建的合成环面上进行验证。

Result: 在非厄米 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger 模型中区分了平庸与非平庸 Zak 相位，并观测到由例外点（exceptional point）诱导的相干绕数 q=±1；在合成环面上得到平庸与非平庸循环对应的第一陈数 Ch₁=0,1。

Conclusion: 结果展示了在强耗散非厄米动力学下实现动量空间拓扑的可编程途径。

Abstract: Topology under non-Hermitian dynamics is encoded in the phase of the bulk evolution, yet strong dissipation suppresses the amplitudes carrying it. We resolve this using a programmable photonic integrated circuit that implements such dynamics in synthetic momentum space through unitary dilation, with the phase recovered by phase-shifted interferometry. For the non-Hermitian Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model, the method distinguishes trivial and non-trivial Zak phases and yields a coherence winding $q=\pm 1$ induced by an exceptional point. Extending to a synthetic torus via a Rice-Mele pump gives the first Chern number $\mathrm{Ch}_1=0,1$ for trivial and non-trivial cycles, showing a programmable route to momentum-space topology under strongly dissipative non-Hermitian dynamics.

</details>


### [187] [Ergotropy from energetic coherence and the third law of thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38724)
*O. S. Yáñez-Soria,G. Guarnieri,B. Çakmak,R. Román-Ancheyta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究分析了由热辅助系统（thermal ancillas）簇通过重复相互作用充电的量子比特电池（qubit battery）的可提取功（ergotropy）与热力学势。单一参数即可刻画任意簇尺寸下的相干性与可提取功；大簇条件下可提取功趋于与浴统计无关的普适值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的在于理解复合系统-浴耦合（composite system-bath coupling）所诱导的能量基下稳态相干（steady-state coherence）如何影响量子电池的可用功与热力学行为，并厘清浴量子统计对充电性能的作用。

Method: 采用与热辅助系统簇（量子比特或谐振子）重复相互作用的充电模型，通过生成能量基下稳态相干性的复合系统-浴耦合，解析求解相干性与可提取功，并以单一参数编码浴量子统计，推导任意簇尺寸下的闭式结果。

Result: 大簇情形下可提取功与浴统计无关，在最优耦合时达到浴温度下量子比特平衡内能的普适比例；由于充电态无布居反转（population inversion），全部可提取功来自相干性。可提取功在低温下呈平坦趋势，归因于第三定律，并与量子比特平衡焓成正比，其斜率随热容消失。多量子比特电池中相干性随比特数指数增长，可提取功线性增长，二者均维持近乎恒定的极大值比例。充电态为零温下有限熵的非平衡稳态（non-equilibrium steady state），其维持成本在平行耦合非零时非零，且依赖浴统计，与可提取功不同。

Conclusion: 可提取功在低温平台的平坦行为可由第三定律解释，其与平衡焓的联系揭示了相干性驱动做功的热力学本质；充电态的有限熵非平衡稳态由开关碰撞相互作用的功维持，其成本依赖浴统计，而可提取功在大簇极限下不依赖浴统计。

Abstract: We study the ergotropy and the thermodynamic potentials of a qubit battery charged by repeated interactions with clusters of thermal ancillas, qubits or oscillators, through a composite system-bath coupling that generates steady-state coherence in the energy basis. A single parameter encoding the bath quantum statistics yields the coherence and ergotropy in closed form for any cluster size. For large clusters, the ergotropy becomes independent of the bath statistics and, at optimal couplings, reaches a universal fraction of the equilibrium internal energy of the qubit at the bath temperature. Since the charged state has no population inversion, all extractable work comes from coherence. The ergotropy is flat at low temperatures. We trace this plateau to the third law, and show that the ergotropy is proportional to the equilibrium enthalpy of the qubit, whose slope vanishes with the heat capacity. For a battery composed of multiple qubits, the coherence grows exponentially with the number of qubits while the ergotropy grows linearly, both remaining a nearly constant fraction of their respective maxima. The charged state is a non-equilibrium steady state with finite entropy at zero temperature, sustained by the work of switching the collisional interaction on and off. The cost is nonzero whenever the parallel coupling is nonzero, and, unlike the ergotropy, it depends on the bath statistics.

</details>


### [188] [Optical studies of negative g-factor dopants in an antiferromagnet: Nd:GdVO$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38892)
*Lachlan W. Cronin,Masaya Hiraishi,Luke S. Trainor,Jevon J. Longdell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究在低于奈尔温度（Néel temperature）2.5 K 的条件下测量 Nd:GdVO₄ 的光学性质，发现窄光学跃迁并观测到负 g 因子（negative g-factor）的清晰证据，为其作为混合量子系统（hybrid quantum systems）候选材料提供了光谱学基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nd:GdVO₄ 是常用的室温激光材料，但宿主中无序钆自旋导致的谱线展宽（linebroadening）使其在量子技术领域的关注有限。作者因此转向低温区间，探索其潜在的光学相干特性。

Method: 在低于 2.5 K 奈尔温度（Néel temperature）的温度下对 Nd:GdVO₄ 进行光学性质研究，并分析交换相互作用（exchange interaction）与塞曼相互作用（Zeeman interaction）之间的竞争关系。

Result: 低温下观测到窄光学跃迁（narrow optical transitions），并通过交换与塞曼相互作用的相互作用得到负 g 因子（negative g-factor）的清晰证据，表明磁性宿主中的稀土离子可用于观测磁 g 因子的符号。

Conclusion: 结果为 Nd:GdVO₄ 提供了详细的光谱学基础，并拓展了对其作为混合量子系统（hybrid quantum systems）材料的适用性认识。

Abstract: Neodymium-doped gadolinium vanadate (Nd:GdVO$_4$) is a popular laser material at room temperature. It has received limited attention for quantum technologies because of the linebroadening effects of disordered gadolinium spins in the host. Here we investigate the optical properties at temperatures below the 2.5 K Néel temperature, revealing narrow optical transitions. We demonstrate that rare earth ions in magnetic hosts give us a new way of observing the signs of magnetic g-factors. The interplay between the exchange and Zeeman interactions means that we see clear evidence of negative g-factors. These results provide a detailed spectroscopic foundation for Nd:GdVO$_4$ and expand the understanding of its candidacy as a material for hybrid quantum systems.

</details>


### [189] [Quantum-echo Markov process for combinatorial optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38943)
*Tatsuhiko Shirai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种用于组合优化（combinatorial optimization）的量子回声马尔可夫过程（quantum-echo Markov process），以量子退火（QA）或量子近似优化算法（QAOA）的动力学构造转移核（transition kernel），并据此提出量子回声局部优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将有限资源下的量子多体动力学（quantum many-body dynamics）用作迭代优化的计算原语（computational primitive），并通过结构化转移核的设计统一理解量子动力学的探索能力与优化性能之间的关系。

Method: 以量子退火或 QAOA 动力学构造转移核：在 QA 中增加退火时间、在 QAOA 中增加层数，可使跃迁探索更远构型同时抑制大的能量变化。据此提出带与不带贪婪下降（greedy descent）的量子回声局部优化（quantum-echo local optimization）。机理上，汉明空间（Hamming-space）去局域化源于算符扩散（operator spreading），能量空间（energy-space）局域化源于动态生成的关联。

Result: 性能由汉明空间非局域性（nonlocality）与能量空间局域性（locality）之间的相互作用决定，过度的能量空间局域化会降低优化性能；引入贪婪下降可显著提升性能，体现量子动力学负责探索、贪婪下降负责开发的互补作用。

Conclusion: 量子回声动力学可作为设计结构化转移核的框架，并为把有限资源量子多体动力学用作迭代优化的计算原语提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce a quantum-echo Markov process for combinatorial optimization. Quantum dynamics based on quantum annealing (QA) or the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) is used to engineer the transition kernel. Increasing the annealing time in QA or the number of layers in QAOA enables transitions to explore distant configurations while suppressing large energy changes. The Hamming-space delocalization originates from operator spreading, whereas the energy-space localization arises from a dynamically generated correlation. For optimization, we propose quantum-echo local optimization with and without greedy descent. We find that its performance is governed by the interplay between Hamming-space nonlocality and energy-space locality, and that excessive energy-space localization can degrade optimization performance. Incorporating greedy descent substantially improves the performance, highlighting the complementary roles of quantum dynamics for exploration and greedy descent for exploitation. These results establish quantum-echo dynamics as a framework for engineering structured transition kernels and provide a route to using finite-resource quantum many-body dynamics as a computational primitive for iterative optimization.

</details>


### [190] [Engineered Dissipation for Thermal-State Tracking Across Quantum Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39084)
*Saikat Mistry,Dibyajyoti Sahu,Suhas Gangadharaiah*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过具有非负跃迁速率的工程化布居流（population currents）构建马尔可夫耗散框架，实现有限时间热态追踪，并在横场伊辛模型（transverse-field Ising model）穿越量子临界点过程中显著抑制热激发的偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限时间驱动穿越量子临界点会产生非绝热激发（nonadiabatic excitations）与相干性，而现有耗散工程（dissipation engineering）方法多刻画固定环境如何改变临界动力学，而非主动将系统导向预设的热轨迹。因此需要一个能主动追踪目标热态的耗散控制框架。

Method: 构建基于工程化布居流的马尔可夫耗散框架（Markovian dissipative framework），采用非负跃迁速率（non-negative transition rates）实现有限时间热态追踪；并将其应用于穿越量子临界点的横场伊辛模型，与幺正动力学（unitary dynamics）及自然热浴进行对比。

Result: 相较幺正动力学与自然热浴，工程化动力学显著抑制了热激发偏离，并通过自由公共流 $Q_k$ 对相干性衰减提供额外控制；同时其残余标度（residual scaling）更陡，为 $δn_{\mathrm{ex}}^{\mathrm{th}}\sim(v/v_{\mathrm{ch}})^2$，而幺正演化则恢复出 Kibble–Zurek 行为。

Conclusion: 结果表明，工程化耗散可作为在驱动量子临界系统中控制热态制备的一条途径。

Abstract: Engineering dissipation has emerged as a powerful strategy for controlling quantum many-body dynamics, complementing purely coherent control for state preparation. However, finite-time driving across a quantum critical point generates nonadiabatic excitations and coherences, and existing dissipation-engineering approaches have largely characterized how a fixed environment modifies critical dynamics rather than actively steering a system toward a prescribed thermal trajectory. Here we develop a Markovian dissipative framework for finite-time thermal-state tracking based on engineered population currents with non-negative transition rates, and apply it to the transverse-field Ising model driven across its quantum critical point. Compared with unitary dynamics and a natural thermal bath, the engineered dynamics strongly suppresses thermal excitation deviations and provides additional control over coherence damping through a free common current $Q_k$, while exhibiting a substantially steeper residual scaling, $δn_{\mathrm{ex}}^{\mathrm{th}}\sim(v/v_{\mathrm{ch}})^2$, than the Kibble--Zurek behavior recovered for unitary evolution. These results point toward engineered dissipation as a route for controlling thermal-state preparation in driven quantum critical systems.

</details>


### [191] [Reservoir- and Measurement-free Microwave Initialization of Semiconductor Spin Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39415)
*Leon C. Camenzind,Ik Kyeong Jin,Akito Noiri,Kenta Takeda,Takashi Nakajima,Takashi Kobayashi,Seigo Tarucha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作在不依赖储层（reservoir）与测量反馈（measurement-based feedback）的条件下，利用固定微波与基带脉冲序列实现硅自旋量子比特对的初始化，中位概率达 99.4%，并可在改进器件条件下预期达到亚微秒时间尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展量子处理器需在大规模阵列中反复初始化量子比特；半导体自旋量子比特（spin qubits）的快速初始化通常依赖局部储层接入或测量反馈，这类专用基础设施随规模扩大而难以分布。因此需要一种免储层、免测量的初始化原语。

Method: 在工业制造的 Si/SiGe 量子点（quantum-dot）器件中，采用固定序列的微波与基带脉冲实现自旋量子比特对的初始化：奇数自旋宇称态弛豫至单重态电荷态，被阻塞的偶数自旋宇称态经微波驱动穿越三重态流形（triplet manifold），再通过单重态-三重态混合（singlet-triplet mixing）与电荷杂化（charge hybridization）转换为单重态。并以微波谱学与时间域测量表征转移与阻塞解除动力学。

Result: 重复循环在各采样制备态下产生单重态相关电荷结果的中位概率为 99.4%；交换谱学（exchange spectroscopy）独立验证了到目标 |↑↓⟩ 操作态的映射。测量识别出受暗态限制的单周期转移及随后的阻塞解除动力学，二者决定初始化时间尺度。该泵浦序列约使用 12 μs 的微波脉冲串与混合驻留时间。

Conclusion: 固定序列微波初始化可作为半导体自旋量子比特处理器的可扩展控制原语，其基础为单重态-三重态物理，并可推广至具有合适泡利阻塞（Pauli blockade）跃迁的平台；作者预期在改进器件条件下可实现亚微秒初始化。

Abstract: Scalable quantum processors require repeated qubit initialization throughout large arrays. In semiconductor spin qubits, fast initialization commonly relies on local reservoir access or measurement-based feedback, requiring dedicated infrastructure that becomes increasingly difficult to distribute as processors scale. Here, we demonstrate reservoir- and measurement-free initialization of a silicon spin-qubit pair in an industrially fabricated Si/SiGe quantum-dot device using a fixed sequence of microwave and baseband pulses. Odd spin-parity states relax to the singlet charge state, whereas blocked even spin-parity states are microwave-driven through the triplet manifold and subsequently converted to the singlet by singlet-triplet mixing and charge hybridization. Repeated cycles produce the singlet-associated charge outcome with a median probability of 99.4% across the sampled preparation states, while exchange spectroscopy independently verifies mapping to the target $|\uparrow\downarrow\rangle$ operational state. Microwave spectroscopy and time-domain measurements identify the dark-state-limited single-cycle transfer and the subsequent blockade-lifting dynamics that set the initialization time scale. The demonstrated pumping sequence uses approximately $12\,μ\mathrm{s}$ of microwave bursts and mixing dwells, while we project sub-microsecond initialization under improved device conditions. These results establish fixed-sequence microwave initialization as a scalable control primitive for semiconductor spin-qubit processors, based on singlet-triplet physics that can be adapted to platforms with suitable Pauli-blockade transitions.

</details>


### [192] [Optimal Scaling of Unitary Design Formation in $U(1)$-Symmetric Random Circuits: A Bottleneck Slower than Charge Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39458)
*Toshihiro Yada*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了在 $U(1)$ 对称随机线路中，酉 $k$-设计形成速率是否由电荷输运决定的开放问题，发现对于 $k\geq 2$，其形成速率实际上由双粒子相遇速率（two-particle encounter rate）支配，而非单粒子输运速率，从而否定了先前的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解守恒律下随机性的形成，对于刻画孤立量子系统中的混沌现象以及受对称性约束的量子信息处理均具有重要意义。$U(1)$ 对称随机线路提供了一个最小可处理的模型，此前猜想其酉设计形成速率由底层线路几何中的电荷输运决定，但因难以给出紧致下界，该猜想一直未被证实或否定。

Method: 作者针对 $k\leq O(\sqrt{\log N})$ 的情形，在包括晶格、扩展图（expander graphs）和全连接相互作用等多种线路几何下确定了酉 $k$-设计形成速率的最优标度。为证明相遇机制确实决定了形成速率，采用了基于辅助子群系综（auxiliary subgroup ensemble）的新颖证明技术来推导匹配下界。

Result: 对于 $k\geq 2$，形成速率由双粒子相遇速率支配，即两个随机行走者在给定线路几何中彼此相遇的速率。该速率在许多几何中参数化地小于单粒子输运速率，从而否定了先前的猜想。作者进一步证明该相遇机制确实设定了设计形成速率，并给出了匹配下界。此外，通过使用破坏对称性的局部门，该瓶颈可被绕过，文中提供了实现更快设计形成的显式协议。

Conclusion: 研究建立了守恒律下随机性形成的新图景：$U(1)$ 对称随机线路中的设计形成受双粒子相遇模式的稳健瓶颈限制，但该瓶颈可通过破坏对称性的局部操作规避。这为刻画和高效生成受对称性约束的随机性提供了指导原则。

Abstract: Understanding randomness formation under conservation laws is important both for characterizing chaotic phenomena in isolated quantum systems and for symmetry-constrained quantum information processing. $U(1)$-symmetric random circuits on an $N$-qubit system provide a minimal tractable setting for this problem, in which the formation rate of unitary designs was conjectured to be governed by charge transport in the underlying circuit geometry. However, whether charge transport indeed sets the slowest relaxation mode has remained an open problem, due to the difficulty in deriving a tight lower bound on the symmetric design formation rate. In this work, we resolve this problem by determining the optimal scaling of the unitary $k$-design formation rate for $k\leq O(\sqrt{\log N})$ across a wide range of circuit geometries, including lattices, expander graphs, and all-to-all interactions. In particular, for $k\geq 2$, we find that the formation rate is governed by a two-particle encounter rate---the rate at which two random walkers encounter each other in a given circuit geometry. This encounter rate is parametrically smaller than the single-particle transport rate in many geometries, thereby disproving the previous conjecture. We further establish that this encounter mechanism indeed sets the design formation rate by deriving its matching lower bound with a novel proof technique based on an auxiliary subgroup ensemble. Although this encounter mode imposes a robust bottleneck on design formation in $U(1)$-symmetric random circuits, we also show that this bottleneck can be circumvented by using symmetry-breaking local gates and provide an explicit protocol that achieves faster design formation. These results establish a new picture of randomness formation under conservation laws and provide guiding principles for characterizing and efficiently generating symmetry-constrained randomness.

</details>


### [193] [Optimal local recovery cannot alter critical orthogonality exponents in quantum spin environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39528)
*Zhixuan Zhao,Jun Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文针对临界 Ising 链（critical Ising chain）的基态，证明了正基态的局域振幅比界与有限区域擦除（finite-region erasure）下保真度（fidelity）的显式反向比较，并给出格点精确计算与恢复测量协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未显式陈述研究动机，仅从问题设定看，其关注点在于有限区域信息擦除后基态可恢复性的定量刻画，以及振幅比、保真度与最优控制（optimal control）之间的关系。未提及具体应用背景或未解决问题。

Method: 方法包括：证明局域振幅比界与保真度反向比较；对连通临界 Ising 链（connected critical Ising chain）进行精确格点计算；推导有限柯西乘积（finite Cauchy product）及其均匀条带渐近（uniform strip asymptotics）；记录精确双自旋优化（two-spin optimization）与用于测量恢复的干涉测量协议（interferometric protocol）；并给出有限中介体定理（finite-mediator theorem）、基态制备 Ramsey 界（ground-preparation Ramsey bound）、更强投影态推断（projected-state inferences）的反例及独立全哈密顿量计算（full-Hamiltonian calculations）。

Result: 精确格点计算得到振幅指数（amplitude exponent）为 1/8；该指数在任意固定自旋集上的任意最优控制下保持不变，并在对数指数（logarithmic exponents）层面于 o(log N) 个自旋上保持不变。另给出有限中介体定理对局域压缩与解码器（decoder）的定量结果，同时精确保留环境其余部分；并给出基态制备 Ramsey 界与对更强投影态推断的反例。

Conclusion: 结论未在摘要中单独陈述。摘要表明所证界与比较、精确指数结果、有限中介体定理及反例共同构成对有限区域擦除下基态恢复性与相关推断的定量刻画，并得到独立全哈密顿量计算的佐证。

Abstract: We prove a local amplitude-ratio bound for positive ground states and an explicit reverse comparison for fidelity under finite-region erasure. An exact lattice calculation for a connected critical Ising chain gives amplitude exponent $1/8$, preserved by arbitrary optimal control on any fixed spin set and, at the level of logarithmic exponents, on $o(\log N)$ spins. We derive the finite Cauchy product and its uniform strip asymptotics, and document exact two-spin optimization and an interferometric protocol for measuring the recovery. A separate finite-mediator theorem quantifies local compression and a decoder while retaining the rest of the environment exactly. We also give a ground-preparation Ramsey bound, counterexamples to stronger projected-state inferences, and independent full-Hamiltonian calculations.

</details>


### [194] [Boron vacancies in bulk h-BN created by high-energy He+ irradiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39535)
*P. G. Vilyuzhanina,S. V. Bolshedvorskii,R. Isayev,A. S. Doroshkevich,A. A. Tatarinova,V. V. Soshenko,I. S. Cojocaru,A. M. Kozodaev,S. M. Drofa,A. Chernyavskiy,N. I. Salangin,A. N. Smolyaninov,A. Y. Kuntsevich,A. V. Akimov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过约1 MeV氦离子注入，在块体六方氮化硼（h-BN）中成功制备了带负电的硼空位色心，并以光致发光和光学检测磁共振证实其形成，同时测量了色心沿c轴的深度并与SRIM模型预测进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金刚石等三维晶体中的色心已成为多种传感器的关键元件，而二维材料中的色心正快速发展并有望用于高技术应用；h-BN中带负电硼空位在磁测量方面显示出潜力，但其可靠制备仍是挑战。

Method: 采用约1 MeV的氦离子注入块体h-BN，通过特征光致发光（photoluminescence）与光学检测磁共振（optically detected magnetic resonance）确认色心形成；测量色心沿h-BN晶体c轴的深度，并与Stop Range of Ions in Matter（SRIM）建模预测结果进行比较。

Result: 所观测共振线宽为180 MHz，并表现出预期的塞曼位移（Zeeman shift）；色心深度的实验值与模型预测在报告的不确定度范围内一致（1σ区间重叠），但实验结果显示深度更大且分布更宽。

Conclusion: 氦离子注入可用于制备块体h-BN中带负电硼空位色心，其磁共振与深度特征得到实验证实，且与SRIM预测在不确定度内相符，尽管实验深度更大、分布更宽。

Abstract: While color centers in diamond and other three-dimensional crystals are nowadays key elements of several types of sensors, color centers in two-dimensional materials are rapidly developing and promise high-tech applications. One of the interesting centers in 2D materials is the negatively charged boron vacancy in hexagonal boron nitride (h-BN), which has already shown some potential for magnetometry, but the reliable creation of boron vacancies remains challenging. Here, we demonstrate the fabrication of negatively charged boron vacancy color centers in bulk h-BN via implantation of helium ions at ~1 MeV, as confirmed by characteristic photoluminescence and optically detected magnetic resonance. The resonance has a width of 180 MHz and exhibits the expected Zeeman shift of the resonance lines. The depth of the color center along the c-axis of the h-BN crystal was measured and compared with predictions from Stopping and Range of Ions in Matter modeling. The experimental value and the modeled prediction are consistent within the reported uncertainties, as their 1σ intervals overlap, although the experiment shows a greater depth and a wider distribution.

</details>


### [195] [Exact Maximum Likelihood Decoding beyond Treewidth via Rank-Decomposition Dynamic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39556)
*Bin Cheng,Feng Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于秩分解动态规划（rank-decomposition dynamic programming, Rank DP）的量子纠错解码算法，将解码配分函数（partition function）表示为局部故障因子的二次幂和，并以Tanner图的秩宽（rank-width）为复杂度指数，实现对若干码族的精确多项式时间解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大似然解码（maximum-likelihood decoding）在随机泡利噪声（stochastic Pauli noise）下理论最优，但计算逻辑类概率困难；主流的精确张量网络收缩（tensor-network contraction）时间随树宽（treewidth）指数增长，限制了其可扩展性。

Method: 以独立局部故障因子表达解码配分函数，将其写为幂的二次和，再施加秩分解动态规划。所得精确求值器的算术复杂度对输入规模为多项式，对（相对故障划分的）Tanner图秩宽为指数，且包含分解构造过程；经高斯消元（Gaussian elimination）后用于特定码族，并给出非负实现下的相对浮点误差界。

Result: 对打孔量子Reed-Muller码（punctured quantum Reed-Muller codes）及距离增长的Steane级联码族，在独立单量子比特泡利噪声下实现多项式时间解码，而对应张量网络的标准收缩需超多项式时间。数值实验中，在部分码容量（code-capacity）与线路级（circuit-level）实例上运行时间优于所测张量网络实现，Reed-Muller码完整似然评估规模达1,023量子比特。

Conclusion: 该工作利用代数结构实现高效量子解码，并将所得似然用于从症候群（syndromes）学习线路噪声参数、评估稀有后选择（postselection）概率及量化解码最优性差距，为量子解码与噪声表征中挖掘代数结构开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Maximum-likelihood decoding provides an optimal decoding strategy for quantum error correction under stochastic Pauli noise. However, computing logical-class probabilities is challenging, and the leading exact tensor-network contraction requires time exponential in treewidth. In this work, we introduce a new decoding algorithm based on rank-decomposition dynamic programming (Rank DP). We express decoding partition functions with independent local fault factors as quadratic sums of powers and apply Rank DP. The resulting exact evaluator has arithmetic complexity polynomial in the input size and exponential in the Tanner graph's rank-width with respect to the fault partition, including decomposition construction. After Gaussian elimination, it gives polynomial-time decoding for punctured quantum Reed-Muller codes and a Steane-concatenated family with growing distance under independent single-qubit Pauli noise. Standard contraction of the corresponding tensor networks requires superpolynomial time. A nonnegative realization gives relative floating-point error bounds under explicit arithmetic assumptions. Numerical experiments demonstrate runtime advantages over the tested tensor-network implementations on selected code-capacity and circuit-level instances, with full likelihood evaluation for Reed-Muller codes up to $1{,}023$ qubits. We further use these likelihoods to learn circuit noise parameters from syndromes, evaluate rare postselection probabilities, and quantify decoder optimality gaps. Our work opens new avenues for exploiting algebraic structure in quantum decoding and noise characterization.

</details>


### [196] [Fault Tolerant Quantum Phases of Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39879)
*Colin V. Coane,Shouzhen Gu,Aleksander Kubica*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于信息能否被保护与容错传输的混合态量子相分类框架，用浅层局部信道线路定义相等价，并证明恢复阈值是整相性质、有限深度线路相连的非零阈值态属同一容错相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有量子相分类多针对纯态或特定码态，缺乏对混合态容错能力的统一刻画，且易偏向规范子系统（gauge subsystem）的某个特定状态。本文旨在建立以容错信息保持为核心的相分类，以摆脱对单一态的依赖。

Method: 以编码逻辑量子信息的态集合为对象，用受局部测量与全局经典通信辅助的浅层局部信道线路（shallow local channel circuits）定义相等价，并要求所有量子操作（含测量）对噪声鲁棒。进一步分析综合征（syndrome）结构对含测量线路容错性的影响。

Result: 证明局部噪声恢复阈值（recovery threshold）的存在性是整个相的性质；任意两个具有非零阈值且由有限深度局部信道线路关联的态集合属于同一容错相。该框架给出不偏好规范子系统任何状态的子系统码（subsystem code）分类，并借助3D规范色码（gauge color code）中的维度跳变证明2D与3D色码（color code）的相等价。此外，具有扩展综合征（extended syndrome）的qudit稳定子码（stabilizer code）可实现单次态制备（single-shot state preparation），而测量点状综合征的广泛浅层线路（含2D拓扑码态制备及具有可解任意子理论（solvable anyon theory）的非阿贝尔拓扑序）不容错。

Conclusion: 容错恢复阈值可作为划分混合态量子相的不变量，浅层局部操作下的相等价为统一刻画码与拓扑序提供框架；综合征的几何结构（扩展vs点状）直接决定含测量线路能否容错，并区分单次可制备与不容错的情形。

Abstract: We introduce a classification of quantum phases of mixed states based on the ability to preserve and fault tolerantly transfer information. We formulate phase equivalence between sets of states that encode logical quantum information and are connected by shallow local channel circuits assisted by local measurements and global classical communication of measurement outcomes, where all quantum operations, including measurements, must be robust to noise. We show that the existence of a recovery threshold for local noise is a property of an entire phase. We prove that any two sets of states with nonzero thresholds that are related by finite-depth local channel circuits belong to the same fault tolerant phase. Our approach leads to a classification of subsystem codes without favoring any state of the gauge subsystem, and we demonstrate phase equivalence between 2D and 3D color codes using dimensional jumps within the 3D gauge color code. Lastly, we study how syndrome structure affects fault tolerance in circuits with measurements. We show that qudit stabilizer codes with extended syndromes permit single-shot state preparation, whereas a broad class of shallow circuits measuring point-like syndromes, including state preparation for 2D topological codes and non-Abelian topological orders with solvable anyon theories, is not fault tolerant.

</details>


### [197] [Dissipation accelerates quantum and classical simulation of open-system dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40174)
*Armando Angrisani,Ricard Puig,Yanting Teng,Zoë Holmes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作表明，一类广泛的泡利噪声（Pauli noise，含去极化）会指数压低海森堡演化可观测量（Heisenberg-evolved observables）中的高权重分量，从而降低而非增加量子与经典模拟成本。对有限度（bounded-degree）Lindblad 动力学，估计局域可观测量的 Trotter 步数可与系统规模无关；结合 Richardson 外推（Richardson extrapolation）给出误差界，并揭示了随耗散减弱而扩大的多项式量子优势空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟开放量子系统（open quantum systems）可刻画环境耦合对弛豫、激发输运与量子关联动力学的影响。但直觉上耗散通道（dissipative channels）会引入额外操作、看似增加量子硬件上的成本，因此有必要澄清耗散究竟如何影响模拟的复杂度。

Method: 证明一类广泛泡利噪声（Pauli noise，包括去极化 depolarization）指数抑制海森堡演化可观测量（Heisenberg-evolved observables）的高权重分量；将该压缩与 Richardson 外推（Richardson extrapolation）结合，针对传播可观测量（propagated observable）的 2-范数给出经典与量子误差界（error bounds）。

Result: 对有限度（bounded-degree）Lindblad 动力学，估计局域可观测量时 Trotter 步数可与系统规模无关。时间 t 的局域可观测量期望值可在最大电路深度 O([1+(t/γ)^{3/2}]log²(1/ε)) 内以精度 ε 估计，且不依赖系统规模 n。经典上，稀疏泡利传播（sparse Pauli propagation）对任意固定 γ>0 的运行时间在 n、t 与 1/ε 上为多项式，弱耗散极限下多项式次数为 O(1/γ)。

Conclusion: 量子与经典上界之间的差距说明：随耗散减弱，可能存在显著的多项式量子优势（polynomial quantum advantage），数值结果亦支持这一观察。结果同时为随机输入态或随机哈密顿量作用于固定态、以及控制（非时序）关联函数（out-of-time-order correlators）提供高概率期望值保证。

Abstract: Simulating open quantum systems reveals how environmental coupling shapes relaxation, excitation transport, and the dynamics of quantum correlations. On quantum hardware, dissipative channels add operations and might seem to increase cost.
