However, we show that a broad class of Pauli noise, including depolarization, can ease quantum and classical simulation by exponentially suppressing high-weight components of Heisenberg-evolved observables. For bounded-degree Lindblad dynamics, this permits system-size-independent Trotter steps when estimating local observables. Combining this compression with Richardson extrapolation, we provide classical and quantum error bounds with respect to the 2-norm of the propagated observable. These give high-probability expectation-value guarantees for random input states or random Hamiltonians acting on a fixed state and control (out-of-time-order) correlators. Concretely, we show that expectation values of local observables at time $t$ can be estimated to accuracy $\varepsilon$ using circuits of maximum depth $O\!\left(\left[1+\left(t/γ\right)^{3/2}\right]\log^2(1/\varepsilon)\right)$, independent of the system size $n$. Classically, we show that sparse Pauli propagation runs in time polynomial in $n$, $t$ and $1/\varepsilon$ for every fixed $γ>0$, with polynomial degree $O(1/γ)$ in the weak dissipation limit. The gap between our quantum and classical upper bounds leaves room for a substantial polynomial quantum advantage as dissipation weakens, an observation further supported by our numerical results.

</details>


### [198] [Gibbs Sampling in the Shattered Phase by Decoded Quantum Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40345)
*Leo Zhou,Noah Shutty,Mark Sellke,Stephen P. Jordan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将经典 Ising 自旋哈密顿量的 Gibbs 测度采样（Gibbs sampling）归约为量子解码（quantum decoding）问题，并提出解码量子干涉（Decoded Quantum Interferometry, DQI）可突破经典 Ising k-自旋玻璃（k-spin glass）采样中的拓扑障碍（topological barrier）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Ising k-自旋玻璃的 Gibbs 采样在预测的动力学相变（dynamical phase transition）温度附近受到破碎（shattering）与无序混沌（disorder chaos）所致的拓扑障碍，该障碍阻碍包括 Glauber 动力学及输出分布对输入扰动“稳定”的算法。论文旨在探索 DQI 能否绕开此类障碍。

Method: 论文首先证明 Gibbs 采样问题可归约为量子解码问题，且 DQI 可达温度由解码算法性能决定；随后在随机 Erdős-Rényi 超图（平均度 D≥k）上的 Ising k-自旋玻璃（即 Max-k-XORSAT）任务中进行分析，并对比基于 Prange 方法的经典算法与配备量子解码器的 DQI。

Result: 在从预测动力学相变 β_dyn(k,D)=√((2 ln k)/D)[1+o(1)] 渐近开始的温度区间内，破碎与无序混沌构成阻碍 Glauber 动力学及任何“稳定”算法的拓扑障碍；但该障碍可被基于 Prange 方法的经典算法与 DQI 突破。例如当 D=αk（α>1 固定）时，二者均可在任意逆温度 β<tanh⁻¹(1/α) 下采样（k 充分大），远超 β_dyn~√(2 ln k/(αk))。

Conclusion: 结果表明 DQI 能够克服阻碍稳定算法的拓扑障碍，且 DQI 可达的采样温度取决于所用解码算法的性能，从而建立 Gibbs 采样与量子解码之间的对应关系。

Abstract: We apply Decoded Quantum Interferometry (DQI) to sample from the Gibbs measures of classical Ising spin Hamiltonians. We show that this Gibbs sampling problem reduces to a quantum decoding problem, and the temperature achievable by DQI is determined by the performance of decoding algorithms. We then focus on the task of Gibbs sampling for classical Ising $k$-spin glasses (or Max-$k$-XORSAT) on random Erdős-Rényi hypergraphs with average degree $D\ge k$. In a temperature range beginning asymptotically at the predicted dynamical phase transition, $β_{\rm dyn}(k,D) = \sqrt{(2\ln k)/D}\times [1+o_{k\to\infty}(1)]$, we show that shattering and disorder chaos form a topological barrier that obstructs many algorithms, including Glauber dynamics and any algorithm whose output distribution is "stable" under perturbations of the input. In contrast, we prove that this barrier can be broken both by a classical algorithm based on Prange's method, and by DQI equipped with a quantum decoder. For example, when $D=αk$ with fixed $α>1$, both Prange's algorithm and DQI can sample at any inverse temperature $β< \tanh^{-1}(1/α)$ for sufficiently large $k$, well beyond the dynamical threshold $β_{\rm dyn} \sim \sqrt{2\ln k / (αk)}$. Therefore, our results show that DQI can overcome topological barriers that obstruct stable algorithms.

</details>


### [199] [Efficient Calculation of Equilibrium Correlation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40349)
*Yizhi Shen,Roel Van Beeumen,Wibe A. de Jong,Efekan Kökcü*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种以量子变量存储时间、用 O(log N_t) 个辅助量子比特（ancilla qubits）将平衡关联函数（equilibrium correlation function）在所有时间点上的计算有效并行化的量子算法，仅需单一量子线路并重复运行 O(log N_t) 次，总资源为 O(N_t log N_t)。作者证明该标度最优，并以一维无自旋 Hubbard 模型的格林函数（Green's function）计算作为演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理系统的大多数可测量动力学量可表示为 G_AB(t-t') = ⟨A(t)B(t')⟩ 形式的平衡关联函数。传统量子算法（如 Hadamard test、变分方法或基于线性响应的算法）需要为每个时间点 t-t' 单独构造量子线路，导致至少 O(N_t) 次测量和至少 O(N_t^2) 个量子门，开销随目标时间点数量急剧增长。该文旨在突破这种逐时刻独立计算的范式，降低时间分辨关联函数计算的量子资源成本。

Method: 方法上，作者将时间作为量子变量，使用 O(log N_t) 个辅助量子比特（ancilla qubits）进行编码，从而在单个量子线路中并行处理所有时间点的关联函数计算；该线路仅需运行 O(log N_t) 次。作者进一步对所提方案的总量子资源标度给出最优性证明（optimality proof），并以一维无自旋 Hubbard 模型（one-dimensional spinless Hubbard model）的格林函数（Green's function）计算作为算法演示。

Result: 在大系统尺寸极限下，该算法总资源需求为 O(N_t log N_t)，相较于传统方法的 O(N_t^2) 量子门开销有显著降低；同时测量次数由至少 O(N_t) 降至 O(log N_t)。作者证明该标度是最优的，并成功演示了算法对一维无自旋 Hubbard 模型格林函数的计算。

Conclusion: 通过把时间编码为量子变量，平衡关联函数的计算得以在单一线路内对所有时间点有效并行化，实现了 O(N_t log N_t) 的量子资源标度并获得最优性证明。该结果提供了一种相较于逐时刻线路构造范式更高效的量子模拟路径，并通过一维无自旋 Hubbard 模型的格林函数计算得到验证。

Abstract: A majority of measurable dynamic quantities of a physical system is described as an equilibrium correlation function of the form $G_{AB}(t-t') = \left<A(t)B(t')\right>$, where $t'$ represents the impact time and $t$ represents the response time. Conventional quantum algorithms to compute such quantities via Hamiltonian simulation such as the Hadamard test, variational methods, or linear-response-based algorithms share one feature in common: each time point $t-t'$ is calculated by a different quantum circuit, leading to at least $O(N_t)$ total shots, and at least $O(N_t^2)$ total quantum gates. Here, we provide a different approach: utilizing $O(\log N_t)$ ancilla qubits to store time as a quantum variable, and effectively parallelize the computation of an equilibrium correlation function by requiring only a single quantum circuit for all time points. We show that this circuit needs to be run only $O(\log N_t)$ times, which in total requires $O(N_t \log N_t)$ quantum resources in the large system size limits. We prove that this scaling is optimal, and demonstrate our algorithm calculating the Green's function of a one-dimensional spinless Hubbard model.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [An atom-based machine-learned dipole-moment model and application to conjugated systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39002)
*Yuansheng Zhao,Tamio Yamazaki,Ryohei Hosoya,Yu-ichiro Matsushita,Shinji Tsuneyuki*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将分子体系总偶极矩按原子贡献分解，并利用图神经网络从原子结构预测有效原子偶极矩的方法，可对复杂共轭体系的偶极矩与介电函数进行准确预测，推理成本随原子数线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有基于化学键（bond-based）的模型在复杂共轭体系（conjugated systems）中预测失败，需要一种能更通用、更准确刻画偶极矩与介电性质的方法。

Method: 利用密度泛函理论（density-functional-theory, DFT）计算得到的波函数（wavefunction），将总偶极矩系统地分解为原子贡献之和，再以图神经网络（graph neural networks）由输入原子结构预测该有效原子偶极矩（effective atomic dipole moment）。

Result: 即使对先前键基模型失败的复杂共轭体系，偶极矩与介电函数（dielectric function）仍可被准确预测；模型推理成本随原子数线性增长，较基于玻恩有效电荷（Born-effective-charge）的方案快约 3 倍，且在太赫兹（terahertz）波段的介电函数精度相当或更优。

Conclusion: 该原子分解结合图神经网络的策略在保持高精度的同时提升了推理效率，为分子体系偶极矩及介电性质的预测提供了可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: We introduce a method for predicting the dipole moments of molecular systems by systematically decomposing the total dipole moment into the sum of atomic contributions using the wavefunction from density-functional-theory calculations, and then use graph neutral networks to predict this effective atomic dipole moment from input atomic structure. It is demonstrated that the dipole moments and dielectric function can be accurately predicted even for complicated conjugated systems where the previous bond-based model [Phys. Rev. B 110, 165159] fails. The inference cost of our model scales linearly with the number of atoms and is about 3 times faster compared with Born-effective-charge-based schemes, but shows similar or better accuracy for dielectric function at terahertz range.

</details>


### [201] [A strategic roadmap for an atomistic machine-learning ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39090)
*Jörg Behler,Michele Ceriotti,Cecilia Clementi,Gábor Csányi,Alin-Marin Elena,Aditi Krishnapriyan,Joseph W. Abbott,Fabio Affinito,Albert P. Bartók,Ilyes Batatia,Filippo Bigi,Florian N. Brünig,Yannick Calvino Alonso,Giuseppe Carleo,Aurélie Champagne,Stefan Chmiela,Marc L. Descoteaux,Ralf Drautz,Alexandra Farcas,Meng Gao,Rohit Goswami,Michael F. Herbst,Christian Holm,James R. Kermode,Alexander L. M. Knoll,Tobias Kreiman,Hoang-Thien Luu,Yury Lysogorskiy,Mihai-Cosmin Marinica,Rocco Meli,Klaus-Robert Müller,Frank Noé,Mohamadhosein Nosratjoo,Simon Olsson,Christoph Ortner,Aldo S. Pasos-Trejo,Anyang Peng,Eric Qu,Andrea Rizzi,Mariana Rossi,Bassem Sboui,Gregor N. C. Simm,Alexandre Tkatchenko,Jacopo Venturin,O. Anatole von Lilienfeld,William C. Witt,Brandon M. Wood,Tigany Zarrouk,Fabian Zills*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该战略路线图论文总结了2026年1月在洛桑CECAM召开的社区会议成果，旨在梳理数据驱动机器学习（machine learning, ML）与原子尺度模拟（atomistic simulations）融合的现状、挑战与长期目标，以构建健康、可持续且有影响力的原子尺度ML生态系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动ML技术已广泛融入原子模拟领域，其成功得益于从第一性原理电子结构计算（first-principles electronic-structure calculations）到分子动力学（molecular dynamics）与统计采样（statistical sampling）的成熟物理建模框架。该融合重塑了精度、效率与尺度之间的传统权衡，但同时带来概念与实践挑战，领域快速演进促使社区反思并协调各方努力。

Method: 若干社区成员于2026年1月在洛桑CECAM召开会议，围绕算法、模型、软硬件基础设施（software and hardware infrastructure）以及借助人工智能（artificial intelligence）实现的最具前景的科学应用展开讨论，本文基于这些讨论形成战略路线图。

Result: 会议梳理了该领域当前的技术水平与开放挑战，涵盖以数据为中心与以物理为基础的建模方法之争，以及将既有软件栈（software stacks）适配至现代硬件加速器（hardware accelerators）与ML库的难题。

Conclusion: 论文提出若干长期目标与具体行动，以协调社区力量，建立健康、可持续且具影响力的原子尺度ML生态系统。

Abstract: Data-driven machine learning (ML) techniques have become an essential tool in many domains of science. Their application to atomistic simulations of matter is particularly widespread and impactful. This success is due largely to the existence of a well-developed and established physics-based modeling framework, ranging from first-principles electronic-structure calculations to molecular dynamics and statistical sampling, into which ML was integrated naturally to reshape long-standing trade-offs between accuracy, efficiency, and scale. Nevertheless, this integration raises both conceptual and practical challenges, from choosing between data-centric and physics-based modeling approaches to adapting established software stacks to modern hardware accelerators and ML libraries. As the field evolves rapidly, fueled in part by widespread enthusiasm but also by tangible impact, it seems appropriate to take a moment to consider the current state of the art and open challenges, and reflect on what can be done to better coordinate efforts across the community. With this goal in mind, several members of this community met in Lausanne in January 2026 at CECAM to discuss algorithms, models, software and hardware infrastructure, and the most promising scientific applications that have become possible thanks to the use of artificial intelligence in atomic-scale simulations. This strategic roadmap paper summarizes the outcomes of these discussions, suggesting some long-term goals, and some concrete actions, to establish a healthy, sustainable and impactful atomistic ML ecosystem.

</details>


<div id='physics.data-an'></div>

# physics.data-an [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Continuous Event Weighting for Resonance Searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38189)
*Yi Yang,Jun-Jie Chang,Hao-Yin Liao*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种连续事件加权框架（continuous event weighting framework），用于共振搜索（resonance searches），在不细分灵敏度类别的前提下利用辅助事件信息，并通过伪实验与CMS公开数据验证其产额恢复、不确定度传播与发现灵敏度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共振搜索依赖硬选择（hard selection）或有限的灵敏度类别划分，会随类别细化导致信息损失或复杂度上升。论文旨在寻找一种既能保留辅助判别信息、又避免灵敏度驱动事件类别爆炸的实用途径。

Method: 将硬选择视为二值权重、有限类别视为分段常权重的特例，在背景主导极限下其细分区趋于连续的信噪密度比权重（signal to background density ratio weight）。据此构建可解释的单一共振谱（resonance spectrum），并推导可计算的统计方差与信号产额恢复。使用Gaussian、Gamma、Beta辅助观测量进行一百万次全显式事件级伪实验（pseudoexperiments），并在CMS Run2016G与Run2016H双光子公开数据（diphoton Open Data）上以探测器级胶子融合希格斯模拟（gluon fusion Higgs simulation）作为信号参考进行真实数据基准测试。

Result: 伪实验验证了产额恢复、不确定度传播与频率派覆盖（frequentist coverage），并显著提升发现灵敏度（discovery sensitivity）。在CMS公开数据基准上，该框架作为方法学测试而非CMS希格斯分析，显示了连续辅助信息利用的实际可行性。

Conclusion: 结果表明存在一条实用路径：在不增殖灵敏度驱动事件类别的情况下，充分利用连续辅助信息（continuous auxiliary information）进行共振搜索。

Abstract: We develop a continuous event weighting framework for resonance searches that uses auxiliary event information without dividing the data into increasingly fine sensitivity categories. A hard selection is a binary weight, finite categories correspond to piecewise constant weights, and in the background dominated limit their fine partition approaches a continuous signal to background density ratio weight. The construction retains auxiliary discrimination in a single physically interpretable resonance spectrum, with calculable statistical variance and direct recovery of the selected signal yield. One million fully explicit event level pseudoexperiments using Gaussian, Gamma, and Beta auxiliary observables verify yield recovery, uncertainty propagation, and frequentist coverage while substantially improving discovery sensitivity. The method is further tested on publicly available CMS Run2016G and Run2016H diphoton Open Data as an independent real data benchmark, using detector level gluon fusion Higgs simulation only as the signal reference. This is a methodological test of the framework, not a CMS Higgs analysis. The results demonstrate a practical route to exploiting continuous auxiliary information without proliferating sensitivity driven event categories.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Traversable Wormholes Supported by Conformally Coupled Scalar Fields in $D \geq 4$: Geometry, Geodesics, and Quasinormal Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38217)
*Pablo Vidal*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文分析由无质量共形耦合标量场（massless conformally coupled scalar field）生成的双参数（μ, b）非对称可穿越虫洞（traversable wormhole）解，考察其正则性、喉部与flare-out条件、光子零测地线运动，并半解析与数值求解Klein-Gordon标量扰动及QNM谱，发现四维下与BBMB/极端Reissner-Nordström黑洞存在等谱性（isospectrality），最后给出可穿越性条件与穿越固有时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于构造并系统刻画一种由无质量共形耦合标量场生成的可穿越虫洞解，检验其在广义相对论（general relativity, GR）框架下对经典能量条件的违反，并探究其准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNM）与黑洞解之间的谱学关系。

Method: 采用双参数（μ, b）非对称虫洞度规，分析时空正则性与两渐近平直区；定义虫洞喉部（throat）并检验flare-out条件；通过有效势（effective potential）研究光子零测地线（null geodesics）并数值计算轨迹、用嵌入图（embedding diagrams）可视化；对无质量Klein-Gordon方程描述的标量扰动进行半解析与数值QNM求解；并仿照Morris与Thorne的思路推导可穿越性条件与穿越固有时间。

Result: 在合适参数范围内，时空处处正则且具有两个渐近平直区；flare-out条件成立但导致GR中经典能量条件被违反，从而需要奇异物质（exotic matter）维持喉部张开。标量扰动仅由单一参数μ主导，在D=4时虫洞与BBMB/极端Reissner-Nordström黑洞之间呈现等谱性（isospectrality）。

Conclusion: 该非对称虫洞解在满足可穿越性条件的参数区间内是物理上自洽的几何结构，但其维持依赖奇异物质；其QNM谱与特定黑洞解等谱，提示两者之间存在值得进一步探讨的对应关系，文中亦讨论了可能的推广与未来研究方向。

Abstract: In this work, we analyze a two-parameter $(μ, b)$ asymmetric family of traversable wormhole solutions generated by a massless conformally coupled scalar field. We show that, for a suitable range of parameters, the spacetime is everywhere regular and has two asymptotically flat regions. We then define the wormhole throat and verify the flare-out condition, which leads to violations of the classical energy conditions in GR, thereby requiring exotic matter to hold the throat open. We further analyze the motion of photons governed by null geodesics through an effective potential. The resulting trajectories are computed numerically and visualized using embedding diagrams. Following this route, we investigate scalar perturbations governed by the massless Klein-Gordon equation and find the QNM spectrum semianalytically and numerically. Interestingly enough, we show that such perturbations are governed by only one parameter $μ$. Therefore, in $D = 4$, we find an isospectrality between the wormhole and the BBMB/Extremal Reissner-Nordström black hole. In the spirit of the seminal work of Morris and Thorne, we derive the conditions for traversability and compute the proper time for a traveler to traverse the wormhole. Finally, we discuss possible extensions and future directions for our work.

</details>


### [204] [A Strong Cosmologically Coupled Mass Growth Solution in Linearized General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38303)
*Kevin S. Croker,Joshua Cohon,Viktor T. Toth,Massimiliano Rinaldi,Nicolas Fernandez,Joel L. Weiner,Duncan Farrah,Gregory Tarlé,Gustavo Niz,Christopher Cain,James W. Rohlf,Alejandro Aviles,Jake Summers,Leander Thiele,Rogier A. Windhorst,Joshua Grumski-Flores*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在广义相对论框架下线性阶构造出 Hernandez-Misner-Sharp 质量随宇宙膨胀被动增长（∝a^κ）的解，首次使 κ∼3 的宇宙学耦合黑洞（cosmologically coupled black holes, CCBH）具备驱动晚期加速膨胀的潜力，为脉冲星双星观测检验奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DESI Data Release 2 提升了以宇宙学耦合黑洞（CCBHs）解释晚期加速膨胀的关注度，但已知 CCBH 模型的质量增长过慢，无法驱动该加速。论文旨在构造质量增长足够快的 CCBH 解。

Method: 在线性阶（linear order）上构造广义相对论（General Relativity）解，使 Hernandez-Misner-Sharp 质量随宇宙膨胀被动增长，比例关系为 ∝a^κ。常数 κ 进入 Weyl 张量与应力张量（stress-tensor）算符的全部本征值，以确认增长在自由场与物质两个扇区均为物理的；并检查解在恒星级坍缩致密天体相关时空上的因果性。

Result: κ 出现在 Weyl 与应力张量算符的所有本征值中，说明质量增长在自由场与物质扇区均为明确的物理效应；该解在相关时空（related to stellar collapse compact objects）中是因果的。并在 κ=3 处出现一团“被激发的真空”（energized vacuum），与别处预测的 energ

ized vacuum black holes 质量按 a^3 标度相互印证。

Conclusion: 该工作为迈向 κ∼3 的 CCBH 精确解的第一步，并使通过脉冲星双星（pulsar binaries）开展观测检验成为可能。

Abstract: DESI Data Release 2 has heightened interest in cosmologically coupled black holes (CCBHs) as an explanation for accelerating late-time expansion, but known models for CCBHs grow too slowly to drive it. We construct at linear order a solution to General Relativity whose Hernandez-Misner-Sharp mass passively grows $\propto a^κ$ as the universe expands. The constant $κ$ enters all eigenvalues of the Weyl and stress-tensor operators, so growth is unambiguously physical in both free-field and material sectors. The solution is causal over spacetime relevant for stellar collapse compact objects. A cloud of energized vacuum emerges at $κ= 3$, complementing the $a^3$ scaling predicted elsewhere for the masses of energized vacuum black holes. This first step toward $κ\sim 3$ CCBH exact solutions enables observational tests with pulsar binaries.

</details>


### [205] [Towards solving General Relativity with Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38331)
*Juan A. Carretero,Jorge F. Urbán,Fernando Abalos,Joan Massó,Oscar A. Reula,Jose A. Pons,Carlos Palenzuela*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在广义相对论（General Relativity）爱因斯坦方程（Einstein's equations）中的应用，通过标准数值相对论（Numerical Relativity）基准测试并进行孤立孤子玻色星（solitonic boson star）动力学演化模拟，验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值相对论方法通常依赖预定义计算网格，而PINNs提供无需网格的连续解表示，可在训练域内任意点求值，因此有必要探索其求解耦合物质-爱因斯坦方程（matter-Einstein equations）的潜力与适用性。

Method: 采用PINNs框架将神经网络约束为满足底层物理定律，讨论数学表述、网络架构、损失函数与训练策略，并在一系列数值相对论标准基准上演示，随后扩展至孤立孤子玻色星的动力学演化场景，直接在三维笛卡尔坐标（three-dimensional Cartesian coordinates）下进行模拟。

Result: PINN能够复现高度致密玻色星的平衡结构，并恢复其特有的径向振荡频率（radial oscillation frequencies），表明其可直接在三维笛卡尔坐标下完成模拟。

Conclusion: 结果表明PINNs是求解耦合物质-爱因斯坦方程及探索数值相对论中替代表述的可行补充框架。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are a machine-learning framework for approximating solutions to systems of partial differential equations by constraining neural networks to satisfy the underlying physical laws. The resulting continuous representation does not require a predefined computational mesh and can be evaluated at arbitrary points within the training domain. In this work, we investigate the application of PINNs to Einstein's equations of General Relativity. We discuss the mathematical formulation, network architectures, loss functions, and training strategies used to obtain accurate spacetime solutions, and demonstrate the approach through a series of standard benchmarks from Numerical Relativity. We then extend the method to a more astrophysical setting by considering the dynamical evolution of an isolated solitonic boson star. Our simulations show that a PINN operating directly in three-dimensional Cartesian coordinates can reproduce the equilibrium structure of a highly compact boson star and recover its characteristic radial oscillation frequencies. These results establish PINNs as a viable complementary framework for solving coupled matter-Einstein equations and exploring alternative formulations in numerical relativity.

</details>


### [206] [Explicit Analytical Representations for the Schwarzschild Radial Equation via Hypergeometric and Frobenius Expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38461)
*J. G. R. Valangelis,Ailton C. Nascimento,Helder A. S. Costa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究史瓦西外部时空下大质量克莱因-戈尔登方程的径向求解问题，指出常用紧致化坐标会导致五阶递推关系、无法退化为三阶，并转而通过视界归一化的 Frobenius 级数构造物理分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量克莱因-戈尔登方程在史瓦西外部时空约化为合流 Heun 型径向微分方程，其精确求解（尤其是准正规模谱的选取）存在技术障碍。作者希望严格处理一个次领头离心项，并厘清超几何展开框架与物理坐标选择之间的相容性问题。

Method: 在 Svartholm–Schmidt 超几何展开框架下，保留一个不改变视界指标指数、但会显著移动辅助参数的次领头离心项，推导三项递推关系与修正的连分数相容条件（将最小尾项与低端递推匹配）。同时论证物理紧致化坐标 z=(r-1)/r 会将不规则奇点移至 z=1，使展开变为五阶递推。为避开该障碍，在 z=0 处直接用 Frobenius 级数构造视界归一化的物理分支。

Result: 物理紧致化坐标导致超几何展开本质上成为五阶递推，无法退化为三项。所构造的 Frobenius 表示表现出高阶收敛性，相对精确径向常微分方程的残差接近精度下限。

Conclusion: 准正规模（quasinormal mode）的谱选择本质上仍是不规则奇异端点处的连接问题（connection problem），坐标选择对递推结构与求解路径具有决定性影响。

Abstract: The massive Klein-Gordon equation on the Schwarzschild exterior reduces to a radial differential equation of confluent-Heun type. We present its exact reduction by rigorously retaining a subleading centrifugal term that preserves the horizon indicial exponents but critically shifts the accessory parameters. Within the Svartholm--Schmidt hypergeometric-expansion framework, we derive the three-term recurrence relation and establish the corrected continued-fraction compatibility condition matching the minimal tail to the lower-end recurrence. Crucially, we demonstrate that the physical compactification coordinate $z=(r-1)/r$ shifts the irregular singular point to $z=1$, inherently transforming the hypergeometric expansion into a five-term recurrence that cannot degenerate to three terms. To circumvent this obstruction, we construct the horizon-normalized physical branch via a direct Frobenius series at $z=0$. This representation exhibits high-order convergence and near-precision-floor residuals against the exact radial ODE, while clarifying why quasinormal-mode spectral selection remains a connection problem at the irregular singular endpoint.

</details>


### [207] [Non-Metricity Trace Forms and Weak-Field Light Deflection: A Geometric Optical Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38568)
*Sabutay Ugur*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在弱场、非度规（non-metric）框架下，将引力光偏折（gravitational light deflection）重构为由非度规迹（non-metricity trace）直接决定的光学响应，并获得与广义相对论一致的Eddington偏折角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望在不引入外部光学类比、也不假设微观形变律（microscopic deformation law）的前提下，建立从宏观膨胀源（dilation sourcing）到光偏折的完整弱场链条。

Method: 在Schwarzschild弱场的各向同性表述中显式计算非度规迹1-形式W与P，利用精确关系d ln n = W - 4P（n = g/f）确定有效折射率（effective refractive index）；并通过观测者投影的宏观膨胀流引入有效标量校准势χ，静态弱场极限下满足∇²χ = -κρ。

Result: 点质量校准给出Φ = χ/C₀ ≃ 2GM/(rc²)，据此光学计算得到Eddington偏折角4GM/(bc²)，与标准结果数值一致。

Conclusion: 该工作的意义不在于修改数值预言，而在于构建了从宏观膨胀源到非度规迹、有效折射率与光偏折的封闭弱场链条，无需为材料杆或时钟假定微观形变律。

Abstract: This paper develops a weak-field, non-metric operational account of gravitational light deflection. In the isotropic formulation of the Schwarzschild weak field, the non-metricity trace 1-forms W and P are computed explicitly and shown to determine the optical response through the exact relation $d\ln n=W-4P$, $n=g/f$. Thus the effective refractive index governing null propagation is not introduced as an external optical analogy, but is tied directly to the trace sector of non-metricity. To connect the trace geometry with matter, an effective scalar calibration potential $χ$ is sourced by an observer-projected macroscopic dilation current, yielding $\nabla^2χ=-κρ$ in the static weak-field limit. The point-mass calibration fixes $Φ=χ/C_0\simeq 2GM/(rc^2)$, and the resulting optical calculation gives the Eddington deflection angle $4GM/(bc^2)$. The significance of the result is not a modified numerical prediction, but a closed weak-field chain from macroscopic dilation sourcing to non-metricity traces, effective refractive index, and light deflection, without postulating a microscopic deformation law for material rods or clocks.

</details>


### [208] [Quasi-Einstein metrics from Horava gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38605)
*Jorge Bellorin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文指出准爱因斯坦度量（quasi-Einstein metric）的几何定义与Hořava引力（Horava gravity）之间存在直接对应关系：Hořava引力大尺度有效作用量（large-distance effective action）的静态正交解即为准爱因斯坦度量，反之在附加函数方程条件下亦成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立几何概念（准爱因斯坦度量）与修改引力理论（Hořava引力）之间的直接联系，从而为两个此前相对独立的研究方向提供统一的表述框架。

Method: 采用理论推导（theoretical derivation）方式，从Hořava引力大尺度有效作用量出发，考察其静态（static）且正交（orthogonal）的解，并与准爱因斯坦度量的几何定义进行对照分析。

Result: 证明了正向命题：Hořava引力大尺度有效作用量的静态正交解构成准爱因斯坦度量；并指出反向命题在准爱因斯坦流形上的函数满足特定方程时同样成立。

Conclusion: 准爱因斯坦度量与Hořava引力之间存在双向对应关系，但反向对应需以特定函数方程为附加条件；该结果确立了两种形式体系间的等价性条件。

Abstract: In this note we present a direct relation between the geometrical definition of a quasi-Einstein metric and Horava gravity: the solutions of the large-distance effective action of Horava gravity that are static and orthogonal are quasi-Einstein metrics. The converse is also true if the function over the quasi-Einstein manifold satisfies a given equation.

</details>


### [209] [Quadrupolar logarithmic tidal response of Einstein Euler Heisenberg black holes: the role of the second electromagnetic invariant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38649)
*Ramon Becar,P. A. Gonzalez,Ali Ovgun,Joel Saavedra,Yerko Vasquez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文从耦合的静态奇宇称（odd-parity）引力与电磁方程出发，确定带电 Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg 黑洞的四极对数潮汐响应（logarithmic tidal response）。在一阶 Euler-Heisenberg 耦合下得到正则跑动矩阵（running matrix），并揭示其与双不变量有效场论结果的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在研究带电黑洞在四极（quadrupolar）通道下由高阶电磁相互作用引起的对数潮汐响应。尽管第二个电磁不变量在电背景上为零，其二次相互作用仍会进入轴向导数涨落方程，这一效应值得刻画。

Method: 采用耦合静态奇宇称引力与电磁方程（coupled static odd-parity gravitational and electromagnetic equations）。在 Euler-Heisenberg 耦合 $a$ 的一阶，通过直接渐近递推（asymptotic recursion）得到正则跑动矩阵，并定义其为 $r^{-2}\log(r_0/r)$ 项中乘正则源矢量的系数。

Result: 得到 $C_{\rm can}^{\rm EH}=(9aQ^2/10)\left(\begin{smallmatrix}0&-Q\\-Q&M\end{smallmatrix}\right)$，其局部系数在一阶微扰下保留对亚极端比 $Q/M$ 的完整依赖。两个四阶不变量在两个非零通道中部分抵消，作用量归一化场使引力电磁互易性（gravitoelectromagnetic reciprocity）显式化。局部极端极限经约定转换后复现双不变量有效场论（effective-field-theory）结果；允许独立四阶耦合时，相同电背景几何可具有不同的轴向跑动矩阵，并在指定双算符模型内给出代数逆关系。

Conclusion: 该计算确定了最小耦合四阶电动力学（minimally coupled quartic electrodynamics）中的局部对数跑动；有限的视界匹配响应（finite horizon-matched response）还需额外的全局匹配计算。

Abstract: We determine the quadrupolar logarithmic tidal response of electrically charged Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg black holes from the coupled static odd-parity gravitational and electromagnetic equations. Although the second electromagnetic invariant vanishes on the electric background, its quadratic interaction contributes to the axial fluctuation equations. At first order in the Euler-Heisenberg coupling $a$, a direct asymptotic recursion gives the canonical running matrix $C_{\rm can}^{\rm EH}=(9aQ^2/10)\left(\begin{smallmatrix}0&-Q\\-Q&M\end{smallmatrix}\right)$, deffined as the coefficients multiplying the canonical source vector in the $r^{-2}\log(r_0/r)$ term. The resulting local coefficients retain the full dependence on the the subextremal ratio $\frac{Q}{M}$ at this perturbative order. The two quartic invariants partially cancel in both nonzero channels, while the action-normalized fields make gravitoelectromagnetic reciprocity explicit. The local extremal limit of the running matrix reproduces the corresponding two-invariant effective-field-theory result after convention conversion. Allowing independent quartic couplings further shows that identical electric background geometries can have different axial running matrices and yields algebraic inverse relations within the specified two-operator model. The calculation determines local logarithmic running within minimally coupled quartic electrodynamics; the finite horizon-matched response requires an additional global matching calculation.

</details>


### [210] [RRR fluctuations from non-singular bounce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38990)
*Sayantan Choudhury*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在包含收缩相（contracting phase）、非奇异反弹（non-singular bounce）与急剧过渡至超慢滚（ultra-slow-roll）暴胀的有效场论框架中，一致处理正则化（regularization）、重整化（renormalization）与重求和（resummation），并实现原初黑洞（primordial black hole, PBH）生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需在统一框架内自洽处理量子圈修正（quantum loop corrections）的发散问题，并规避单纯暴胀难以生成太阳质量原初黑洞的禁行定理（no-go theorem）。

Method: 采用有效场论（effective field theory）方法，计算跨越各阶段的功率谱（power spectrum）量子圈修正；通过重整化消除二次紫外发散（quadratic UV divergence），通过重求和软化对数红外发散（logarithmic IR divergence），并验证方案独立性（scheme independence）。

Result: 引入收缩与反弹后可生成太阳质量原初黑洞，$M_{\rm PBH}\sim{\cal O}(M_\odot)$，所需最小暴胀量 $ΔN_{\rm Total}\sim{\cal O}(60)$；在 $0.88\le c_s\le1$ 时峰值谱幅度落入 $10^{-3}\le A\le10^{-2}$，保持因果性与幺正性；对 $10^{-33}\!-\!10^{-27}$ 与 $10^{-6}\!-\!10^{-1}M_\odot$ 区间的 PBH 丰度与微引力透镜（microlensing）约束进行对照以控制过量产生。

Conclusion: 该处理表明正则化、重整化与重求和可自洽结合于收缩—反弹—暴胀框架，既回避禁行定理、又满足因果性与幺正性，并通过微引力透镜约束调控 PBH 丰度。

Abstract: We present a consistent treatment of regularization, renormalization, and resummation in a framework involving a contracting phase, a non-singular bounce, and inflation with a sharp transition from slow-roll to ultra-slow-roll, enabling primordial black hole production. Within an effective field theory approach, we compute quantum loop corrections to the power spectrum across all phases. Quadratic UV divergences are removed through renormalization, while logarithmic IR divergences are softened via resummation, demonstrating scheme independence. The inclusion of contraction and bounce allows the generation of solar-mass PBHs, $M_{\rm PBH}\sim{\cal O}(M_\odot)$, with minimal inflation $ΔN_{\rm Total}\sim{\cal O}(60)$, thereby evading the no-go theorem. For $0.88\le c_s\le1$, the peak spectrum amplitude lies within $10^{-3}\le A\le10^{-2}$, preserving causality and unitarity. PBH abundances across $10^{-33}\!-\!10^{-27}$ and $10^{-6}\!-\!10^{-1}M_\odot$ are confronted with microlensing constraints to control PBH overproduction.

</details>


### [211] [Primordial Tensor Signatures and Gravitational Wave Constraints in Lorentz Violating Inflation with Non-Canonical Kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39087)
*Dibash Plaban Kalita,Shreya Banerjee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在洛伦兹破缺（Lorentz violation）暴胀框架下，结合非正则标量场（non-canonical scalar field）动力学，推导张量扰动传播速度及张量谱指数与其跑动的关联关系，并借助原初引力波（primordial gravitational wave）观测约束洛伦兹破缺耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索洛伦兹破缺与非正则暴胀动力学对原初张量扰动的联合影响，并寻求原初引力波谱作为洛伦兹破缺的互补探针。摘要强调，晚期引力波速度的强约束需借助额外假设才能转化为原初约束。

Method: 采用 Einstein-Æther 型框架，引入高阶动能项 $K_3X^2$，在解析可处理的势能无关背景（potential free background）下推导张量传播速度及其对有效洛伦兹破缺耦合的依赖。选取两个代表性耦合形式 $q_k=\lambda_T\phi_k^2$ 与 $q_k=\lambda_T\phi_k^4$ 进行分析。

Result: 所得张量谱指数（tensor spectral index）$n_T$ 及其跑动（running）$\alpha_T$ 满足各自不同的自洽关系（consistency relations），为原初引力波谱中的洛伦兹破缺提供潜在特征。基于张量倾斜与跑动导出相应约束，并与 GW170817/GRB170817A 给出的晚期引力波速度强约束进行对比。

Conclusion: 结果表明原初张量观测量可作为洛伦兹破缺及其与非正则暴胀动力学相互作用的互补探针。同时指出，将晚期引力波速度约束转译为原初约束，需要关于张量扇区耦合在暴胀至晚期宇宙之间演化的假设。

Abstract: We investigate primordial tensor perturbations in a Lorentz violating inflationary framework with non-canonical scalar field dynamics. Using an Einstein-Æther type with a higher order kinetic term $K_3X^2$, we derive the tensor propagation speed and its dependence on an effective Lorentz violating coupling. We consider an analytically tractable potential free background and study two representative couplings, $q_k=λ_Tφ_k^2$ and $q_k=λ_Tφ_k^4$. The resulting tensor spectral index $n_T$ and its running $α_T$ satisfy distinct consistency relations, providing potential signatures of Lorentz violation in the primordial gravitational wave spectrum. We derive corresponding constraints from the tensor tilt and running and compare them with the stringent late-time bound on the gravitational wave speed from GW170817/GRB170817A. We emphasize that translating this bound into primordial constraint requires an assumption about the evolution of tensor sector coupling between inflation and the late Universe. Our results demonstrate that primordial tensor observables can provide a complementary probe of Lorentz violation and its interplay with non-canonical inflationary dynamics.

</details>


### [212] [Second-order multipole response of two nearby incoherent sources in wave-optical imaging of a Schwarzschild black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39110)
*Wenfu Cao,Hongsheng Zhang,Zong-Kuan Guo,Rong-Gen Cai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究施瓦西黑洞对两个邻近、互不相干点源的波动光学成像，通过有限孔径傅里叶变换构建图像并与亮度质心的单源参考像比较，以分离双星结构。结果显示双星可分辨性本质上是色散的，并给出残余信号既可探测又可用二阶矩准确描述的波长区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究邻近双星（binary）在黑洞强引力透镜波动光学成像中能否被识别，以及这种可识别性如何依赖于波长与源参数。传统瑞利判据（Rayleigh criterion）下该双星始终未被分辨，因此需要借助结构残差寻找新的分辨途径。

Method: 利用球谐加法定理（spherical-harmonic addition theorem）为任意源方向构造分波场（partial-wave fields），再通过有限孔径傅里叶变换（finite-aperture Fourier transform）成像。将双星图像与位于亮度质心、总亮度相等的单源图像相减以隔离双星结构，并在质心处展开，使一阶项被精确消除，主导结构修正由二阶中心矩（second central moment）$\\mu_2=4a^2q/(1+q)^2$ 控制。

Result: 数值计算证实残差（residual）随源半间距与亮度比的依赖关系符合预期。长波长下成像核（imaging kernel）在源间距内变化很小，双星与质心参考几乎不可区分；波长减小后更细的条纹增强残差，在 $3\\leq M\\omega\\leq12$（$2.09\\geq\\lambda/M\\geq0.52$）内残差增大约三倍，尽管该双星在瑞利意义下仍未被分辨。同时二阶近似逐渐失效，其相对误差首次达到 1 发生在 $17<M\\omega<18$（$0.349<\\lambda/M<0.370$），表明需要更高阶源矩。

Conclusion: 结果表明邻近双星的可分辨性本质上是色散的（intrinsically chromatic），并据此界定了双星既能通过残差被探测、又能被二阶矩准确描述的波长范围。

Abstract: We study wave-optical imaging of two nearby, mutually incoherent point sources by a Schwarzschild black hole. Using the spherical-harmonic addition theorem, we construct partial-wave fields for arbitrary source directions and form images through a finite-aperture Fourier transform. To isolate the binary structure, we compare the binary image with that of a single source of equal total brightness placed at the brightness centroid. Expanding about the centroid removes the first-order term exactly, so the leading structural correction is controlled by the second central moment, $μ_2=4a^2q/(1+q)^2$. Numerical calculations confirm the expected dependence of the residual on source half-separation and brightness ratio. We then study its wavelength dependence. At long wavelengths, the imaging kernel varies little across the source separation, making the binary nearly indistinguishable from the centroid reference. As the wavelength decreases, finer fringes enhance the residual, which grows by about a factor of three over $3\leq Mω\leq12$ ($2.09\geqλ/M\geq0.52$), even though the pair remains unresolved in the Rayleigh sense. Meanwhile, the second-order approximation gradually breaks down: its relative error first reaches unity for $17<Mω<18$ ($0.349<λ/M<0.370$), indicating the need for higher-order source moments. These results demonstrate that the distinguishability of a nearby binary is intrinsically chromatic and identify the wavelength range in which the binary is both detectable through its residual and accurately described at second order.

</details>


### [213] [Black Holes With Complete Asymptotic Throats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39186)
*Yi-Bo Liang,Hong-Rong Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: Error


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Error

Method: Error

Result: Error

Conclusion: Error

Abstract: We investigate whether a spherically symmetric black hole can have a complete inner end at finite, nonzero areal radius while satisfying the standard energy conditions sufficiently far outside its horizon. Retaining two independent metric functions, we treat both null-type and spacelike-type asymptotic throats and derive a necessary and sufficient integral criterion for causal geodesic completeness. Under boundedness and finite-limit assumptions on the independent curvature components, null-type ends have limiting curvature fixed by the throat radius, whereas spacelike-type ends admit an additional nonnegative curvature parameter. We obtain criteria for bounded curvature in timelike and null parallel-propagated frames. Complete spacelike-type examples can have bounded scalar invariants and bounded timelike-frame curvature but unbounded null-frame curvature at infinite affine parameter. We also prove that the radial null energy condition must fail arbitrarily close to every complete inner end in the stated class. Nevertheless, explicit real-analytic constructions with either type of throat satisfy the null, weak, dominant, and strong energy conditions throughout a sufficiently distant exterior region. Smooth constructions can become exactly Schwarzschild beyond a finite radius. These results establish the compatibility of complete asymptotic throats with exterior energy conditions while distinguishing completeness from different levels of curvature regularity.

</details>


### [214] [Nonlinear scalarization and thermodynamics in Einstein-Phantom scalar-Gauss-Bonnet theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39236)
*Tian-Zhen Dai,Yun Soo Myung,De-Cheng Zou,Meng-Yun Lai,Hyat Huang,Wen-Cong Gan,Lina Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究幻场标量（phantom scalar）耦合高斯-博内不变量（Gauss–Bonnet invariant）时史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black-hole）的非线性标量化（nonlinear scalarization）。通过三类多项式耦合构造探测解与完全反作用解，发现因幻场因子ε在探测标量方程中消失，其非线性演化与正则标量理论一致，并最终指出幻场动能理论不存在非线性稳定的标量相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察幻场标量（phantom scalar）与高斯-博内不变量（Gauss–Bonnet invariant）非线性耦合下的黑洞标量化行为，特别是非线性耦合依赖性与相稳定性问题。

Method: 引入三类满足 ζ''(0)=0 的多项式耦合以刻画非线性耦合依赖；构建无节点探测解（nodeless probe solutions），并求解完全反作用（fully backreacted）的下支与主支两组解。

Result: 在等质量与等温条件下，下支较主支具有更高的 Wald 熵（Wald entropy）与更低的亥姆霍兹自由能（Helmholtz free energy），但其热容（heat capacity）为负；此外，幻场动能理论中不存在非线性稳定的标量相。

Conclusion: 结果表明下支优于主支，且对于幻场动能理论而言不存在非线性稳定的标量相（nonlinearly stable scalar phase）。

Abstract: We study nonlinear scalarization of Schwarzschild black-holes for a phantom scalar coupled to the Gauss--Bonnet invariant. Three polynomial couplings satisfying $ζ''(0)=0$ are introduced to investigate its nonlinear coupling dependence. Since the phantom factor $ε$ disappears in the probe scalar equation, its nonlinear evolution is the same as in the canonical scalar theory. We construct nodeless probe solutions and fully backreacted two branches of lower and primary. At equal mass and equal temperature, respectively, the lower branches have higher Wald entropy and lower Helmholtz free energy than primary branch, but their heat capacities are negative. This implies that lower branch favors than primary branch. Finally, we show that there is no nonlinerly stable scalar phase for a phantom kinetic theory.

</details>


### [215] [Analysis of Phantom Cosmology With Different Potentials: Inflation and Isotropization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39281)
*Behzad Tajahmad,Hossein Kheiri*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究幻场（phantom-field）模型在五类势函数下于比安基I型（Bianchi I）、比安基III型（Bianchi III）与康托夫斯基-萨克斯（Kantowski-Sachs）背景上的暴胀动力学与各向同性化（isotropization），通过动力学系统（dynamical-systems）方法识别并分析87个临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察幻场模型在各向异性宇宙学背景下的暴胀与各向同性化行为，并针对Heusler命题的局限性寻求更完整的分析工具。

Method: 首先建立模型的一般性质并推广Heusler命题；随后采用动力学系统方法，对五类势函数在三种各向异性背景下的宇宙演化进行综合分析，共识别87个临界点并分析其性质。

Result: 共识别出87个临界点并对其性质进行了分析；在此框架下重新审视了Collins与Hawking以及Burd与Barrow的结论，并提出并讨论了若干具有深刻概念含义的挑战性问题。

Conclusion: 动力学系统方法可弥补Heusler命题的不足，为幻场模型的暴胀与各向同性化提供全面分析；研究强调数学结果的物理解释，并推动对各向异性宇宙学既有结论的再思考。

Abstract: We investigate inflationary dynamics and isotropization in a phantom-field model with five families of potentials on Bianchi I, Bianchi III, and Kantowski-Sachs backgrounds. First, the general properties of the model are established, and Heusler's proposition is extended. To address the limitations of this proposition, a powerful dynamical-systems approach is then adopted to provide a comprehensive analysis of the cosmological evolution. In total, 87 critical points are identified and their properties are analyzed. Throughout the analysis, we emphasize the physical interpretation of the mathematical results and aim to provide a thorough explanation of the rationale behind the choice of each mathematical tool and variable. Within this framework, the conclusions of Collins and Hawking and of Burd and Barrow are revisited, and several challenging questions with deep conceptual implications are posed and discussed.

</details>


### [216] [Global Integrated Null Energy Contribution : Classification of Traversable Wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39468)
*Soumya Chakrabarti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文论证可穿越虫洞（traversable wormhole）喉部的零能量条件（null energy condition, NEC）局域违反并不必然意味着体积积分后的零能量贡献为负，并可重建一族虫洞几何，同时提出基于全局体积积分度量的虫洞分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有认识中，虫洞喉部的 NEC 局域违反常被等同于整体能量条件被破坏，但积分贡献实际可任意接近零，这促使其重新审视局域违反与全局贡献之间的关系。

Method: 以体积积分的零能量贡献为分析量，论证其可为正值，并据此反推（reconstruct）构建一族虫洞几何；此外依据该全局积分度量对可穿越虫洞进行重新分类。

Result: 结果表明，体积积分后的零能量贡献不仅可以任意接近零，甚至可以取正值；由此获得了一族新的虫洞几何。

Conclusion: 喉部 NEC 的局域违反不足以判定全局 NEC 违反；基于全局体积积分度量的分类为可穿越虫洞提供了新的刻画方式。

Abstract: We argue that the local violation of null energy condition at the throat of a traversable wormhole does not necessarily indicate that the total volume integrated null energy contribution is negative. It is already known that it can be arbitrarily close to zero. We show that it can even be positive and use this argument to reconstruct a family of wormhole geometries. We also propose a novel classification of traversable wormholes based on the global volume integrated measure of null energy condition.

</details>


### [217] [Conserved currents associated with conformal symmetry in linearized gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39476)
*Gabor Zsolt Toth*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在拉格朗日场论框架下研究线性化引力（linearized gravity）中与洛伦兹变换、伸缩变换和特殊共形变换相关的守恒流，并给出相应的角动量张量、伸缩流与特殊共形张量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作为此前关于闵可夫斯基背景（Minkowski background）下线性化引力能量-动量张量工作的延伸，本文旨在系统考察与其相关的其他守恒流，以完善线性化引力的对称性分析。

Method: 采用拉格朗日场论（Lagrangian field theory）方法，并针对在施加规范固定条件（gauge fixing conditions）等附加条件时的情况，提出诺特定理（Noether's theorem）的推广形式；同时通过参数化族的方式构造守恒流。

Result: 给出了与洛伦兹和伸缩对称性对应的角动量张量与伸缩流，并在广义调和规范（generalized harmonic gauges）下证明特殊共形变换是线性化引力的诺特对称性，得到特殊共形张量；同时确定了多参数族的张量并导出含物质时的局域平衡方程，其成员不依赖于线性化度规高于一阶的导数。

Conclusion: 线性化引力中与洛伦兹、伸缩和特殊共形变换相关的守恒流可在拉格朗日框架下统一构造，并可与一般能量-动量张量族相配套；所导出的局域平衡方程适用于存在物质的情形。

Abstract: Extending a previous work on the energy-momentum tensor of the linearized gravitational field in Minkowski background, conserved currents in linearized gravity associated with Lorentz transformations, dilatations and special conformal transformations are explored in the framework of Lagrangian field theory. Both outstanding special cases and general parametric families of currents are considered. Regarding the former, the distinguished energy-momentum tensor $T_{\mathrm{lg}}^{ab}$ we found earlier, guided by similarities between linearized gravity and electrodynamics, is supplemented with an angular momentum tensor $M_{\mathrm{lg}}^{abc}$ and a dilatation current $D_{\mathrm{lg}}^a$, associated with Lorentz and dilatation symmetries. Concerning special conformal transformations, a generalization of Noether's theorem that is useful if additional conditions (such as gauge fixing conditions) are imposed is formulated, it is shown that special conformal transformations are Noether symmetries of linearized gravity in the generalized harmonic gauges, and a special conformal tensor $C_{\mathrm{lg}}^{ab}$, accompanying $T_{\mathrm{lg}}^{ab}$, is obtained using the generalized Noether's theorem. The angular momentum tensor, dilatation current and special conformal tensor accompanying two other notable energy-momentum tensors are discussed as well. General multi-parameter families of $M^{abc}$, $D^a$ and $C^{ab}$ tensors accompanying a general family of energy-momentum tensors, which was found earlier and includes canonical energy-momentum tensors, are also determined, and local balance equations that are relevant if matter is present are derived for them. Their members do not depend on higher than first derivatives of the linearized metric.

</details>


### [218] [Phenomenology of dynamical trapped regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39543)
*Julio Arrechea,Stefano Liberati,Hooman Neshat,Vania Vellucci*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在预先设定的含时 Hayward 类几何上数值演化线性标量扰动，追踪无地平线致密天体（BHM）与规则黑洞（RBH）之间单次或多次反弹的历史，发现晚时信号（含回声）的振幅与相位依赖于中间束缚区持续时间，且更长的束缚阶段导致更强的能量放大，表明晚时响应可保留内部动力学历史的记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规则黑洞（regular black holes, RBHs）与无地平线黑洞模仿体（horizonless black hole mimickers, BHMs）常被作为经典黑洞的稳态替代方案研究，但其内部可能不稳定或处于弛豫过程，这一演化带来的现象学后果尚未被充分考察。

Method: 在给定的含时 Hayward 类几何上数值演化线性标量扰动，采用可穿透地平的 Painlevé–Gullstrand 坐标（horizon-penetrating Painlevé–Gullstrand coordinates），穿越束缚地平线（trapping horizons）的形成与消失过程，并计算波形与标量场能量。

Result: 对于初末几何相同的历史，改变中间规则黑洞阶段的持续时间会在晚时信号的振幅与相位（包括回声）上产生差异；更长的束缚阶段带来更大的能量放大，与内束缚地平线附近出射模式的蓝移一致。

Conclusion: 动力学弛豫中的 BHM 的晚时响应不必仅由其最终构型决定，它可以保留其内部动力学历史的记忆。

Abstract: Regular black holes (RBHs) and horizonless black hole mimickers (BHMs) are often studied as stationary alternatives to classical black holes, although their interiors may be unstable or undergoing relaxation. We investigate the phenomenological consequences of this evolution by numerically evolving linear scalar perturbations on prescribed, time-dependent Hayward-like geometries. We consider BHM--RBH--BHM histories in which an initially horizonless object temporarily develops a trapped region before returning to a horizonless configuration, through either a single bounce or a sequence of repeated bounces. Using horizon-penetrating Painlevé--Gullstrand coordinates, we follow the perturbations through the formation and disappearance of trapping horizons and compute the resulting waveforms and scalar-field energy. For histories with identical initial and final geometries, we find that changing the duration of the intermediate regular black hole phase produces differences in the amplitude and phase of the late-time signal, including its echoes. Longer trapped phases also yield greater energy amplification, consistent with the blueshift of outgoing modes near the inner trapping horizon. These results show that the late-time response of a dynamically relaxing BHM need not be determined by its final configuration alone: it can retain a memory of the dynamical history of its interior.

</details>


### [219] [MADGRAV: a multilevel anomaly-detection pipeline for gravitational-wave searches applied to LIGO data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39583)
*Gianluca Inguglia,Huw Haigh,Ulyana Dupletsa,Alessandro Longo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: MADGRAV 是一套基于深度学习（deep learning）的高质量致密双星并合（compact binary coalescence）搜寻流程，应用于 LIGO 第三及第四观测运行数据，报告 47 例误报率（false alarm rate）低于 1 yr⁻¹ 的引力波探测，其恢复率随质量升高而提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向高质量致密双星系统，探索独立于匹配滤波（matched filtering）的探测通道。摘要指出，异常检测流程（anomaly detection pipeline）可作为高质量、高信噪比（high-SNR）区间内与匹配滤波互补的独立探测渠道。

Method: MADGRAV 由一系列串行卷积神经网络（convolutional neural networks）组成，依次执行异常检测、毛刺分类（glitch classification）、相干性检验（coherence testing）与信号排序（signal ranking）。使用 Hanford 与 Livingston 两台 LIGO 探测器的 1 秒 Q 变换（Q-transform）窗口数据，分别单独及相干地进行分析。

Result: 通过全部流程阶段的候选体中，48 例达到显著性阈值，报告 47 例误报率低于 1 yr⁻¹、天体物理起源概率 p_astro > 0.9 的引力波探测；其中 44 例与最小模型化的相干 WaveBurst 搜寻共享。由官方引力波瞬变星表提取的源框架质量（source-frame mass）范围为 14–236 M_⊙，中位数 69 M_⊙，信噪比（SNR）中位数为 16。在 LIGO 网络 SNR > 10 条件下，恢复的置信探测比例随质量上升：低于 30 M_⊙ 为 8.1%、30–100 M_⊙ 为 39.8%、高于 100 M_⊙ 为 53.3%；对应相干 WaveBurst 同区间事件的 33.3%、45.5% 与 53.3%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，异常检测流程可作为独立探测通道，在高质量、高信噪比区间与匹配滤波形成互补。

Abstract: We present the results of \textbf{MADGRAV}, a deep-learning-based search for high-mass compact binary coalescences, applied to the data collected by the LIGO interferometers during the third observing run and during the first and second part of the fourth observing run. The \textbf{MADGRAV} pipeline consists of a series of sequential convolutional neural networks that perform anomaly detection, glitch classification, coherence testing, and signal ranking. Data from the Hanford and Livingston LIGO detectors are studied (both individually and in coherence) by way of 1 second Q-transform windows. Of the candidates that survive every stage of the pipeline, 48 reach the significance threshold, and we report 47 gravitational wave detections characterised by a false alarm rate below $1\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$ with a probability of astrophysical origin $p_{\rm astro}>0.9$. Of the 47 detections, 44 are shared with the minimally modelled coherent WaveBurst search. The observed total source-frame masses, extracted from official gravitational wave transient catalogues, are in the $14-236 M_{\odot}$ range with a median of $69 M_{\odot}$, and a median SNR of 16. We note that the recovered fraction of confident detections rises with mass: for LIGO detectors network SNR $>10$ the pipeline recovers $8.1\%$ of confident catalog events below $30 M_{\odot}$, $39.8\%$ between $30$ and $100 M_{\odot}$, and $53.3\%$ above $100 M_{\odot}$, corresponding to $33.3\%$, $45.5\%$ and $53.3\%$ of the events detected by coherent WaveBurst in the same bins. These results suggest that anomaly detection pipelines can serve as an independent detection channel complementary to matched filtering in the high-mass high-SNR regime.

</details>


### [220] [Complete total-transmission modes of Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39609)
*Changkai Chen,Xiaohua Zhang,Zhoujian Cao,Jiliang Jing,Sheng Long*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文构造了Kerr黑洞引力全透射模式（total-transmission modes, TTMs）的完整谱，识别出四个全局连续族（n∞=1,2,3,4），修正并扩展了Cook-Lu的三族分类，并揭示了轴对称扰动下的例外点合并与异常邻近现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前Cook与Lu给出的三族分类可能存在将复共轭与镜像对称混淆的问题，导致出现与对称性无关的额外根。本文旨在建立更统一、无冗余的全局分类框架，并厘清各分支在小自旋极限与角向排序中的行为。

Method: 通过全局连续延拓（global continuation）方法构造Kerr黑洞TTM谱，将固定m下的复共轭与连接m与−m谱的镜像对称加以区分；并利用高达ℓ=32的高精度数值数据进行分析，同时考察轴对称扰动情形。

Result: 得到四个全局连续族：n∞=3族趋于Schwarzschild代数特殊频率，而n∞=1,2,4族沿下半平面方向（−150°、−90°、−30°）以ω∝a^(−4/3)发散。高精度数据重现了Cook-Lu小自旋渐近行为。轴对称扰动下，n∞=2与n∞=3分支在例外点（exceptional points）合并后演化至不同的小自旋极限，而非Cook-Lu提出的泛音多重态分裂；此外发现n∞=3族与非常规Kerr准正规模（quasinormal-mode）序列间存在量级达10^(-8)的异常邻近。

Conclusion: Kerr黑洞TTM谱可由四个全局连续族统一描述，无需引入与对称性无关的额外根。球面极限极向标记与大|aω|角向排序之间的关系呈族依赖性。轴对称情形下的例外点合并机制取代了先前的泛音多重态分裂图景，而n∞=3族与非常规准正规模序列的异常邻近为进一步研究提供了新线索。

Abstract: We construct the complete spectrum of gravitational total-transmission modes (TTMs) of Kerr black holes and find four globally continuous families, $n_\infty=1,2,3,4$. Compared with the three-family classification of Cook and Lu [Phys. Rev. D 107, 044043 (2023)], our global continuation separates complex conjugation at fixed $m$ from the mirror symmetry connecting the $m$ and $-m$ spectra, yielding a uniform four-family classification without additional symmetry-unrelated roots. The $n_\infty=3$ family approaches the Schwarzschild algebraically special frequency, whereas the $n_\infty=1,2,$ and $4$ families diverge as $ω\propto a^{-4/3}$ along lower-half-plane directions $-150^\circ$, $-90^\circ$, and $-30^\circ$. High-precision data up to $\ell=32$ recover the Cook--Lu small-spin asymptotics and show how the divergent branches are embedded in the global four-family spectrum. The spherical-limit polar labeling and large-$|aω|$ angular ordering are related in a family-dependent manner. For axisymmetric perturbations, we show that the $n_\infty=2$ and $n_\infty=3$ branches coalesce at exceptional points and subsequently evolve toward distinct small-spin limits, rather than exhibiting the overtone-multiplet splitting proposed by Cook and Lu. Finally, we identify a previously unreported anomalous proximity between the $n_\infty=3$ TTM family and the unconventional Kerr quasinormal-mode sequence, with separations reaching order $10^{-8}$ while remaining numerically well resolved.

</details>


### [221] [Neutrino Oscillations without Mass: A Re-analysis of KamLAND Data following the Dirac Equation in Curved Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39638)
*Golam Mortuza Hossain,Pushpit Kumar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在静态弯曲时空（stationary curved spacetime）中，狄拉克方程（Dirac equation）要求将双味中微子振荡（two-flavor neutrino oscillations）中各传播态的不变质量（invariant mass）与守恒能量（conserved energy）区别对待，因为引力对能量与质量的影响不同。通过重新分析公开的KamLAND数据集，将无质量中微子的味态建模为具有能量依赖能级劈裂的两能级量子态（类似于修正的Jaynes-Cummings模型），其拟合观测数据的似然高于标准的有质量中微子范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准中微子振荡范式基于有质量中微子（massive neutrinos），但引力对能量与质量的作用并不相同，这一差异在弯曲时空描述中未被充分体现。该研究旨在检验是否存在替代建模方式，能更好地解释已公开的中微子观测数据。

Method: 从静态弯曲时空中的狄拉克方程出发，将双味中微子振荡描述为需分别处理每个传播态的不变质量与守恒能量。随后重新分析公开的KamLAND数据集，把无质量中微子（massless neutrinos）的味态建模为两能级量子态（two-level quantum states），其能级劈裂（level splitting）依赖于能量，结构类比于修正的Jaynes-Cummings模型。

Result: 该无质量中微子两能级模型的拟合似然（likelihood）高于标准的有质量中微子范式，即在重新分析KamLAND数据时对观测数据给出更优拟合。

Conclusion: 摘要提示，基于弯曲时空狄拉克方程并引入能量依赖能级劈裂的建模，可能为解释中微子振荡数据提供相较标准有质量中微子范式更优的描述，但其结论仅建立在KamLAND公开数据的重新分析之上。

Abstract: The Dirac equation in stationary curved spacetime implies that describing two-flavor neutrino oscillations requires treating the invariant mass and conserved energy of each propagating state distinctly, as gravity affects energy differently than mass. By re-analysing the publicly released KamLAND dataset, we show that modeling flavor states of massless neutrinos as two-level quantum states with energy-dependent level splitting, analogous to modified Jaynes-Cummings models, results in a higher-likelihood fit to the observed data than the standard massive-neutrino paradigm.

</details>


### [222] [Spherical thin shells with charge in unimodular gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39664)
*Gabriel R. Bengochea,Ernesto F. Eiroa,Griselda Figueroa-Aguirre*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在单模引力（unimodular gravity, UG）框架下构建球对称薄壳（thin shell）模型，并分析其在径向、保持对称性扰动下的动力学稳定性，应用于带电真空泡（charged vacuum bubble）的构造与引力理论对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将薄壳动力学稳定性研究从广义相对论（general relativity, GR）推广至单模引力（UG），以考察 UG 非守恒参数对稳定性区域与物质成分的影响。

Method: 采用 UG 的连接条件（junction conditions），并选取由径向电场供能的非守恒带电外部解（non-conservative charged exterior solutions）。在此基础上构造带电真空泡：内部为闵可夫斯基（Minkowski）时空，外部为带电薄壳并与 UG 外部几何匹配。

Result: 所得时空无事件视界（event horizon）与中心奇点（central singularity）；在类德西特（de Sitter-like）情形下，壳外可能存在宇宙学视界（cosmological horizon）。与广义相对论对应结果比较，稳定性区域与物质成分存在差异，且该差异受 UG 非守恒参数控制。

Conclusion: 该工作将既有结果进一步推广，表明单模引力中的薄壳稳定性与物质组成可因非守恒参数而区别于广义相对论。

Abstract: We construct spherically symmetric thin shells in unimodular gravity (UG) and analyze their dynamical stability under radial, symmetry-preserving perturbations. We use the UG junction conditions and adopt non-conservative charged exterior solutions sourced by a radial electric field. As an application, we build charged vacuum bubbles: a Minkowski interior surrounded by a charged thin shell and matched to an exterior UG geometry. The resulting spacetimes are free of event horizons and central singularities, while in de Sitter-like cases a cosmological horizon may exist outside the shell. We compare the stability regions and the matter content with their counterparts in general relativity, finding some differences controlled by the UG non-conservation parameter. This work extends previously published results.

</details>


### [223] [Poisson spot and wave scattering of scalar fields by conformal anomaly black holes: probing the near-horizon geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39672)
*Zeming Zhuang,Hongsheng Zhang,Zong-Kuan Guo,Rong-Gen Cai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量平面波（scalar plane wave）被静态共形反常黑洞（static conformal anomaly black hole）散射与衍射的过程，通过部分波级数（partial-wave series, PWS）方法计算全波形，揭示泊松亮斑（Poisson spot）与同心衍射环结构，并讨论吸收截面（absorption cross section）与荣耀散射（glory scattering）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形反常（conformal anomaly）对黑洞时空几何的修正在近视界区域可能产生可观测的散射特征差异，因此有必要系统分析该类黑洞的波动散射行为，并与已知黑洞（如 RN 黑洞）进行对比以识别反常修正的效应。

Method: 采用部分波级数（PWS）方法，确定有限距离下的截断要求以计算完整波形；通过分析势垒（potential barrier）研究吸收截面，并使用级数约化方法（series reduction method）加速微分散射截面（differential scattering cross section）的 PWS 收敛。

Result: 全波形呈现清晰衍射图样：负 z 轴上场退化为入射平面波，正 z 轴上畸变平面波与散射球面波共存，θ=0 处出现明亮泊松亮斑并被同心衍射环环绕；轴上强度对近视界几何最为敏感，近视界处与 RN 黑洞显著不同而远场趋于一致。低频与高频吸收截面分别与势垒的 ℓ=0 部分及依赖 ℓ 的部分强相关；微分截面与荣耀散射主要来自排除外视界小邻域的有限区域；电荷与共形反常参数 α̃ 对荣耀峰宽有相反效应，对峰高则有相同效应。

Conclusion: 共形反常修正以 O(α̃M²/r⁴) 衰减，其可观测影响集中于近视界区域，并主要体现在相位增量与干涉条纹的压缩和位移上；电荷与反常参数在荣耀散射特征上表现出可区分的组合效应。

Abstract: We investigate the scattering and diffraction of scalar plane waves by static conformal anomaly black holes. We determine the truncation requirements for the partial-wave series (PWS) method at finite distances and compute the full waveforms, which display a clear diffraction pattern: on the negative $z$-axis the field reduces to the incident plane wave, while on the positive $z$-axis a distorted plane wave coexists with a scattered spherical wave, and a bright Poisson spot appears at $θ=0$ surrounded by concentric diffraction rings. We obtain the on-axis intensity of the Poisson spot as a function of the radial coordinate. The intensity is most sensitive to the near-horizon geometry: close to the horizon it differs markedly from that of the RN black hole, whereas in the far field the two curves converge. We trace this to the metric functions, where different values of $f'(r_+)$ produce different phase increments that compress and shift the interference fringes, while the conformal anomaly correction falls off as $O(\tildeαM^2/r^4)$. By analyzing the potential barrier we study the absorption cross section, and use the series reduction method to accelerate the PWS convergence for the differential scattering cross section. The low- and high-frequency absorption cross sections are strongly correlated with the $\ell=0$ and $\ell$-dependent parts of the potential barrier, respectively. The differential cross section and glory scattering arise mainly from scattering in a finite region excluding a small neighborhood of the outer horizon. Charge and $\tildeα$ have opposite effects on the width of the glory peak but the same effect on its height.

</details>


### [224] [Gravitational Memory: an approximate description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39694)
*Q. -L. Zhao,P. -M. Zhang,M. Elbistan,J. Balog,P. A. Horvathy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文提出用连续但不一定光滑的轮廓（profile）近似三明治引力波（sandwich gravitational wave）的大尺度行为，从而简化粒子运动描述，并指出该近似模型与 Carroll 对称性（Carroll symmetry）相容，以 Poschl-Teller 轮廓为例进行说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三明治引力波在远距离（large-distance）尺度上的行为较为复杂，需要一种简化描述以方便研究粒子运动。为此，作者寻求一种既连续又不必光滑的近似轮廓，同时保持与相关对称性的一致性。

Method: 将三明治引力波的大尺度行为用连续但不要求光滑的轮廓进行近似，从而给出粒子运动的简化描述；该方法强调近似模型与 Carroll 对称性（Carroll symmetry）相容，并以 Poschl-Teller 轮廓作为具体示例加以说明。

Result: 近似模型给出了粒子运动的一种简化描述，并与 Carroll 对称性保持一致；通过 Poschl-Teller 轮廓的示例展示了该策略的可行性。

Conclusion: 用连续而不必光滑的轮廓近似三明治引力波的大尺度行为是一种可行策略，可在保持 Carroll 对称性的同时简化粒子运动分析，Poschl-Teller 轮廓可作为该策略的示范案例。

Abstract: The large-distance behaviour of a sandwich gravitational wave can be approximated by a continuous but not necessarily smooth profile, providing us with a simplified description of particle motion. Our approximate model is consistent with the Carroll symmetry. Our strategy is illustrated by the Poschl-Teller profile.

</details>


### [225] [Nontrivial Symmetries in $k$-essence Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39729)
*Andrés Lueiza-Colipí,Nikolaos Dimakis,Andronikos Paliathanasis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在空间平坦的 FLRW 背景下，对拉格朗日密度为 f1(φ)R+f2(φ,X) 的 k-essence 模型开展对称性分类，通过拉格朗日乘子引入动力学项 X 并将 lapse 函数视为动力学变量，在约束系统上于规范固定前进行对称性分析，并利用 Noether 守恒律给出精确宇宙学解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在揭示因在作用量层面固定 lapse 函数而丢失的对称性，并系统分类具有非平凡对称性的 k-essence 模型族，从而为构造可积的宇宙学模型提供依据。

Method: 在空间平坦 FLRW 背景上，对形如 f1(φ)R+f2(φ,X) 的拉格朗日密度进行对称性分类；以拉格朗日乘子（Lagrange multiplier）将动力学项 X 作为独立自由度引入，并将 lapse 函数作为动力学变量处理；对称性分析施加于规范固定（gauge fixing）之前的约束系统，随后借助相应的 Noether 守恒律（Noetherian conservation laws）求解精确宇宙学解。

Result: 所得具有非平凡对称性的 k-essence 模型族归为两大类：与引力最小耦合（minimally coupled）和非最小耦合（nonminimally coupled）的理论。当守恒荷（conserved charges）的数值为零时，场方程约化为一个代数关系，进而得到标度因子（scale factor）的幂律表达式，其指数由特定的 k-essence 函数决定。

Conclusion: 在规范固定前对约束系统进行对称性分析可保留在固定 lapse 时丧失的对称性，并据此系统分类出最小耦合与非最小耦合两类 k-essence 模型，同时通过 Noether 守恒律获得精确宇宙学解与幂律标度因子。

Abstract: In the context of a spatially flat FLRW background, we perform a symmetry classification of $k$-essence models with Lagrangian densities of the form $f_1(φ)R+f_2(φ,X)$. The kinetic term $X$ is introduced as an independent degree of freedom via a Lagrange multiplier, and the lapse function is treated as a dynamical variable. The symmetry analysis is applied to the constrained system prior to any gauge fixing. This approach reveals symmetries which otherwise are lost when the lapse is fixed at the level of the action. The derived families of $k$-essence models admitting nontrivial symmetries fall to two general classes: minimally coupled and nonminimally coupled theories to gravity. We use the corresponding Noetherian conservation laws to derive exact cosmological solutions. We find that, when the numerical value of the conserved charges is zero, the field equations reduce to an algebraic relation. We subsequently derive power-law expressions for the scale factor with exponents determined by the particular $k$-essence function.

</details>


### [226] [Testing charged bumblebee black holes through high-frequency quasi-periodic oscillations in X-ray binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39864)
*Qi-Qi Liang,De-Jiang Yin,Zi-Qiang Cai,Zheng-Wen Long*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究具有自发洛伦兹破缺（spontaneous Lorentz violation）的静态球对称带电 bumblebee 黑洞的强场轨道动力学，并借助高频准周期振荡（HFQPOs）的相对论进动模型（relativistic precession model）对三类黑洞 X 射线双星进行贝叶斯参数推断，发现洛伦兹破缺与电荷效应存在内在参数简并，观测数据未给出统计显著的净洛伦兹破缺修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洛伦兹破缺（Lorentz violation）与黑洞电荷效应在强场轨道运动中的可观测印记尚不明确，且二者对频率类观测量的贡献可能高度混合。本文旨在检验能否利用 HFQPO 观测区分洛伦兹破缺修正与电荷相关效应，并对其基本参数给出约束。

Method: 求解零测地线与类时测地线的圆轨道，得到光子球半径（photon-sphere radius）、最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）半径及特征轨道频率。在 HFQPOs 的相对论进动模型下，双峰频率由基本参数 M、X=r/M、l_1、l_2、Q_0/M 决定；由于 l_1、l_2、Q_0/M 仅以组合形式进入频率表达式而存在内在简并，故引入有效参数集 Θ=(M,X,C,β)，并利用 GRO J1655--40、XTE J1550--564、GRS 1915+105 三个双星的 HFQPO 观测数据进行贝叶斯 MCMC 参数推断。

Result: 复合参数 C 的 68% 可信区间（credible interval）均包含 Reissner--Nordström 极限 C=1，表明在本文模型假设下不存在统计显著的净洛伦兹破缺修正。不同参数三元组 (l_1,l_2,Q_0/M) 可给出完全相同的 QPO 预测，因此仅凭 HFQPO 观测无法区分洛伦兹破缺效应与电荷相关效应。

Conclusion: HFQPO 数据本身不足以打破洛伦兹破缺参数与电荷参数之间的简并（degeneracy）；对这些基本量的精确约束需要额外且更精确的观测数据。

Abstract: We investigate strong-field orbital dynamics for static spherically symmetric charged bumblebee black holes featuring spontaneous Lorentz-violating effects. Analysis of null and timelike circular geodesics yields the photon-sphere radius, the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) radius, and characteristic orbital frequencies for test particles. Within the relativistic precession model for high-frequency quasi-periodic oscillations (HFQPOs), observed twin-peak frequencies are governed by the full set of fundamental parameters $M$, $X=r/M$, $l_1$, $l_2$, and $Q_0/M$. Among them, $l_1$, $l_2$, and $Q_0/M$ enter frequency expressions only in combined forms, giving rise to intrinsic parameter degeneracy. An effective-parameter set $Θ=(M,X,C,β)$ is accordingly introduced to characterize these composite contributions. Bayesian MCMC parameter inference is carried out using HFQPO observational data from three black-hole X-ray binaries: GRO J1655--40, XTE J1550--564, and GRS 1915+105. The 68\% credible intervals of composite parameter $C$ all contain the Reissner--Nordström limit $C=1$, revealing no statistically significant net Lorentz-violating correction under our model assumptions. Distinct triples $(l_1,l_2,Q_0/M)$ yield identical QPO predictions, so HFQPO observations alone cannot disentangle Lorentz-violating and charge-related effects; precise constraints on these fundamental quantities require additional, more precise observational data.

</details>


### [227] [Zero modes and oscillatory instabilities of a Lorentz-violating Kalb-Ramond field on a Schwarzschild background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39936)
*Hong-Da Lyu,Zhi Xiao,Zhong-Xi Yu,Shoulong Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究 Schwarzschild 背景上带四次对称破缺势与非最小 Riemann 耦合的 Lorentz 破缺 Kalb–Ramond 场的平衡构型与线性扰动，发现单极电响应增强源于代数约束而非动力学不稳定性，并给出高阶多极的零频模式谱及 ℓ=1 时由纯虚模合并产生的振荡不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究 Lorentz 破缺（Lorentz-violating）Kalb–Ramond 场在弯曲时空中的平衡结构与扰动稳定性，特别是非最小 Riemann 耦合与对称破缺势共同作用下可能出现的退化组态与不稳定模式。

Method: 在固定 Schwarzschild 背景上处理含四次对称破缺势与非最小 Riemann 耦合的 Kalb–Ramond 场：求静态球对称构型，分析由特征函数有限半径双根所标记的退化组态；计算单极与高阶多极（multipole）的电响应及零频与有限频扰动谱。

Result: 单极电响应在趋近退化组态时按 |ê(ω,r_c)| ∝ (γ_c−γ)^{−1/2} 增强，而传播的单极振幅保持正则；高阶多极存在精确的零频模式塔 ξ_{ℓn} = −(ℓ+n+1)(ℓ+n+2)/3；ℓ=1 的有限频谱含两支低频分支，随 Riemann 耦合更负，纯虚不稳定模式合并并离虚轴为 ω_± = ±ω_R + iω_I，近合并处实部劈裂遵循平方根律。

Conclusion: 所观察到的单极增强由代数约束（algebraic constraint）驱动，而非动力学不稳定性；Riemann 耦合的增强可驱动 ℓ=1 模式的振荡不稳定性（oscillatory instability），其模式合并行为具有平方根型标度特征。

Abstract: We study equilibrium configurations and linear perturbations of a Lorentz-violating Kalb--Ramond field with a quartic symmetry-breaking potential and a nonminimal Riemann coupling on a fixed Schwarzschild background. For static spherical configurations, the electric component is determined algebraically by a characteristic function that can develop a finite-radius double root. Approaching this degenerate configuration, the monopole electric response scales as $|\widehat e(ω,r_c)|\propto(γ_c-γ)^{-1/2}$, while the propagating monopole amplitude remains regular, showing that the enhancement originates from the algebraic constraint rather than from a dynamical instability. For higher multipoles, we obtain an exact tower of zero-frequency modes, $ξ_{\ell n}=-(\ell+n+1)(\ell+n+2)/3$. For $\ell=1$, the finite-frequency spectrum contains two distinct low-frequency branches. As the Riemann coupling becomes more negative, the corresponding purely imaginary unstable modes coalesce and leave the imaginary axis as $ω_\pm=\pmω_R+iω_I$, producing an oscillatory instability. Near the merger, the real-part splitting follows a square-root law.

</details>


### [228] [On the topology of the space of vacuum initial data sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39987)
*Romain Gicquaud,Jonathan Glöckle*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文证明闭流形上真空初始数据集空间（space of vacuum initial data sets）通常具有许多非平凡同伦群（homotopy groups），其方法基于共形方法（conformal method）的参数化版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有结果构造出满足严格主导能量条件（strict dominant energy condition）的初始数据集空间同伦群中的非平凡元素，并进一步显示这些元素在放弃严格性假设后依然保留；本文旨在将这些元素提升到真空初始数据集空间，从而研究该空间的整体拓扑（global topology）。

Method: 采用共形方法（conformal method）的参数化版本（parametrized version），并依赖两项具独立意义的结果：可将容许共形 Killing 向量（conformal Killing vectors）的度量从度量空间中移除而不改变其弱同伦型（weak homotopy type）；在剩余度量上可族化地实施 York 分解（York decomposition），其中 TT-张量（TT-tensors）构成平凡 Hilbert 丛（trivial Hilbert bundle）。

Result: 上述非平凡同伦元素可由映入真空初始数据集空间的映射表示，即该空间具有非平凡同伦群；移除共形 Killing 向量度量不改变弱同伦型，且 TT-张量族构成平凡 Hilbert 丛。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是关于真空初始数据集空间整体拓扑的首个结果，表明该空间的同伦结构比预期更为丰富。

Abstract: We show that the space of vacuum initial data sets on a closed manifold often has many non-trivial homotopy groups. The starting point is a result of the second named author, which constructs non-trivial elements in the homotopy groups of the space of initial data sets satisfying the strict dominant energy condition, together with a later result in joint work with Bernd Ammann showing that these elements often persist when the strictness assumption is dropped. In this work, we use a parametrized version of the conformal method to show that these elements may also be represented by maps into the space of vacuum initial data sets. This requires two results that may be of independent interest: metrics admitting conformal Killing vectors can be removed from the space of metrics without changing its weak homotopy type, and over the remaining metrics the York decomposition can be carried out in families, the TT-tensors forming a trivial Hilbert bundle. To our knowledge, this is the first result on the global topology of this space.

</details>


### [229] [Projective Symmetry and Its Breaking in Quadratic Metric-Affine Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39988)
*Carmen Ferrara,María José Guzmán,Laur Järv*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究四维宇称偶（parity-even）度规仿射引力（metric-affine gravity）中广义射影对称性（generalized projective symmetry）及其破缺，建立对称性适配的场方程求解方法，并将其与物质耦合及规范模式识别相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度规仿射引力中仿射几何（affine geometry）的自由度众多，需寻找统一原理以约束引力系数与物质耦合；广义射影对称性被视为可识别仿射规范模式（affine gauge modes）并限制物理解的候选原则。

Method: 取曲率线性、挠率（torsion）与非度规性（nonmetricity）至多二次的作用量；推导广义射影变换对仿射几何不可约分量（irreducible components）的作用，确定轴向、度规迹与完全射影不变理论的耦合关系；针对连接算符奇异（connection operator singular）问题发展对称性适配的真空场方程求解方法，并分析其与Dirac、电磁及复Klein-Gordon场的耦合。

Result: 完全不变性将12个引力系数约化为五参数族，并产生四个矢量Noether恒等式；局部精确轴向射影变换对应无质量费米子的手征转动（chiral rotations），covector组合充当Abelian连接并与标量U(1)对称性关联；标准Maxwell理论不依赖仿射连接，而依赖挠率的Maxwell型扩展可借补偿Stückelberg场（Stückelberg fields）同时保持电磁与射影不变性；精确射影不变性使相应连接方向非动力学并迫使物质流为零，受控显式破缺将这些零模提升为辅助场（auxiliary fields）。

Conclusion: 射影对称性为识别仿射规范模式、约束物质耦合以及将引力部门对称性破缺与有效物质相互作用相关联提供了统一原则。

Abstract: We investigate generalized projective symmetry and its breaking in four-dimensional parity-even metric-affine gravity, considering an action linear in curvature and at most quadratic in torsion and nonmetricity. We derive the action of the generalized projective transformation on the irreducible components of the affine geometry and determine the coupling relations defining the axial, metric-trace, and fully projectively invariant theories. Complete invariance reduces the $12$ gravitational coefficients to a five-parameter family and generates four vectorial Noether identities for the connection equations. Projective invariance makes the connection operator singular, thus we develop a symmetry-adapted method for solving its vacuum field equations. We then analyze couplings to Dirac, electromagnetic, and complex Klein-Gordon fields. Locally exact axial-projective transformations correspond to chiral rotations for massless fermions at the classical level, while a combination of covectors acts as an Abelian connection linking a locally exact vector-projective representative to scalar $U(1)$ symmetry. Standard Maxwell theory is independent of the affine connection, whereas a torsion-dependent Maxwell-like extension can preserve both electromagnetic and projective invariance through compensating Stückelberg fields. Exact gravitational projective invariance makes the corresponding connection directions nondynamical and forces matter currents sourcing them to vanish. Controlled explicit breaking lifts these zero modes and converts them into auxiliary fields. Thus, projective symmetry provides a unified principle for identifying affine gauge modes, constraining matter couplings, and relating symmetry breaking in the gravitational sector to effective matter interactions.

</details>


### [230] [Axion corrections to photon superradiant scattering by Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40166)
*Zhi-Qing Zhu,Jun Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究在轴子-光子耦合（axion-photon coupling）存在时，克尔黑洞（Kerr black hole）对光子的超辐射散射（superradiant scattering），并考察该耦合对电磁放大因子（electromagnetic amplification factor）的修正。作者在完全相对论框架下求解耦合的轴子与电磁方程，按被激发的轴子频率与轴子质量的关系区分两种区域，发现轴子传播与束缚行为会分别导致光子放大被抑制或增强/抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清轴子-光子耦合如何改变黑洞光子超辐射的放大特性。摘要未进一步说明具体应用背景或观测动机。

Method: 在完全相对论框架（fully relativistic framework）下求解耦合的轴子与电磁方程（coupled axion and electromagnetic equations）。依据被激发的轴子频率（sourced axion frequency）与轴子质量（axion mass）之间的关系划分两种区域进行分析。

Result: 在辐射区域（radiative regime），轴子可传播至空间无穷远（spatial infinity）并带走能量，从而抑制光子放大。在亚阈值区域（subthreshold regime），轴子被质量势垒（mass barrier）束缚，并通过准束缚态激发（quasibound-state excitations）与黑洞视界（black-hole horizon）交换能量；此时光子放大可被增强或抑制，取决于相关轴子模式是否满足超辐射条件（superradiance condition）。

Conclusion: 轴子-光子耦合对克尔黑洞光子超辐射放大的影响依赖于轴子频率与轴子质量的相对关系，并呈现两类截然不同的行为：辐射区域表现为抑制，亚阈值区域则依超辐射条件满足与否而增强或抑制。

Abstract: We study photon superradiant scattering by a Kerr black hole in the presence of an axion-photon coupling and investigate how this coupling modifies the electromagnetic amplification factor. We solve the coupled axion and electromagnetic equations in a fully relativistic framework. We distinguish two regimes according to the relation between the sourced axion frequency and the axion mass. In the radiative regime, the axion can propagate to spatial infinity and carry away energy, thereby suppressing photon amplification. In the subthreshold regime, the axion is confined by the mass barrier and exchanges energy with the black-hole horizon through quasibound-state excitations. In this regime, photon amplification can be either enhanced or suppressed, depending on whether the relevant axion modes satisfy the superradiance condition.

</details>


### [231] [Flat limit of artificial cosmology for scalar waves in Schwarzschild-de Sitter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40243)
*Anıl Zenginoğlu,Govind Arun Kumar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在 Schwarzschild–de Sitter 背景上以标量波为对象，定量研究宇宙学波形对渐近平坦波形的近似程度，并借助正则平坦极限与 Misner 人工宇宙学（artificial cosmology）方法考察其适用性与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机来自观测到的极小宇宙学常数（cosmological constant），以及 Misner 提出通过共形紧化（conformal compactification）调控 Einstein 方程中形式奇异项的思想。核心问题是：在宇宙学常数很小时，宇宙学波形如何近似孤立系统的波形。

Method: 采用 Schwarzschild–de Sitter 背景上的标量波（scalar waves）进行定量分析；要求合适的平坦极限需使叶状结构（foliation）在未来共形边界处保持正则。比较从黑洞视界延伸至宇宙学视界的桥式叶状结构（bridge foliations）与具有正则渐近平坦极限的情形，并在几何匹配的数据上比较两类波形。此外扩展 Misner 人工宇宙学，引入不改变近区几何的宇宙学源。

Result: 在几何匹配数据上的比较显示，平坦极限在数值上表现良好，并再现了渐近平坦波形的基本特征。引入保持近区几何不变的宇宙学源后，ringdown 波形的一致性得到改善，但未能准确刻画中间的多项式尾（polynomial tail）行为。

Conclusion: 小宇宙学常数下宇宙学波形可在数值上良好逼近渐近平坦波形的主要特征；所提出的人工宇宙学扩展改善了 ringdown 一致性，但在中间多项式尾阶段的描述仍不充分，表明该近似方法存在明确适用范围与局限。

Abstract: How do cosmological waveforms approximate waveforms from isolated systems for a small cosmological constant? This question is motivated by the observed small value of the cosmological constant, and also by Misner's idea to regulate the formally singular terms introduced into the Einstein equations by conformal compactification. We investigate this question quantitatively using scalar waves on Schwarzschild--de Sitter backgrounds. A suitable flat limit requires the foliation to remain regular at the future conformal boundary. We compare bridge foliations extending from the black hole horizon to the cosmological horizon with a regular asymptotically flat limit. Comparisons of cosmological and asymptotically flat waveforms on geometrically matched data show that the flat limit is numerically well behaved and recovers the essential features of the asymptotically flat waveform. We extend Misner's artificial cosmology by introducing a cosmological source that leaves the near-zone geometry unchanged and show that it improves agreement of the ringdown waveform but fails to capture the intermediate polynomial tail behavior accurately.

</details>


### [232] [Slowly rotating Black Holes in DHOST Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.17614)
*Hugo Candan,Karim Noui,David Langlois*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在退化高阶标量张量（Degenerate Higher Order Scalar Tensor, DHOST）理论框架下研究慢转黑洞解，证明控制参考系拖曳函数（frame-dragging function）ω 的微分方程对任意 DHOST 理论均可积，并给出其显式形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DHOST 理论作为可规避 Ostrogradski 不稳定性的高阶标量张量理论，其旋转黑洞解尚缺乏系统研究。本文旨在将静态球对称解推广至慢转情形，检验旋转对可观测量的影响。

Method: 以某 DHOST 理论的静态球对称度规解为起点，采用 Hartle-Thorne 拟设（Hartle-Thorne ansatz）描述慢转时空；先假设 ω 仅依赖径向坐标并求解其控制微分方程，再考察 ω 的角向依赖；最后以近期获得的带原初毛发（primary hair）黑洞解为例，计算赤道面上最内稳定圆轨道（Innermost Stable Circular Orbit, ISCO）与圆光轨迹。

Result: 控制 ω 的微分方程对任意允许该解的 DHOST 理论均可积，可获得 ω 的显式形式；引入角向依赖后，视界（horizon）与无穷远处的正则性要求排除该依赖，结论与广义相对论（General Relativity）一致。在带原初毛发的黑洞解中，进一步给出了旋转对 ISCO 及赤道面圆光轨迹的影响。

Conclusion: 慢转 DHOST 黑洞的参考系拖曳结构具有与广义相对论类似的性质：ω 可由可积方程显式求得，且角向依赖因正则性要求被禁止。该形式体系可用于分析具体 DHOST 黑洞解的旋转修正，如原初毛发情形下 ISCO 与光轨迹的变化。

Abstract: We study slowly rotating black hole solutions within Degenerate Higher Order Scalar Tensor (DHOST) theories. Starting from a static, spherically symmetric metric solution of a DHOST theory, we employ the Hartle-Thorne ansatz to model a slowly rotating spacetime. We show that the differential equation governing the frame-dragging function $ω$ (which is supposed to depend on the radial coordinate only) is integrable for any DHOST theory allowing us to obtain its explicit form. We also consider angular dependence in $ω$ and show that regularity at the horizon and at infinity forbids it, as in General Relativity. As an illustration of the formalism introduced here, we study the slowly-rotating version of black hole solutions with primary hair obtained recently, examining the influence of the rotation on the Innermost Stable Circular Orbit (ISCO) and on the circular light trajectories in the equatorial plane.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Derivation of the Enskog--Enskog System from a Particle System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38475)
*Zhe Chen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 从硬球（hard-sphere）BBGKY 层级严格推导出气-颗粒混合物（gas–particle mixture）的耦合 Enskog 系统，保留碰撞中心间有限间距，得到相应守恒律与熵不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两相流（two-phase flows）中体积效应（volume effect）不可忽略的场景缺乏合适的动理学（kinetic）出发点，而经典 Enskog 型处理未充分保留碰撞中心间的有限间距。

Method: 基于硬球 BBGKY 层级进行形式化推导，保留碰撞中心间的有限分离，纳入同种与异种间的碰撞，并显式给出空间位移方向与四个碰撞算子（collision operators）的依赖于质量的前因子（mass-dependent prefactors）。随后在单位接触因子（unit contact factors）情形下推导对应的气相与颗粒相守恒律（balance laws）。

Result: 得到耦合 Enskog 系统，为体积效应不可忽略的两相流提供动理学起点；并导出气相与颗粒相的守恒律，在适当正则性假设（regularity assumptions）下建立熵不等式（entropy inequality）。

Conclusion: 该耦合 Enskog 系统可作为含体积效应两相流的动理学基础，其守恒律与熵不等式为后续分析与建模提供支撑。

Abstract: We formally derive a coupled Enskog system for a gas--particle mixture from the hard-sphere BBGKY hierarchy. The derivation retains the finite separation between colliding centres, includes collisions within and between species and explicitly states the direction of spatial shifts and the mass-dependent prefactors of the four collision operators. The resulting system provides a kinetic starting point for two-phase flows for which the volume effect cannot be ignored. For unit contact factors, we derive the corresponding gas and particle balance laws, and establish an entropy inequality under suitable regularity assumptions.

</details>


### [234] [Global Energy Solutions for Normalized Linear Stochastic Schr{ö}dinger Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39244)
*Théo Hérouard*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究马尔可夫开放量子系统（Markovian open quantum systems）中出现的非线性薛定谔方程（nonlinear Schrödinger equations），该方程由线性随机薛定谔方程归一化导出。作者在较弱正则性的能量空间（energy space）证明线性方程整体适定性，并在放宽单调性（monotonicity）假设下证明非线性随机偏微分方程（SPDE）解的不可区分性，进而证明所需非线性随机方程的适定性（well-posedness）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机源于马尔可夫开放量子系统中出现的非线性薛定谔方程，这类方程由线性随机薛定谔方程的归一化得到。作者希望处理相比通常空间正则性更低的能量空间中的适定性问题，并放宽常用的单调性假设，以扩展相关方程的可处理范围。

Method: 方法分三步：首先在能量空间（正则性低于该方程通常所在空间）证明线性方程的整体适定性（global well-posedness）；其次在放宽通常单调性（monotonicity）假设的条件下，证明非线性 SPDE 解的不可区分性（indistinguishability）；最后通过证明路径唯一性（pathwise uniqueness）并对线性方程解进行归一化，证明所需非线性随机方程类的适定性。

Result: 作者成功在能量空间中建立了线性方程的整体适定性，并在较弱单调性条件下得到了非线性 SPDE 解的不可区分性。通过路径唯一性证明与线性方程解的归一化，进一步获得了目标非线性随机方程的适定性结果。

Conclusion: 研究表明，即使在正则性更低的能量空间以及放宽单调性假设的情形下，源于马尔可夫开放量子系统的非线性薛定谔型随机方程仍具有适定性。该结论将相关适定性结果推广至更一般、限制更少的条件。

Abstract: In this paper, we study nonlinear Schr{ö}dinger equations arising in the study of Markovian open quantum systems. These equations are derived from the normalization of linear stochastic Schr{ö}dinger equations. First, we prove the global well-posedness of the linear equation in the energy space, which is less regular than the usual space for this equation. Then, we show the indistinguishability of solutions to the nonlinear SPDE under a relaxation of the usual monotonicity assumption.
