Finally, by proving the pathwise uniqueness and normalizing the solutions to the linear equation, we are able to prove the well-posedness of solutions to the required class of nonlinear stochastic equations.

</details>


### [235] [Hydrodynamic Limit of a Viscous BGK Model of the Boltzmann Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39283)
*Gabriella Puppo,Thomas Rey,Tommaso Tenna*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究 Boltzmann 方程（Boltzmann equation）BGK 模型的粘性正则化（viscous regularization）及其流体动力学极限（hydrodynamic limit）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注 BGK 模型粘性正则化后的流体动力学极限问题，旨在建立向粘性 Euler 方程（viscous Euler equations）的严格收敛，并厘清其与熵不等式及对称双曲热力学相容理论之间的关系。

Method: 利用源自空间 Fisher 信息（spatial Fisher information）的界，建立向粘性 Euler 方程的严格收敛；并对单组分与多组分情形进行形式推导分析，结合熵不等式（entropy inequality），与对称双曲热力学相容理论（symmetric hyperbolic thermodynamically compatible theory）进行比较。

Result: 获得了向粘性 Euler 方程的严格收敛性结果；在单组分与多组分情形下给出带熵不等式的 Euler 方程形式推导，并与对称双曲热力学相容理论进行了对照。

Conclusion: 空间 Fisher 信息界为粘性正则化 BGK 模型的流体动力学极限提供了有效分析工具，所得结果可与对称双曲热力学相容理论框架相互比较。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate a viscous regularization for the BGK model of the Boltzmann equation and its hydrodynamic limit. First, we establish rigorous convergence toward the viscous Euler equations, by exploiting bounds derived from spatial Fisher information. We then analyze the formal derivation of the Euler equations with an entropy inequality for both single-and multi-species case, comparing our results against symmetric hyperbolic thermodynamically compatible theory.

</details>


### [236] [Tomography of reactive Boltzmann kernels from product fluxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39770)
*Yi-Hsuan Lin,Hongyu Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究稀薄气体中可逆反应 1+2⇌3+4 的 Boltzmann 系统，证明在给定容许的相对速度与出射方向集合上，产物之一的出射通量可确定前向碰撞核（forward collision kernel），并给出 Fourier 重建方法及有限束速、包宽、探测器分辨率与噪声的误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在已知弹性碰撞律及由互易关系（reciprocity relation）固定的逆向核条件下，从可观测的产物通量反推前向碰撞核，为气体反应动力学参数的原位测量提供理论基础。

Method: 采用两个同步入射波包（incoming packets），固定相对速度并令公共速度趋于无穷；在角积分碰撞率有界的假设下，利用短穿越时间隔离单一前向反应，并对产物通量做适当测试以获得核的空间线积分与 Fourier 重建，避免对非线性边界映射求导。

Result: 证明产物出射通量在指定容许集上唯一确定前向碰撞核，并给出核的线积分表达与 Fourier 重建；同时量化有限束速、波包宽度、探测器分辨率和测量噪声对恢复线积分的影响。

Conclusion: 结果表明产物通量测量可作为前向碰撞核的无导数反演手段，且估计在波包集中时保持一致，为稀薄气体反应碰撞核的实验反演提供了可行框架与误差界限。

Abstract: We study the reversible reaction $1+2\rightleftarrows3+4$ in a dilute gas described by a Boltzmann system. We prove that the outgoing flux of one product species determines the forward collision kernel throughout the domain on a prescribed admissible set of relative velocities and outgoing directions. The elastic collision laws are known, and the reverse kernel is fixed by a prescribed reciprocity relation. The measurements use two synchronized incoming packets with fixed relative velocity and a common velocity tending to infinity. Under bounded angularly integrated collision rates, the short transit time allows us to isolate the contribution of one forward reaction, with estimates uniform as the packets concentrate. Suitable tests of the product flux yield the spatial line integrals of the kernel and a Fourier reconstruction, without differentiating the nonlinear boundary map. We also estimate the effects of finite beam speed, packet width, detector resolution, and measurement noise on the recovered line integrals.

</details>


### [237] [Kerr stability in the full subextremal range](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40068)
*Jérémie Szeftel*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文将 Klainerman 等人关于缓慢旋转（slowly rotating）情形 $|a|\ll m$ 下 Kerr 稳定性的结果推广至完整次极端区间（subextremal range）$|a|<m$，从而完成 Kerr 稳定性猜想（Kerr stability conjecture）的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作（KS-GCM1、KS-GCM2、KS:Kerr、GKS22、Shen）仅覆盖 $|a|\ll m$ 的缓慢旋转机制，该限制实质上来自 \cite{GKS22} 中波方程与 Bianchi 系统（Bianchi system）的估计，需将其去除以获得完整次极端区间结论。

Method: 关键依赖于作者与 Siyuan Ma 的两篇配套论文（\cite{MaSz24}、\cite{MaSz26}），其中分别在 $|a|<m$ 的 Kerr 扰动（perturbations of Kerr）上证明了非齐次标量波方程（inhomogeneous scalar wave equation）与非齐次 Teukolsky 方程（inhomogeneous Teukolsky equations）的能量-Morawetz 估计（energy-Morawetz estimates）。

Result: 在去除 $|a|\ll m$ 限制后，本文推广了上述系列结果，覆盖完整次极端区间 $|a|<m$，并证明部分结果亦见于配套论文 \cite{Sze}。

Conclusion: 本工作与相关配套研究共同完成了 Kerr 稳定性猜想的证明。

Abstract: In the present paper, we extend the results in the sequence of works \cite{KS-GCM1} \cite{KS-GCM2} \cite{KS:Kerr} by Sergiu Klainerman and the author, \cite{GKS22} by Elena Giorgi, Sergiu Klainerman and the author, and \cite{Shen} by Dawei Shen from the slowly rotating regime $|a|\ll m$ to the full subextremal range $|a|<m$, thereby completing the proof of the Kerr stability conjecture. The restriction $|a|\ll m$ enters in fact only through the estimates for wave equations and for the Bianchi system established in \cite{GKS22}. To remove this restriction, we crucially rely on the two companion papers \cite{MaSz24} \cite{MaSz26} by Siyuan Ma and the author, in which we prove energy-Morawetz estimates respectively for the inhomogeneous scalar wave equation and for inhomogeneous Teukolsky equations on perturbations of Kerr with $|a|<m$. Additionally, some of the results of the present paper are proved in our companion paper \cite{Sze}.

</details>


### [238] [Teukolsky equations in perturbations of Kerr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40315)
*Jérémie Szeftel*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文是 Kerr 稳定性猜想（Kerr stability conjecture）完整证明工作 \cite{Sze} 的配套论文，旨在补齐该文中关于 Teukolsky 波动-输运系统（wave-transport system）推导与能量-Morawetz 估计（energy-Morawetz estimates）两项未加证明的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kerr 稳定性猜想此前已在慢旋转情形（|a|≪m）得到证明，并被作者推广至完整亚极端（subextremal）范围 |a|<m，从而完成猜想证明；但该完整证明中依赖的两项关键结果尚未给出证明，构成本文的动机。

Method: 本文作为 \cite{Sze} 的配套论文（companion paper），为其中两项结果提供证明：一是在 Kerr 扰动（perturbations of Kerr）背景下推导 Teukolsky 波动-输运系统，二是该文设定下 Teukolsky 方程的能量-Morawetz 估计。

Result: 摘要未给出新结果的具体陈述，仅说明本文补充证明了 \cite{Sze} 中所引用的两项结果，这些结果又依赖于 Ma 与作者关于标量波动方程（scalar wave equation）和 Teukolsky 方程的能量-Morawetz 估计的姊妹论文 \cite{MaSz24} \cite{MaSz26}。

Conclusion: 本文补全了 Kerr 稳定性猜想完整证明链条中的技术细节，为 \cite{Sze} 中未经证明的 Teukolsky 波动-输运系统推导与能量-Morawetz 估计提供严格证明。

Abstract: The Kerr stability conjecture has been proved in the slowly rotating case, i.e., $|a|\ll m$, in the sequence of works \cite{KS-GCM1} \cite{KS-GCM2} \cite{KS:Kerr} by Sergiu Klainerman and the author, \cite{GKS22} by Elena Giorgi, Sergiu Klainerman and the author, and \cite{Shen} by Dawei Shen, and extended in \cite{Sze}, by the author, to the full subextremal range $|a|<m$, thereby completing the proof of the Kerr stability conjecture. The proof in \cite{Sze} crucially relies on the two companion papers \cite{MaSz24} \cite{MaSz26} by Siyuan Ma and the author, in which we prove energy-Morawetz estimates respectively for the scalar wave equation and for Teukolsky equations on perturbations of Kerr with $|a|<m$. In addition, two results in \cite{Sze}, concerning the derivation of the Teukolsky wave-transport system in perturbations of Kerr and energy-Morawetz estimates for Teukolsky in the setting of \cite{Sze}, are stated without proofs. The goal of the present paper companion paper to \cite{Sze} is to provide the proof of these two results.

</details>


### [239] [Magnetic Stabilization of the Greenhill Instability and Hysteretic Switching in Slender Rods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38676)
*Vivekanand Dabade,Likhit Ganedi,Mohd Tahseen,Raghavendra Venkatraman*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该研究建立铁磁Kirchhoff杆（ferromagnetic Kirchhoff rod）的三维变分理论，揭示细长几何如何使磁能与弹性能尺度相当，并通过分岔分析、数值计算与实验验证磁场对Greenhill失稳的稳定化作用及磁滞开关行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块体铁磁体中磁能比弹性能弱约六个数量级，二者尺度悬殊使磁弹耦合效应通常可忽略；但细长杆（slender rod）中两种能量随长径比的标度律不同，从而消除该差异，使普通镍丝在中等外场下即产生非线性变形，亟需相应的理论框架。

Method: 作者发展了受重力与磁载荷作用下铁磁Kirchhoff杆的三维变分理论（three-dimensional variational theory），并采用分岔分析（bifurcation analysis）、数值计算与实验相结合的研究路径；同时提出一种在开关事件附近良态的数值方法。

Result: 均匀磁场可稳定竖直杆以抵抗自重屈曲（即经典Greenhill失稳），作者以Airy型主特征值（Airy-type principal eigenvalue）给出固支与铰支的精确判据，并刻画了与非磁性情形定性不同的分岔图景。在永磁体旋转加载下发现丰富分岔结构，证明失稳仅经由平面模态（planar mode）发生，每次跳变均为非退化鞍结分岔（nondegenerate saddle-node），并定位了产生磁滞的两个折叠点。50 μm镍丝实验证实了Greenhill阈值、约30 mT下的再稳定化以及磁滞回线的开关角、宽度与折叠几何。

Conclusion: 分析与实验为场稳定化细丝（field-stabilized filaments）及远程开关双稳态驱动器（remotely switched bistable actuators）提供了有原则的设计规则；所提出的良态数值方法在直接数值极小化严重病态的开关事件附近仍然有效。

Abstract: In a bulk ferromagnet, magnetic energy is six orders of magnitude weaker than
  elastic energy. Slenderness removes this disparity. In a thin ferromagnetic rod, the two energies
  scale differently with aspect ratio, and an ordinary nickel wire deforms nonlinearly even
  under modest remote fields. We develop a three-dimensional variational theory of a
  ferromagnetic Kirchhoff rod under gravity and magnetic loading and study it through
  bifurcation analysis, computation, and experiment. A uniform field can stabilize a vertical rod
  against buckling under its own weight, the classical Greenhill instability. We give sharp
  criteria for clamped and pinned supports in terms of an Airy-type principal
  eigenvalue, and give a fine description of the character of the bifurcation landscape, which has qualitatively different features than its non-magnetic counterpart. When subject to the field generated by a
  permanent magnet, we identify a rich bifurcation landscape as the magnet is rotated quasistatically along a circle of radius slightly larger than the rest length of the rod. We prove that instability occurs only through a
  planar mode and that each snap is a nondegenerate saddle-node. We also locate the two
  folds that produce hysteresis.
  Experiments on $50\,μ$m nickel wires confirm the
  Greenhill threshold, restabilization at ~$30$ mT for both clamped and pinned supports, as well as the salient features of the hysteresis loop such as switching angles, width, and geometry of folds accurately.
  Our analysis and experiments offer principled design rules for field-stabilized filaments and remotely switched bistable
  actuators. Our analysis also offers a well-conditioned numerical method is valid near the switching events, where the direct numerical minimization is strongly ill-conditioned.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [Mixed bubbles in a binary mixture of spin-1 Bose-Einstein condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39231)
*Uyen Ngoc Le,Ha Phuong Uyen Mai,Hieu Binh Le,Hiroki Saito*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作表明，在自旋-1 的 ${}^{7}\mathrm{Li}$ 与 ${}^{23}\mathrm{Na}$ 玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate, BEC）二元混合物中，混合气泡态（mixed-bubble state）可在平均场（mean-field）框架内出现，并存在可被局域微扰触发转变的亚稳均匀混合态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平均场理论下，均匀体系中双组分 BEC 的基态通常只有均匀混合或完全分离两种。混合气泡态此前被认为需要超越平均场（beyond-mean-field）效应才能引入，本文旨在探究该相能否在平均场层面出现。

Method: 研究对象为自旋-1 ${}^{7}\mathrm{Li}$ 与 ${}^{23}\mathrm{Na}$ BEC 的二元混合物，在平均场框架内进行分析，并考察局域微扰（local perturbation）对亚稳均匀混合态的影响。

Result: 在该二元混合物中，混合气泡态可在平均场框架内涌现；此外还存在一个亚稳的均匀混合态，该态会在局域微扰触发下转变为混合气泡态。

Conclusion: 混合气泡态并非必须依赖超越平均场效应，在特定双组分 BEC 混合物中于平均场层面即可实现；亚稳均匀混合态对局域微扰的敏感性为其相变调控提供了可能途径。

Abstract: In mean-field theory, the ground state of a two-component Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC) in a uniform system is either uniformly mixed or totally separated. Beyond-mean-field effects introduce an additional phase, the mixed-bubble state, in which the two components remain mixed within one of the separated domains. Here we show that a mixed-bubble state can emerge in a binary mixture of spin-1 ${}^{7}{\rm{Li}}$ and ${}^{23}{\rm{Na}}$ BECs even within the mean-field framework. We further demonstrate the existence of a metastable uniformly-mixed state, which undergoes a transition to the mixed-bubble state triggered by a local perturbation.

</details>


### [241] [Room-temperature polariton supersolids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40009)
*Yuanhao Gong,Jingwen Ma,Shuang Zhang,Xiaobo Yin,Xiang Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作首次报道在室温下于光子晶体极化激元凝聚体中观测到超固态（supersolid），将超固现象从极低温拓展至室温平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室温下探索奇异量子物相（quantum phases of matter）是现代物理的前沿挑战，而此前的超固态演示均受限于低温条件，亟需室温可行的实现途径。

Method: 将室温稳定的钙钛矿（perovskite）半导体与色散工程设计的（dispersion-engineered）光子晶体波导集成，构建具有多模色散与显著参量非线性（parametric nonlinearities）的极化激元凝聚体，并在超过临界凝聚密度时进行观测。

Result: 在超过临界凝聚密度后，相互作用的凝聚体自发破缺连续平移对称性，形成非刚性超固态，同时展现涌现的晶体序与全局量子相干性。

Conclusion: 该成果建立了用于研究量子流体动力学（quantum hydrodynamics）的室温平台，并为开发相干量子模拟器件提供基础。

Abstract: Exploring exotic quantum phases of matter at room temperature represents a frontier challenge in modern physics. The supersolid phase, uniquely merging crystalline order with frictionless superfluid flow, stands among the most intriguing macroscopic quantum phenomena. However, all previous demonstrations of supersolidity, whether in ultracold atomic gases or III-V semiconductor-based polariton systems, have been strictly confined to cryogenic temperatures. Here, we report the observation of room-temperature supersolids in photonic-crystal polariton condensates. By integrating a room-temperature-stable perovskite semiconductor with a dispersion-engineered photonic-crystal waveguide, we create a polariton condensate with multi-mode dispersion landscapes and pronounced parametric nonlinearities. Above a critical condensation density, the interacting condensates spontaneously break continuous translational symmetry, creating a non-rigid supersolid phase that simultaneously exhibits emergent crystalline order and global quantum coherence. This work establishes a room-temperature platform for investigating quantum hydrodynamics and developing coherent quantum simulation devices.

</details>


### [242] [Collective excitations of driven-dissipative quantum fluids of light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40110)
*Alberto Bramati,Iacopo Carusotto*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文综述光学腔中驱动-耗散量子光流体（driven-dissipative quantum fluids of light）的多体物理研究进展，重点分析稳态集体激发及其色散关系，并讨论其超流性质与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子因腔空间限制获得有限质量，并借材料Kerr非线性（Kerr nonlinearity）产生光子-光子相互作用，可类比玻色气体；但光子存在不可避免的辐射与非辐射损耗，需外部泵浦补偿，稳态由泵浦与损耗的动力学平衡决定，因而偏离热平衡范式，亟需系统梳理其多体物理图像。

Method: 以综述形式整合数十年理论与实验研究，聚焦稳态周围集体激发（collective excitations）的性质，刻画驱动-耗散特性对色散关系（dispersion relation）的影响，并针对实验中最常用的若干泵浦构型分别讨论。

Result: 不同泵浦构型导致显著不同的色散行为，包括有能隙（gapped）、无能隙（gapless）、声速型（sonic）、能带粘滞（band-sticking）以及扩散型Goldstone模（diffusive Goldstone）等，并阐明其对超流（superfluidity）性质的观测后果。

Conclusion: 文章展望未来重点方向：光超固态（supersolid states of light）同时具备超流与固体性质的观测效应，以及强非线性下强相互作用光子气体量子关联态（如Mott绝缘体态，Mott insulator states）的集体动力学。

Abstract: In this work we give an overview on a couple of decades of theoretical and experimental research on the many-body physics of driven-dissipative quantum fluids of light in optical cavities. These systems consist of a large number of photons enclosed in a cavity device where spatial confinement induces a finite photon mass and the Kerr optical nonlinearity of the cavity material mediates finite photon-photon interactions. Differently from standard Bose gases of material particles like liquid Helium or ultracold atomic gases, photons are inevitably subject to radiative and/or non-radiative losses, which must be compensated by some pumping mechanism. As a result, the properties of the steady state are not imposed by a thermal equilibrium condition, but are determined by a dynamical interplay of pumping and losses. Among the many collective phenomena that are observed in these systems, we focus here on the collective excitations around the steady state and we characterize the impact of the driven-dissipative nature of the gas on their properties, in particular on their dispersion relation. Different pumping configurations give very different behaviors: a specific discussion is provided for the most important configurations used in the experiments. Observable consequences of the various forms of the dispersion -gapped, gapless, sonic, band-sticking, diffusive Goldstone- are illustrated with a special eye towards their consequences on the superfluidity properties. We conclude the article with a presentation of a few research avenues that we personally find most exciting for the next years, namely the osbervable consequences of the concurrently superfluid and solid nature of supersolid states of light and the collective dynamics of quantum correlated states of strongly interacting photon gases in the presence of strong nonlinearities, in particular Mott insulator states.

</details>

### [243] [Shifted symplectic structures and Poisson vertex algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.17840)
*Wenda Fang*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文在弧空间（arc spaces）上借助度-1 分次辛几何（degree-1 graded symplectic geometry）给出的 Maurer–Cartan 数据，刻画了 Poisson 顶点代数（Poisson vertex algebra, PVA）Hamilton 结构，并证明度-2 Hamilton 量与经典主方程（classical master equation）及 PVA 丛的一一对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是为弧空间上的 PVA Hamilton 结构寻找统一几何来源，并将其与经典 R-矩阵（R-matrix）及经典 𝒲-代数联系起来。

Method: 设 X 为有限型光滑概型，Y=T^*[1]X，研究 Y_∞ 上的度-2 Hamilton 量，利用局部泛函（local functionals）Lie 代数中的 Maurer–Cartan 数据构造 X_∞ 上的 PVA 丛。

Result: 证得度-2 Hamilton 量定义 PVA 丛当且仅当其类满足经典主方程，且每个此类 PVA 括号唯一确定一个度-2 局部泛函；经典 R-矩阵恰为对应 PVA Hamilton 结构的 Maurer–Cartan 形变。应用于 ℙ¹ 上整体标量 PVA 结构的分类及有限与仿射经典 𝒲-代数的 R-矩阵形式化；在 subregular 𝔰𝔩₃ 情形构造相容 Poisson pencil 与中心约化 RDW 系统，其一般约化为亏格二超椭圆系统并具显式 Mumford 型 Lax 表示，仿射对应情形给出常数与一阶微分 R-形变及对易 Hamilton 流。

Conclusion: 度-1 分次辛几何的 Maurer–Cartan 数据为弧空间上的 PVA Hamilton 结构提供了统一框架，并通过 R-矩阵形变与显式可积系统实现具体应用。

Abstract: We identify Poisson vertex algebra (PVA) Hamiltonian structures on arc spaces with Maurer--Cartan data arising from degree-$1$ graded symplectic geometry. Let $X$ be a smooth scheme of finite type and set $Y=T^*[1]X$. We prove that a degree-$2$ Hamiltonian on $Y_\infty$ defines a sheaf of PVAs on $X_\infty$ precisely when its class in the Lie algebra of local functionals satisfies the classical master equation; conversely, every such PVA bracket determines a unique degree-$2$ local functional. We further show that classical $R$-matrices are exactly Maurer--Cartan deformations of the corresponding PVA Hamiltonian structure. As applications, we classify a class of global scalar PVA structures on $\mathbb P^1$ and apply the $R$-matrix formalism to finite and affine classical $\mathcal W$-algebras. For the subregular $\mathfrak{sl}_3$ example, we construct a compatible Poisson pencil and the centrally reduced RDW system, whose generic reduction is a genus-two hyperelliptic system admitting an explicit Mumford-type Lax representation. Its affine counterpart yields constant and first-order differential $R$-deformations, together with commuting Hamiltonian flows.

</details>


### [244] [$K_{\mathfrak{q}}$-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38702)
*Davide Gaiotto*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文提出 \(K_{\mathfrak{q}}\)-代数（\(K_{\mathfrak{q}}\)-algebra）与 \(K_{\mathfrak{q}}\)-代数重整化群流（RG flow of \(K_{\mathfrak{q}}\)-algebras）两个概念性公理化框架，分别刻画四维（4d）\({\cal N}=2\) 超对称量子场论（Supersymmetric Quantum Field Theory）中旋转等变 BPS 线缺陷（rotation-equivariant BPS line defects）的融合代数及其在 Seiberg-Witten 型重整化群流下的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在为四维 \({\cal N}=2\) 超对称量子场论中旋转等变 BPS 线缺陷的融合代数提供公理化刻画，并刻画 Seiberg-Witten 型重整化群流对线缺陷的作用，从而统一并丰富若干既有代数结构理论。

Method: 方法为概念性与猜想性的公理化定义：引入 \(K_{\mathfrak{q}}\)-代数（\(K_{\mathfrak{q}}\)-algebra）以公理化融合代数，引入 \(K_{\mathfrak{q}}\)-代数的重整化群流（RG flow of \(K_{\mathfrak{q}}\)-algebras）以公理化相应 RG 流的作用。摘要未给出具体构造、证明或计算细节。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定理或计算结果；所述对象均为猜想性（conjectural）框架。其提出的定义为簇代数（cluster algebras）、\(K\)-理论库仑分支代数（\(K\)-theoretic Coulomb branch algebras）、辫代数（skein algebras）与量子群（quantum groups）的理论增添了新内容。

Conclusion: 作者预期 \(K_{\mathfrak{q}}\)-代数及其 RG 流的概念可为线缺陷融合代数与 Seiberg-Witten 型 RG 流提供统一公理化语言，并与簇代数、\(K\)-理论库仑分支代数、辫代数和量子群等既有结构相互联系与丰富。相关命题有待进一步验证。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of $K_{\mathfrak{q}}$-algebra, a conjectural axiomatization of the fusion algebra of rotation-equivariant BPS line defects in a 4d ${\cal N}=2$ Supersymmetric Quantum Field Theory. We also introduce the notion of RG flow of $K_{\mathfrak{q}}$-algebras, a conjectural axiomatization of the action of Seiberg-Witten type RG flows on line defects. These definitions enrich the theory of cluster algebras, $K$-theoretic Coulomb branch algebras, skein algebras and quantum groups.

</details>

### [245] [Probing jet quenching via the correlation of groomed jet substructure observables in Pb$-$Pb and pp collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39515)
*ALICE Collaboration*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 在 5 TeV 的 pp 与 0–10% 中心 Pb–Pb 碰撞中，ALICE 测量带电粒子喷注首次硬劈裂的劈裂角 θ_g 与动量分配分数 z_g 的相关性；Pb–Pb 中喷注更窄，且该效应在平衡喷注中更显著，模型比较显示 HYBRID 中的弹性散射（elastic scattering）更有利于描述大 θ_g 的不平衡喷注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在考察夸克胶子等离子体（quark-gluon plasma）对部分子簇射（parton shower）中首次硬劈裂（hard splitting）动力学的修正，通过 θ_g 与 z_g 的关联区分不同介质效应的实现方式。测量覆盖 pp 与 0–10% 中心 Pb–Pb 碰撞，为喷注淬火（jet quenching）机制提供更精细的观测量。

Method: 利用 ALICE 探测器在 √s_NN = 5 TeV 下重建带电粒子喷注（charged-particle jets），采用 anti-k_T 算法与 R = 0.2，横动量范围为 60 ≤ p_T,ch jet < 80 GeV/c。使用 Soft Drop  grooming 算法识别部分子簇射中的首次劈裂，并测量 θ_g 与 z_g 的相关性。pp 结果与 PYTHIA 8、POWHEG、HERWIG 比较，Pb–Pb 结果与多种模型比较。

Result: Pb–Pb 碰撞中观测到的喷注比 pp 碰撞中更窄，且该效应在平衡喷注（balanced jets）中比不平衡喷注（unbalanced jets）更显著。小开角喷注未见 z_g 的显著修正；大 θ_g 喷注在 Pb–Pb 中相比 pp 呈现横动量更不平衡的迹象。pp 中 θ_g–z_g 关联可被 PYTHIA 8 与 POWHEG 较好描述，而 HERWIG 给出的大开角喷注过少。Pb–Pb 测量对不同的介质响应（medium response）实现不敏感。

Conclusion: 在 Pb–Pb 碰撞中，包含弹性散射（elastic scattering）的 HYBRID 模型更受青睐，可描述大 θ_g 的不平衡喷注；而平衡喷注对弹性散射较不敏感。该测量对介质响应的不同实现不敏感，表明 θ_g–z_g 关联可作为约束弹性散射效应的判别性观测量。

Abstract: A measurement of the correlation between the splitting angle $θ_{\rm g}$ and the momentum-sharing fraction $z_{\rm g}$ of the first hard splitting in a parton shower in pp collisions and 0$-$10% central Pb$-$Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}$ = 5 TeV with the ALICE detector is reported. Charged-particle jets are reconstructed using the anti-$k_{\rm T}$ algorithm with a jet resolution parameter $R$ = 0.2, in the transverse-momentum range $60 \leq p_{\rm T,ch\;jet} < 80$ GeV/$c$. The Soft Drop grooming algorithm is used to identify the first splitting in the parton shower. Jets observed in Pb$-$Pb collisions are narrower than in pp collisions. This effect is more pronounced for balanced than for unbalanced jets. No significant modification of the momentum-sharing fraction is observed for jets with small opening angles. In contrast, there is a hint that jets with a large opening angle may be less balanced in transverse momentum in Pb$-$Pb collisions compared to pp collisions. The correlations between $θ_{\rm g}$ and $z_{\rm g}$ are well described in pp collisions by PYTHIA 8 and POWHEG while HERWIG shows too few jets with large opening angle. In Pb$-$Pb collisions the measurement is compared to a variety of models. The measurement is insensitive to the different implementations of medium response. Inclusion of elastic scatterings, as implemented in the HYBRID model is preferred to describe the unbalanced jets with large $θ_{\rm g}$ in Pb$-$Pb collisions. The balanced jets, however, are less sensitive to elastic scatterings.

</details>

### [246] [Packaged DNA genome sets the long-range electrostatic anisotropy of a virus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38429)
*Jeffrey C. Everts,Anže Božič,Rudolf Podgornik*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究指出，包装基因组（packaged genome）而非衣壳（capsid）对称性，是充盈病毒长程静电各向异性（long-range electrostatic anisotropy）的起源，并建立了含各向异性介电张量（anisotropic dielectric tensor）的泊松--玻尔兹曼（Poisson--Boltzmann）连续理论予以支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病毒是生物中电荷密度最高的对象之一，静电相互作用贯穿其生命周期各阶段；但空载二十面体衣壳在电解质环境中几乎呈各向同性，其电荷分布的各向异性仅在高多极阶（high multipole order）出现，且被静电屏蔽（electrostatic screening）在可探测距离之前消除。因此，充盈病毒是否存在长程静电各向异性及其来源尚待阐明。

Method: 采用一种新的泊松--玻尔兹曼连续理论（Poisson--Boltzmann continuum theory），其中双链DNA（dsDNA）的取向序通过各向异性介电张量（anisotropic dielectric tensor）与非均匀体电荷分布（inhomogeneous volume-charge distribution）引入，并与含可电离氨基酸残基的电荷调节壳层（charge-regulating shell）耦合。以噬菌体λ为模型体系，并在另外两种噬菌体上检验结果稳健性。

Result: 计算得到的静电势分布显示，以反线轴（inverse-spool）几何包装的DNA在外衣壳表面印刻出高度非均匀的势分布，其角向结构直接源于DNA缺失的轴向空腔（DNA-free axial void）。高盐浓度下，该分布的多极谱（multipole spectrum）在远距离仍持续存在；低盐浓度下高阶多极随远离表面衰减更快。残余四极（surviving quadrupole）使病毒--壁与病毒--病毒相互作用在$k_\mathrm{B}T$量级上具有取向依赖性，其择优取向由外电荷符号与pH控制。上述行为在多种pH与离子强度下于三种噬菌体中一致出现。

Conclusion: 充盈病毒的长程静电各向异性源自其包装基因组，而非衣壳对称性；该结论在噬菌体λ及另外两种噬菌体中于一系列pH与离子强度下稳健成立。

Abstract: Viruses are among the most highly charged objects in biology, and electrostatic interactions permeate nearly every stage of their life cycle. At the same time, an empty icosahedral capsid is almost isotropic as far as the surrounding electrolyte is concerned---its charge distribution is anisotropic only at high multipole order, and electrostatic screening removes such anisotropy long before it can be probed at a distance. Here, we show that the packaged genome of a filled virus imparts a long-range electrostatic anisotropy that extends throughout the surrounding medium, which is not present for empty capsids. We support these findings with a novel Poisson--Boltzmann continuum theory where the orientational order of dsDNA enters through an anisotropic dielectric tensor and an inhomogeneous volume-charge distribution, which is coupled to a charge-regulating shell containing ionizable amino acid residues of the capsid proteins. The resulting electrostatic potential profiles show that DNA packed in an inverse-spool geometry imprints a highly inhomogeneous potential distribution on the outer capsid surface, whose angular structure is a direct result of the DNA-free axial void. At high salinity, the multipole spectrum of this distribution persists at large distances, whereas at low salt concentration, higher-order multipoles decay more rapidly away from the surface. The surviving quadrupole renders virus--wall and virus--virus interactions orientation dependent at the $k_\mathrm{B}T$ level, with a preferred orientation controlled by the sign of the external charge and the pH. Using phage $λ$ as a model system and checking robustness of our results on two other phages, we find the same behavior across a range of pH and ionic strengths. This identifies the packaged genome, rather than the symmetry of the capsid, as the origin of the long-range electrostatic anisotropy of a filled virus.

</details>


### [247] [Axonemal bending stiffness of $\mathit{Chlamydomonas}$ cilia implies single-motor forces above $5\,\mathrm{pN}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39562)
*Maximilian Kotz,Veikko F. Geyer,Benjamin M. Friedrich*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 通过轴丝弯曲刚度估算莱茵衣藻鞭毛中动力蛋白马达的主动力，得出单个马达头部峰值力下界超过10 pN，高于典型失速力，提示轴丝刚度被高估或密集马达阵列存在协同增强效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纤毛与鞭毛的规则弯曲波是分子马达集体动力学的标志性模型系统；已知轴丝中动力蛋白（dynein）马达的规则排布，为将介观纤毛性质（如轴丝弯曲刚度）与微观马达性质（如单个马达的力）相联系提供了可能。

Method: 利用已有的轴丝弯曲刚度（axonemal bending stiffness）估计值，估算莱茵衣藻（Chlamydomonas）轴丝中动力蛋白分子马达集合所产生的主动力（active force），再除以活跃马达头部的最大可能数目，从而得到单个马达头部峰值力的下界。

Result: 计算得到单个马达头部峰值力的下界为 >10 pN，而典型分子马达的失速力（stall force）为 <5 pN，前者明显超过后者。

Conclusion: 该差异表明两种可能：其一，微管（microtubule）与轴丝的弯曲刚度此前被高估；其二，密集马达阵列中的集体力生成可超过单个马达预期贡献的简单加和。

Abstract: The regular bending waves of cilia and flagella provide an iconic model system for the collective dynamics of molecular motors. The known regular arrangement of dynein motors in a cilium's axoneme allows us to connect mesoscopic cilia properties, such as axonemal bending stiffness, to microscopic motor properties, such as the force exerted by an individual motor. We estimate the active force generated by the collection of dynein molecular motors in $\mathit{Chlamydomonas}$ axonemes using previous estimates of its bending stiffness. Divided by the maximal possible number of active motor heads, this provides a lower bound of ${>}10\,\mathrm{pN}$ for the peak force generated by a motor head, which exceeds typical stall forces ${<}5\,\mathrm{pN}$ of molecular motors. This discrepancy suggests that either the bending stiffness of microtubules and axonemes was previously overestimated, or that collective force generation in dense motor arrays can surpass the sum of expected contributions of individual motors.

</details>

### [248] [Spectral numbers in Tate Rabinowitz Floer homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39951)
*Kai Cieliebak,Urs Frauenfelder*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 该文表明，Tate–Rabinowitz Floer 同调（Tate Rabinowitz Floer homology）中的圆周值谱值（circle valued spectral values）可产生量子化能级（quantized energy levels），并复现非耦合谐振子（uncoupled harmonic oscillators）系统的量子力学谱（quantum mechanical spectrum）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确阐述研究动机或所针对的问题背景。

Method: 采用 Tate–Rabinowitz Floer 同调框架，考察其中取值于圆周的谱值（circle valued spectral values）。

Result: 该同调中的圆周值谱值给出量子化能级（quantized energy levels），并成功恢复非耦合谐振子系统的量子力学谱。

Conclusion: Tate–Rabinowitz Floer 同调的谱数据与谐振子系统的量子谱之间存在对应关系，提示该同调工具可用于刻画量子化能级。摘要未给出更进一步的结论或推广。

Abstract: We show that circle valued spectral values in Tate Rabinowitz Floer ho- mology give rise to quantized energy levels, which recover the quantum mechanical spectrum of a system of uncoupled harmonic oscillators.

</details>

### [249] [Exact analytic solutions of classical time crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39201)
*Stavros Theodorakis,Athina Hadjizorzi*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 论文给出抛物线势（parabolic potential）中经典时间晶体（classical time crystal）的精确解析解，其动能对速度分段抛物线且在非零速度处取极小，运动周期且速度存在周期性间断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确交代研究动机与背景问题，仅以“present examples”引出对经典时间晶体的解析求解尝试。

Method: 在位置抛物线势中构造动能随速度呈分段抛物线（piecewise parabolic）形式的经典时间晶体模型，求精确解析解；并进一步考察四阶动能（quartic kinetic energy）情形，用分段抛物线动能对其进行模拟（emulate）。

Result: 解为周期性的，速度出现确定的周期性间断（periodic discontinuities）；动能极小值位于非零速度。解在作用量（action）较低的支（branch）上保持稳定。对四阶动能情形，分段抛物线模拟给出准确且清晰的解。

Conclusion: 分段抛物线动能可有效模拟四阶动能，从而为经典时间晶体提供简单、透明且精确的解析描述。

Abstract: We present examples of classical time crystals (in a parabolic potential for the position) that admit simple exact analytic solutions. The kinetic energy in these crystals is piecewise parabolic as a function of speed and is minimised at nonzero values of the speed. The solutions are periodic, while the speed acquires definite periodic discontinuities. The solutions are stable along the branches where the action is lower. We also examine the classical time crystal with quartic kinetic energy. We show how the quartic kinetic energy can be emulated by a piecewise parabolic one, leading to accurate and transparent solutions.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [250] [Edgeworth expansions of extreme eigenvalue distributions for random unitary ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39169)
*Junwen Liu,Qianlu Mao,Lun Zhang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文建立了两类随机酉系综（random unitary ensembles）在谱边缘处极端特征值分布的 Edgeworth 展开（Edgeworth expansions），并揭示了修正项的普适结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究集中于随机矩阵谱边缘极端特征值分布的精细修正项，旨在刻画高斯型与 Laguerre 型系综在软边缘与硬边缘处修正项是否具有普适结构。

Method: 证明高斯型酉系综与 Laguerre 型酉系综的相关核（correlation kernels）分别在软边缘（soft edge）与硬边缘（hard edge）容许完整展开；系数为 Airy 函数或第一类 Bessel 函数及其导数与多项式系数的有限和；并将核展开提升至相应的 Fredholm 行列式。

Result: 获得高斯型酉系综最大特征值分布与 Laguerre 型酉系综最小特征值分布的完整展开；其中首个修正项涉及主导项的导数。

Conclusion: 两类酉系综在谱边缘的极端特征值分布存在具有普适结构的 Edgeworth 展开，修正项由 Airy 或 Bessel 函数类构成，且首个修正项与主导项导数相关。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish Edgeworth expansions of extreme eigenvalue distributions for two types random unitary ensembles at the spectral edges and reveal certain universal structures for the correction terms. More precisely, we show the correlation kernels for the Gaussian-type unitary ensembles and Laguerre-type unitary ensembles admit full expansions at the soft edge and the hard edge, respectively. The coefficients of the correction terms are given by finite sums of Airy function or the Bessel functions of the first kind and their derivatives with polynomial coefficients. By lifting the kernel expansion to the associated Fredholm determinant, we further obtain full expansions for the largest eigenvalue distribu- tion of the Gaussian-type unitary ensembles and the smallest eigenvalue distribution of the Laguerre-type unitary ensembles. In addition, the first correction term therein involves the derivatives of the leading term.

</details>


### [251] [Limit Theorems for Disordered Quantum Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40096)
*Yeor Hafouta,Lubashan Pathirana*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究无序环境中有限维系统的离散时间量子轨迹（quantum trajectories），其中每步测量仪器由可逆保测动力系统决定。在淬火（quenched）Doeblin条件下，作者构造并刻画了等变随机后验律族及其重心作为非选择性信道余循环的唯一动态稳态，进而证明了逐路径遍历定理、淬火方差渐近、中心极限定理与Berry--Esseen界，并给出α-混合继承与满足条件的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实量子测量常发生在无序、非自治环境中，仪器序列由外部动力系统驱动；现有量子轨迹理论多假设环境固定或独立同分布。本文旨在为这种环境依赖（environment-dependent）的量子轨迹建立严格的随机动力学与极限定理框架。

Method: 对标准Borel结果空间上的一般量子仪器（quantum instruments），在固定无序实现与给定初态下构造测量结果序列的淬火概率律。引入带均匀确定性小化常数与环境依赖小化测度的淬火Doeblin条件，用随机后验律族、重心及非选择性信道余循环（non-selective channel cocycle）等工具进行分析。

Result: 在淬火Doeblin条件下证明等变随机后验律族存在唯一，其重心是动态稳态的唯一解；环境遍历时，对每个可测初态与几乎每个无序实现，后验态时间平均几乎必然收敛到动态稳态的无序平均；还得到淬火方差渐近，方差为正时的中心极限定理与标准化Berry--Esseen界；稳态退火联合过程继承仪器过程的α-混合性（差一个指数衰减项）。

Conclusion: 该工作将随机动力系统与量子轨迹理论结合，为无序环境中的量子测量统计提供了普适的遍历性与极限定理，并给出涵盖完美与不完美测量、连续或有限结果空间的满足Doeblin条件的实例类。

Abstract: We study discrete-time quantum trajectories of a finite-dimensional system in a disordered environment, where the instrument applied at each step is determined by an invertible measure-preserving dynamical system. For general quantum instruments on a standard Borel outcome space, we construct the quenched probability law on sequences of measurement outcomes for a fixed realization of the disorder and a given initial state. Under a quenched Doeblin condition with a uniform deterministic minorization constant and an environment-dependent minorizing probability measure, we establish the existence and uniqueness of an equivariant family of random posterior laws and identify its barycenter as the unique dynamically stationary state for the associated non-selective channel cocycle. If the environment is ergodic, we prove a pathwise ergodic theorem valid for every measurable choice of initial state: for almost every realization of the disorder, time averages of posterior states converge almost surely under the corresponding quenched law to the disorder average of the unique dynamically stationary state. For an ergodic environment, we establish quenched variance asymptotics and, when the asymptotic variance is positive, a central limit theorem and standardized Berry--Esseen bounds for additive functionals generated by bounded measurable real-valued functions of the environment and posterior state. These results hold for every measurable initial state and almost every disorder realization. We then show that the stationary annealed joint process of instruments and posterior states inherits the $α$-mixing of the instrument process, up to an exponentially decaying term. Finally, we provide classes of examples involving perfect and imperfect measurements, including models with continuous or finite outcome spaces, that satisfy the standing Doeblin condition.

</details>


### [252] [Half-space KPZ equation from a class of nonlinear SPDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40352)
*Kevin Yang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文在弱非线性标度（weak nonlinearity scaling）下，由一类广义金兹堡-朗道随机偏微分方程（Ginzburg-Landau SPDEs）取连续极限，导出半空间 KPZ 方程（half-space KPZ equation）。据作者所知，这是可积模型及其扰动之外的首个半空间 KPZ 普适性（universality）结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KPZ 方程的普遍性此前主要依赖可积模型（integrable models）及其扰动来建立，半空间情形尤受此限制。本文旨在突破该限制，为更一般的非可积方程类建立半空间普适性。

Method: 方法为连续极限（continuum limit）分析：从一类广义的金兹堡-朗道随机偏微分方程出发，在弱非线性标度下取极限，从而推导出半空间 KPZ 方程。

Result: 在一类广义金兹堡-朗道随机偏微分方程与弱非线性标度假设下，成功导出了半空间 KPZ 方程作为其连续极限。作者指出该结果为可积模型及其扰动之外的首个半空间 KPZ 普适性结果。

Conclusion: 半空间 KPZ 普适性可在不依赖可积性的情形下成立，从而将 KPZ 普适类（KPZ universality class）的适用范囿从可积模型扩展至更一般的随机偏微分方程类。

Abstract: We derive the half-space KPZ equation as a continuum limit from a general class of Ginzburg-Landau SPDEs under weak nonlinearity scaling. To our knowledge, this is the first half-space universality result for the KPZ equation beyond integrable models (and their perturbations).

</details>


### [253] [Positive-temperature spin-glass order on the three-dimensional Migdal-Kadanoff lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38220)
*Yan Ru Pei*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究菱形分层格（diamond hierarchical lattice）上独立同分布耦合的伊辛自旋玻璃（Ising spin glass），证明在低温下自旋玻璃序（spin-glass order）存在，并给出蓝簇（blue cluster）密度不平衡。相较此前仅对超过 $10^{18}$ 分支成立的结果，本文大幅降低了所需分支数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前在具有阻挫独立耦合的此类格子上，自旋玻璃序仅在分支数超过 $10^{18}$ 时得证（Collet 与 Eckmann, 1984），所需分支数极高，限制了对低维（如 $\mathbb{Z}^3$ 对应的 Migdal-Kadanoff 格子）情形的推论。

Method: 核心步骤是证明有效耦合（effective coupling）在精确重整化映射（exact renormalization map）下增长：对 $n\ge4$，通过 Rogozin 不等式对高概率事件上的密度界作单步收缩；在 $T_c$ 附近则借助尖峰序（peakedness order）中的区间算术（interval-arithmetic）界。随后沿 Collet-Eckmann 的祖先链论证（ancestor-chain argument）建立转移定理，将增长转化为体相结论。

Result: 对 $n\ge4$ 及任何有界密度耦合律，在足够低温度 $T>0$ 下一致地（对格子层级一致）证明极点有序、每个自旋的 Edwards-Anderson 参数为 $1-O(T)$、两自由边界复本的重叠二阶矩为 $1-O(T)$。对标准高斯耦合，在 $n=3,4,5,7,8$ 及相应温度 $T\le T^*(n)$ 下经计算机辅助证明同样结论。在 Chayes-Machta-Redner 表示下，同一输入给出含两极点的蓝簇概率趋于一、期望密度有正下界，而最大其他蓝簇密度依概率与 $L^1$ 趋于零。

Conclusion: 本文首次在非完全图的图上证明了自旋玻璃的蓝簇密度不平衡（blue-cluster density imbalance）。其温度阈值可达数值估计 $T_c$ 的 $0.89$ 至 $0.98$ 倍，显著扩展了此类格子自旋玻璃序的适用范围。

Abstract: We study the Ising spin glass with independent, identically distributed couplings on the diamond hierarchical lattice with $n$ branches, of effective dimension $1+\log_2 n$; $n=4$ is the Migdal-Kadanoff lattice of $\mathbb{Z}^3$. For $n\ge4$ and every coupling law with a bounded density we prove that at sufficiently low temperature $T>0$, uniformly in the level of the lattice, the poles are ordered, every spin has Edwards-Anderson parameter $1-O(T)$ when the poles are fixed, and the overlap of two free-boundary replicas has second moment $1-O(T)$. For standard Gaussian couplings, $n=3,4,5,7,8$ and every $T\le T^*(n)=4/15,\,6/7,\,6/5,\,7/4,\,2$ ($0.89$ to $0.98$ times numerical estimates of $T_c$), a computer-assisted proof gives pole order and, as the level tends to infinity, site-overlap order and the blue-cluster statements below; likewise for every larger $n$ at these temperatures. Previously, spin-glass order on these lattices with frustrated independent couplings had been proved only for more than $10^{18}$ branches (Collet and Eckmann, 1984). The key step is growth of the effective coupling under the exact renormalization map: for $n\ge4$, by a one-step contraction, via Rogozin's inequality, of a density bound on an event of high probability; near $T_c$, by interval-arithmetic bounds in the peakedness order. A transfer theorem following the ancestor-chain argument of Collet and Eckmann turns this growth into bulk statements. In the Chayes-Machta-Redner representation the same input gives a blue cluster that contains both poles with probability tending to one and whose expected density is bounded away from zero, while the largest other blue cluster has density tending to zero in probability and in $L^1$; hence blue-cluster density imbalance. To our knowledge this is the first proof of such an imbalance for a spin glass on a graph other than the complete graph.

</details>


### [254] [Computing Extinction Barriers in a Quorum-Sensing Reaction Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39946)
*Mario Ayala,Johannes Zimmer*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个将细胞密度与信号密度耦合的群体感应（quorum-sensing）反应网络模型，研究信号生产与移除速率的统一缩放因子 $r$ 如何在不改变确定性平衡点的前提下，改变稀有灭绝跃迁的准势垒（quasipotential barrier）$\Delta V(r)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性分析中速率缩放 $r$ 不改变平衡点及其稳定性类型，因此该缩放常被视为无关参数；论文旨在揭示其对随机跃迁速率仍具决定性影响，即对稀有灭绝事件平均时间的指数级调制。

Method: 采用路径作用量最小化（最小作用量路径）计算势垒，将信号密度保留为涨落坐标；并与种群阈值穿越时间的精确随机模拟（按系统尺寸 $N$ 回归）进行对比。

Result: 最小作用量势垒满足 $\Delta V(r)=\Delta V_\infty+O(1/r)$，其中 $\Delta V_\infty$ 为优先消去信号后的结果；$r=1$ 时势垒比 $\Delta V_\infty$ 大 55%。$r=0.5$ 时最廉价穿越比鞍点势垒低 7.7% 作用量，保持高信号水平且通常随后恢复。模拟到达时间（含失败尝试）的回归斜率在每个测试速率下均落在鞍点势垒的两个拟合标准误内，若省略对数前因子项则在 $0.004$ 以内。

Conclusion: 两种回归模型在 $r\le2$ 时以至少 $4.4$ 个拟合标准误排除 $\Delta V_\infty$，且不依赖作用量求解器。结果表明，即便确定性平衡保持不变，细胞-信号网络速率的统一缩放仍显著调制稀有灭绝跃迁的指数时间尺度。

Abstract: We introduce a simple reaction-network model of a quorum-sensing population that couples the cell density $x$ to the signal density $w$. In the four-channel cell-signal network studied here, scaling signal production and removal by the same factor $r$ leaves all deterministic equilibria, and their stability types, unchanged. Nevertheless, we show that $r$ shifts the quasipotential barrier $ΔV(r)$ for rare transitions towards extinction, and thus, under metastable exit assumptions, the mean time to reach a fixed neighbourhood of the extinction state on the exponential scale $e^{NΔV(r)}$. We compute the barrier by minimization of the path action with the signal retained as a fluctuating coordinate, and we compare it with exact stochastic simulation of population-threshold crossing times regressed in $N$. Over a range of $r$, the minimum-action barriers satisfy $ΔV(r)=ΔV_\infty+O(1/r)$, where $ΔV_\infty$ is obtained by eliminating the signal first. At $r=1$, the barrier is 55% larger than $ΔV_\infty$. The saddle barrier $ΔV(r)$ is also the least action needed to enter the basin of extinction, but the density threshold can be crossed more cheaply: at $r=0.5$ the cheapest crossing costs 7.7% less action, keeps the signal high, and is usually followed by recovery. Simulated arrival times in this neighbourhood, which include failed attempts, grow with slopes within two fitted standard errors of the saddle barrier at every tested rate, and within $0.004$ of it if the logarithmic prefactor term is omitted. Under either regression model they exclude $ΔV_\infty$ at $r\le2$ by at least $4.4$ fitted standard errors, without using the action solver.

</details>

### [255] [Ca$^{2+}$-tunable mechanics and recoil in reconstituted Tcb2 networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38667)
*Xiangting Lei,K. R. Prathyusha,Carlos Floyd,Jerry Honts,Saad Bhamla*

Main category: q-bio.BM

TL;DR: 该研究利用光镊（optical tweezers）在微流控芯片中对重组 Tcb2 网络进行主动微流变学（active microrheology）测量，发现 Ca²⁺ 浓度从 1 增至 100 mM 可使网络有效刚度提升近两个数量级，并揭示其从黏性主导向弹性主导的转变及正交加载后的对角回弹行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tcb2（Tetrahymena calcium-binding protein）可形成具收缩行为的 Ca²⁺ 响应网络，但 Ca²⁺ 浓度如何调控其局部力学响应尚不清楚。

Method: 将重组 Tcb2 网络置于可精确控制 Ca²⁺ 浓度的微流控装置（microfluidic device）内，使用光镊进行主动微流变学测量，并结合回弹实验（recoil assay）与两次正交拉伸测试。

Result: Ca²⁺ 由 1 mM 升至 100 mM 时，有效刚度（effective stiffness）从约 1×10⁻³ pN/nm 增至约 7×10⁻² pN/nm，提升近两个数量级，网络由黏性主导转为更具弹性且机械稳健；形变后弹性能迅速释放，且两次正交拉伸后微粒沿对角线回弹而非沿加载路径返回。

Conclusion: Tcb2 网络可作为可调的最小模型系统，用于探究 Ca²⁺ 调控蛋白网络中的化学-力学耦合（chemomechanical coupling）与黏弹性（viscoelasticity）。

Abstract: Tetrahymena calcium-binding protein (Tcb2) forms Ca$^{2+}$-responsive networks that exhibit contractile behavior, yet how Ca$^{2+}$ concentration controls their local mechanical response remains poorly understood. Here, we use optical tweezers to perform active microrheology on reconstituted Tcb2 networks inside a microfluidic device that enables precise control of Ca$^{2+}$ concentration, allowing systematic tuning of network structure and mechanics. We find that increasing Ca$^{2+}$ from 1 to 100~mM enhances the effective stiffness by nearly two orders of magnitude, from $\sim$1$\times$10$^{-3}$ to $\sim$7$\times$10$^{-2}$~pN/nm, corresponding to a transition from a viscosity-dominated to a more elastic and mechanically robust network. Recoil assays further reveal rapid release of stored elastic energy following deformation. After two orthogonal pulls, the bead recoils along the diagonal rather than retracing the loading path, indicating that stresses from different directions combine to produce a resultant restoring response. These results establish Tcb2 networks as a minimal, tunable system for probing chemomechanical coupling and viscoelasticity in Ca$^{2+}$-regulated protein networks.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [256] [Multiscale Renormalization of Weighted Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38283)
*Mattia Marzi,Frank P. Pijpers,Diego Garlaschelli*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文将网络重整化（network renormalization）从二值图（binary graph）扩展至加权随机图（weighted random graph），借助生成函数（generating-function）方法识别出一个权重多尺度模型，其完整概率分布在任意节点聚合（node aggregation）下具有不变的复合泊松（compound-Poisson）形式，从而实现加权网络的跨尺度标定与重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有网络重整化工作刻画了随机图模型在节点聚合下的重整化流（renormalization flow），并找到一个聚合不变的固定点模型，但此前仅局限于二值图。为将这一分辨率不变（resolution-invariant）的多尺度建模范式推广至带权网络，需要重新表述加权随机图的重整化框架。

Method: 采用生成函数（generating-function）方法对加权随机图的多尺度网络重整化（multiscale network renormalization）进行重新表述，推导出同时刻画连边生成与权重赋值的完整概率律（full probability law），并给出跨聚合层级变换参数的重整化规则（renormalization rules）。

Result: 识别出一个加权多尺度模型，其完整概率分布在任意节点聚合下保持复合泊松（compound-Poisson）不变形式。相应的重整化规则支持在一个层级标定系综（ensemble）并在另一层级应用，从而对加权网络进行一致的跨尺度建模与聚合数据重构。

Conclusion: 加权随机图的重整化可纳入统一的分辨率不变框架，使多尺度网络建模、潜在空间嵌入（latent-space embedding）与细粒度重构等原本针对二值图的能力具备推广到加权网络的原理性基础。

Abstract: Recent progress in network renormalization has identified various ways to transform network representations consistently across resolution levels. In particular, the renormalization flow describing how random graph models transform under node aggregation has identified a fixed point corresponding to an aggregation-invariant model that preserves the form of the connection probability across arbitrary scales. While this probabilistic multiscale approach has successfully been used to model real-world networks, construct latent-space node embeddings, and reconstruct finer-grained versions of aggregate network data in a principled resolution-invariant way, it has so far been restricted to binary graphs. Here we reformulate multiscale network renormalization for weighted random graphs. Via a generating-function approach, we identify a weighted multiscale model whose full probability law, encoding both link creation and weight assignment, has an invariant compound-Poisson form under arbitrary node aggregation. The renormalization rules that transform parameters across aggregation levels allow the ensemble to be calibrated at one level and applied at another, enabling consistent modeling and reconstruction of weighted networks across arbitrary scales.

</details>


### [257] [Opinion dynamics under large-scale exogenous events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39858)
*Gerard Argany Herrera,Lucila G. Alvarez-Zuzek,Oriol Artime*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个通用的随机过程（stochastic process）框架，用于刻画交互主体在突发宏观外生冲击（exogenous shock）下的观点形成动力学，并以选民模型（voter model）及其变体为例验证与展开分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治丑闻、虚假信息运动等大规模事件会突然改变群体集体观点，而社会物理学（sociophysics）中标准的局部交互模型无法刻画此类宏观外生冲击带来的影响，因此需要新的建模框架。

Method: 构建适用于任意观点动力学模型的通用框架，将群体置于突发、宏观的外生冲击之下；以选民模型及其变体为示例，并推导其首次通过（first-passage）性质的数学描述。

Result: 该数学描述在完全图（complete graph）与复杂网络（complex networks）上都能很好地刻画选民模型达成共识（consensus）的首次通过性质；识别出冲击有利于或不利于达成共识的条件；在全连接含噪选民模型（all-to-all noisy voter model）情形下给出包含新型三峰（trimodal）与非对称双峰（asymmetric bimodal）状态的丰富相图（phase diagram）。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解观点动力学在反复外生压力源（recurrent exogenous stressor）下的非平衡（out-of-equilibrium）行为提供了严谨而灵活的随机过程框架。

Abstract: Large-scale events such as political scandals or misinformation campaigns can abruptly shift the collective opinion of a population and reshape the dynamics in ways that standard local-interaction models of sociophysics do not capture. Here, we investigate the dynamics of opinion formation when a group of interacting agents is subjected to sudden, macroscopic exogenous shocks. Our framework is general and applies to arbitrary opinion dynamics models; to illustrate its scope, we consider the voter model and variations thereof. We verify that the mathematical description is excellent in describing the first-passage properties to consensus of the voter model on the complete graph and on complex networks. Remarkably, we identify the conditions under which shocks can be advantageous or detrimental to reach consensus. For the case of the all-to-all noisy voter model under exogenous events, we unveil a rich phase diagram characterized by novel trimodal and asymmetric bimodal states. Ultimately, this work provides a rigorous yet flexible stochastic-process framework for understanding the out-of-equilibrium behavior of opinion dynamics under recurrent exogenous stressors.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [258] [How Shaped Waves Propagate in Scattering Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39586)
*David Gaspard,Arthur Goetschy*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出以矩阵输运方程（matrix transport equation）为核心的新输运理论，用于描述波前调控的相干波（coherent waves）在散射介质中的传播。该理论传播一个保留相位信息的复二阶矩阵，且满足非线性输运方程，可用于解析推导透射本征通道（transmission eigenchannels）的能量与电流密度空间分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统辐射输运方程（radiative transport equation）虽能描述散射介质中的波传播，但无法处理入射波前被刻意调控的相干波。因此需要一个能刻画波前调控相干波传播的输运理论框架。

Method: 建立基于矩阵输运方程的输运理论。与传统辐射输运传播标量辐射度（scalar radiance）不同，该理论传播一个保留相位信息的复二阶矩阵（complex two-by-two matrix），并服从非线性输运方程（nonlinear transport equation），尽管底层波动力学是线性的。据此推导任意扩散系统中透射本征通道（由闭通道到开通道）的能量与电流密度空间分布。

Result: 透射本征通道的能量与电流密度空间分布可解析地用同介质中随机波的常规辐射输运方程（conventional radiative transport equation）的解表达。理论进一步推广至能量损耗（含吸收与泄漏入不可控通道），揭示复杂结构中透射本征通道的对称破缺（symmetry breaking）。结果经波方程数值解验证。

Conclusion: 矩阵输运（matrix transport）被确立为描述与调控复杂介质中相干波传播的框架，其有效性经波方程数值解验证。

Abstract: The radiative transport equation provides a powerful framework for describing wave propagation in scattering media. However, it cannot describe coherent waves whose incident wavefront is deliberately tailored. Here we establish a transport theory for wavefront-controlled waves based on a matrix transport equation. Unlike conventional radiative transport, which propagates a scalar radiance, our theory propagates a complex two-by-two matrix that retains phase information and obeys a nonlinear transport equation, despite the underlying wave dynamics being linear. We use this matrix transport equation to derive the spatial profiles of the energy and current densities of transmission eigenchannels, from closed to open channels, in arbitrary diffusive systems. Remarkably, these profiles can be expressed analytically in terms of the solution of the conventional radiative transport equation for a random wave in the same medium. The theory further extends to energy loss, including absorption and leakage into uncontrolled channels, revealing symmetry breaking of transmission eigenchannels in complex structures. Validated against numerical solutions of the wave equation, we establish matrix transport as a framework for describing and controlling coherent wave propagation in complex media.

</details>


### [259] [Surface quantum response of plasmonic heterodimers: revealing the hidden nonlocality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38870)
*Ida Juliane Bundgaard,Christos Mystilidis,Frederik Wulff Christensen,Lorenz Huber,P. Elli Stamatopoulou,Carsten Rockstuhl,P. A. D. Gonçalves,Ulrich Hohenester,N. Asger Mortensen,Christos Tserkezis*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作基于 Feibelman 参数（Feibelman parameters）的表面响应形式，研究等离激元异质二聚体（plasmonic heterodimers）在扩展源与局域源下的量子响应。发现相反方向的量子表面修正可相互抵消，使介观光谱与局域响应近似（local-response approximation）预测一致；而使用局域源则可独立探测各粒子的量子响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究不同激发源类型（平面波等扩展源与点偶极、电子束等局域源）如何影响等离激元异质二聚体的量子响应，并揭示量子表面修正在其中的作用机制。

Method: 采用基于 Feibelman 参数（Feibelman parameters）的表面响应形式（surface-response formalism），研究纳米球异质二聚体（nanosphere heterodimers），其中一个粒子表现为电子溢出（spill-in）、另一个表现为电子溢出（spill-out），并分别施加扩展源（平面波）与局域源（点偶极和电子束）。

Result: 在扩展源下，两种方向相反的量子表面修正（quantum surface corrections）可相互抵消，产生与局域响应近似（local-response approximation）无差别的介观光谱（mesoscopic spectra）。在局域源下，该现象显著改变：可独立选择性探测各粒子的量子响应，其中电子束通过高阶模式（higher-order modes）实现，点偶极源则可探测完整的模式图景（modal landscape）。

Conclusion: 该描述为极端等离激元结构（extreme plasmonic architectures）中光—物质相互作用（light--matter interactions）的机制提供了新见解，并为理解与调控等离激元—量子发射器（plasmonic--quantum emitter）结构开辟了新视角。

Abstract: We probe the quantum response of plasmonic heterodimers with extended (plane waves) and localized (point dipoles and electron beams) sources. Implementing the surface-response formalism based on Feibelman parameters, we explore nanosphere heterodimers where one particle is characterized by electron spill-in, while the other by spill-out. We identify situations where the two quantum surface corrections that act in opposite spectral-shift directions can cancel out, producing mesoscopic spectra that do not differ from the predictions of the local-response approximation. This picture changes dramatically once localized sources are implemented, allowing one to selectively probe the quantum response of the particles independently. Such probing occurs in terms of higher-order modes in the case of electron beams, or for the complete modal landscape in the case of point dipole sources. This description offers a new insight into the mechanisms governing light--matter interactions in extreme plasmonic architectures, and opens new perspectives for the understanding and tailoring of plasmonic--quantum emitter architectures.

</details>


### [260] [Topological Fermi-polaron Polaritons with Electrically Invertible Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39791)
*Xin Xie,Chenxi Liu,Yuze Liu,Lingxiao Zhou,Chulwon Lee,Yuhan Zhang,Nathanial Lydick,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Kai Sun,Hui Deng*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作提出并实现“费米极化子极化激元（Fermi-polaron polaritons）”这一拓扑玻色-费米混合物，通过费米极化子多体相互作用、光子对称性与强光-物质耦合的相互作用，实现大带隙与可电学翻转的拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑极化激元（topological polaritons）可用于构建光学陈能带（Chern bands），但半导体极化激元体系中时间反演对称性破缺（time-reversal-symmetry breaking）通常受限于微弱塞曼劈裂（Zeeman splitting），难以获得大带隙或高效电学调控。

Method: 采用栅极调控的单层过渡金属硫族化合物（transition-metal dichalcogenide, TMD）与对称性工程设计的（symmetry-engineered）光子晶体集成，构建具有拓扑能带结构的费米极化子极化激元。

Result: 能带拓扑与栅极可调多体相互作用之间的直接耦合，提供了超越裸塞曼劈裂的放大时间反演对称性破缺机制，并实现了拓扑带隙（topological gaps）、贝里曲率（Berry curvature）与陈数（Chern numbers）的电学翻转。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了面向相互作用驱动拓扑态（interaction-driven topological states）与可编程拓扑光子学（programmable topological photonics）的电学可重构平台。

Abstract: Topological polaritons provide a route to engineer optical Chern bands, but realizing large gaps or efficient electrical tunability remains challenging because time-reversal-symmetry breaking in semiconductor polariton systems is typically limited by weak Zeeman splittings. Here, we demonstrate a topological Bose-Fermi mixture --- Fermi-polaron polaritons ---where large gaps and electrically invertible topology emerge from the interplay of Fermi-polaron many-body interactions, photonic symmetry, and strong light--matter coupling. Using a gate-controlled monolayer transition-metal dichalcogenide integrated with a symmetry-engineered photonic crystal, we create Fermi-polaron polaritons with topological band structures. The direct coupling between band topology and gate-tunable many-body interactions provides a mechanism for amplified time-reversal-symmetry breaking beyond bare Zeeman splitting and enables electrical inversion of topological gaps, Berry curvature, and Chern numbers. These results establish an electrically reconfigurable platform for interaction-driven topological states and programmable topological photonics.

</details>


### [261] [Design of Mechanically Compliant Membrane Reflectors for Giant Optical Phase Nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40354)
*Lior Michaeli,Harry A. Atwater*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作基于实测参数，预测一种带蛇形弹簧的氮化硅薄膜蹦床结构（trampoline）可产生 263 rad W⁻¹ 的巨相位响应度及 5.4×10⁻⁷ m² W⁻¹ 的等效 n₂，并提出强度-量程-孔径（strength-range-aperture）评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光非线性（optical nonlinearity）源于光子系统响应依赖光强，而辐射压（radiation pressure）可通过推动机械柔性反射镜改变反射光相位来实现此类非线性；需评估并比较现有机械谐振器作为反射式相位元件的性能。

Method: 基于实测性质，对带蛇形弹簧（serpentine springs）的氮化硅（silicon nitride）薄膜蹦床结构进行相位响应度与等效非线性折射率（n₂,eff）预测；提出强度-量程-孔径指标，并在统一的直接反射协议（direct-reflection protocol）下与已报道机械谐振器进行对比。

Result: 预测得到 263 rad W⁻¹ 的巨相位响应度与 5.4×10⁻⁷ m² W⁻¹ 的器件等效 n₂,eff；该蹦床在对比中为离群值，兼具高柔顺性、实用光学可及性，并在完整 2π 反射相位移动中保持 99.85% 的小信号相位响应度。

Conclusion: 实际实现需光学与热学协同设计（optical and thermal co-design）；该框架为未来机械-光学（mechano-optical）相位元件的设计优先事项提供了指引。

Abstract: Optical nonlinearities arise when the response of a photonic system depends on light intensity. Radiation pressure can create such a nonlinearity by moving a mechanically compliant reflector, thereby shifting the phase of the reflected light. Based on measured properties, we predict a giant phase responsivity of 263 rad W$^{-1}$ and a device-equivalent $n_{2,\mathrm{eff}} = 5.4 \times 10^{-7}$ m$^2$ W$^{-1}$ for a silicon nitride membrane trampoline with serpentine springs. We introduce strength-range-aperture metrics for reflective phase elements. Among reported mechanical resonators compared under a common direct-reflection protocol, this trampoline is an outlier, combining high compliance, practical optical accessibility, and 99.85% retention of its small-signal phase responsivity through a full $2π$ reflected-phase shift. Practical implementation requires optical and thermal co-design. Our framework indicates design priorities for future mechano-optical phase elements.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [262] [Resurgent rigidity of mock theta functions: uniqueness and natural boundary crossing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40276)
*Ovidiu Costin,Gerald V. Dunne,Ali Saraeb*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文发展了一套针对 mock theta 函数（mock theta functions）的 resurgence 跨级数（transseries）方法，揭示 Mordell–Appell 积分是其模结构与 resurgence 结构的底层来源，并据此实现自然边界（natural boundary）的典范穿越与新型 mock theta 函数的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: mock theta 函数具有模变换性质却非模形式，且在单位圆上有自然边界，其解析与代数结构长期缺乏统一的 resurgent 解释。本文旨在通过 Mordell–Appell 积分为其模变换律、唯一性刻画与边界穿越提供系统框架。

Method: 将 Mordell–Appell 积分表示为初等 resurgence 函数（resurgent functions）的 Laplace 变换，并将 Laplace 围道旋转至 Stokes 线；利用其 Stokes 现象与模变换律复现相应 unary series 及 mock theta 函数的性质。同时把模变换律视为刻画 mock theta 向量（mock theta vectors）的函数方程，并在典范极小增长归一化下求解。

Result: 对 Ramanujan 的 order-3 对 (f,ω) 及满足完整 SL(2,Z) 变换律的 order 5、7、11 的区分性 mock theta 向量，证明该函数方程在单位圆内于典范极小增长归一化下唯一确定全纯解，且该解正是经典 mock theta 向量；若舍去该归一化，则得到 order 5、7、11 齐次问题的显式非平凡解，即系数增长更快的新 mock theta 函数。

Conclusion: 给出两种穿越自然边界 |q|=1 的内在构造：其一延续模函数方程并取另一侧的唯一全纯解；其二在尖点（cusp）处对 resurgence 渐近级数作 τ↦−τ 并进行 Écalle–Borel 求和。二者被证明一致，从而给出典范且显式的边界穿越。

Abstract: We develop a resurgent transseries approach to mock theta functions and show that the associated Mordell--Appell integrals underlie their modular and resurgent structure. From these integrals, we recover the modular transformation laws, obtain uniqueness characterizations of the mock theta vectors, provide a canonical crossing of their natural boundary, and produce new mock theta functions.
  After representing the Mordell--Appell integrals as Laplace transforms of elementary resurgent functions, we rotate the Laplace contour to a Stokes line. Their Stokes phenomena and their modular transformation laws reproduce those of the associated unary series and, combined with our uniqueness results, those of the mock theta functions. Conversely, we regard these modular transformation laws as functional equations governing the mock theta vectors.
  For Ramanujan's order-$3$ pair $(f,ω)$ and the distinguished mock theta vectors of orders $5$, $7$, and $11$, satisfying the full $SL(2,\mathbb Z)$ transformation laws, we prove that these functional equations uniquely determine a holomorphic solution in the unit disk under canonical minimal-growth normalization. This unique solution is precisely the classical mock theta vector. We show that the normalization is essential: for orders $5$, $7$, and $11$ we determine explicit nontrivial solutions of the homogeneous problem, yielding new mock theta functions with faster-growing coefficients.
  Finally, we give two intrinsic and natural constructions for crossing the natural boundary $|q|=1$. The first continues the modular functional equations and takes their unique holomorphic solutions on the other side. The second acts on the resurgent asymptotic series at the cusp: we replace $τ$ by $-τ$ and apply Écalle--Borel summation. We prove that the constructions agree, giving a canonical and explicit boundary crossing.

</details>
